Āpastambadharmasūtram
Haradattamiśraviracitayā Ujjvalābhyayā Vyākhyayā Sametam
praṇipatya mahādevaṃ haradattena dhīmatā |
dharmākhyapraśnayor eṣā kriyate vṛttir ujjvalā ||
athātas sāmayācārikān dharmān vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || 1.1.1 ||
athaśabda ānantarye | ataśśabdo hetau | uktāni śrautāni gāryāṇi ca karmāṇi | tāni vakṣyamāṇān dharmān apekṣante | katham | “ācāntena kartavyam,” “śucinā kartavyam,” “yajñopajīvinā kartavyam,” iti vacanād ācamanaśaucādīn apekṣante |
saṃdhyāhīno ‘śucir nityam anarhaḥ sarvakarmasu |
iti vacanāt saṃdhyāvandanam api | evam “aśucikaranirveṣaḥ,” “dvijātikarmabhyo hāniḥ patanam” iti vacanāt brahmahatyādiprāyaścittāni ca | evam anyeṣv api yathāsaṃbhavam apekṣā draṣṭavyā | atas tadanantaraṃ “sāmayācārikān dharmān vyākhyāsyāmaḥ” | pauruṣeyī vyavasthā samayaḥ | sa ca dvividhaḥ, vidhir niyamaḥ pratiṣedhaś ceti | tatra pravṛttiprayojano vidhiḥ, “saṃdhyoś ca bahir grāmād āsanaṃ vāgyataś ca” ityādi | nivṛttiprayojanāv itarau | “prāṅmukho ‘nnāni bhuñjīta” iti niyamaḥ | kṣudupaghātārthā bhojane pravṛttiḥ | śakyaṃ ca yat kiṃcid diṅmukhenāpi bhuñjānena kṣud upahantum | tatra niyamaḥ kriyate, prāṅmukha eva bhuñjīta na dakṣiṇāmukha iti | parisaṃkhyā tu niyamasyaiva kiyān api bhedaḥ | evaṃ dravyārjane rāgāt pravṛttaṃ prati niyamaḥ kriyate, yājanādhyāpanapratigrahair eva brāhmaṇo dhanam ārjayet, na tu kṛṣivāṇijyādineti | “brāhmaṇasya gaur iti padopasparśanaṃ varjayet” iti pratiṣedhaḥ | samayamūlā ācārāḥ samayācārāḥ, teṣu bhavāḥ sāmayācārikāḥ, evaṃbhūtān dharmān iti | karmajanyo ‘bhyudayaniḥśreyasahetur apūrvākhya ātmaguṇo dharmaḥ, taddhetubhūtakarmavyākhyānam eva dharmavyākhyānam | tatra vidhiṣu tāvad vidhiviṣayānuṣṭhānād dharma iti nāsti vipratipattiḥ | niyameṣv api niyamānuṣṭhānād dharmaḥ | niṣedheṣv api nañarthānuṣṭhānāt dharmaḥ iti kecit | ata eva dharmān ity aviśeṣeṇāha | anye tu – vidhiṣv eva dharmaḥ | itarayos tu viparītānuṣṭhānād adharmaḥ kevalam, na tu viṣayānuṣṭhānāt kaścid dharmaḥ | na hy apratigṛhṇann apiban vā surāṃ dhārmika iti loke prasiddhaḥ | sūtre tu dharmagrahaṇam adharmasyāpy upalakṣaṇārtham iti sthitiḥ || 1.1.1 ||
kiṃ nu bhoḥ! samayo ‘pi pramāṇaṃ yadi syāt idam api pramāṇaṃ bhavitum arhati – “caityaṃ vandeta svargakāmaḥ,” “prage bhuñjīta,” “keśān luñchet,” “tiṣṭhan bhuñjīta,” “nagnaḥ snāyāt” iti | tatrāha |
dharmajñasamayaḥ pramāṇam || 1.1.2 ||
na brūmaḥ samayamātraṃ pramāṇam iti | kiṃ tarhi | dharmajñā ye manvādayas teṣāṃ samayaḥ pramāṇaṃ dharmādharmayoḥ |
kathaṃ punar idam avagamyate manvādayo dharmajñā na buddhādaya iti | yady ucyeta buddhādīnām atīndriye dharme jñānaṃ na saṃbhavatīti, tan manvādiṣv api samānam | atha teṣāṃ dharmajñād atiśayād atīndriye ‘pi jñānaṃ saṃbhavatīti, tad buddhādiṣv api samānam | yathāha |
sugato yadi sarvajñaḥ kapilo neti kā pramā |
tāv ubhau yadi sarvajñau matibhedaḥ kathaṃ tayoḥ || iti |
vaktavyo vā viśeṣaḥ || 1.1.2 ||
tam āha |
vedāś ca || 1.1.3 ||
co ‘vadhāraṇe | vedā eva mūlapramāṇaṃ dharmādharmayoḥ | na ca nityeṣu nirdoṣeṣu vedeṣūktopālambhasaṃbhavaḥ | svataḥ pramāṇasya hi śabdasya na vaktṛdoṣanibandhanam aprāmāṇyam | tad ihāsmadādīnāṃ dharmajñasamayaḥ pramāṇam, dharmajñānāṃ tu vedāḥ pramāṇam | manur apy āha |
vedo ‘khilo dharmamūlaṃ smṛtiśīle ca tadvidām |
ācāraś caiva sādhūnām ātmanas tuṣṭir eva ca || iti | [MDh 2.6]
gautamo ‘pi: “vedo dharmamūlaṃ tadvidāṃ ca smṛtiśīle” [GDh 1.1–2] iti | yady apy apratyakṣo vedo dharmamūlabhūto ‘smadādibhir nopalabhyate, tathāpi manvādaya upalabdhavanta ity anumīyante | vakṣyati ca “teṣām utsannāḥ pāṭhāḥ prayogād anumīyante” [ĀpDh 1.12.10] iti
|| 1.1.3 ||
catvāro varṇā brāhmaṇakṣatriyavaiśyaśūdrāḥ || 1.1.4 ||
brāhmaṇādyāś catvāro varṇasaṃjñikāḥ | te ca sāmayācārikair dharmair adhikriyante | caturṇām evopadeśe ‘pi punaś caturgrahaṇaṃ yathā kathaṃcic caturṣv antarbhūtānām api grahaṇārtham | tataś ca brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyo vaiśya ity bodhāyanādibhir uktānām anulomajānām apy atra grahaṇam | tathā ca gautamaḥ pratilomānām eva dharme anadhikāram āha: “pratilomās tu dharmahīnāḥ” [GDh 4.17] iti || 1.1.4 ||
teṣāṃ pūrvaḥ pūrvo janmataḥ śreyān || 1.1.5 ||
janmana iti vacanāt sadvṛttād api śūdrāt vaiśyabruvo ‘pi śreyān | evaṃ vaiśyāt kṣatriyaḥ, kṣatriyād brāhmaṇaḥ || 1.1.5 ||
aśūdrāṇām aduṣṭakarmaṇām upāyanaṃ vedādhyayanam
agnyādheyaṃ phalavanti ca karmāṇi || 1.1.6 ||
śūdravarjitānāṃ trayāṇāṃ varṇānām aduṣṭakarmaṇām upāyanādayo dharmāḥ | upāyanam upanayanam | nātra traivarṇikānām upanayanādīni vidhīyante, prāptatvāt | nāpi śūdrāṇāṃ pratiṣidyate, prāptyabhāvāt | tathā hi upanayanaṃ tāvat gṛhye “garbhāṣṭameṣu brāhmaṇam” ityādinā traivarṇikānām iti vihitam, ihāpi tathaiva vidhāsyate | adhyayanam api “upetasyācāryakule brahmacārivāsaḥ” [ĀpDh 1.2.11] ity ārabhya vidhānād anupanītasya śūdrasyāprāptam eva | kiṃ ca “śmaśānavac chūdrapatitau” [ĀpDh 1.9.9] ity adhyayanapratiṣedhaprakaraṇe vakṣyate | tataś ca yasya samīpe ’pi nādhyeyaṃ sa kathaṃ svayam adhyetum arhati | agnyādheyam api “vasante brāhmaṇaḥ” [ĀpDh 1.1.19] ityādinā traivarṇikānām eva vihitam | phalavanti cāgnihotrādīni karmāṇi “sa trayāṇāṃ varṇānām” [ĀpDh 1.18.9] ity uktatvāt traivarṇikānām eva niyatāni, vidyāgnyabhāvāc ca śūdrāṇām aprasaktāni | ukto vidyāgnyabhāvaḥ | tasmād duṣṭakarmapratiṣedhārthaṃ sūtram, yathā śāstrāntare, “dvijātikarmabhyo hāniḥ patanam” [GDh 21.4] iti | apratiṣedhe tu duṣṭakarmaṇām apy adhikāro bhavaty eva, “phalavanti ca karmāṇi” ity abhidhānāt, kriyata iti karmeti nirvacanāt | “prāg upanayanāt kāmacāravādabhakṣaḥ” [GDh 2.1] iti gautamasmaraṇaṃ brahmahatyādimahāpātakavyatiriktaviṣayam ity anupetasyāpi duṣṭakarmatvasaṃbhavād aduṣṭakarmaṇām ity uktam | śūdrapratiṣedhas tu prāptānuvādaḥ || 1.1.6 ||
yathā brāhmaṇādīnām upanayanādayo dharmāḥ pradhānabhūtāḥ tādṛśaṃ śūdrasya dharmam āha |
śuśrūṣā śūdrasyetareṣāṃ varṇānām || 1.1.7 ||
itareṣāṃ brāhmaṇādīnāṃ varṇānāṃ yā śuśrūṣā sā śūdrasya paramo dharmaḥ || 1.1.7 ||
tatra viśeṣam āha |
pūrvasmin pūrvasmin varṇe niḥśreyasaṃ bhūyaḥ || 1.1.8 ||
sarvaprakāraṃ kṛtāyā vaiśyaśuśrūṣāyā mātrayāpi kṛtā kṣatriyaśuśrūṣā bahataraṃ phalaṃ sādhayati | evaṃ kṣatriyaśuśrūṣāyā api brāhmaṇaśuśrūṣā || 1.1.8 ||
“upāyanaṃ vedādhyayanam” [ĀpDh 1.1.6] ityādi yad uktaṃ asmin krame uapanayane viśeṣam āha |
upanayanaṃ vidyārthasya śrutitaḥ saṃskāraḥ || 1.1.9 ||
vidyayā arthaḥ prayojanaṃ yasya sa vidyārthaḥ | tasyāyaṃ śrutivihitaḥ saṃskāraḥ | upanayanaṃ nāma vidyārthasyeti vacanān mūkāder na bhavati | tathā ca śaṅkhalikhitau: “nonmattamūkānt saṃskuryāt” iti | liṅgasya vivakṣitatvāt striyā api na bhavati, yady api tasyāḥ “agne gṛhapate” ityādikayā vidyayā arthaḥ | śrutita iti vacanaṃ tadatikrame śrautātikramaprāyaścittaprāptyartham
|| 1.1.9 ||
anekavedādhyāyināṃ vedavratavad upanayanam api prativedaṃ bhedena kartavyam iti prāpte ucyate |
sarvebhyo vai vedebhyaḥ sāvitry anūcyata iti hi
brāhmaṇam || 1.1.10 ||
tribhya eva tu vedebhyaḥ pādam pādam aduduhat |
tad ity ṛco ‘syāḥ sāvitryāḥ parameṣṭhī prajāpatiḥ || [MDh 2.77]
iti manuvacanam | tataś ca upanayane yat sāvitryā anuvacanaṃ tanmukhena sarve vedā anūktā bhavantīty agṛhyamāṇaviśeṣatvāt ekam evopanayanaṃ sarvārtham iti | asminn arthe pramāṇam api bhavati, brāhmaṇam iti hi vācā paṭhitam | atharvaṇasya tu vedasya pṛthagupanayanaṃ kartavyam | tathā ca tatraiva śrutiḥ: “nānyatra saṃskṛto bhṛgvaṅgiraso ‘dhīyīta” iti || 1.1.10 ||
vidvān evopanetābhigamya iti vidhātum aviduṣo nindām āha |
tamaso vā eṣa tamaḥ praviśati yam avidvān upanayate yaś
cāvidvān iti hi brāhmaṇam || 1.1.11 ||
yathā kaścit tamasaḥ sakāśāt tama eva praviṣṭo na kiṃcij jānāti evam evaiṣaḥ yaṃ māṇavakam avidvān upanayate | tathā yaś cāvidvān | upanayate ity apekṣate | yaś ca svayam avidvān sann upanayate so ‘pi tamasa eva tamaḥ praviśati | asminn arthe brāhmaṇaṃ bhavati || 1.1.11 ||
kīdṛśas tarhy upanetābhigamya | tatrāha |
tasminn abhijanavidyāsamudetaṃ samāhitaṃ
saṃskartāram īpset || 1.1.12 ||
avicchinnavedavedisaṃbandhe janmābhijanaḥ | ṣaḍbhir aṅgair saha yathāvad arthajñānaparyantam adhīto vedo vidyā | sarvāsaṃbhave veda eva vā | tasminn upanayane kartavye tābhyām abhijanavidyābhyāṃ samudetaṃ saṃpannaṃ samāhitaṃ vihitapratiṣiddheṣv avahitamanasaṃ saṃskartāram ācāryam īpset | icchayā karaṇaṃ lakṣyate, āpnuyāt abhigacched iti || 1.1.12 ||
tasmiṃś caiva vidyākarmāntam avipratipanne
dharmebhyaḥ || 1.1.13 ||
tasminn evopanetari vidyākarma vidyāgrahaṇaṃ kartavyam āntam ā samāpteḥ, avipratipanne dharmebhyaḥ yady asāv ācāryo dharmebhyaḥ pracyuto na bhavati | pracyute tu tasminn asaṃparkārhe anyato ‘pi vidyākarma bhavaty eva || 1.1.13 ||
ācāryaśabdaṃ nirāha |
yasmād dharmān ācinoti sa ācāryaḥ || 1.1.14 ||
yasmāt puruṣād ayaṃ māṇavako dharmān ācinoti ātmanaḥ pracinoti śikṣate sa ācāryaḥ | “apy akṣarasāmyān nirbrūyāt” iti cakāramātreṇedaṃ nirvacanam | anena prakāreṇa māṇavakam ācāryaḥ śaucācārān śikṣayed ity uktaṃ bhavati || 1.1.14 ||
tasmai na druhyet kadācana || 1.1.15 ||
evaṃbhūtāyācāryāya kadācana kadācid api na druhyet, tadviṣayam apakāraṃ na kuryāt || 1.1.15 ||
kasmād ity ata āha |
sa hi vidyātas taṃ janayati || 1.1.16 ||
sa hi ācāryas taṃ māṇavakaṃ vidyāto janayati, yathā pitā mātṛtaḥ |
tatrāsya mātā sāvitrī pitā tv ācārya ucyate | [MDh 2.170]
iti śāstrāntaram || 1.1.16 ||
tac cheṣṭhaṃ jama || 1.1.17 ||
tad vidyāto janma śreṣṭhaṃ praśastatamam, abhyudayaniḥśreyasahetubhūtatvāt || 1.1.17 ||
mātāpitṛbhyām ācāryaḥ śreṣṭha ity āha |
śarīram eva mātāpitarau janayataḥ || 1.1.18 ||
mātāpitarau śarīram eva kāṣṭhakuḍyādisamaṃ janayataḥ | ācāryas tu sarvapuruṣārthakṣamaṃ rūpaṃ janayatīti | “ācāryaḥ śreṣṭho gurūṇām” [GDh 2.50] iti gautamaḥ || 1.1.18 ||
vasante brāhmaṇam upanayīta grīṣme rājanyaṃ śaradi vaiśyaṃ
garbhāṣṭameṣu brāhmaṇaṃ garbaikādaśeṣu rājanyaṃ
garbhadvādaśeṣu vaiśyam || 1.1.19 ||
“vasante brāhmaṇam” ityādi gṛhye gatam || 1.1.19 ||
atha kāmyāni || 1.1.20 ||
kāmanimittāny upanayanāni vakṣyante || 1.1.20 ||
saptame brahmavarcasakāmam || 1.1.21 ||
aṣṭama āyuṣkāmam || 1.1.22 ||
navame tejaskāmam || 1.1.23 ||
daśame ‘nnādyākāmam || 1.1.24 ||
ekādaśa indriyakāmam || 1.1.25 ||
dvādaśe paśukāmam || 1.1.26 ||
etāni ṣaṭsūtrāṇi spaṣṭārthāni | sarvatropanayītety apekṣyate || 1.1.21–26 ||
“ācāryādhīnaḥ syāt” [ĀpDh 1.2.19] ityādīni yāni brahmacārivratāni vakṣyante teṣv asamarthānāṃ kumārāṇāṃ varṇakrameṇānukalpān āha |
ā ṣoḍaśād brāhmaṇasyānātyaya ā dvāviṃśāt kṣatriyasyā
caturviṃśād vaiśyasya yathā vrateṣu samarthaḥ syād yāni
vakṣyāmaḥ || 1.1.27 ||
ākāro ‘bhividhau | atyayo ‘tikramaḥ, sa evātyayaḥ, yādṛcchiko dīrghaḥ | āṅo vā praśleṣaḥ | prakaraṇād upanayanakālasyeti gamyate | yathā vrateṣu samarthaḥ syāt tathā etāvān kālaḥ pratīkṣyaḥ | pūrvam eva tu sāmarthye sati aṣṭavarṣādyatikrame vakṣyamāṇaṃ prāyaścittam eva bhavati | evaṃ ṣoḍaśādibhya ūrdhvaṃ kiyantaṃ cit kālam asamarthānāṃ paścāt sāmarthye sati prāyaścittaṃ bhavaty eva || 1.1.27 ||
tad idānīṃ prāyaścittam āha |
atikrānte sāvitryāḥ kāla ṛtuṃ traividyakaṃ brahmacaryaṃ
caret || 1.1.28 ||
yasya yaḥ sāvitryāḥ kāla uktaḥ tadatikrame traividyakam, tryavayavā vidyā trividyā, tām adhīyate traividyāḥ, teṣāṃ idaṃ traividyakam | “caraṇād dharmāmnāyayoḥ” iti vuñ | evaṃbhūtaṃ brahmacaryam agniparicaryām adhyayanaṃ guruśuśrūṣām iti parihāpya sakalaṃ brahmacāridhamaṃ caret | kiyantaṃ kālam | ṛtum | “kālādhvanoḥ” iti dvitīyā | ṛtum iti vacanād ṛtvārambhe prāyaścittārambham icchanti || 1.1.28 ||
athopanayanam || 1.1.29 ||
evaṃ caritavrataḥ upanetavyaḥ || 1.1.29 ||
tataḥ saṃvatsaram udakopasparśanam || 1.1.30 ||
tata upanayanād ārabhya saṃvatsaram udakopasparśaṇaṃ snānaṃ kartavyam | śaktasya triṣavaṇaṃ snānam, anyasya yathāśakti || 1.1.30 ||
athādhyāpyaḥ || 1.1.31 ||
evaṃ caritavrataḥ paścād adhyāpyaḥ || 1.1.31 ||
atha yasya pitā pitāmaha ity anupetau syātāṃ te
brahmahasaṃstutāḥ || 1.1.32 ||
yasya māṇavakasya pitā pitāmahaś cānupetau syātāṃ svayaṃ ca, te tathāvidhāḥ samāṇavakās te brahmahasaṃstutāḥ brahmaṇa ity evaṃ kīrtitā brahmavādibhiḥ | atasmiṃs tacchabdas taddharmaprāptyarthaḥ | evaṃ ca “śmaśānavac chūdrapatitau” [ĀpDh 1.9.9] ity adhyayanapratiṣedhaprakaraṇe vakṣyate | tataś ca yathā brahmaghnasamīpe nādhyetavyam evam eṣām apīti || 1.1.32 ||
teṣām abhyāgamanaṃ bhojanaṃ vivāham iti ca
varjayet || 1.1.33 ||
teṣām eteṣām abhyāgamanam ābhimukhyena gamanaṃ mātāpitṛputradāraśarīrarakṣaṇārtham api varjayet, yady api sarvato rakṣā pratigrāhyeti vakṣyate | bhojanam uddhṛtam api varjayet, “api duṣkṛtakāriṇaḥ” [ĀpDh 1.19.13] iti saty api vacane | vivāhaṃ ca varjayet, yady api “strīratnaṃ duṣkulād api” [MDh 2.238] iti mānavasmaraṇam || 1.1.33 ||
teṣām icchatāṃ prāyaścittam || 1.1.34 ||
icchatām iti vacanāt balātkāreṇa prāyaścittaṃ na kārayitavyam || 1.1.34 ||
yathā prathame ‘tikrama ṛtur evaṃ saṃvatsaraḥ || 1.1.35 ||
yathā prathame ‘trikrame brahmacaryasya ṛtuḥ kālaḥ, evam asminn atikrame saṃvatsaraḥ kālaḥ || 1.1.35 ||
athopanayanaṃ tata udakopasparśanam || 1.1.36 ||
gate || 1.1.36 ||
iti prathamaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
pratipūruṣaṃ saṃkhyāya saṃvatsarān yāvanto ‘nupetāḥ
syuḥ || 1.2.1 ||
yadi pitaivānupetaḥ tataḥ saṃvatsaram ekam | atha pitāmaho ‘pi tato dvau | atha svayam api yathākālam anupanītas tataḥ saṃvatsarān iti || 1.2.1 ||
athodakasparśanamantrāḥ |
saptabhiḥ pāvamānībhir yad anti yac ca dūraka ity etābhir
yajuḥpavitreṇa sāmapavitreṇāṅgiraseneti || 1.2.2 ||
pavamānaḥ somo devatā yāsāṃ tāḥ pāvamānyaḥ | “yad anti yac ca dūrake bhayaṃ vindati mām iha” [RV 9.67.21] ity etābhiḥ ṛgbhiḥ | yajuḥpavitreṇa “āpo asmān mātaraḥ śundhantu” ity anena | sāmapavitreṇa “kayā naś citra ābhuvan” iti tyṛcena [tyādi-] gītena vā vāmadevyena sāmnā | āṅgirasena “haṃ saḥ śuciṣat” ity anena | etair añjalinā śirasy āpo niṣiñcet || 1.2.2 ||
api vā vyāhṛtibhir eva || 1.2.3 ||
pūrvaḥ saha vyāhṛtīnāṃ vikalpaḥ || 1.2.3 ||
athādhyāpyaḥ || 1.2.4 ||
gatam || 1.2.4 ||
“atha yasya pitā pitāmahaḥ” [ĀpDh 1.1.32] ity upakrame yasyety ekavacanam, anye ‘pi “athādhyāpyaḥ” [ĀpDh 1.2.4] iti, madhye tu “te brahmahasaṃstutāḥ [ĀpDh 1.1.32], “teṣām abhyāgamanam” [ĀpDh 1.1.33] “teṣām icchatāṃ prāyaścittam” [ĀpDh 1.2.6] iti bahuvacanam | tatropakramopasaṃhārayor anurodhena māṇavakasyaiva prāyaścittam upanayanam adhyāpanaṃ ca | bahuvacanaṃ tu tathāvidhamāṇavakabahutvāpeṣkayety avocāma ||
atha yasya prapitāmahādi nānusmaryata upanayanaṃ te
śmaśānasāṃstutāḥ || 1.2.5 ||
prapitāmahādi prapitāmahād ārabhya, prapitāmahaḥ pitāmahaḥ pitā svayaṃ ca yathākālam atīte tathāvidhamāṇavakāḥ śmaśānasaṃstutāḥ | tena “śmaśāne sarvataḥ śamyāprāsāt” [ĀpDh 1.9.6] ity adhyayananiṣedha eṣām api saṃnidhau bhavati || 1.2.5 ||
teṣām abhyāgamanaṃ bhojanaṃ vivāham iti ca varjayet
teṣām icchatāṃ prāyaścittaṃ dvādaśavarṣāṇi traividyakaṃ
brahmacaryaṃ cared athopanayanaṃ tata udakopasparśanaṃ
pāvamānyādibhiḥ || 1.2.6 ||
gatam | pāvamānyādibhir ity anenaiva “pratipūrūṣaṃ saṃkhyāya saṃvatsarān” [ĀpDh 1.2.1] ity etad api draṣṭavyam || 1.2.6 ||
atha gṛhamedhopadeśanam || 1.2.7 ||
gṛhamedho gṛhyaśāstraṃ gṛhasthadharmo vā || 1.2.7 ||
nādhyāpanam || 1.2.8 ||
nādhyāpanaṃ kṛtsnavedasya, kiṃ tu gṛhyamantrāṇām eva || 1.2.8 ||
tato yo nivartate tasya saṃskāro yathā prathamātikrame || 1.2.9 ||
tata evaṃ kṛtaprāyaścittād gṛhasthībhūtād yo nivartate utpadyate tasyopanayanasaṃskāraḥ kartavyaḥ | katham | yathā prathame ‘tikrame, ṛtuṃ traividyakaṃ brahmacaryaṃ caritvety arthaḥ || 1.2.9 ||
tata ūrdhvaṃ prakṛtivat || 1.2.10 ||
tatas tu yo nivartate tasya prakṛtivat yathāprāptam upanayanaṃ kartavyam iti | yasya tu prapitāmahasya pitur ārabhya nānusmaryata upanayanaṃ tasya prāyaścittaṃ noktam, dharmajñais tūhitavyam | evaṃ tataḥ pūrveṣv api nirūpitam upanayanam || 1.2.10 ||
athādhyayanavidhiḥ |
upetasyācāryakule brahmacārivāsaḥ || 1.2.11 ||
evaṃ yathāvidhy upetasya brahmacāriṇaḥ sata ācāryakule brahmacārivāso bhavati | brahma vedas tadarthaṃ vrataṃ caratīti brahmacārī | adhyayanāṅgāni vratāni caratā ācāryakule vastavyam ity uktaṃ bhavati || 1.2.11 ||
tatra kālaḥ |
aṣṭācatvāriṃśad varṣāṇi || 1.2.12 ||
caturṇāṃ vedānām adhyayanakāla eṣo ‘ṣṭācatvāriṃśad varṣāṇi, prativedaṃ dvādaśa || 1.2.12 ||
pādūnam || 1.2.13 ||
sa eva kālaḥ pādūnaṃ vā pratyetavyaḥ | pādena ūnaṃ pādūnam | pararūpaṃ katantavat | ṣaṭtriṃśadvarṣāṇi, prativedaṃ nava || 1.2.13 ||
ardhena || 1.2.14 ||
ūnam ity upasamastam apekṣate | caturviṃśativarṣāṇi, prativedaṃ ṣaṭ || 1.2.14 ||
tribhir vā || 1.2.15 ||
pādair ūnam iti prakaraṇād gamyate | dvādaśa varṣāṇi, prativedaṃ trīṇi || 1.2.15 ||
dvādaśāvarārdhyam || 1.2.16 ||
avarārdhyaśabdo ‘varamātrety etasminn arthe vartate | dvādaśavarṣāṇy avaramātrā yathā bhavati tathā brahmacāriṇā gurukule vastavyam | pūrveṇaiva siddhe, yo brahmacārī atimedhāvitayā caturo ‘pi vedān ito ‘lpīyasā kālena gṛhṇāti tenāpy etāvantaṃ kālaṃ gurukule vastavyam | vidyayā snātīty asmin pakṣe nātitvaritena snātavyam ity evamartham idam ārabhyate | etenaikasya vedasya trīṇi varṣāṇi brahmacaryam avaśyaṃbhāvīty ayam arthaḥ siddhaḥ | manur apy āha |
ṣaṭtriṃśadābdikaṃ caryaṃ gurau traividyakaṃ vratam |
tadardhikaṃ padikaṃ vā grahaṇāntikam eva vā || iti | [MDh 3.1]
trayāṇāṃ vedānāṃ ṣaṭtriṃśat, ekaikasya dvādaśa | tadardhakaṃ trayāṇām aṣṭādaśa, ekaikasya ṣaṭ | pādikaṃ vā, trayāṇāṃ nava, ekaikasya trīṇi | grahaṇāntikam eva vety ekaikasya tribhya ūrdham aniyamaḥ, na prāg ity artho draṣṭavyaḥ || 1.2.16 ||
na brahmacāriṇo vidyārthasya paropavāso ‘sti || 1.2.17 ||
brahmacārividyārthaśabdayor artha uktaḥ | yo brahmacārī vidyārtho bhavati tena divasamātram api parasya samīpe na vastavyam | ācāryasamīpa eva vastavyam ity uktaṃ bhavati | vidyārthasyeti vacanān naiṣṭhikasya kadācid anyatra vāse ‘pi na doṣaḥ | yad vā – bhojananivṛttir evopavāsaḥ, paralokārtha upavāsaḥ paropavāsaḥ | atra pakṣe “āhitāgnir anaḍvāṃś ca brahmacārī ca” [ĀpDh 2.9.13] ityādi vidyārthabrahmacariviṣayam || 1.2.18 ||
atha brahmacaryavidhiḥ || 1.2.18 ||
brahma vedaḥ, tadarthaṃ yad vrataṃ caritavyaṃ tad brahmacaryam, tad adhikriyate || 1.2.18 ||
ācāryādhīnaḥ syād anyatra patanīyebhyaḥ || 1.2.19 ||
“ācāryādhīno bhava” ity upanayanānte yat saṃśāsanaṃ tat siddhaivācāryādhīnatānūdyate “anyatra patanīyebhyaḥ” iti viśeṣaṃ vakṣyāmīti | patanīya iti karaṇe kṛtpratyayaḥ | asmadarātiṃ brāḥmaṇaṃ vyāpādayety ācāryeṇa codito ‘py evamādi na kuryād iti || 1.2.19 ||
hitakārī guror apratilomayan vācā || 1.2.20 ||
ācāryeṇa prayukto ‘py aprayukto ‘pi tasmai hitam eva kuryāt vācā prātikūlyam akurvan
|| 1.2.20 ||
adhāsanaśāyī || 1.2.21 ||
śayanaṃ śāyaḥ | “kṛtyalyuṭo bahulam” [Pāṇ 3.3.113] iti bahulavacanād ghañ | adha āsanaśāyau yasya sa adhāsanśāyī | gurusaṃnidhāv adha āsīta, adhaḥ śayītety uktaṃ bhavati | adhaḥśabdasya savarṇadīrghaś chāndasaḥ, apapāṭho vā | tṛṇeṣu saṃstareṣu cāsanaśayane śiṣṭācārasiddhe || 1.2.21 ||
nānudeśya bhuñjīta || 1.2.22 ||
anudeśyaṃ śrāddhārtham uddiṣṭhaṃ devatārthaṃ voddiṣṭaṃ na bhuñjīta || 1.2.22 ||
tathā kṣāralavaṇamadhumāṃsāni || 1.2.23 ||
na bhuñjītety eva | kṣārādīni gṛhye gatāni || 1.2.23 ||
adivāsvāpī || 1.2.24 ||
divā na supyāt || 1.2.24 ||
agandhasevī || 1.2.25 ||
candanādigandhadravyāṇi na seveta || 1.2.25 ||
maithunaṃ na caret || 1.2.26 ||
upacārakriyā keliḥ sparśo bhūṣaṇavāsasām |
ekaśayyāsanaṃ krīḍā cumbanāliṅgane tathā ||
ity evamādeḥ sarvasyopalakṣaṇam || 1.2.16 ||
utsannaślāghaḥ || 1.2.27 ||
ślāghā śobhā, sā utsannā yasya sa utsannaślāghaḥ | evaṃbhūto bhavet | mrakṣaṇādinā mukhādikam ujjvalitaṃ na kuryād iti || 1.2.17 ||
aṅgāni na prakṣālayīta || 1.2.28 ||
vinā śirasā sukhārtham uṣṇāmbvādinā śarīraṃ na prakṣāleyet || 1.2.18 ||
prakṣālayīta tv aśuciliptāni guror asaṃdarśe || 1.2.29 ||
yāni mūtrapurīṣādyaśuciliptāny aṅgāni tāni kāmaṃ mṛdvāribhiḥ prakṣālayet, yāvad gandho lepaś cāpaiti | tad api guror asaṃdarśe, yatra gurur na paścyati tatra | ācāryaprakaraṇe gurugrahaṇāt pitrādīnām api grahaṇam || 1.2.29 ||
nāpsu ślāghamānaḥ snāyāt || 1.2.30 ||
snāne prāpte na ślāghamānaḥ snāyāt | kiṃ tu daṇḍavat plavet | snānīyair dravyair malāpakarṣaṇaṃ ślāghā, krīḍā vā | apara āha – “aṅgāni na prakṣālayīta” [ĀpDh 1.2.28] ity ā samāvartanān nityasnānasya patiṣedhaḥ, “prakṣālayīta tv aśuciliptāni” [ĀpDh 1.2.29] iti naimittikasnānavidhiḥ, “nāpsu ślāghamānaḥ snāyāt” iti tatraiva ślāghapratiṣedha iti || 1.2.30 ||
jaṭilaḥ || 1.2.31 ||
sarvān eva keśān jaṭāṃ kṛtvā bibhṛyāt || 1.2.31 ||
śikhājaṭo vā vāpayed itarān || 1.2.32 ||
atha vā śikhām eva jaṭāṃ kṛtvā itarān keśān vāpayen nāpitena || 1.2.32 ||
mauñjī mekhalā trivṛd brāhmaṇasya śaktiviṣaye
dakṣiṇāvṛttānām || 1.2.33 ||
muñjānāṃ vikāro mauñjī | trivṛt triguṇā | evaṃbhūtā brāhmaṇasya mekhalā bhavati | sā śaktiviṣaye śaktau satyāṃ dakṣiṇāvṛttānāṃ kartavyā | taddhitārthe guṇabhūtānām api mujñānām etad viśeṣaṇam || 1.2.33 ||
jyā rājanyasya || 1.2.34 ||
spaṣṭam || 1.2.34 ||
mauñjī vāyomiśrā || 1.2.35 ||
atha vāyomiśrā kvacit kālāyasena baddhā mauñjī mekhalā bhavati rājanyasya || 1.2.35 ||
āvīsūtraṃ vaiśyasya || 1.2.36 ||
avir ūrṇāyuḥ kambalaprakṛtiḥ, tatsaṃbandhinī ūrṇā āvī, tatkṛtaṃ sūtram āvīsūtram, sā mekhalā vaiśyasya bhavati || 1.2.37 ||
sairī tāmalī vety eke || 1.2.37 ||
sairī sīravāhayoktrarajjuḥ | tāmalo mūlodakasaṃjño vṛkṣaḥ, tasya tvacā grathitā tāmalī || 1.2.37 ||
pālāśo daṇḍo brāhmaṇasya, naiyygrodhaskandhajo ‘vāṅgro
rājanyasya, bādara audumbaro vā vaiśyasya, vārkṣo daṇḍa ity
avarṇasaṃyogenaika upadiśanti || 1.2.38 ||
gṛhye gatam || 1.2.38 ||
vāsaḥ || 1.2.39 ||
vasyate kaupīnam ācchādyate yena tad vāsaḥ || 1.2.39 ||
tad vakṣyate |
śāṇikṣaumājināni || 1.2.40 ||
śaṇasya vikāraḥ śāṇī paṭī | kṣumā atasī, tasyā vikāraḥ kṣaumaḥ | śvetapaṭṭākhyo vāsoviśeṣa ity anye | ajinaṃ yasya kasyacin medhyasya paśoḥ | trīṇy etāni varṇānupūrvyeṇa vāsāṃsi || 1.2.40 ||
kāṣāyāṃ caike vastram upadiśanti || 1.2.41 ||
eke ācāryāḥ vastraṃ ca dhāryam upadiśanti | vastraṃ ca kārpāsam, tac ca kāṣāyaṃ kaṣāyeṇa raktam | brāhmaṇasyety arthād gamyate, itarayos tu vakṣyamāṇatvāt || 1.2.41 ||
iti dvitīyaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
māñjiṣṭhaṃ rājanyasya || 1.3.1 ||
mañjiṣṭhayā raktaṃ māñjiṣṭham || 1.3.1 ||
hāridraṃ vaiśyasya || 1.3.2 ||
haridrayā raktaṃ hāridram | etāny uttarīyāṇi || 1.3.2 ||
hāriṇam aiṇeyaṃ vā kṛṣṇaṃ brāhmaṇasya || 1.3.3 ||
“bastājinam” [ĀpDh 1.3.6] iti vakṣyamāṇatvād ihāpy ajinam iti gamyate | “ajinam uttaram uttarayā” ity upanayane yad ajinaṃ dhāryam uktaṃ taddhāriṇaṃ brāhmaṇasya | hariṇo mṛgas tasya vikāro hāriṇam | aiṇeyaṃ vā kṛṣṇam, eṇī mṛgī, tasyā vikāra aiṇeyam | “eṇyā ḍhañ” [Pāṇ 4.3.159] | dvividhā eṇāḥ kṛṣṇāś ca gaurāś ca, ato viśeṣyate kṛṣṇam aiṇeyam iti || 1.3.3 ||
asmin pakṣe viśeṣam āha |
kṛṣṇaṃ ced anupastīrṇāsanaśāyī syāt || 1.3.4 ||
kṛṣṇaṃ ced bibhṛyān na hāriṇam, tatas tasminn upastīrṇe nāsīta na ca śayīta | ayaṃ tāvad arthaḥ: śabdanirvāhas tu “adhāsanaśāyī” [ĀpDh 1.2.21] ity atra kṛtaḥ || 1.3.4 ||
rauravaṃ rājanyasya || 1.3.5 ||
rurur bindumān mṛgaḥ || 1.3.5 ||
bastājinaṃ vaiśyasya || 1.3.6 ||
bastaś chāgaḥ || 1.3.6 ||
āvikaṃ sārvavarṇikam || 1.3.7 ||
avir ūrṇāyuḥ, sa evāvikaḥ, tasya carma āvikam | tat sarveṣām eva varṇānām | asya hāriṇādibhir vikakpaḥ || 1.3.7 ||
kambalaś ca || 1.3.8 ||
ayam apy āvika eva prāvaraṇaḥ sarveṣām || 1.3.8 ||
“kāṣāyaṃ caike vastram upadiśanti” [ĀpDh 1.2.41] ity ārabhya vāsāṃsy ajināni ca vihitāni | tatra kāmavaśena viśeṣam āha |
brahmavṛddhim icchann ajināny eva vasīta kṣatravṛddhim
icchan vastrāṇy evobhayavṛddhim icchann ubhayam iti hi
brāhmaṇam || 1.3.9 ||
brahmavṛddhir brāhmaṇavṛddhiḥ | kṣatravṛddhiḥ kṣatriyavṛddhiḥ || 1.3.9 ||
atha svapakṣam āha |
ajinaṃ tv evottaraṃ dhārayet || 1.3.10 ||
uttaram uttarīyam ajinam eva dhārayet || 1.3.10 ||
anṛttadarśī || 1.3.11 ||
nṛttaṃ na paśyet || 1.3.11 ||
sabhāḥ samājāṃś cāgantā || 1.3.12 ||
dyūtādisthānaṃ sabhā | utsavādiṣu samavāyaḥ samājaḥ | tāḥ sabhāḥ samājāṃś ca agantā tācchīlyena na gacchet | yadṛcchāgamane na doṣaḥ || 1.3.12 ||
ajanavādaśīlaḥ || 1.3.13 ||
janavādaḥ parivādaḥ lokavārtā vā, tacchīlo na syāt || 1.3.13 ||
rahaḥśīlaḥ || 1.3.14 ||
sati saṃbhave rahaḥśīlaḥ syāt || 1.3.14 ||
guror udācāreṣv akartā svairikarmāṇi || 1.3.15 ||
yeṣu pradeśeṣu gurur udācarati paunaḥpunyena carati teṣu svairikarmāṇi maitraprasādhanādīni na kuryāt || 1.3.15 ||
strībhir yāvadarthasaṃbhāṣī || 1.3.16 ||
strībhiḥ saha yāvat prayojanam eva saṃbhāṣeta, na prasaktānuprasaktam aticiram |
balavān indriyagrāmo vidvāṃsam api karṣati | [MDh 2.215cd]
iti | atibālābhir ativṛddhābhiś ca na doṣaḥ || 1.3.16 ||
mṛduḥ || 1.3.17 ||
kṣamāvān || 1.3.17 ||
śāntaḥ || 1.3.18 ||
indriyāṇām asadviṣaye pravṛttabhāvaḥ śamaḥ, tadvān śāntaḥ || 1.3.18 ||
dāntaḥ || 1.3.19 ||
vihiteṣu karmasv aglānir damaḥ, tadvān || 1.3.19 ||
hrīmān || 1.3.20 ||
hrīr lajjā, tadvān || 1.3.20 ||
dṛḍhadhṛtiḥ || 1.3.21 ||
labdhe naṣṭe mṛte vā dhṛtāv evāvasthitaḥ syāt, na hṛṣyet, na ca viṣīdet || 1.3.21 ||
aglāṃsnuḥ || 1.3.22 ||
utsāhasaṃpannaḥ | “glājisthaś ca snuḥ” | atrānusvārapāṭhaś chāndasaḥ, apapāṭho vā || 1.3.22 ||
akrodhanaḥ || 1.3.23 ||
na kasmaicid api krudhyet || 1.3.23 ||
anasūyuḥ || 1.3.24 ||
parabhāgyānusantāpo ‘sūyā, tacchīlo na syāt || 1.3.24 ||
sarvaṃ lābham āharan gurave sāyaṃ prātar amatreṇa
bhikṣācaryaṃ cared bhikṣamāṇo ‘nyatrāpapātrebhyo
‘bhiśastāc ca || 1.3.25 ||
apapātrāḥ pratilomajā rajakādayaḥ | apagatāni hi teṣāṃ pātrāṇi pākādyarthāni caturbhir varṇaiḥ saha | abhiśastān vakṣyati “atha patanīyāni” [ĀpDh 1.21.7] ityādinā | tān ubhayān varjayitvānyatra bhikṣeta | tatra ca bhikṣamāṇaḥ sarvaṃ lābhaṃ yac ca yāvac ca labdhaṃ gohiraṇyādi tat sarvam amāyayā gurave āharet | evam ahar ahaḥ kurvan sāyaṃ prātar amatreṇa na hastādinā bhikṣācaryaṃ bhikṣācaraṇaṃ caret kuryāt | sāyaṃ prātar iti vacanān na sāyaṃgṛhītena prātarāśaḥ, nāpi prātargṛhītena sāyamāśaḥ || 1.3.25 ||
atha bhikṣāpratyākhyānaṃ nindituṃ brāhmaṇam āghuṣtate |
strīṇāṃ pratyācakṣāṇānāṃ samāhito brahmacārīṣṭaṃ dattaṃ
hutaṃ prajāṃ paśūn brahmavarcasam annādyaṃ vṛṅkte |
tasmād u ha vai brahmacārisaṅghaṃ carantaṃ na
pratyācakṣītāpi haiṣv evaṃvidha evaṃvrataḥ syād iti hi
brāhmaṇam || 1.3.26 ||
vyākhyātaḥ samāhitaḥ | samāhito brahmacārī yābhiḥ strībhir bhikṣā pratyākhyāyate tāsāṃ pratyācakṣāṇām iṣṭaṃ yāgenārjitaṃ dharmam, dattaṃ dānenārjitam, hutaṃ darvīhomaiś ca gārhyair ārjitam, sarvam eva dharmaṃ vṛṅkte ācchinatti | yasmād evaṃ tasmād brahmacārisaṃghaṃ carantaṃ na pratyācakṣīta | u ha vā iti nipātā vākyālaṅkārārthāḥ | apihaśabdau kadācid ity evamarthaṃ dyotayataḥ | eṣu saṅghībhūteṣu brahmacāriṣu kadācid evaṃvidhaḥ samāhitaḥ evaṃvrataḥ syāt, “atha brahmacaryavidhiḥ” [ĀpDh 1.2.18] ity ārabhya yāni vratāny uktāni tais tadvān brahmacārī syāt | saṃbhāvanāyāṃ liṅ | saṃbhavet | tasmān na pratyācakṣītety evaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ bhavatīti || 1.3.26 ||
nānumānena bhaikṣam ucchiṣṭaṃ dṛṣṭaśrutābhyāṃ tu || 1.3.27 ||
bhikṣāṇāṃ samūho bhaikṣam | na talliṅgābhāsenocchiṣṭaṃ mantavyam, kiṃ tu dṛṣṭaśrutābhyām eva | dṛṣṭam ātmanaḥ pratyakṣam, śrutam āptopadeśaḥ, tābhyām eva tad ucchiṣṭam avagantavyam | ayam aṃśaḥ prāptānuvādo ‘pūrvam aṃśaṃ vidhātum, yathā “nānuvaṣaṭkaroty api vopāṃśv anuvaṣaṭkuryāt” iti || 1.3.27 ||
bhavatpūrvayā brāhmaṇo bhikṣeta || 1.3.28 ||
brāhmaṇo brahmacārī bhavatpūrvayā vācā bhikṣeta bhikṣāṃ yāceta “bhavati bhikṣāṃ dehi”
iti || 1.3.28 ||
bhavanmadhyayā rājanyaḥ || 1.3.29 ||
“bhikṣāṃ bhavati dehi” iti rājanyo bhikṣeta || 1.3.29 ||
bhavadantyayā vaiśyaḥ || 1.3.30 ||
“bhikṣāṃ dehi bhavati” iti || 1.3.30 ||
“sarvaṃ lābham āharan gurave” [ĀpDh 1.3.25] ity uktam |
athāhṛtya kiṃ kartavyam ity ata āha |
tat samāhṛtyopanidhāyācāryāya prabrūyāt || 1.3.31 ||
tad bhaikṣaṃ samāhṛtya samīpe nidhāyācāryāya prabrūyād “idam ittham āhṛtam” iti || 1.3.31 ||
tena pradiṣṭaṃ bhuñjīta || 1.3.32 ||
tenācāryeṇa pradiṣṭaṃ “saumya tvam eva bhuṅkṣva” ity uktaṃ bhuñjīta || 1.3.32 ||
vipravāse guror ācāryakulāya || 1.3.33 ||
yadi gurur viproṣitaḥ asaṃnihitaḥ syāt tata ācāryakulāya ācāryasya yat kulaṃ bhāryāputrādi tasmai prabrūyāt || 1.3.33 ||
tair vipravāse ‘nyebhyo ‘pi śrotriyebhyaḥ || 1.3.34 ||
taiḥ svakulaiḥ saha guror vipravāse anyebhyo ‘pi śrotriyebhyaḥ prabrūyāt | taiḥ pradiṣṭaṃ bhuñjīteti vipariṇāmenānvayaḥ | gautamo ‘py āha: “asaṃnidhau tadbhāyāputrasabrahmacārisadbhyaḥ” iti [GDh 2.40] || 1.3.34 ||
nātmaprayojanaś caret || 1.3.35 ||
ātmā prayojanaṃ prayojako yasya sa ātmaprayojanaḥ, evaṃbhūto bhikṣāṃ na caret, ātmārthaṃ na cared ity arthaḥ | asya prayojanaṃ yadā śrotriyā api na labhyante tadā “proṣito bhaikṣād agnau kṛtvā bhuñjīta” [ĀpDh 1.3.42] iti vakṣyamāṇam aproṣite ‘pi yathā syād iti || 1.3.35 ||
bhuktvā svayam amatraṃ prakṣālayīta || 1.3.36 ||
amatraṃ bhojanapātram, bhuktveti saṃnidhānāt | tat svayam eva prakṣālayīta prakṣālayet | bhikṣāpātrasya tv anyenāpi prakṣālane na doṣaḥ | ubhayor api pātrayor grahaṇam ity anye
|| 1.3.36 ||
na cochiṣṭaṃ kuryāt || 1.3.37 ||
yāvac chaknoti bhoktuṃ tāvad eva bhojanapātre kṛtvā bhuñjīta || 1.3.37 ||
aśaktau bhūmau nikhanet || 1.3.38 ||
bhojane pravṛtto yadi tāvad bhoktuṃ na śaknuyāt tadā bhūmau nikhanet || 1.3.38 ||
apsu vā praveśayet || 1.3.39 ||
apsu prakṣipet || 1.3.39 ||
āryāya vā paryavadadhyāt || 1.3.40 ||
āryas traivarṇikas tasmai anupanītāya paryavadadhyāt sarvam ekasmin pātre nidhāya tatsamīpe bhūmau sthāpayet || 1.3.40 ||
antardhine vā śūdrāya || 1.3.41 ||
antardhānam antardhiḥ so ‘syāstīti | vrīhyāditvād iniḥ | antardhī dāsaḥ | antarhitaṃ hi tasya śūdratvam, āśauceṣu svāmitulyatvāt | prakaraṇāc cācāryasyeti gamyate | ācāryadāsāya śūdrāya paryavadadhyāt || 1.3.41 ||
proṣito bhaikṣād agnau kṛtvā bhuñjīta || 1.3.42 ||
yadi śiṣya ātmārtham ācāryārthaṃ vā proṣitaḥ syāt tadā bhaikṣāt kiṃcid ādāyāgnau kṛtvā prakṣipya śeṣaṃ bhuñjīta śrotriyāṇāṃ sadbhāve ‘sadbhāve ca | “anyebhyo ‘pi śrotriyebhyaḥ” [ĀpDh 1.3.34] ity etat tv atra na bhavati | yadi syāt tatraivāyaṃ brūyāt “tadabhāve ‘gnau kṛtvā bhuñjīta” iti | yady api tatrācāryasya vipravāsaḥ prakṛtaḥ, tathāpi nyāyasāmyāc chiṣyasyāpi vipravāso bhaviṣyati || 1.3.42 ||
atha brahmacāriṇo yajñaṃ saṃpādayituṃ havirādīni saṃpādayati |
bhaikṣaṃ haviṣā saṃstutaṃ tatrācāryo devatārthe || 1.3.43 ||
bhaikṣaṃ haviṣā haviṣṭvena saṃstutaṃ kīrtitam | tatra tasmin haviṣi ācāryo devatārthe devatākārye, tatprītyarthatvāt tasyeti || 1.3.43 ||
āhavanīyārthe ca || 1.3.44 ||
tasya jaṭharāgnau hūyamānatvāt || 1.3.44 ||
taṃ bhojayitvā || 1.3.45 ||
iti tṛtīyaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
yad ucchiṣṭaṃ prāśñāti || 1.4.1 ||
anuvādeṣu sarvatra vidhiḥ kalpyate, taṃ bhojayet, bhojayitvā tasyocchiṣṭhaṃ prāśnīyāt | prāśñātīti ñakāro 'papāṭhaś chāndaso vā, "śāt" iti cutvapratiṣedhāt || 1.4.1 ||
havirucchiṣṭam eva tat || 1.4.2 ||
iḍābhakṣaṇādisthānīyam ity arthaḥ || 1.4.2 ||
yad anyāni dravyāṇi yathālābham upaharati dakṣiṇā
eva tāḥ || 1.4.3 ||
yad anyāni dravyāṇi gavādīni bhikṣācaraṇe labdhāni samidādīni ca svayam āhṛtāni yathālābham upaharati dakṣiṇā eva tāḥ | dakṣiṇāsāmānādhikaraṇyāt tā ity uktam || 1.4.3 ||
sa eṣa brahmacāriṇo yajño nityapratataḥ || 1.4.4 ||
sa eṣa evaṃbhūto yajño brahmacāriṇo nityaṃ pratāyate, evaṃ kurvatā brahmacāriṇā yajña eva nityaṃ kriyata ity arthaḥ || 1.4.4 ||
na cāsmai śrutipratiṣiddham ucchiṣṭaṃ dadyāt || 1.4.5 ||
asmai śiṣyāyācāryaḥ śrutivipratiṣiddhaṃ śāstrapratiṣiddham ucchitṣṭaṃ na dadyāt || 1.4.5 ||
kiṃ punas tat |
yathā kṣāralavaṇamadhumāṃsānīti || 1.4.6 ||
yatheti vacanāt, śrutivipratiṣiddham iti lakṣaṇataḥ pratiṣedhāc ca kṣārādigrahaṇam evaṃvidhasyopalakṣaṇam || 1.4.6 ||
etenānye niyamā vyākhyātāḥ || 1.4.7 ||
abhyaṅgaśeṣo gandhaśeṣo mālyaśeṣa ityādayo brahmacāriṇaḥ pratiṣiddhāḥ ācāryeṇa na deyā ity uktaṃ bhavati || 1.4.7 ||
kecit tu śrutivipratiṣiddham apy ācāryasya śeṣam upabhuñjānā dṛśyante pūrvaḥ pūrvaḥ ācāraḥ pramāṇam iti vadantaḥ | tān nirākaroti |
śrutir hi balīyasy ānumānikād ācārāt || 1.4.8 ||
anumānasya prabhavatīty ānumānikaḥ | ācārād dhi śrutiḥ smṛtir vānumīyate | tasmād ānumānikād ācārāt pratyakṣā śrutir balīyasī | tadvirodhe nānumātuṃ śakyate, “anumānām abādhitam” ity nyāyāt | evaṃ ca bruvatā brahmacāriṇaḥ kṣāralavaṇādipratiṣedhaḥ pratyakṣabrāhmaṇamūla iti darśitaṃ bhavati | yady api kṣārādipratiṣedhaśrutir ucchiṣṭavyatirikto viṣayaḥ saṃbhavati, tathāpi saṅkoco ‘pi tasyā aviśeṣapravṛttāyā ānumānikād ācārād ayuktaḥ || 1.4.8 ||
nanu parasparaviruddhā api śrutaya upalabhyante, yathā – “gṛhṇāti,” “na gṛhṇāti” iti | tat kim ācārāt saṅkocikā śrutir nānumīyate | ata āha |
dṛśyate cāpi pravṛttikāraṇam || 1.4.9 ||
syād evaṃ yady ayam ācāro ‘gṛhyamānakāraṇaḥ syāt | gṛhyate tu tatra kāraṇam || 1.4.9 ||
kiṃ tat |
prītir hy upalabhyate || 1.4.10 ||
kṣārādibhojane bhuñjānasya prītir hy udbhavati | tataś ca yatra prītyupalabdhitaḥ pravṛttiḥ na tatra śāstram astīti nyāyāt na saṅkocikā śrutir anumīyate iti || 1.4.10 ||
pitur jyeṣṭhasya ca bhrātur ucchiṣṭaṃ bhoktavyam || 1.4.11 ||
spaṣṭam || 1.4.11 ||
dharmavipratipattāv abhojyam || 1.4.12 ||
yadi tayor dharmād vipratipattir apāyo bhavati tato na bhojyam | yad vā – bhuñjānasya brahmacāriṇaḥ yadi dharmavipratiṣedho bhavati madhumāṃsādimiśritatvena tato na bhojyam iti || 1.4.12 ||
sāyaṃ prātar udakumbham āharet || 1.4.13 ||
ācāryasya snānapānādyartham || 1.4.13 ||
sadāraṇyād edhān āhṛtyādho nidadhyāt || 1.4.14 ||
sadā pratyaham araṇyāt na pitrādigṛhāt edhān kāṣṭhāni ācāryagṛhe pākādyartham āharet, āhṛtya cādho nidadhyāt | adhonidhānam ācāryasya putrādiṣu bāleṣu patanaśaṅkayā | apara āha – ātmanaḥ samidādhānārtham edhāharaṇam iti | uktaṃ hi gṛhye: “evam anyasminn api sadāraṇyād edhān āhṛtya” iti [ĀpGṛ 11.22] | tadanuvādenādhonidhānam atra vidhīyate dṛṣṭārtham adṛṣṭārthaṃ veti || 1.4.14 ||
nāstam ite samiddhāro gacchet || 1.4.15 ||
astam ite āditye samidha āhartuṃ na gacchet, coravyāghrādisaṃbhavāt | samiddhāra it “aṇ karmaṇi ca” iti tumarthe ‘ṇprayayaḥ || 1.4.15 ||
agnim iddhvā parismūhya samidha ādadhyāt sāyaṃ prātar
yathopadeśam || 1.4.16 ||
parisamūhanaṃ parito mārjanam | viprakīrṇasyāgne rāśīkaraṇam ity anye | yathopadeśaṃ yathā gṛhya uktaṃ tathā samidha ādadhyāt | gṛhye vihitam api samidādhānaṃ vidhīyate sarvācaraṇārtham, sāyaṃ prātar ityādikān viśeṣān vakṣyāmīti ca || 1.4.16 ||
sāyam evāgnipūjety eke || 1.4.17 ||
eka ācāryāḥ sāyam evāgnipūjā kāryā na prātar iti manyante || 1.4.17 ||
samiddham agniṃ pāṇinā parisamūhen na
samūhanyā || 1.4.18 ||
samidādhānena samiddham agniṃ pāṇinā parisamūhet, na samūhanyā | samūhanī saṃmārjanī darbhanirmitā vedākṛtiḥ, ācārāt || 1.4.18 ||
prāk tu yāthākāmī || 1.4.19 ||
prāk tu samidādhānāt parisamūhane yāthākāmī bhavati | yathākāmasya bhāvo yāthākāmī | ṣyañ, ṣittvād īkāraḥ || 1.4.19 ||
nāgnyudakaśeṣeṇa vṛthākarmāṇi kurvītācāmed vā || 1.4.20 ||
agniparcaryāyāṃ parisamūhane pariṣecane ca yad upayuktam udakaṃ taccheṣeṇa vṛthākarmāṇi adṛṣṭaprayojanarahitāni pādaprakṣālanādīni na kurvīta, nāpy ācāmet | avṛthākarmatvād asya punaḥ pratiṣedhaḥ || 1.4.20 ||
pāṇisaṃkṣubdhenodakenaikapāṇyāvarjitena ca
nācāmet || 1.4.21 ||
pāṇisaṃkṣubdhaṃ kumbhādigataṃ yat pāṇinā saṃkṣobhitaṃ tenodakena nācāmet | nedaṃ taṭākādiṣu svayam ācamane | yadā para ācāmayati tadā ekena pāṇinā yad āvarjitam udakaṃ tena nācāmet, kiṃ tūbhābhyāṃ hastābhyāṃ karakādi gṛhītvā yad āvarjitaṃ tenācāmet | evaṃ ca svayaṃ vāmahastenāvarjitenāpi nācāmet | alābupātreṇa nālikereṇa vaiṇavena carmamayena tāmramayena vā pātreṇa svayam ācamanam ācaranti śiṣṭāḥ || 1.4.21 ||
svapnaṃ ca varjayet || 1.4.22 ||
pūrvaṃ “adivāsvāpī” [ĀpSh 1.2.24] ity anena divāsvāpaḥ pratiṣiddhaḥ | anena tu rātrāv api yāvad ācāryo na svapiti tāvantaṃ kālaṃ svāpaḥ pratiṣidhyate | svapnakathanaṃ ca varjayed ity anye
|| 1.4.22 ||
athāhar ahar ācāryaṃ gopāyed dharmārthayuktaiḥ
karmabhiḥ || 1.4.23 ||
atha svapnasya prakṛtatvāt svapnānantaraṃ brāhme muhūrte utthāyety arthaḥ | ahar ahar nityam ācāryaṃ gopāyed rakṣet | kiṃ daṇḍādi gṛhītvā | nety āha – dharmārthayuktaiḥ karmabhiḥ darmayuktāni karmāṇi samitkuśapuṣpāharaṇādīni, arthayuktāni yugyaghāsāharaṇādīni
|| 1.4.23 ||
sa guptvā saṃviśaṇ brūyād dharmagopāyam ājūgupam aham
iti || 1.4.24 ||
sa brahmacārī yāvad utthānād asya yāvac ca saṃveśanāt evam ācāryaṃ guptvā saṃviśan śayanaṃ bhajan “dharmagopāyam ājūgupam aham” iti mantraṃ brūyāt | dharmaṃ gopāyatīti dharmagopāya ācāryaḥ tam aham ājūgupaṃ ābhimukhyena rakṣitavān asmi, idānīṃ tu saṃviśāmīti mantrasyārthaḥ | apara āha – he dharma māṃ gopāya rakṣa, yasmād ahaṃ ājūjupam ācāryam etāvantaṃ kālam iti || 1.4.24 ||
pramādād ācāryasya buddhipūrvaṃ vā niyamātikramaṃ
rahasi bodhayet || 1.4.25 ||
pramādo ‘navadhānam, pramādād buddhipūrvaṃ vācāryasya yo niyamātikramaḥ taṃ rahasi bodhayet, ittham ayaṃ niyamaḥ pūjyapādair atikramyata iti || 1.4.25 ||
anivṛttau svayaṃ karmāṇy ārabheta || 1.4.26 ||
yadi bodhito ‘py ācāryas tato na nivarteta tataḥ svayam eva tasya kartavyāni brahmayajñādīni karmāṇi ārabheta kuryāt || 1.4.26 ||
nivartayed vā || 1.4.27 ||
prasahya vā svayaṃ nivartayet | pitrādibhir vā nivartayet || 1.4.27 ||
atha yaḥ pūrvotthāyī jaghanyasaṃveśī tam āhur
na svapitīti || 1.4.28 ||
yaḥ pūrvam ācāryād uttiṣṭhati prabudhyati | jaghanyaśabdaḥ paścādarthe | jaghanyaṃ ca saṃviśati taṃ brahmacāriṇaṃ na svapitīti dharmajñā āhuḥ | prayojanam – upanayane “mā suṣupthāḥ” iti saṃśāsanasyāyam arthaḥ, na svāpasyātyantābhāva iti | athaśabdaś ca vākyopakrame || 1.4.28 |||
sa ya evaṃ praṇihitātmā brahmacāry atraivāsya sarvāṇi
karmāṇi phalavanty avāptāni bhavanti yāny api gṛhamedhe
|| 1.4.29 ||
“ācāryādhīnaḥ syāt” [ĀpDh 1.2.19] ity ārabhya yasya niyamā uktāḥ sa brahmacārī evam uktena prakāreṇa praṇihitātmā prakarṣeṇa nihita ācāryakule sthāpita ātmā yena sa tathoktaḥ | prakarṣaś cāntāt tatraiva śarīranyāsaḥ | vakṣyati “ācāryakule śrīranyāsaḥ” iti | asyaivaṃvidhasya brahmacāriṇo ‘traiva brahmacaryāśrame sarvāṇi karmāṇi phalavanti jyotiṣṭomādīni karmāṇy avāptāni bhavanti | tatphalāvāptir eva tadavāptiḥ | yāny api karmāṇi gṛhamedhe gṛhyaśāstre vivāhādiny aṣṭakāntāni tāny apy avāptāni bhavanti | tad etan naiṣṭhikabrahmacāriviṣayam idaṃ sūtram || 1.4.19 ||
iti caturthaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
iti śrīharadattamiśraviracitāyām āpastambadharmasūtravyākhyāyām
prathamaḥ paṭalaḥ
niyameṣu tapaḥśabdaḥ || 1.5.1 ||
“ācāryādhīnaḥ syāt” [ĀpDh 1.2.19] ityevamādayo ye niyamāḥ asmin brahmacāriprakaraṇe nirdiṣṭāḥ tapaḥśabdas teṣu draṣṭavyaḥ, na tu kṛcchrādiṣu || 1.5.1 ||
tadatikrame vidyākarma niḥsravati brahma sahāpatyād
etasmāt || 1.5.2 ||
teṣāṃ niyamānām atikrame vidyākarma vidyāgrahaṇaṃ brahma niḥsravati gṛhītaṃ vedaṃ niḥsārayati | kutaḥ | etasmān niyamātikrameṇādhyetuḥ puruṣāt | na kevalād etasmāt, kiṃ tarhi sahāpatyāt | apatyena saha vartata iti sahāpatyaḥ | “vopasarjanasya” iti sabhāvābhāve rūpam | apatyād api niḥsārayati | yady apy apatyaṃ niyamātikramakāri na bhavati, tathāpi pitṛdoṣād eva tato ‘pi brahma niḥsārayati | niyamātikrameṇa vidyāgrahaṇaṃ kurvataḥ puruṣāt sahāpatyād gṛhītaṃ brahma niḥsravati, brahmayajñādiṣūpayujyamānam apy akiṃcitkaraṃ bhavatīty artho vivakṣitaḥ | sravateś ca sakarmakasya prayogo bhāṣye dṛṣṭaḥ, sravaty udakaṃ kuṇḍiketi |
apara āha – tadatikrame niyamātikrame vidyākarma vidyāgrahaṇaṃ na kartavyam | kutaḥ | yato niḥsravati brahma niḥsaratīty arthaḥ | śeṣaṃ samānam iti |
vidyākarma niḥsravati brahma ca niḥsravatīty anye |
kurvata iti vā adhyāhāraḥ | tad atikrameṇa vidyākarma kurvato brahma niḥsravati
|| 1.5.2 ||
na kevalam akiṃcitkaraṃ niyamātikrameṇa vidyāgrahaṇaṃ pratyutānarthakārīty āha |
kartapatyam anāyuṣyaṃ ca || 1.5.3 ||
kartaśabdena śvabhrābhidhāyinā narako lakṣyate | pataty aneneti patyam | evaṃbhūtaṃ vidyāgrahaṇaṃ narakapātahetur bhavati | anāyuṣyaṃ ca anāyuṣkaraṃ ca || 1.5.3 ||
tasmād ṛṣayo ‘vareṣu na jāyante niyamātikramāt || 1.5.4 ||
ata evāvareṣv arvācīneṣu kaliyugavartiṣu ṛṣayo na jāyante mantradṛśo na bhavanti, niyamātikramasyedānīm avarjanīyatvāt || 1.5.4 ||
kathaṃ tarhy adyatanā atikrāmanto ‘pi niyamān alpenaiva yatnena caturo vedān gṛhṇanti | yugāntare samyag anuṣṭhitasya niyamakarmaṇaḥ phalaśeṣeṇety āha |
śrutarṣayas tu bhavanti kecit karmaphalaśeṣeṇa
punaḥsaṃbhave || 1.5.5 ||
punaḥsaṃbhavaḥ punarjanma || 1.5.5 ||
atrodāharaṇam |
yathā śvetaketuḥ || 1.5.6 ||
śvetaketuḥ svalpenaiva kālena caturo vedān jagrāha | tathā ca chāndogyam: “śvetaketur hāruṇeya āsa | taṃ ha pitovāca śvetaketo vasa brahmacaryaṃ na vai saumyāsmatkulīno ‘nanūcya brahmabandhur iva bhavati | sa ha dvādaśavarṣa upetya caturviṃśativarṣaḥ sarvān vedān adhītya mahāmanā anūcānamānī stabdha evāya” iti || 1.5.6 ||
evaṃ niyamātikrame doṣam uktvā tadanuṣṭhāne siddhim āha |
yat kiṃ ca samāhito ‘brahmapy ācāryād upayuṅkte brahmavad
eva tasmin phalaṃ bhavati || 1.5.7 ||
abrahmapi | pararūpaṃ katantavat | aper vā akāralopaḥ, pihitapinaddhādivat | vedavyatiriktam api yat kiṃcid viṣamantrādikaṃ samāhito niyamavān bhūtvā ācāryād upayuṅkte gṛhṇāti brahmavad eva tasmin phalaṃ bhavati || 1.5.7 ||
nigrahānugrahaśaktir apy asya bhavatīty āha |
atho yat kiṃ ca manasā vācā cakṣuṣā vā saṃkalpayan
dhyāyaty āhābhivipaśyati vā tathaiva tad bhavatīty
upadiśanti || 1.5.8 ||
atho api ca yat kiṃ ca nigrahātmakam anugrahātmakaṃ vā saṃkalpayan cikīrṣan manasā nirdayena śivena vā dhyāyati ittham idam asyāstv iti tathaiva tad bhavati | tathā yat kiṃ ca saṃkalpayan vācā krūrayā madhurayā vāha idam ittham asyāstv iti tathaiva tad bhavati | evaṃ yat kiṃ ca saṃkalpayan cakṣuṣā ghoreṇa maitreṇa vābhivipaśyati tathaiva tad bhavatīty upadiśanti dharmajñāḥ || 1.5.8 ||
avaśyaṃ dharmayuktenādhyetavyam ity uktaṃ bhavati | idānīṃ te dharmāḥ lakṣaṇatas trividhā ity āha |
guruprasādanīyāni karmāṇi svastyayanam adhyayanasaṃvṛttir
iti || 1.5.9 ||
yair anuṣthitair guruḥ prasīdati tāni guruprasādanīyāni pādaprakṣālanādīni karmāṇi | svastīty avināśanāma, tatprāptisādhanaṃ svastyayanam | tac ca trividham, dṛṣṭārtham adṛṣṭārtham ubhayārthaṃ ceti | dṛṣṭārthaṃ bāhutaraṇādiniṣedhaḥ | adṛṣṭārthaṃ kṣārādiniṣedhaḥ | ubhayārthaṃ bhikṣācaraṇādi | adhyayanasaṃvṛttir adhītasya vedasyābhyāsaḥ || 1.5.9 ||
ato ‘nyāni nivartante brahmacāriṇaḥ karmāni || 1.5.10 ||
etebhyo ‘nyāni karmāṇi tīrthayātrādīni nivartante brahmacāriṇaḥ, na kartavyānīty arthaḥ
|| 1.5.10 ||
svādhyāyadhṛg dharmarucis tapasvy ṛjur mṛduḥ sidhyati
brahmacārī || 1.5.11 ||
svādhyāyadhṛk adhītasya svādhyāyasya dhārayitā avismartā | dharme rucir yasya sa dharmaruciḥ | tapasvī niyameṣu tapaḥśabdas tadvān | ṛjur amāyāvī | mṛduḥ kṣamāvān | evaṃbhūto brahmacārī sidhyati siddhiṃ prāpnoti | uktā siddhiḥ “atho yat kiṃ ca manasā” [ĀpDh 1.5.8] iti | tatroktānāṃ punarvacanam ādarārtham, tadanuṣṭhāne phalabhūmā, tadatikrame doṣabhūmeti || 1.5.11 ||
sadā mahāntam apararātram utthāya guros tiṣṭhan prātar
abhivādam abhivādayītāsāv ahaṃ bho iti || 1.5.12 ||
sadā pratidinaṃ mahāntam apararātraṃ rātryāḥ paścime yāme uttiṣṭhet, utthāya ca samīpe tiṣṭhan guroḥ prātar abhivādam abhivādayīta asāv ahaṃ bho iti bruvan | asāv ity atrātmano nāmanirdeśaḥ, yathā abhivādaye yajñaśarmāhaṃ bho iti || 1.5.12 ||
samānagrāme ca vasatām anyeṣām api vṛddhatarāṇāṃ
prāk prātarāśāt || 1.5.13 ||
prātarbhojanāt prāk prātarabhivādam abhivādayīta, te cet vṛddhatarāḥ samānagrāme vasanti
|| 1.5.13 ||
proṣya ca samāgame || 1.5.14 |
yadā svayaṃ proṣya samāgato bhavati, ācāryādayo vā, tadāpy abhivādayīta | idaṃ naimittikam, pūrvaṃ nityam || 1.5.14 ||
atha kāmyam |
svargam āyaś cepsan || 1.5.15 ||
abhivādayītety eva || 1.5.15 ||
abhivādanaprakāraṃ varṇānupūrvyeṇāha |
dakṣiṇaṃ bāhuṃ śrotrasamaṃ prasārya brāhmaṇo
‘bhivādayītoraḥsamaṃ rājanyo madhyasamaṃ vaiśyo
nīcaiḥ śūdraḥ prāñjaliḥ || 1.5.16 ||
brāhmaṇo ‘bhivādayamāna ātmano dakṣiṇaṃ bāhuṃ śrotrasamaṃ prasāryābhivādayīta | uraḥsamaṃ rājanyaḥ, dakṣiṇaṃ bāhuṃ prasāryābhivādayītety anuvartate | evam uttarayor api | evaṃ madhyasamam udarasamaṃ vaiśyaḥ | ūrusamam ity anye | nīcaiḥ pādasamaṃ śūdro ‘bhivādayīta prāñjalir yathā bhavati tathā abhivādayīta, añjaliṃ kṛtvety arthaḥ | prāñjalir iti yuktaḥ pāṭhaḥ || 1.5.16 ||
plāvanaṃ ca nāmno ‘bhivādanapratyabhivādane ca pūrveṣāṃ
varṇānām || 1.5.17 ||
abhivādanasya yat pratyabhivādanaṃ tatrābhivādayitur nāmnaḥ plāvanaṃ kartavyam, plutaḥ kartavya ity arthaḥ | pūrveṣāṃ varṇānāṃ śūdravarjitānām abhivādayamānānām | “pratyabhivāde ‘śūdre” [Pāṇ 8.2.83] iti pāṇinīyasmṛteḥ tatra “vākyasya ṭeḥ” [Pāṇ 8.2.82] ityanuvṛtteḥ pratyabhivādavākyasyānte nāmaprayogaḥ, tasya deḥ plutaḥ | āyuṣmān bhava saumya iti prayoktavyaḥ | smṛtyantaravaśān nāmnaś ca paścād akāraḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
āyuṣmān bhava saumyeti vācyo vipro ‘bhivādane |
akāraś cāsya nāmno ‘nte vācyaḥ pūrvākṣaraḥ plutaḥ || [MDh 2.125]
āyuṣmān bhava saumya devadattā3a iti prayogaḥ | śambhur viṣnuḥ pinākapāṇiḥ ityādīnāṃ nāmnāṃ saṃbuddhau guṇe kṛte “ecopragṛhyasyādūrād dhūte pūrvasyārdhasyāduttarasyedutau” ity ayam api vidhir bhavati | ante akāraḥ “tayor yvāvaci saṃhitāyām” [Pāṇ 8.2.108] iti yakāravakārau bhavataḥ | śambhā3va, viṣṇā3va, pinākapāṇā3ya, iti | atra sūtre “pratyabhivādane ca” iti cakārasyārthaṃ na paśyāmaḥ | apara āha – abhivādane pratyabhivādane ca plāvanam iti | asminn api pakṣe dvandvenābhihitatvāc caśabdo ‘narthaka eva | abhivādane ca śāstrāntare na kvāpi pluto vihitaḥ, tasmād anarthaka eva cakāraḥ | anarthakāś ca nipātā bahulaṃ prayujyante || 1.5.17 ||
udite tv āditya ācāryeṇa sametyopasaṃgrahaṇam || 1.5.18 ||
udite tv āditye ācāryeṇādhyayanārthaṃ sametya vakṣyamāṇena vidhinopasaṃgrahaṇaṃ
kuryāt || 1.5.18 ||
sadaivābhivādanam || 1.5.19 ||
anyadā sarvadā pūrvoktaprakāreṇābhivādanam eva | ayantv anuvāda uttaravivakṣayā || 1.5.19 ||
upasaṃgrāhya ācārya ity eke || 1.5.20 ||
abhivādanaprasaṅge sadaivopasaṃgrāhya ācārya ity eke manyante || 1.5.20 ||
kim idam upasaṃgrahaṇam | tad āha |
dakṣiṇena pāṇinā dakṣiṇaṃ pādam adhastād abhyadhimṛśya
sakuṣṭhikam upasaṃgṛhṇīyāt || 1.5.21 ||
ātmano dakṣiṇena pāṇinā ācāryasya dakṣiṇaṃ padaṃ adhastād abhyadhimṛśya | adhiśabda uparibhāve | adhastāc copariṣṭāc cābhimṛśya sakuṣṭhikaṃ sagulpham, śāṅguṣṭham ity anye | upasaṃgṛhṇiyāt, idam upasaṃgrahaṇam, etat kuryāt || 1.5.21 ||
ubhābhyām evobhāv abhipīḍayata upasaṃgrāhyāv ity
eke || 1.5.22 ||
ubhābyāṃ pāṇibhyām ubhāv evācāryasya pādāv abhipīḍayato māṇavakasya upasaṃgrāhyāv ity eke manyante | abhipīḍayata iti “kṛtyānāṃ kartari vā” [Pāṇ 2.3.7] iti kartari ṣaṣṭhī | atra manuḥ |
vyatyastapāṇinā kāryam upasaṃgrahaṇaṃ guroḥ |
savyena savyaḥ spraṣṭavyo dakṣiṇena tu dakṣiṇaḥ || iti || 1.5.22 ||
sarvāhṇaṃ suyukto ‘dhyayanād anantaro ‘dhyāye || 1.5.23 ||
sarvaṃ ca tad ahaś ca sarvāhṇam | “rājāhaḥ sakhibhyaṣ ṭac” [Pāṇ 5.4.91], “ahnohṇa etebhyaḥ” [Pāṇ 5.4.88], ity ahnādeśaḥ, “ahnodantāt” [Pāṇ 8.4.7] iti ṇatvam, atyantasaṃyoge dvitīyā | sarvāhṇaṃ suyuktaḥ susamāhito ‘nanyacittaḥ | adhyayanād anantaraḥ, nāntarayatīty anantaraḥ, adhyayanād yathātmānaṃ nāntarayati, yathā adhyayanān na vicchidyate, tathā syāt | adhyāye svādhyāyakāle | adhyāya ity anuvādaḥ, “manasā cānadhyāye” [ĀpDh 1.5.25] iti viśeṣavidhānāt | “adhyāyet” iti prāyeṇa paṭhanti | tatra takāro ‘papāṭhaś chāndaso vā || 1.5.23 ||
tathā gurukarmasu || 1.5.24 ||
gurukarmasu ca tathā syāt, suyukto ‘nantaraś ca syāt || 1.5.24 ||
manasā cānadhyāye || 1.5.25 ||
anadhyāyakāle ca manasādhyayanād anantaraḥ syāt, saṃdehasthānāni manasā nirūpayet, adhyayanaviṣayām eva cintāṃ kuryāt || 1.5.25 ||
āhūtādhyāyī ca syāt || 1.5.26 ||
ācāryeṇāhūtaḥ sann adhīyīta | nādhyāpane svayaṃ pravartayet || 1.5.26 ||
iti pañcamaḥ khaṇdaḥ
sadā niśāyāṃ guruṃ saṃveśayet tasya pādau prakṣālya
saṃvāhya || 1.6.1 ||
sadā pratyahaṃ niśāyāṃ atikrānte pradoṣe guruṃ saṃveśayet | katham | tasya guroḥ pādau prakṣālya saṃvāhya ca | saṃvāhanaṃ mardanam || 1.6.1 ||
anujñātaḥ saṃviśet || 1.6.2 ||
paścād guruṇānujñātas tu svayaṃ saṃviśet śayīta || 1.6.2 ||
na cainam abhi prasārayīta || 1.6.3 ||
enam ācāryaṃ prati pādau na prasārayet || 1.6.3 ||
na khaṭvāyāṃ sato ‘bhiprasāraṇam astīty eke || 1.6.4 ||
yadā guruḥ khaṭvāyāṃ śete tadā taṃ prati pādayoḥ prasāraṇaṃ na doṣāyety eke manyante | svapakṣas tu tatrāpi doṣa iti || 1.6.4 ||
na cāsya sakāśe saṃviṣṭo bhāṣeta || 1.6.5 ||
asyācāryasya sakāśe svayaṃ saṃviṣṭaḥ śayāno na bhāṣeta | kāryāvedanādāv utthāyaiva bhāṣeta
|| 1.6.5 ||
abhibhāṣitas tv āsīnaḥ pratibrūyāt || 1.6.6 ||
ācāryeṇābhibhāṣitaḥ sann āsīnaḥ pratibrūyāt | etad ācārye āsīne śayāne vā || 1.6.6 ||
anūtthāya tiṣṭhantam || 1.6.7 ||
yadā ācāryas tiṣṭhan brūte tadotthāya pratibrūyāt || 1.6.7 ||
uttare dve sūtre spaṣṭārthe |
gacchantam anugacchet || 1.6.8 ||
dhāvantam anudhāvet || 1.6.9 ||
na sopānadveṣṭitaśirā avahitapāṇir vāsīdet || 1.6.10 ||
uttaratropānatpratiṣedhāt [ĀpDh 1.7.5] “na sopānat” ity anuvādaḥ “adhvāpannas tu” [ĀpDh 1.6.11] iti pratiprasotum | ācāryaṃ na sopānatka āsīdet | nāpi veṣṭitaśirāḥ | avahitapāṇiḥ dātrādihastaḥ, evaṃbhūto ‘pi nāsīdet || 1.6.10 ||
adhvāpannas tu karmayukto vāsīdet || 1.6.11 ||
prāptādhvā adhyāpannaḥ | karmaṇi dātrādisādhye pravṛttaḥ karmayuktaḥ | evaṃbhūtas tu sopānatko ‘py āsīdet || 1.6.11 ||
na ced upasīdet || 1.6.12 ||
na ced ācāryasamīpe upasīdet upaviśet | yadi tūpaviśed adhvāpannaḥ karmayukto vā tadopānatprabhṛtīni vihāyopaviśet || 1.6.12 ||
devam ivācāryam upāsītāvikathayann avimanā vācaṃ
śuśrūṣamāṇo ‘sya || 1.6.13 ||
yo yaṃ devaṃ bhajate sa tadbhāvanayācāryam upāsīta | avikathayan, vyarthā kathā vikathā, tām akurvan | avimanāḥ avikṣiptamanāḥ | asyācāryasya vācaṃ śuśrūṣamāṇaḥ || 1.6.13 ||
anupasthakṛtaḥ || 1.6.14 ||
upasthakaraṇaṃ prasiddham | tat kṛtvā nopāsīta || 1.6.14 ||
anuvāti vītaḥ || 1.6.15 ||
vāte anuvāti sati vītaḥ viparyayeṇeta upāsīta | prativātas tu vakṣyamāṇena pratiṣidhyate | manur apy āha |
prativāte ‘nuvāte ca nāsīta guruṇā saha | iti | [MDh 2.203]
|| 1.6.15 ||
apratiṣṭabdhaḥ pāṇinā || 1.6.16 ||
pratiṣṭabdho na syāt, pāṇitalaṃ bhūmau kṛtvā pāṇyālambano nāsīta || 1.6.16 ||
anapaśrito ‘nyatra || 1.6.17 ||
anyatra kuḍyādāv āśrito na syāt, kuḍyādyapāśrito nāsīta || 1.6.17 ||
yajñopavītī dvivastraḥ || 1.6.18 ||
yadā dvivastras tadā vāsasānyatareṇa yajñopavītī syāt | “api vā sūtram evopavītārthe” [ĀpDh 2.4.22] ity eṣa kalpas tadā na bhavati || 1.6.18 ||
adhonivītas tv ekavastraḥ || 1.6.19 ||
yadā tv ekavastro bhavati tadādhonivītaḥ syāt, na tasya dīrghasyāpy ekadeśenottarīyaṃ kuryāt
|| 1.6.19 ||
abhimukho ‘nabhimukham || 1.6.20 ||
svayam ācāryābhimukha ātmānaṃ praty anabhimukham ācāryam upāsīta | svayam ācāryam eva paśyann ācāryasya purata ārjavenāsīta || 1.6.20 ||
anāsanno ‘natidūre || 1.6.21 ||
atyāsanna atidūre ca na syāt || 1.6.21 ||
yāvad āsīno bāhubhyāṃ prāpnuyāt || 1.6.22 ||
yāvaty antarāle āsīna ācāryaṃ bāhubhyāṃ prāptuṃ śaknuyāt tāvaty āsīta || 1.6.22 ||
aprativātam || 1.6.23 ||
ācāryasya prativātaṃ nāsīta || 1.6.23 ||
ekādhyāyī dakṣiṇaṃ bāhuṃ praty upasīdet || 1.6.24 ||
yadā eka evādhīte tadācāryasya dakṣiṇaṃ bāhuṃ prati dakṣiṇapārśve upasīdet upaviśet
|| 1.6.24 ||
yathāvakāśaṃ bahavaḥ || 1.6.25 ||
bahavas tu śiṣyā yathāvakāśam upasīdeyuḥ || 1.6.25 ||
tiṣṭhati ca nāsītānāsanayogavihite || 1.6.26 ||
āsanayoga āsanakalpanā | āsanayogena vihitaḥ saṃbhāvitaḥ āsanayogavihitaḥ | āsanayogenāsaṃbhāvite ācārye tiṣṭhati svayaṃ nāsīta || 1.6.26 ||
āsīne ca na saṃviśet || 1.6.27 ||
aśayanayogavihita iti pūrvānusāreṇa gamyate | śayanayogenāsaṃbhāvite ācārye āsīne svayaṃ na saṃviśet na śayīta || 1.6.27 ||
ceṣṭati ca cikīrṣan tacchaktiviṣaye || 1.6.28 ||
vyatyayena prasmaipadam | ācārye ceṣṭati sati svayam api cikīrṣan syāt | kim aviśeṣeṇa | tacchaktiviṣaye, yady ācāryeṇa kriyamāṇam ātmanaḥ śakter viṣayo bhavati | cikīrṣann iti sanpratyayayogād icchām eva pradarśayet, nācchidya kuryāt | pradarśitāyām icchāyām ācāryaś ced anujānīyāt kuryāt | aśaktiviṣaye tu necchāpi pradarśayitavyā | cikīrṣed iti yuktaḥ pāṭhaḥ
|| 1.6.28 ||
na cāsya sakāśe ‘nvaksthāninam upasaṃgṛhṇīyāt || 1.6.29 ||
ācāryavyatiriktā guravo ‘nvaksthānina iti smārto vyavahāraḥ; “ācāryaḥ śreṣṭho gurūṇām” [GDh 2.50] tam avekṣyānvaksthānaṃ padam eṣām iti kṛtvā | ācāryasya saṃnidhāv anvaksthāninaṃ nopasaṃgṛhṇīyāt || 1.6.29 ||
gotreṇa vā kīrtayet || 1.6.30 ||
na cainam anvaksthāninaṃ gotreṇābhijanakulādinā vā kīrtayet stuvīta bhārgavo ‘yaṃ mahākulaprasūta iti || 1.6.30 ||
na cainaṃ pratyuttiṣṭhed anūttiṣṭhed vā || 1.6.31 ||
pratyutthānam apy asya na kartavyam ācāryasya sakāśe | yadā punar ācāryasya sakāśe āsitvā gamanāyottiṣṭhati tadānūtthānam api na kartavyam || 1.6.31 ||
api cet tasya guruḥ syāt || 1.6.32 ||
yady apy asau tasyācāryasya mātulādir guruḥ syāt tadā | “ācāryaprācāryasaṃnipāte” [ĀpDh 1.8.19] iti pūjāṃ vakṣyati, tenaiva nyāyena mātulādiṣv api prasaṅge idam uktam || 1.6.32 ||
deśāt tv āsanāc ca saṃsarpet || 1.6.33 ||
kiṃ tu deśād āsanāc ca saṃsarpet tasya saṃmānārtham || 1.6.33 ||
nāmnā tadantevāsinaṃ gurum apy ātmana ity eke || 1.6.34 ||
tasyācāryasyāntevāsinaṃ nāmnaiva kīrtayet, yajñaśarmann iti, yady apy asāv ātmano gurur bhavatīty eke manyante | svapakṣas tu guror nāmagrahaṇaṃ na kartavyam iti || 1.6.34 ||
yasmiṃs tv anācāryasaṃbandhād gauravaṃ vṛttis
tasminn anvaksthānīye ‘py ācāryasya || 1.6.35 ||
yasmiṃs tu puruṣe śiṣyācāryabhāvam antareṇāpi vidyācāritrādinā laukikānāṃ gauravaṃ tasminn anvaksthānīye ‘pi ācārye yā vṛttiḥ sā kartavyā, anvaksthānīye ‘py anvaksthānīyaiva || 1.6.35 ||
bhuktvā cāsya sakāśe nānūtthāyocchiṣṭaṃ prayacchet || 1.6.36 ||
ācāryasya bhuñjānasyābhuñjānasya vā sakāśe bhuktvā anūtthāya | chaṇdaso dīrghaḥ | utthānam akṛtvā ucchiṣṭaṃ na prayacchet, “āryāya vā paryavadadhyāt” [ĀpDh 1.3.40] iti yad vihitam
|| 1.6.36 ||
ācāmed vā || 1.6.37 ||
ācamanam apy anutthāya na kuryāt || 1.6.37 ||
kiṃ karavāṇīty āmantrya || 1.6.38 ||
ācamya kiṃ karavāṇīti gurum āmantrya || 1.7.38 ||
iti ṣaṣṭhaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
uttiṣṭhet tūṣṇīṃ vā || 1.7.1 ||
vikalpaḥ | āmantryeti liṅgād utthāyāpy ācamanaṃ na kuryāt, ācāryasakāśa evācāmet || 1.7.1 ||
nāpaparyāvarteta guroḥ pradakṣiṇīkṛtyāpeyāt || 1.7.2 ||
utthāya kāryavattayā gantum icchan guror apasavyaṃ na paryāvarteta, kiṃ tu pradakṣiṇīkṛtyāpeyāt || 1.7.2 ||
na prekṣeta nagnāṃ striyam || 1.7.3 ||
yāṃ prekṣamāṇasya manaso vikāro bhavati tāṃ nagnāṃ striyaṃ nekṣeta || 1.7.3 ||
oṣadhivanaspatīnām ācchidya nopajighret || 1.7.4 ||
oṣadhayaḥ phalapākāntāḥ, vanaspatayaḥ ye puṣpair vinā phalanti, vīrudhvṛkṣāṇām apy upalakṣaṇam | teṣāṃ patrapuṣpāṇy ācchidya nopajighret | ācchidyeti vacanād yādṛcchike gandhagrahaṇe na doṣaḥ || 1.7.4 ||
upānahau chatraṃ yānam iti ca varjayet || 1.7.5 ||
yānaṃ śakaṭādi | itiśabda evaṃprakārāṇām upalakṣaṇārthaḥ | tatra gautamaḥ: “varjayen madhumāṃsagandhamālyadivāsvapnāñjanābhyañjanayānopānacchatrakāmakrodhalobhamoha-vādavādanasnānadantadhāvanaharṣanṛttagītaparivādabhayāni” iti [GDh 2.13] || 1.7.5 ||
na smayeta || 1.7.6 ||
smitaṃ na kuryāt || 1.7.6 ||
yadi smayetāpigṛhya smayeteti hi brāhmaṇam || 1.7.7 ||
yadi harṣātirekād dhārayituṃ na śaknoti tadā apigṛhya hastena mukhaṃ pidhāya smayeta, iti brāhmaṇaṃ “na sameta” ity ārabhya || 1.7.7 ||
nopajighret striyaṃ mukhena || 1.7.8 ||
snātām anuliptāṅgāṃ vā striyaṃ bālām api mukhena nopajighret | mukheneti vacanād yādṛcchike gandhagrahaṇe na doṣaḥ || 1.7.8 ||
na hṛdayena prārthayet || 1.7.9 ||
hṛdayena manasā striyaṃ na prārthayet apīyaṃ me syād iti || 1.7.9 ||
nākāraṇād upaspṛśet || 1.7.10 ||
kāraṇena vinā striyaṃ nopaspṛśet | kāraṇaṃ yoktrasannahanavimocanaviṣamapatana-
dhāraṇādi || 1.7.10 ||
rajasvalo raktadant satyavādī syād iti brāhmaṇam || 1.7.11 ||
rajasvalo malinagātraḥ, raktā dantā yasya sa raktadan | chāndaso dannādeśaḥ | paṅkiladanta ity arthaḥ | tad ubhayam “utsannaślāghaḥ” [ĀpDh 1.2.27] ity anena gatam api punar ucyate śrautaprāyaścittam atikrame syād iti | “anṛtaṃ voktvā” [ĀpDh 1.26.3] iti prāyaścittam vakṣyati | “satyavādī syād” iti brāhmaṇam || 1.7.11 ||
yāṃ vidyāṃ kurute gurau te ‘py asyācāryā ye tasyāṃ guror
vaṃśyāḥ || 1.7.12 ||
ātmīye gurau yāṃ vidyāṃ kurute ‘dhīte tasyāṃ vidyāyāṃ guror ye vaṃśyā ācāryās te ‘py asya māṇavakasyācāryāḥ | yady api sākṣāt tebhyo na gṛhyate vidyā, tathāpi te ācāryavad upacaritavyāḥ | tasyām iti vacanād vidyāntareṣu ye vaṃśyās teṣu nāyaṃ vidhiḥ || 1.7.12 ||
yān anyān paśyato ‘syopasaṃgṛhṇīyāt tadā tv eta
upasaṃgrāhyāḥ || 1.7.13 ||
asya māṇavakasya paśyataḥ asmin māṇavake paśyati yān anyān ācārya upasaṃgṛhṇīyāt te māṇavakasyāpy upasaṃgrāhyāḥ | kiṃ sadā | nety āha “tadā tv ete” iti, tasyāṃ daśāyām | apara āha — tadā prabhṛty eta upasaṃgrāhyāḥ | tuśabdāt samāvṛttenāpi || 1.7.13 ||
gurusamavāye bhikṣāyām utpannāyāṃ yam anubaddhas
tadadhīnā bhikṣā || 1.7.14 ||
yadā dvitīyaṃ tṛtīyaṃ vā vedam adhīyānasya māṇavakasya gurusamavāyo bhavati guravaḥ samavetā bhavanti tadā bhikṣāyām utpannāyāṃ yaṃ gurum idānīm anubaddho māṇavakaḥ yato ‘dhīte tadadhīnā bhikṣā, yac ca yāvac ca labdhaṃ tat tasmai nivedanīyam | taduktaś ca viniyogaḥ || 1.7.14 ||
samāvṛtto mātre dadyāt || 1.7.15 ||
kṛtasamāvartano vivāhāt prāg ārjitaṃ mātre dadyāt || 1.7.15 ||
mātā bhartāraṃ gamayet || 1.7.16 ||
mātā patiṃ prāpayet || 1.7.16 ||
bhartā gurum || 1.7.17 ||
so ‘pi guruṃ prāpayen māṇavakasya | māṇavakārjitaṃ dravyaṃ tadgāmi yuktam || 1.7.17 ||
dharmakṛtyeṣu vopayojayet || 1.7.18 ||
dharmakṛtyāni vivāhādīni, teṣu vopayojayet, guror abhāve bhartā tadabhāve mātā, sarveṣām abhāve samāvṛttaḥ svayam eva vā || 1.7.18 ||
kṛtvā vidyāṃ yāvatīṃ śaknuyād vedadakṣiṇām āhared
dharmato yathāśakti || 1.7.19 ||
yāvatīṃ vidyāṃ kartuṃ śaknuyād vedaṃ vedau vedān vā, tāvatīṃ kṛtvā adhītya, gurave dakṣiṇām āhared dadyād yathāśakti dharmato labdhāṃ nyāyārjitām || 1.7.19 ||
dharmata ity asyāpavādaḥ |
viṣamagate tv ācārya ugrataḥ śūdrato vāharet || 1.7.20 ||
yadā tv ācāryo viṣamagata āpadgatas tadā ugrataḥ śūdrato vā pratigṛhya dakṣiṇām āharet | vaiśyāc chūdrāyāṃ jāta ugraḥ | ugrakarmā vā dvijātiḥ || 1.7.20 ||
sarvadā śūdrata ugrato vācāryārthasyāharaṇaṃ
dharmyam ity eke || 1.7.21 ||
sarvadā āpady anāpadi ca ācāryārthasyācāryāya yo ‘rtho deyas tasyogrataḥ śūdrato vāharaṇaṃ dharmyaṃ dharmād anapetam ity eke manyante | “dhārmyam” iti pāṭhe svārthe ṣyañ || 1.7.21 ||
datvā ca nānukathayet || 1.7.22 ||
ācāryāyaivam āhṛtya datvā na kīrtayet etan mayā dattam iti || 1.7.22 ||
kṛtvā ca nānusmaret || 1.7.23 ||
gurave prāṇasaṃśayādau mahāntam upakāraṃ kṛtvā nānusmaret nānucintayed aho mayaitat kṛtam iti || 1.7.23 ||
ātmapraśaṃsāṃ paragarhām iti ca varjayet || 1.7.24 ||
itikaraṇād evaṃprakārāṇām ātmanindādīnām api pratiṣedhaḥ || 1.7.24 ||
preṣitas tad eva pratipadyeta || 1.7.25 ||
idaṃ kurv ity ācāryeṇa preṣitas tad eva pratipadyeta kuryāt kriyamāṇam api karma vihāya, yady api tad ācāryasya bhavati || 1.7.25 ||
śāstuś cānāgamād vṛttir anyatra || 1.7.26 ||
“tasmiṃs caiva vidyākarmāntam” [ĀpDh 1.1.13] ity asyāpavādaḥ | yady adhigantum iṣṭā vidyā śāstuḥ śāsitur ācāryasya samyaṅ nāgacchati, tadā tasyānāgamād anyatra puruṣāntare vṛttir bhavaty eva, yasya samyag āgacchati || 1.7.26 ||
anyatropasaṃgrahaṇād ucchiṣṭāśanāc cācāryavad ācāryadāre
vṛttiḥ || 1.7.27 ||
anyatrety ubhayoḥ śeṣaḥ | ācāryavad ācāryadāreṣu vṛttiḥ kartavyā | kim aviśeṣeṇa | anyatropasaṃgrahaṇād ucchiṣṭāśanāc ca, pādopasaṃgrahaṇam ucchiṣṭāśanaṃ cety ubhayaṃ varjayitvā | atra manuḥ |
gurupatnī tu yuvatir nābhivādyeha pādayoḥ | iti | [MDh 2.212ab]
gautamas tu: “tadbhāryāputreṣu caivam | nocchiṣṭāśanasnāpanaprasādhanapāda-prakṣālanonmardanopasaṃgrahaṇāni” iti [GDh 2.31–32] | dāra ity ekavacanaṃ chāndasam || 1.7.27 ||
tathā samādiṣṭe ‘dhyāpayati || 1.7.28 ||
ya ācāryeṇa samādiṣṭo niyukto ‘dhyāpayati tasminn ācāryadāravad vṛttiḥ | adhyāpayatīti vartamānanirdeśād yāvad adhyāpanaṃ tāvad evātideśaḥ || 1.7.28 ||
vṛddhatare ca sabrahmacāriṇi || 1.7.29 ||
adhyāpayatīti nānuvartate | taramnirdeśāt jñānavayobhyām ubhābhyāṃ vṛddho gṛhyate | sabrahmacārī sahādhyāyī, samāne brahmaṇi vrataṃ caratīti | tasminn apy ācāryadāravad vṛttiḥ,
ācāryāt pādam ādatte pādaṃ śiṣyaḥ svamedhayā |
pādaṃ sabrahmacāribhyaḥ pādaḥ kālena pacyate ||
ity adhyayane upayogasaṃbhavat ¦¦ 1.7.29 ||
ucchiṣṭāśanavarjam ācāryavad ācāryaputre vṛttiḥ || 1.7.30 ||
ucchiṣṭāśanavarjam iti vacanād upasaṃgrahaṇaṃ bhavati | etac ca jñānavayobhyāṃ vṛddhe | tadarthaṃ vṛddhatara ity anvartate | gautamīyas tūpsaṃgrahaṇapratiṣedho vṛddhatarād anyaviṣayaḥ || 1.7.30 ||
samāvṛttasyāpy etad eva sāmayācārikam eteṣu || 1.7.31 ||
kṛtasamāvartanasyāpy etad evānantaroktam eteṣv ācāryādiṣu putrānteṣu sāmayācārikaṃ samayācāraprāptaṃ vṛttam | “samādiṣṭe ‘dhyāpayati” [ĀpDh 1.7.28] iti viśeṣa uktaḥ || 1.7.31 ||
iti saptamaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
yathā brahmacāriṇo vṛttam || 1.8.1 ||
samāvṛttasyety anuvartate | samāvṛttasya brahmacāriṇo ‘kṛtavivāhasya yathā vṛttaṃ vartanaṃ tathā vakṣyāmaḥ || 1.8.1 ||
māly āliptamukha upaliptakeśaśmaśrur akto ‘bhyakto veṣṭity
upaveṣṭitī kāñcukyupānahī [Har: kañcukopānahī] pādukī || 1.8.2 ||
mālī mālyavān | āliptamukhaś candanādinā | mukhagrahaṇam upalakṣaṇam, “mukham agre brāhmaṇo ‘nulipyet” [ĀśGṛ 3.8.11] ity āśvalāyanavacanāt | sugandhibhir āmalakādibhir dravyair upaliptaṃ saṃskṛtaṃ keśaśmaśru yasya sa upaliptakeśaśmaśruḥ | aktaḥ añjanenākṣṇoḥ | abhyaktas tailena | veṣṭitī veṣṭitaśirāḥ | upaveṣṭitī kaṭipradeśo dvitīyena vāsasā veṣṭito yasya sa upaveṣṭitī | kañcukaṃ copānac ca kañcukopānaham | “dvandvāc cudaṣahāntāt” [Pāṇ 5.4.106] ity acsamāsāntaḥ | tad asyāsti “kañcukopānahī” | “dvandvopatāpagarhyāt” [Pāṇ 5.2.128] itīnipratyayaḥ | prasiddhe pāṭhe kañcukam eva kāñcukaṃ tadvān kāñcukī | upānadvān upānahī | vrīhyāditvād iniḥ | pāduke dārumaye pādarakṣaṇe, tadvān pādukī || 1.8.2 ||
udācāreṣu cāsyaitāni na kuryāt kārayed vā || 1.8.3 ||
asyācāryādeḥ putrāntasya upacāreṣu dṛṣṭigocareṣu deśeṣu etāni mālyādīni na kuryāt kārayed vā
|| 1.8.3 ||
svairikarmasu ca || 1.8.4 ||
etāni na kuryāt kārayed vā || 1.8.4 ||
atrodāharaṇam |
yathā dantaprakṣālanotsādanāvalekhanānīti || 1.8.5 ||
dantaprakṣālanaṃ dantadhāvanam | utsādanam udvartanam | avalekhanaṃ kaṅkatādinā keśānāṃ vibhāgenāvasthāpanam | itiśabdaḥ pradarśanārthaḥ, tena bhojanamūtroccārādiṣv api pratiṣedhaḥ || 1.8.5 ||
taddravyāṇāṃ ca na kathayed ātmasaṃyogenācāryaḥ || 1.8.6 ||
tasya śiṣyasya gṛhasthībhūtasya yāni dravyāṇy upasthāpitāni teṣāṃ madhye ekenāpi dravyeṇa yathātmā saṃyuyate na tathā kathayet | ācāryaḥ śiṣyagṛhaṃ pratyāgataḥ aho darśanīyaṃ bhojanapātram ityādi lipsā yathā gamyate tathā na kathayed iti || 1.8.6 ||
snātas tu kāle yathāvidhy abhihṛtam āhūto ‘bhyeto vā na
pratisaṃhared ity eke || 1.8.7 ||
“vedam adhītya snāsyan” [ĀpGṛ 12.1] iti vidhinā snātaḥ tasmin kāle yathāvidhy abhihṛtam ābaddhaṃ sragādy ācāryeṇāhūtaḥ svayam eva vā tatsamīpam abhyeto na pratisaṃharen na vimuñced ity eke manyante | svapakṣas tu tadāpi pratimuñced iti | “kāle yathāvidhy abhihṛtam” iti vacanād paredyur ārabhya pratisaṃhared eva || 1.8.7 ||
uccaistarāṃ nāsīta || 1.8.8 ||
svārthe tarap | ācāryasyāsanād uccatara āsane nāsīta || 1.8.8 ||
tathā bahupāde || 1.8.9 ||
nīce ‘py āsane bahupāde nāsīta || 1.8.9 ||
sarvataḥ pratiṣṭhite || 1.8.10 ||
āsane tv āsīta | ācāryaṃ pīṭhādāv upaveśya svayaṃ vetrāsanādāv āsīta, tad dhi bhūmau sarvataḥ pratiṣṭhitaṃ bhavati || 1.8.10 ||
śayyāsane cācarite nāviśet || 1.8.11 ||
ācāryeṇācarita upabhukte śayyāsane nāviśet | śayane na śayīta, āsane nāsīta | pitrādiṣv api guruṣu samānam idam | tathā ca manur aviśeṣeṇāha |
śayyāsane cādhyuṣite śreyasā na samāśrayet || iti | [MDh 2.119]
gataṃ samāvṛttasya vaiśeṣikam | atha brahmacaryavidher eva śeṣaḥ || 1.8.11 ||
yānam ukto ‘dhvany anvārohet || 1.8.12 ||
yānaṃ śakaṭādi, ārohety ukto guruṇā paścād ārohet | “chatraṃ yānam iti varjayet” [ĀpDh 1.7.5] iti pūrvoktasya pratiṣedhasyāpavādaḥ | yānaṃ ca gurvārūḍham anyad vā || 1.8.12 ||
sabhānikaṣakaṭasvastarāṃś ca || 1.8.13 ||
ukto ‘nvārohed ity eva | “sabhāḥ samājāṃś cāgantā” [ĀpDh 1.3.12] ity asyāpavādārthaṃ sabhāgrahaṇam | nikaṣo nāma kṛṣīvalānām upakaraṇam, kṛṣṭaṃ kṣetraṃ yena samīkriyate | tac ca kasmiṃścid ārūḍhe kenacid ākṛṣyate | tatra guruṇākṛṣyamāṇe ‘pi tenoktaḥ sann ārohet, na tv anaucityabhayān nārohed iti | kaṭo vīraṇanirmitā śayyā | tatra guruṇoktaḥ san sahāsīta | utsavādāv eṣa ācāraḥ | svastaro nāma palālaśayyā, “navasvastare saṃviśanti” [ĀpGṛ 19.9] iti darśanāt | tatrāpi guruṇoktaḥ san sahasanādi kuryāt || 1.8.13 ||
nānabhibhāṣito gurum abhibhāṣeta priyād anyat || 1.8.14 ||
guruṇānabhibhāṣito guruṃ prati na kiṃcid brūyāt priyād anyat | priyavacanaṃ tu brūyāt yathā putro jāta iti || 1.8.14 ||
vyupatodavyupajāpavyabhihāsodāmantraṇanāmadheya-
grahaṇapreṣaṇānīti guror varjayet || 1.8.15 ||
vyupatodo ‘ṅgulyādinā ghaṭṭanam, yadābhimukhārthaṃ kriyate | vyupajāpaḥ karṇayor muhurjalpanam | vakāraś chāndaso ‘papāṭho vā | vyabhihāsaḥ ābhimukhyena hasanam | udāmantraṇam uccaiḥ saṃbodhanaṃ yathā badhiraṃ prati | nāmadeyagrahaṇaṃ daśamyāṃ pitṛvihitasya nāmadheyasya grahaṇam, na pūjyanāmno bhagavadādeḥ | preṣaṇam ājñāpanam | etāni guruviṣaye na kartavyāni | itikaraṇād evaṃprakārāṇāṃ cānyeṣām api pratiṣedhaḥ | yathāha manuḥ |
nodāharet tasya nāma parokṣam api kevalam |
na caivāsyānukurvīta gatibhāṣitaceṣṭitam || iti [MDh 2.199] || 1.8.15 ||
āpady arthaṃ jñāpayet || 1.8.16 ||
āpadi vyupatodādibhir apy artham abhipretaṃ jñāpayet | asati puruṣāntare vacanenāpi bodhayet, na sākṣāt preṣayet; yathā śūlatodo me bhavati, sa cāgninā śāmyati, na cātra kaścit saṃnihitaḥ, kiṃ karomi mandabhāgya iti || 1.8.16 ||
uttare sūtre samāvṛttaviṣaye |
sahavasaṃt sāyaṃ prātar anāhūto guruṃ darśanārtho
gacchet || 1.8.17 ||
ekasmin grāme vasan sāyaṃ prātar anāhūto ‘pi guruṃ darśanārtho nānyaprayojanārtho gacchet
|| 1.8.17 ||
viproṣya ca tad ahar eva paśyet || 1.8.18 ||
yadā grāmāntaraṃ gataḥ pratyāgacchati tadā tad ahar evācāryaṃ paśyet || 1.8.18 ||
ācāryaprācāryasaṃnipāte prācāryāyopasaṃgṛhyopsaṃjighṛkṣed
ācāryam || 1.8.19 ||
ācāryasyācāryaḥ prācāryaḥ, prapitāmaha itivat | yadā ācāryasya prācāryasya ca kāryavaśāt saṃnipāto melanaṃ bhavati, tadā prācāryāya | dvitīyārthe caturthī | prācāryaṃ pūrvam upasaṃgṛhya paścāt svācāryam upasaṃgṛhītum icchet | na kevalaṃ manasā, kiṃ tu, yathācāryo jānāti mām ayam upasaṃjighṛkṣatīti tathā ceṣṭeta | anyathādṛṣṭārtham upadiṣṭaṃ syāt || 1.8.19 ||
pratiṣedhed itaraḥ || 1.8.20 ||
itara ācāryaḥ pratiṣedhet, vatsa mopasaṃgrahīr iti || 1.8.20 ||
lupyate pūjā cāsya sakāśe || 1.8.21 ||
asya prācāryasya sakāśe saṃnidhau ācāryasya pūjā lupyate na kāryā, na kevalam upasaṃgrahaṇam eva || 1.8.21 ||
uttarasūtraṃ samāvṛttaviṣayam |
muhūṃś cācāryakulaṃ darśanārtho gacched yathāśakty
adhihastyam ādāyāpi dantaprakṣālanānīti || 1.8.22 ||
muhūṃś cety anusvāradīrghau chāndasau | vīpsālopaś cātra draṣṭavyaḥ | muhur muhur iti vivakṣitam | grāmāntare vasann api muhur muhur ācāryakulaṃ darśanārtho gacchet | yathāśakti gorasāpūpādyadhihastyaṃ hastebhavam ādāya svayam eva gṛhītvety arthaḥ | apiśabdaḥ abhāve ‘pi vidhiṃ dyotayati, gorasādyabhāve dantakāṣṭhāny apīti | itiśabdo ‘ntevāsidharmāṇāṃ samāptidyotanārthaḥ || 1.8.22 ||
tasmin guror vṛttiḥ || 1.8.23 ||
tasminn antevāsini guror vṛttir vṛtteḥ prakāro vakṣyate || 1.8.23 ||
putram ivainam anukāṅkṣant sarvadharmeṣv
anapacchādayamānaḥ suyukto vidyāṃ grāhayet || 1.8.24 ||
enaṃ śiṣyaṃ putram ivāsyābhyudayaḥ syād ity anukāṅkṣyant sarveṣu dharmeṣv apy anapacchādayamānaḥ agūhan suyuktaḥ suṣṭv avahitas tatparo bhūtvā vidyāṇ grāhayet || 1.8.24 ||
na cainam adhyayanavighnenātmārtheṣūparundhyād
anāpatsu || 1.8.25 ||
na cainaṃ śiṣyam adhyayanavighnenātmaprayojaneṣūparundhyāt | uparodho ‘svatantrīkaraṇam | anāpatsv iti vacanād āpady adhyayanavidhānenāpy uparodho na doṣaḥ || 1.8.25 ||
antevāsy anantevāsī bhavati vinihitātmā gurāv anaipuṇam
āpadyamānaḥ || 1.8.26 ||
āpadyamāna ity antarbhāvitṇyarthaḥ | yo ‘ntevāsī vinihitātmā dvayor ācāryayor vidhivan nihitātmā gurāv anaipuṇam āpādayati, nānenāyaṃ pradeśaḥ samyag ukta iti, so ‘ntevāsī na bhavati, sa tyājya ity arthaḥ | atra manuḥ |
dharmārthau yatra na syātāṃ śuśrūṣāpi na tadvidhā |
tatra vidyā na vaptavyā śubhaṃ bījam ivoṣare || [MDh 2.112]
apara āha – yo ‘ntevāsī vāṅmanaḥkāyakarmabhir anaipuṇam āpadyamānaḥ gurau visadṛśaṃ nihitātmā bhavaty anurūpaṃ na śuśrūṣate, so ‘ntevāsī na bhavati || 1.8.26 ||
ācāryo ‘py anācāryo bhavati śrutāt pariharamāṇaḥ || 1.8.27 ||
ācāryo ‘py anācāryo bhavatīti, tyājya ity arthaḥ | kiṃ kurvan | śrutāt pariharamāṇaḥ, tena tena vyājena vidyāpradānam kurvan || 1.8.27 ||
aparādheṣu cainaṃ satatam upalabheta || 1.8.28 ||
aparādheṣu kṛteṣu enaṃ śiṣyaṃ satatam upālabheta, idam ayuktaṃ tvayā kṛtam iti || 1.8.28 ||
abhitrāsa upavāsa udakopasparśanam adarśanam iti daṇḍā
yathāmātram ā nivṛtteḥ || 1.8.29 ||
abhitrāso rajjuveṇvādinā bhayotpādanam | upavāso bhojanalopaḥ | udakopasparśanaṃ śītodakena snāpanam | adarśanaṃ yathāsāv ātmānaṃ na paśyati tathā karaṇam, gṛhapraveśaniṣedhaḥ | sarvatra ṇyantāt pratyayaḥ | ity ete daṇḍāḥ śiṣyasya yathāmātraṃ yāvaty aparādhamātrā tadanurūpaṃ vyastāḥ samastāś ca | ā nivṛtteḥ, yāvad asau tato ‘parādhān nivartate tāvad ete daṇḍāḥ || 1.8.29 ||
nivṛttaṃ caritabrahmacaryam anyebhyo dharmebhyo ‘nantaro
bhavety atisṛjet || 1.8.30 ||
evaṃ caritabrahmacaryaṃ nivṛttaṃ gurukulāt, kṛtasamāvartanam ity arthaḥ | evaṃbhūtam enam anyebhyo dharmebhyo yam asāv āśramaṃ pratipatsyate tatra tebhyo dharmebhyo ‘nantaro bhava, tebhyo dharmebhyo yathā tvam antarito na bhavasi tathā bhavety uktvātisṛjet tam āśramaṃ pratipattum utsṛjet || 1.8.30 ||
ity aṣṭamaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
iti śrīharadattamiśraviracitāyāṃ āpastambadharmasūtravyākhyāyāṃ
ujjvalākhyāyāṃ dvitīyaḥ paṭaḷaḥ
śrāvaṇyāṃ paurṇamāsyām adhyāyam upākṛtya māsaṃ pradoṣe
nādhīyīta || 1.9.1 ||
evam adhyetur adhyāpayituś ca dharmā uktāḥ | atha deśakālakṛtādhyayanadharmā ucyante |
meṣādisthe savitari yo yo darśaḥ pravartate |
cāndramāsās tat tad antāś caitradyā dvādaśa smṛtāḥ ||
teṣu yā yā paurṇamāsī sā sā caitryādikā smṛtāḥ |
kācāditkena yogena nakṣatrasyeti nirṇayaḥ ||
tad evaṃ siṃhasthe savitari yāmāvāsyā tadante cāndramase māse yā madhyavartinī paurṇamāsī sā śrāvaṇī | śravaṇayogas tu bhavatu, mā vā bhūt | tasyāṃ śrāvaṇyāṃ paurṇamāsyām adhyāyam upākṛtya gṛhyoktena vidhinopākarma kṛtvā svādhyāyam adhīyīta | adhīyānaś ca māsam ekaṃ pradoṣe prathame rātribhāge, nādhīyīta grahaṇādhyayanaṃ dhāraṇādhyayanaṃ ca na kuryāt | pradoṣagrahaṇād rātrāv apy ūrdhvaṃ na doṣaḥ || 1.9.1 ||
taiṣyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ rohiṇyāṃ vā viramet || 1.9.2 ||
tiṣyaḥ puṣyaḥ tena yuktā paurṇamāsī taiṣī, śrāvaṇīvat | tasyāṃ viramet utsarjanaṃ kuryāt | tasyāpi prayogo gṛhyokta eva | rohiṇyāṃ vā, taiṣe māsi tiṣyāt pūrvā yā rohiṇī tasyāṃ vā viramet | anayoḥ pakṣayoḥ pañca māsān adhīyīta || 1.9.2 ||
ardhapañcamāṃś caturo māsān ity eke || 1.9.3 ||
ardhaṃ pañcamaṃ yeṣāṃ te ardhapañcamāḥ | ardhādhikāṃś caturo māsān adhīyītety eke manyante | asmin pakṣe proṣṭhapadyām upākaraṇam, śāstrāntaradarśanāt utsarjanasya vāpakarṣaḥ | utsarjane kṛte śrāvaṇyāḥ prāk śuklapakṣeṣu dhāraṇādhyayanaṃ vedasya, kṛṣṇapakṣeṣu vyākaraṇādyaṅgānām adhyayanam, punaś ca śrāvaṇyām upākṛtya agṛhītabhāgasya grahaṇādhyayanam iti | prapañcitam etad gṛhye || 1.9.3 ||
nigameṣv adhyayanaṃ varjayet || 1.9.4 ||
nigamāś catvarāḥ, grāmanirgamamārgā vā, niyamena gamyante yeṣv iti | teṣu sarvaprakāram adhyayanaṃ varjayet || 1.9.4 ||
ānaḍuhena vā śakṛtpiṇḍenopalipte ‘dhīyīta || 1.9.5 ||
anaḍutsaṃbandhinā śakṛtpiṇḍena upalipya nigameṣv adhīyīta || 1.9.5 ||
śmaśāne sarvataḥ śamyāprāsāt || 1.9.6 ||
śmaśāne cādhyayanaṃ varjayet | sarvataḥ sarvāsu dikṣu śamyāprāsāt | pañcamīnirdeśād arvāg iti gamyate | śamyā kṣiptā yāvati deśe patati tato ‘rvāg iti || 19.6 ||
grāmeṇādhyavasite kṣetreṇa vā nānadhyāyaḥ || 1.9.7 ||
yadā śmaśānaṃ grāmatayā kṣetratayā vādhyavasitaṃ svīkṛtaṃ bhavati tadā nānadhyāyaḥ, adhyetavyam eva || 1.9.7 ||
jñāyamāne tu tasminn eva deśe nādhīyīta || 1.9.8 ||
yadā tu tadadhyavasitam api śmaśānaṃ jñāyate ayaṃ sa pradeśa iti, tadā tāvaty eva pradeśe nādhīyīta, na śamyāprāsāt || 1.9.8 ||
śmaśānavac chūdrapatitau || 1.9.9 ||
śūdrapatitayoḥ sakāśe ‘pi śamyāprāsān nādhyeyam || 1.9.9 ||
samānāgāra ity eke || 1.9.10 ||
eke manyante samānāgāre śūdrapatitau varjyau, na śamyā prāsād iti || 1.9.10 ||
śūdrāyāṃ tu prekṣaṇapratiprekṣaṇayor
evānadhyāyaḥ || 1.9.11 ||
śūdrāyāṃ tu yadā parasparaprekṣaṇaṃ bhavati, tadaivānadhyāyaḥ, na samānāgāre nāpi śamyāprāsād iti || 1.9.11 ||
tathānyasyāṃ striyāṃ varṇavyatikrāntāyāṃ maithune || 1.9.12 ||
śūdrāvyatirikāpi yā strī maithune varṇavyatikrāntā nīcavarṇagāminī, tasyām api prekṣaṇapratiprekṣaṇayor evānadhyāyaḥ || 1.9.12 ||
brahmādhyeṣyamāṇo malavadvāsasecchant saṃbhāṣituṃ
brāhmaṇena saṃbhāṣya tathā saṃbhāṣeta saṃbhāṣye tu
brāhmaṇenaiva saṃbhāṣyādhīyītaivaṃ tasyāḥ prajāniḥśreyasam
|| 1.9.13 ||
yo vedam adhyeṣyamāṇo malavadvāsasā rajasvalayā saha saṃbhāṣitum icchati, sa pūrvaṃ brāhmaṇena saṃbhāṣya paścāt tayā saṃbhāṣeta | saṃbhāṣya ca punar api brāhmaṇenaiva saṃbhāṣyādhīyīta | kim evaṃ sati bhavati | evaṃ tasyā malavadvāsasa āgāminī yā prajā tasyā niḥśreyasam abhyudayo bhavati | prajārūpaṃ vā niḥśreyasaṃ tasyā bhavati | prajāniḥśreyasam iti vacanād vidhavādibhiḥ saṃbhāṣaṇe naitat kartavyam || 1.9.13 ||
antaḥśavam || 1.9.14 ||
antaścāṇḍālam || 1.9.15 ||
antaḥśavo yatra grāme tatra nādhyeyam | etenāntaścāṇḍālam iti vyākhyātam | caṇḍāla eva cāṇḍālaḥ | ubhayatra prathamā saptamyarthe, avyayībhāvo vā vibhaktyarthe draṣṭavyaḥ
|| 1.9.14–15 ||
abhinirhṛtānāṃ tu sīmny anadhyāyaḥ || 1.9.16 ||
yadā tu śavā grāmasīmny abhinirhṛtā bhavanti tadā tatrānadhyāyaḥ || 1.9.16 ||
saṃdarśane cāraṇye || 1.9.17 ||
araṇye ca yāvati pradeśe śavaś cāṇḍālo vā dṛśyate tāvaty anadhyāyaḥ || 1.9.17 ||
tad ahar āgateṣu ca grāmaṃ bāhyeṣu || 1.9.18 ||
bāhyā ugraniṣādādayaḥ paripanthinaḥ | teṣu ca grāmam āgateṣu tad ahar anadhyāyaḥ | tasminn ahani nādhyetavyam || 1.9.18 ||
api satsu || 1.9.19 ||
ye vidyācāritrādibhir mahāntaḥ santas teṣu ca grāmam āgateṣu tad ahar anadhyāyaḥ || 1.9.19 ||
saṃdhāv anustanite rātrim || 1.9.20 ||
saṃdhiḥ saṃdhyā | tasmin saṃdhāv anustanite meghagarjite sati rātrim anadhāyaḥ sarvāṃ rātriṃ nādhīyīta | varṣartāv idam | anyasminn adhikaṃ vakṣyati || 1.9.20 ||
svapnaparyāntaṃ vidyuti || 1.9.21 ||
svaptaparyāntam | antyo dīrgha upāntyo hrasvaḥ | viparyāsaś chāndasaḥ, apapāṭho vā | saṃdhau vidyuti satyāṃ svapnaparyantāṃ rātrim anadhyāyaḥ, na sarvām | svapnaparyantā rātriḥ praharāvaśiṣṭā || 1.9.21 ||
evaṃ sāyaṃsaṃdhyāyām uktam | prātaḥsaṃdhyāyām āha |
upavyuṣaṃ yāvatā vā kṛṣṇāṃ rohiṇīm iti śamyāprāsād
vijānīyād etasmin kāle vidyotamāne sapradoṣam ahar
anadhyāyaḥ || 1.9.22 ||
uṣaḥsamīpe upavyuṣam | tatrāpi vidyotamāne vidyuti satyām aparedyuḥ sapradoṣam ahar anadhyāyaḥ | pradoṣād ūrdhvaṃ rātrāv adhyayanam | yāvatā vā kālena śamyāprāsād arvāg avasthitāṃ gāṃ kṛṣṇām iti vā rohiṇīm iti vā vijānīyāt, etasmin kāle upavyuṣaṃ vidyotamāna ity anvayaḥ | rohiṇī gaur avarṇā | itiśabdaprayoge dvitīyā prayujyate | tatrānvayaprakāraś cintyaḥ
|| 1.9.22 ||
dahre ‘pararātre stanayitnunā || 1.9.23 ||
rātres tṛtīyā bhāgaḥ sarvo ‘pararātraḥ | tasya tredhā vibhaktasyādyāṃśo mahārātraḥ, antyo dahraḥ | tasmin dahre ‘pararātre stanayitnunā nimittena sapradoṣam ahar anadhyāyaḥ || 1.9.23 ||
ūrdhvam ardharātrād ity eke || 1.9.24 ||
ardharātrād ūrdhvaṃ stanite sapradoṣam ahar anadhyāya ity eke manyante | svapakṣas tu dahra eveti || 1.9.24 ||
gavāṃ cāvarodhe || 1.9.25 ||
dasyuprabhṛtibhir avaruddhāsu goṣu tāvantaṃ kālam anadhyāyaḥ | avarodho grāmān nirgamananirodhaḥ || 1.9.25 ||
vadhyānāṃ ca yāvatā vā hanyante || 1.9.26 ||
vadhārhāṇāṃ corādīnām uparodhe yāvatā kālena te hanyante tāvantaṃ kālam anadhyāyaḥ
|| 1.9.26 ||
pṛṣṭhārūḍhaḥ paśūnāṃ nādhīyīta || 1.9.27 ||
hastyaśvādīnāṃ paśūnāṃ pṛṣṭhārūḍhas tatrāsīnaḥ san nādhīyīta || 1.9.27 ||
ahorātrāv amāvāsyāsu || 1.9.28 ||
amāvāsyāsu dvāv ahorātrau nādhīyīta | tāsu ca pūrvedyuś caturdaśīṣu ca | tathā ca manuḥ |
amāvāsyācaturdaśyoḥ paurṇamāsyaṣṭakāsu ca || iti | [MDh 4.113cd]
|| 1.9.28 ||
iti navamaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
cāturmāsīṣu ca || 1.10.1 ||
caturṣu māseṣu bhavāś cāturmāsyaḥ | saṃjñaiṣā tisṝṇāṃ paurṇamāsīnām, yāsu cāturmāsyāni kriyante | kāḥ punas tāḥ | phālgunyāṣāḍhīkārtikyaḥ | cāturmāsyo yajñaḥ | “tatra bhavaḥ” iti vartamāne “saṃjñāyām aṇ” ity aṇpratyayaḥ | tāsu ca cāturmāsīṣu pūrvavad dvāv ahorātrāv anadhyāyaḥ | gautamas tu svaśabdenāha: “kārtikīphālgunyāṣāḍhī paurṇamāsī” iti [GDh 16.32] | paurṇamāsyantare pratipatsu ca śāstrāntaravacanād anadhyāyaḥ, yathāh uśanāḥ: “parvaṇītihāsavarjitānāṃ vidyānām anadhyāyaḥ” iti, “pratipatsu na cintayet” iti ca | evaṃ caturdaśīmātrasya varjane śāstrāntaraṃ mūlam | tatra yājñavalkyaḥ |
pañcadaśyāṃ caturdaśyām aṣṭamyāṃ rāhusūtake |
ṛtusaṃdhiṣu bhuktvā ca śrāddhikaṃ pratigṛhya ca || [YDh 1.146]
|| 1.10.1 ||
vairamaṇe guruṣv aṣṭākya aupākaraṇa iti tryahāḥ || 1.10.2 ||
viramaṇam utsarjanam, tad eva vairamaṇam, tasmin vairamaṇe | prathamāntapāṭhe saptamyarthe prathamā | guruṣu śvaśurādiṣu | saṃsthiteṣv iti prakaraṇād gamyate | aṣṭakaivāṣṭakyam | svārthe ṣyañ | ādau prāptā vṛddhir madhye kṛtā | upākaraṇam evaupākaraṇam | eteṣu nimitteṣu tryahā adhyayanarahitāḥ | tatra guruṣu maraṇadivasād ārabhya tryahāḥ, itareṣu tu pūrvedyur aparedyus tasmiṃś ca dine nādhīyīta | tatra gautamaḥ: “tisro ‘ṣṭakās trirātram … abhito vārṣikam” iti [GDh 16.38, 40] | upākaraṇād ūrdhvaṃ prāg utsarjanād yad adhyayanaṃ tad vārṣikam, tad abhitas tasyādāv ante ca yat karma kriyate tatrāpi trirātram ity arthaḥ | uśanasā ca vyaktam uktam:         upākarmaṇi cotsarge trirātraṃ kṣapaṇaṃ smṛtam | iti || 1.10.2 ||
tathā saṃbandheṣu jñātiṣu || 1.10.3 |
saṃnikṛṣṭā ye jñātayaḥ bhrātṛtatputrapitṛvyādayaḥ teṣv api mṛteṣu tathā tryaham anadhyāyaḥ | brahmacāriṇo vidhir ayam | āśaucavatāṃ tu yāvad āśaucam anadhyāyaḥ śāstrāntarasiddhaḥ | tatra manuḥ |
ubhayatra daśāhāni kulasyānnaṃ na bhujyate |
dānaṃ pratigraho homaḥ svādhyāyaś ca nivartate || [MDh 5.61 add]
ubhayatra, janane maraṇe ca || 1.10.3 ||
mātari pitary ācārya iti dvādaśāhāḥ || 1.10.4 ||
mātrādiṣu mṛteṣu dvādaśāham anadhyāyaḥ | ayaṃ vidhiḥ gṛhasthānām api | kecid āśaucam api tāvantaṃ kālam icchanti | nety vayam, anadhyāyaprakaraṇāt || 1.10.4 ||
teṣu codakasparśanaṃ tāvantaṃ kālam || 1.10.5 ||
mātrādiṣv adhikam – tāvantaṃ kālam ahar ahas snānam api kāryam, na kevalam anadhyāyaḥ
|| 1.10.5 ||
anubhāvināṃ ca parivāpanam || 1.10.6 ||
anu paścādbhūtā jātā anubhāvinaḥ mṛtāpekṣayāvaravayasaḥ, teṣāṃ parivāpanam api bhavati keśānām | “kṛty acaḥ” [Pāṇ 8.4.29] iti prāptasya ṇatvasya “ṇer vibhāṣā” [Pāṇ 8.4.30] iti vikalpaḥ | anye tu – śāvaṃ duḥkham anubhavatāṃ sarveṣāṃ parivāpanam icchanti | apara āha – anubhāvinaḥ udakārhāḥ, teṣāṃ maraṇe parivāpanam iti || 1.10.6 ||
na samāvṛttā vaperann anyatra vihārād ity eke || 1.10.7 ||
vihāro yajñadīkṣā, tato ‘nyatra na samāvṛttā vapernn ity eke manyante | svamataṃ tu vaperann eveti || 1.10.7 ||
tatra vapanasyāmaṅgalatvaṃ guṇavidhinā parihāraṃ ca vaktuṃ brāhmaṇam udāharati |
athāpi brāhmaṇam | rikto vā eṣo ‘napihito yan muṇḍas
tasyaitad apidhānaṃ yac chikheti || 1.10.8 ||
rikto ‘ntaḥśūnyo ghaṭādiḥ | so ‘yam anapihitaḥ pidhānarahito yādṛśaḥ tādṛśa eṣa yan muṇḍo nāma | tasya riktasyāpidhānam etat yac chikhā nāma | anena caitad darśitam – niṣedhaśāstraṃ saha śikhayā vapanapratiṣedhaparam iti || 1.10.8 ||
kathaṃ tarhi sattreṣu “śikhām anu pra vapanta ṛddhyai” [TS 7.4.9] iti vacanam | tasya balīyastvād ity āha |
sattreṣu tu vacanād vapanaṃ śikhāyāḥ || 1.10.9 ||
spaṣṭam || 1.10.9 ||
ācārye trīn ahorātrān ity eke || 1.10.10 ||
ācārye saṃsthite trīn ahorātrān adhyayanaṃ varjayed ity eke manyante | svapakṣas tu dvādaśāhāḥ pūrvoktaḥ || 1.10.10 ||
śrotriyasaṃsthāyām aparisaṃvatsarāyām ekām || 1.10.11 ||
śrotriyaṃ vakṣyati | tasya saṃsthāyām aparipūrṇasaṃvatsarāyāṃ śrutāyām ekāṃ rātrim ekam ahorātram anadhyāyaḥ | atra saṃsthāśravaṇād gurvādiṣv api saiva nimittam anadhyāyam asya
|| 1.10.11 ||
sabrahmacāriṇīty eke || 1.10.12 ||
eke tu sabrahmacārimaraṇa evānantaroktam anadhyāyam icchanti, na śrotriyasāmānyamaraṇe
|| 1.10.12 ||
śrotriyābhyāgame ‘dhijigāṃsamāno ‘dhīyāno
vānujñāpyādhīyīta || 1.10.13 ||
śrotriye ‘bhyāgate adhyetukāmo ‘dhīyānaś ca tam anujñāpyādhīyīta || 1.10.13 ||
adhyāpayed vā || 1.10.14 ||
adhyapayitukāmo ‘dhyāpayan veti prakaraṇād gamyate | so ‘pi śrotriyam abhyāgatam anujñāpyādhyāpayed iti || 1.10.14 ||
gurusaṃnidhau cādhīhi bho ity uktvādhīyīta || 1.10.15 ||
dhāraṇādhyayanaṃ pārāyaṇādhyayanaṃ vā kurvan gurau saṃnihite sati adhīhi bho ity uktvādhīyīta || 1.10.15 ||
adhyāpayed vā || 1.10.16 ||
adhyāpayann api gurau saṃnihite evam evoktvādhyāpayet || 1.10.16 ||
ubhayata upasaṃgrahaṇam adhijigāṃsamānasya
cādhītya ca || 1.10.17 ||
ubhayato ‘dhyayanasyādāv ante ca upasaṃgrahaṇaṃ kartavyam, adhyetukāmasyādāv adhītasya cānte || 1.10.17 ||
adhīyāneṣu vā yatrānyo vyaveyād etam eva śabdam
utsṛjyādhīyīta || 1.10.18 ||
bahuvacanam atantram | adhīyāneṣu ca yatrānyo vyaveyād antar āgacchet, tatrāpi “adhīhi bho” ity etam eva śabdam utsṛjyoccāryādhīyīta | pratyekam upadeśād ekavacanam | adhīyīran
|| 1.10.18 ||
śvagardabhanādāḥ salāvṛkyekasṛkolūkaśabdāḥ sarve
vāditaśabdā rodanagītasāmaśabdāś ca || 1.10.19 ||
śunāṃ gardabhānāṃ ca bahūnāṃ nādāḥ, bahuvacananirdeśāt | salāvṛkī vṛkajātāv avāntarabhedaḥ, kroṣṭrīty anye | liṅgasyāvivakṣitatvāt puṃso ‘pi grahaṇam, “indro yatīn sālāvṛkebhyaḥ” [TS 6.2.7] ityādau darśanāt | sarvatrādimaḥ svaro dīrghaḥ | sa evāyaṃ vikṛtaḥ prayuktaḥ | ekasṛkaḥ ekacaraḥ sṛgālaḥ | ulūko divābhītaḥ | eteṣāṃ ca śabdāḥ | vāditāni vāditrāṇi vīṇāveṇumṛdaṅgādīni | teṣāṃ ca sarve śabdāḥ rodanaśabdā gītaśabdāḥ sāmaśabdāś ca, ete śrūyamāṇā anadhyāyasya hetavaḥ || 1.10.19 ||
śākhāntare ca sāmnām anādhyāyaḥ || 1.10.20 ||
vedāntarasya sakāśe sāma nādhyeyam, “gītiṣu sāmākhyā” [PMS 2.1.36] | tadyogād vedavacanaḥ sāmaśabda ity anye || 1.10.20 ||
sarveṣu ca śabdakarmasu yatra saṃsṛjyeran || 1.10.21 ||
ākrośaparihāsādiṣu sarveṣu śabdakarmasu anadhyāyaḥ, yatrādhyayanaśabdena te saṃsṛjyeran
|| 1.10.21 ||
chardayitvā svapnāntam || 1.10.22 ||
chardanaṃ vamanam, tat kṛtvā svapnāntaṃ yāvan nādhīyīta || 1.10.22 ||
sarpir vā prāśya || 1.10.23 ||
atha vā sarpiḥ prāśyādhīyīta || 1.10.23 ||
pūtigandhaḥ || 1.10.24 ||
durgandhaḥ | sa copalabhyamāno ‘nadhyāyahetuḥ || 1.10.24 ||
śūktaṃ cātmasaṃyuktam || 1.10.25 ||
