yat pakvaṃ kālapākenāmlaṃ jātaṃ tac chūktam | tad yāvad ātmasaṃyuktaṃ svodarastham ajīrṇam, yāvat tadanuguṇa udgāro vā, tāvad anadhyāyahetuḥ || 1.10.25 ||
prodoṣe ca bhuktvā || 1.10.26 ||
nādhīyīteti śeṣaḥ | tenādhītyaiva bhuñjīta || 1.10.26 ||
prodakayoś ca pāṇyoḥ || 1.10.27 ||
bhuktvety eva | bhuktvā yāvat prodakau pāṇī ārdrau tāvan nādhīyīta | kecid bhuktveti nānuvartayanti || 1.10.27 ||
pretasaṃkḷptaṃ cānnaṃ bhuktvā sapradoṣam
anadhyāyaḥ || 1.10.28 ||
yo mṛto ‘sapiṇḍīkṛtaḥ sa pretaḥ | taduddeśena dattam annaṃ bhuktvā sapradoṣam ahar nādhīyīta | prodoṣād ūrdhvaṃ na doṣaḥ | atra manuḥ |
yāvad ekānudiṣṭasya gandho lepaś ca tiṣthati |
viprasya viduṣo dehe tāvad brahma na kīrtayet || iti | [MDh 4.111]
|| 1.10.28 ||
ā ca vipākāt || 1.10.29 ||
yadi tāvatā kālena tad annaṃ jīrṇaṃ vipakvaṃ na bhavati tataś cā vipākāt tasya nādhīyīta
|| 1.10.29 ||
aśrāddhena tu paryavadadhyāt || 1.10.30 ||
jīrṇe ajīrṇe ca tasmin aśrāddhenānnena paryavadadhyāt | tasyopary aśrāddham annaṃ bhūñjītety uktaṃ bhavati | kecit – aśrāddheneti vacanāt pūrvatrāpi pretānnam iti śrāddhamātraṃ vivakṣitaṃ manyante || 1.10.30 ||
iti daśamaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
kāṇḍopākaraṇe cāmātṛkasya || 1.11.1 ||
kāṇḍopākaraṇaṃ kāṇḍavratādeśanam | tasminn ahany amātṛkasyānnaṃ bhuktvā sapradoṣam ahar anadhyāyaḥ | apara āha – bhuktveti nānuvartate | tathā cottaratra bhuktvāgrahaṇam | kāṇḍopākaraṇe nimitte ‘mātṛkasya māṇavakasya sapradoṣam ahar anadhyāya iti | etenottaraṃ vyākhyātam || 1.11.1 ||
kāṇḍasamāpane cāpitṛkasya || 1.11.2 ||
kāṇḍasamāpanaṃ vratavisargaḥ || 1.1.2 ||
manuṣyaprakṛtīnāṃ ca devānāṃ yajñe bhuktvety eke || 1.11.3 ||
ye manuṣyā bhūtvā prakṛṣṭena tapasā devāḥ saṃpannās te manuṣyaprakṛtayo devāḥ, nandīśvarakumārādayaḥ | teṣāṃ yajñe tatprītyarthe brāhmaṇabhojane bhuktvā sapradoṣam ahar anadhyāya ity eke manyante | manuṣyamukheṣu deveṣv ijyamāneṣv ity anye || 1.11.3 ||
paryuṣitais taṇḍulair āmamāṃsena ca nānadhyāyaḥ || 1.11.4 ||
“pretasaṃkḷptam” [ĀpDh 1.10.28] ity asyāpavādaḥ | paryuṣitā rātryantaritāḥ | hyaḥ pratigṛhīteṣu taṇḍuleṣu adya paktvā bhujyamāneṣu nānadhyāyaḥ | tathāmamāṃsena tad ahar bhakṣitenāpi nānadhyāyaḥ | paryuṣitenety anye | paryuṣitair iti vacanāt tad ahar bhuktaiḥ sapradoṣam anadhyāyaḥ || 1.11.4 ||
tathauṣadhivanaspatimūlaphalaiḥ || 1.11.5 ||
oṣadhigrahaṇena vīrudho ‘pi gṛhyante | vanaspatigrahaṇenāpi vṛkṣamātram | teṣāṃ mūlaiḥ sūraṇakandādibhiḥ phalaiś cāmrādibhiḥ pakvair apakvaiś ca tad ahar bhakṣitair api nānadhyāyaḥ | paryuṣitenety anye || 1.11.5 ||
yat kāṇḍam upākurvīta yasya cānuvākyaṃ kurvīta na tat tad
ahar adhīyīta || 1.11.6 ||
yasminn ahani yat kāṇḍam upākṛtaṃ na tat tad ahar adhīyīta | tathā śrāvaṇyāṃ paurṇamāsyām upākṛtya praśaste ‘harantare yasya kāṇḍasyānuvākyam adhyetum ārambhaṃ kurvīta, na tat tad ahar adhīyīta | ahar ity ahorātropalakṣaṇam || 1.11.6 ||
upākaraṇasamāpanayoś ca pārāyaṇasya tāṃ vidyām || 1.11.7 ||
anekavedādhyāyī yadaikasya vedasya pārāyaṇaṃ kurute, tadā tasya pārāyaṇasya ye upākaraṇotsarjane tayoḥ kṛtayos tāṃ vidyāṃ tad ahar nādhīyīta | etad eva jñāpakaṃ pārāyaṇasyāpy upākaraṇotsarjane bhavata iti | “tāṃ vidyām” iti vacanād vidyāntarādhyayane na doṣaḥ || 1.11.7 ||
vāyur ghoṣavān bhūmau vā tṛṇasaṃvāho varṣati vā yatra dhārāḥ
pravahet || 1.11.8 ||
ghoṣavān karṇaśrāvaḥ | bhūmāv avasthitāni tṛṇāni saṃvahati utkṣipya gamayatīti tṛṇasaṃvāhaḥ | varṣati vā meghe dhārāḥ pravahet vikṣipet | yatra deśe evaṃvidho vāyus tatra tāvantaṃ kālaṃ nādhīyīta | atra manuḥ |
karṇaśrave ‘nile rātrau divā pāṃsusamūhane || iti | [MDh 4.102]
|| 1.11.8 ||
uttare sūtre nigadasiddhe |
grāmāraṇyayoś ca saṃdhau || 1.11.9 ||
mahāpathe ca || 1.11.10 ||
viproṣya ca samadhyayanaṃ tad ahaḥ || || 1.11.11 ||
yadā sahādhīyāneṣu kecit kāraṇavaśād vipravaseyuḥ, kecic cācāryeṇa saṃgatāḥ, tadā samadhyayanaṃ sahādhīyamānaṃ pradeśaṃ tad ahar nādhīyīta | viproṣitānāṃ yad ahaḥ punar melanaṃ tad ahar nādhīyītety anye || 1.11.11 ||
svairikarmasu ca || 1.11.12 ||
nādhīyītety eva || 1.11.12 ||
tatrodāharaṇam |
yathā pādaprakṣālanotsādanānulepanāṇīti || 1.11.13 ||
ṇatvam ākasmikam, apapāṭho vā || 1.11.13 ||
tāvantaṃ kālaṃ nādhīyītādhyāpayed vā || 1.11.14 ||
teṣu svairikarmasu tāvantaṃ kālam adhyayanam adhyāpanaṃ ca varjayet || 1.11.14 ||
saṃdhyoḥ || 1.11.15 ||
“sajyotiṣy ā jyotiṣo darśanāt” [GDh 2.11] | ubhe saṃdhye, tayos tāvantaṃ kālaṃ nādhīyītādhyāpayed vā | evam uttaratrāpy anuvṛttiḥ || 1.11.15 ||
uttarasūtre nigadasiddhe |
tathā vṛkṣam ārūḍhaḥ || 1.11.16 ||
apsu cāvagāḍhaḥ || 1.11.17 ||
naktaṃ cāpāvṛte || 1.11.18 ||
vivṛtadvāram apāvṛtam | tatra naktaṃ nādhīyīta || 1.1.18 ||
divā cāpihite || 1.11.19 ||
saṃvṛtadvāram apihitam | tatra divā nādhīyīta || 1.11.19 ||
avihitam anuvākādhyayanam āṣāḍhavāsantikayoḥ || 1.11.20 ||
vāsantiko vasantotsavaḥ, sa caitre māsi śuklatrayodaśyāṃ bhavati | āṣāḍhaśabdenāpi tasmin māse kriyamāṇas tādṛśaḥ kaścid indrotsavādir vivakṣitaḥ | tayos tad ahar anuvākādhyayanam avihitam | anuvākagrahaṇān nyūne na doṣaḥ | apara āha – anuvākagrahaṇān mantrabrāhmaṇayor eva pratiṣedho nāṅgānām iti || 1.11.20 ||
nityapraśnasya cāvidhinā || 1.1.21 ||
nityaṃ praśnādhyayanaṃ yatra sa nityapraśno brahmayajñaḥ | tasya cāvidhinā vakṣyamāṇena prakāreṇa vinānuvākādhyayanam avihitam | yady api nityaṃ brahmayajñādhyayanam, tathāpi kenacid apy aṅgena vinā na kartavyam | tena vismṛtya prātarāśe kṛte prāyaścittam eva, na brahmayajñaḥ | manuḥ |
vedoditānāṃ nityānāṃ karmaṇāṃ samatikrame |
snātakavratalope ca prāyaścittam abhojanam || iti | [MDh 11.204]
|| 1.11.21 ||
tasya vidhiḥ || 1.11.22 ||
tasya nityapraśnasya vidhir vakṣyate || 1.11.22 ||
akṛtaprātarāśa udakāntaṃ gatvā prayataḥ śucau deśe ‘dhīyīta
yathādhyāyam utsṛjan vācā || 1.11.23 ||
akṛtaprātarāśaḥ akṛtadivābhojanaḥ | udakāntam udakasamīpaṃ gatvā | prayataḥ snānamārjanādinā śuddhaḥ śucau deśe prācyām udīcyāṃ vā diśy achadir darśe ‘dhīyīta yathādhyāyaṃ yathāpāṭham anuṣaṅgarahitam | utsṛjan, ādita ārabhya prathamādiṣv ahaḥ svadhītaṃ dvitīyādiṣv ahaḥsūtsṛjya tataḥ param adhīyīta | vācā uccair ity arthaḥ || 1.11.23 ||
manasā cānadhyāye || 1.11.24 ||
anadhyāye ca manasādhīyīta nityasvādhyāyam || 1.11.24 ||
vidyuti cābhyagrāyāṃ stanayitnāv aprāyatye pretānne
nīhāre ca mānasaṃ paricakṣate || 1.11.25 ||
vidyuty abhyagrāyām aviratāyām | stanayitnau cābhyagre | aprāyatye ātmano ‘śucibhāve | pretānne ca bhukte | nīhāro himānī, tasmiṃś ca vartamāne | mānasam anantaroktam adhyayanaṃ paricakṣate varjayanti || 1.11.25 ||
śrāddhabhojana evaike || 1.11.26 ||
eke tv ācāryāḥ śrāddhabhojana eva mānasaṃ paricakṣate, na vidyudādiṣu || 1.11.26 ||
vidyutstanayitnur vṛṣṭiś cāpartau yatra saṃnipateyus tryaham
anadhyāyaḥ || 1.11.27 ||
apartau, yasmin deśe yo varṣakālas tato ‘nyas tatrāpartuḥ, tatra yadi vidyudādayaḥ saṃnipateyuḥ samuditāḥ syus tadā tryaham anadhyāyaḥ || 1.11.27 ||
yāvad bhūmir vyudakety eke || 1.11.28 ||
yāvatā kālena bhūmir vigatodakā bhavati tāvantaṃ kālam anadhyāya ity eke manyante
|| 1.11.28 ||
ekena dvābhyāṃ vaiteṣām ākālam || 1.11.29 ||
eteṣāṃ vidyudādīnāṃ madhye ekena dvābhyāṃ vā yoge ākālam anadhyāyaḥ, aparedyur ā tasya kālasya prāpter ity arthaḥ || 1.11.29 ||
sūryācandramasor grahaṇe bhūmicale ‘pasvāna ulkāyām
agnyutpāte ca sarvāsāṃ vidyānāṃ sārvakālikam
ākālam || 1.11.30 ||
sūryācandramasor iti vacanaṃ bṛhaspatyādinivṛttyartham | bhūmicale bhūmikampe | apasvāne nirghāte | ulkāyām ulkāpāte | agnyutpāte grāmādidāhe | eteṣu nimitteṣu sarvāsāṃ vidyānām –
aṅgāni vedāś catvāro mīmāṃsā nyāyavistaraḥ |
purāṇaṃ dharmaśāstraṃ ca vidyā hy etāś caturdaśa ||
ityuktānāṃ sārvakālikam ṛtau cāpartau cākālam anadhyāyaḥ | sarvāsām iti vacanād anyatra vedānām eva pratiṣedhaḥ | aṅgānām apīty anye || 1.11.30 ||
abhraṃ cāpartau sūryācandramasoḥ pariveṣa indradhanuḥ
pratisūryamatsyaś ca vāte pūtigandhe nīhāre ca sarveṣv eteṣu
tāvatkālam || 1.11.31 ||
apartāv abhraṃ dṛśyamānaṃ yāvad dṛśyate tāvat kālam anadhyāyaḥ | evaṃ pariveṣādiṣv api yojyam | atrāpi bṛhaspatyādipariveṣe na doṣaḥ | indradhanuḥ prasiddham | sūryasamīpe tadākṛtiḥ pratisūryaḥ | matsyaḥ pucchavan nakṣatram | samāhāradvandve chāndaso liṅgavyatyayaḥ | sarveṣv eteṣu vātādiṣu ca triṣu tāvatkālam anadhyāyaḥ | vāte ghoṣavati | pūtigandhe durgandhe | nīhāre himānyām | vātādigrahaṇaṃ pūrvoktānāṃ śvagardabhādīnām upalakṣaṇārtham | punar iha vacanaṃ tāvatkālam iti vidhātum | atraiva śvagardabhādigrahaṇe kartavye pūvatra pāṭhasya cintyaṃ prayojanam || 1.11.31 ||
muhūrtaṃ virate vāte || 1.11.32 ||
vāte ghoṣavati virate ‘pi muhūrtamātram anadhyāyaḥ | dve nāḍike muhūrtaḥ || 1.11.32 ||
salāvṛkyām ekasṛka iti svapnaparyāntam || 1.11.33 ||
tāvatkālam ity asyāpavādo ‘yam | salāvṛkyekasṛkaśabdau vyākhyātau || 1.11.33 ||
naktaṃ cāraṇye ‘nagnāv ahiraṇye vā || 1.11.34 ||
rātrāv agnivarjite hiraṇyavarjite vāraṇye nādhīyīta || 1.11.34 ||
ananūktaṃ cāpartau chandaso nādhīyīta || 1.11.35 ||
utsarjanād ūrdhvam upākaraṇād arvāg apartuḥ | tatra chandaso ‘nanūktam aṃśam apūrvaṃ nādhīyīta, grahaṇādhyayanam apartau na kartavyam | yady api “taiṣyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ rohiṇyāṃ vā viramet” [ĀpDh 1.9.2], tathāpi kiyantaṃ kālaṃ tadviramaṇam, kasmād adhyayanāt, ity apekṣāyām ucyate – etāvantaṃ kālaṃ grahaṇādhyayanaṃ na kartavyam | dhāraṇādhyayane tu na doṣaḥ | tathā “chandasaḥ” iti vacanād aṅgānāṃ grahaṇādhyayane ‘pi na doṣaḥ || 1.11.35 ||
pradoṣe ca || 1.11.36 ||
pradoṣe cānanūktam ṛtāv api nādhīyīta | “māsaṃ pradoṣe nādhīyīta” [ĀpDh 1.9.1] ity etat tu dhāraṇādhyayanasyāpi pratiṣedhārtham | apara āha – yasyāṃ rātrau dvādaśī trayodaśī ca miśre bhavatas tasyāṃ pradoṣe nādhīyītānanūktam anūktaṃ ca | ṛtāv apartau caiṣa ācāra iti || 1.11.36 ||
sārvakālikam āmnātam || 1.11.37 ||
āmnātam adhītam, tat sārvakālikam apartau pradoṣe ca sarvasmin kāle ‘dhyeyam || 1.11.37 ||
yathoktam anyad ataḥ pariṣatsu || 1.11.38 ||
ata eva tasmād anadhyayanaprakaraṇād anyad anadhyayananimittaṃ pariṣatsu mānavādiṣu dharmaśāstreṣu yathoktaṃ tathā draṣṭavyam | tatra vasiṣṭhaḥ: “digdāhaparvataprapāteṣūpala-rudhirapāṃsuvarṣeṣv ākālikam” iti [VaDh 13.35] | yamaḥ:
śleṣmātakasya śalmalyā madhūkasya tathāpy adhaḥ |
kadācid api nādhyeyaṃ kovidārakapitthayoḥ ||
“saṃgrāmodyānadevatāsamīpeṣu nādhīyīta” || 1.11.38 ||
ity ekādaśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
iti śrīharadattamiśraviracitāyām āpastambadharmasūtravyākhyāyām
tṛtīyaḥ paṭalaḥ
tapaḥ svādhyāya iti brāhmaṇam || 1.12.1 ||
yo ‘yaṃ nityasvādhyāyas tat tapaḥ, kṛcchrātikṛcchracāndrāyaṇādilakṣaṇaṃ tapo yāvat kālaṃ sādhayati tāvat sādhayatīty arthaḥ || 1.12.1 ||
tatra śrūyate | sa yadi tiṣṭhann āsīnaḥ śayāno vā svādhyāyam
adhīte tapa eva tat tapyate tapo hi svādhyāya iti || 1.12.2 ||
tatraiva brāhmaṇe sa yadi tiṣṭhann āsīna ity āpatkalpaḥ śrūyate | tatra “dharbhāṇāṃ mahad upastīryopasthaṃ kṛtvā prāṅāsīnaḥ svādhyāyam adhīyīta” [TaitĀr 2.11] ityādi mukhyaḥ kalpo brāhmaṇa evoktaḥ | iha punar āsīnavacanaṃ yathākathaṃcid āsanārtham | sarvathāpy adhīyānas tapa eva tat tapyata iti brāhmaṇārthaḥ | manur apy āha |
āhaiva sa nakhāgrebhyaḥ paramaṃ tapyate tapaḥ |
yaḥ sragvy api dvijo ‘dhīte svādhyāyaṃ śaktito ‘nvaham || iti | [MDh 2.167]
sragvīti svairaṃ darśayati || 1.12.2 ||
evaṃ kartur niyamo nāpady atīvādaraṇīya ity uktvā kāle ‘py āha |
athāpi vājasaneyibrāhmaṇam | brahmayajño ha vā eṣa yat
svādhyāyas tasyaite vaṣaṭkārā yat stanayati yad vidyotate yad
avasphūrjati yad vāto vāti | tasmāt stanayati vidyotamāne
‘vasphūrjati vāte vā vāyaty adhīyītaiva vaṣaṭkārāṇām
achambaṭkārāyeti || 1.12.3 ||
athāpi api ca svādhyāyo nāma yad eṣa brahmayajñaḥ, brahma vedaḥ, tatsādhano yāgaḥ, yathā darśapūrṇamāsādayaḥ puroḍāśādisādhanāḥ | havaiśabdau prasiddhiṃ dyotayataḥ | tasya yajñasyaite vakṣyamāṇāḥ stanayitnvādayo vaṣaṭkārāḥ | bahuvacananirdeśād vaṣaṭkārānuvaṣaṭkārasvāhākārādayaḥ sarve pradānārthāḥ gṛhyante | stananaṃ meghaśabdaḥ | vidyotanaṃ vidyudvyāpāraḥ | avasphūrjanam aśnipātaḥ, tatra “avasphūrjathur liṅgam” iti darśanāt | vāyatīti o vai śoṣaṇe ity asya rūpam | yathārdrapradeśaḥ śuṣko bhavati tathā vātīty arthaḥ | yasmād ete vaṣaṭkārā brahmayajñasya, tasmāt stananādiṣv anadhyāyanimitteṣu satsv apy adhīyītaiva, na punar anadhyāya iti nādhīyīta, brahmayajñaṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ | kim artham | vaṣaṭkārāṇām eteṣām achambaṭkārāya avyarthatvāya | anyathā ete vaṣaṭkārā vyarthāḥ syuḥ | tataś ca yathā hotrā vaṣaṭkṛte ‘dhvaryur na juhuyāt tādṛg etat syāt || 1.12.3 ||
tasya śākhāntare vākyasamāptiḥ || 1.12.4 ||
tasya vājasaneyibrāhmaṇasya śākhāntare vākyasamāptir bhavati | na tāvatā paryavasānam
|| 1.12.4 ||
tad eva śākhāntaraṃ paṭhati |
atha yadi vāto vā vāyāt stanayed vā vidyoteta vāvasphūrjed
vaikāṃ varcam ekaṃ vā yajur ekaṃ vā sāmābhivyāhared bhūr
bhuvaḥ suvaḥ satyaṃ tapaḥ śraddhāyāṃ juhomīti vaitat | teno
haivāsyaitad ahaḥ svādhyāya upātto bhavati || 1.12.5 ||
ante itiśabdo ‘dhyāhartavyaḥ | vātādiṣu satsu ekām ṛcam adhīyīta prāpte pradeśe | yajurvedādhyayana ekaṃ yajuḥ | sāmavedādhyana ekaṃ sāma | sarveṣu vā vedeṣu bhūr bhuvaḥ suvar ityādikaṃ yajur abhivyāharet, na punar yathāpūrvaṃ praśnamātram | tenaitāvad evāsyādhyetus tad ahas tasminn ahani svādhyāya upātto bhavati svīkṛto bhavati, adhīto bhavatīty arthaḥ | kecit tu – bhūr bhuvaḥ suvar ityādikaṃ brāhmaṇabhāgādhyayanaviṣayaṃ manyante, na sāvitrikam || 1.12.5 ||
kasmāt punar vājasaneyibrāhmaṇasyodāhṛte śākhāntare vākyasamāptir āśrīyate, na punar yathāśrutamātraṃ gṛhyate | tatrāha |
evaṃ saty āryasamayenāvipratiṣiddham || 1.12.6 ||
evaṃ sati evaṃ vākyasamāptāv āśrīyamāṇāyām, āryasamayena, āryāḥ śiṣṭā manvādayaḥ, teṣāṃ samayo vyavasthā, tenāvipratiṣiddhaṃ bhavati | itarathā vipratiṣiddhaṃ syāt || 1.12.6 ||
katham |
adhyāyānadhyāyaṃ hy upadiśanti tad anarthakaṃ syād
vājasaneyibrāhmaṇaṃ ced avekṣeta || 1.12.7 ||
āryā hy adhyāyam anadhyāyaṃ copadiśanti | tadupadeśanam anarthakaṃ syāt yadi vājasaneyibrāhmaṇaṃ yathāśrutam avekṣetādhyetā || 1.12.7 ||
āha – anarthakam etad astu śrutivirodhāt | tatrāha |
āryasamayo hy agṛhyamānakāraṇaḥ || 1.12.8 ||
yo ‘yam adhyāyānadhyāyaviṣaya āryasamayaḥ na tatra kiṃcit kāraṇaṃ gṛhyate, yathā “vaisarjanahomīyaṃ vāso ‘dhvaryave dadāti” ity atrāgṛhyamāṇakāraṇaś cāryasamayaḥ śrutyanumānadvāreṇa pramāṇam | ato vākyasamāptir eva yuktā | evaṃ hi vājasaneyibrāhmaṇasyāpi nātyantabādhaḥ | anadhyāyopadeśanasyāpi prabhūtādhyayanaviṣayatayārthavattvam iti | sūtre “agṛhyamānakāraṇaḥ” iti ṇatvābhāvaḥ chāndasaḥ || 1.12.8 ||
kā punar asau smṛtiḥ yā brahmayajñe ‘py anadhyāyam upadiśati | mānave tāvad viparyayaḥ śrūyate:
naityake nāsty anadhyāyo brahmasattraṃ hi tat smṛtam || iti | [MDh 2.106]
sāmānyenānadhyāyopadeśas tu brahmayajñād anyatra caritārthaḥ | tasmāt tādṛśī smṛtir mṛgyā | evaṃ tarhy agnihotrādiṣv api mantrāṇām anadhyāyaḥ prāpnoti | nety āha –
vidyāṃ praty anadhyāyaḥ śrūyate na karmayoge
mantrāṇām || 1.12.9 ||
vidyā vedanam adhyayanam, tāṃ praty anadhyādaḥ śrūyate, na punar mantrāṇāṃ karmayoge | hetuḥ paribhāṣāyām uktaḥ “arthāntaratvāt” iti [ĀpPar 1.40] | arthāntaraṃ hi karmaṇi prayogo mantrāṇāṃ na punar grahaṇārtham adhyayanam anuṣṭheyārthaprakāśakatayā | evaṃ ca pārāyaṇādhyayanamadhye anadhyāyāgame adhyayanaṃ bhavati na veti cintyam | evaṃ rudrādijape ‘pi || 1.12.9 ||
kathaṃ punar āryasamayaḥ pramāṇaṃ yāvatā na teṣām atīndriyārthe jñānaṃ saṃbhavatīti | tatrāha |
brāhmaṇoktā vidhayas teṣām utsannāḥ pāṭhāḥ prayogād
anumīyante || 1.12.10 ||
vidhīyanta iti vidhayaḥ karmāṇi | te sarve ‘pi smārtā api brāhmaṇeṣv evoktāḥ | nanv idānīṃ brāhmaṇāni nopalabhyante | satyam | teṣām utsannāḥ pāṭhāḥ, adhyetṛdaurbalyāt | kathaṃ tarhi teṣām astitvam | prayogād anumīyante | prayogaḥ smṛtinibandhanam anuṣṭhānaṃ ca, tasmād brāhmaṇāny anumīyante manvādibhir upalabdhānīti | katham aparathā smareyur anuṣṭheyur vā | saṃbhavati ca teṣāṃ vedasaṃyogaḥ || 1.12.10 ||
atha prasaṅgād apasmṛtir ucyate |
yatra tu prītyupalabdhitaḥ pravṛttir na tatra śāstram
asti || 1.12.11 ||
yatra tu mātulasutāpariṇayanādau prītyupalabdhitaḥ pravṛttir na tatrotsannapāṭhaṃ śāstram anumīyate, prīter eva pravṛttihetoḥ saṃbhavāt || 1.12.11 ||
tataś ca |
tadanuvartamāno narakāya rādhyati || 1.12.12 ||
tadvidhānam anutiṣṭhan narakāyaiva kalpate || 1.12.12 ||
atha brāhmaṇoktā vidhayaḥ || 1.12.13 ||
evaṃ smṛtyācāraprāptānāṃ śrutimūlatvam uktam | athedānīṃ pratyakṣabrāhmaṇoktā eva kecid vidhayo vyākhyāyante teṣām api smārteṣv anupraveśārtham | tena tadatikrame smārtātikramanimittam eva prāyaścittaṃ bhavati || 1.12.13 ||
teṣāṃ mahāyajñā mahāsattrāṇīti saṃstutiḥ || 1.12.14 ||
teṣāṃ vakṣyamāṇānāṃ mahāyajñā iti saṃstutiḥ svādhyāyabrāhmaṇe | mahāsattrāṇīti ca saṃstutir bhavati bṛhadāraṇyakādau | saṃstutigrahaṇena saṃstutimātram idaṃ na nāmadheyaṃ dharmātideśārtham iti darśayati | tena mahāyajñeṣu somayāgeṣu ye dharmā uktāḥ “na jyeṣṭhabhrātaram atītya somena yaṣṭavyam” ityādayaḥ, ye ca mahāsattrasya gavāmayanasya dharmāḥ “iṣṭaprathamayajñair yaṣṭavyam” ityādayaḥ, ubhaye ‘pi te vakṣyamāṇeṣu pañcamahāyajñeṣu na bhavanti || 1.12.14 ||
ke punas te | tān āha |
ahar ahar bhūtabalir manuṣyebhyo yathāśakti dānam || 1.12.15 ||
iti dvādaśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
devebhyaḥ svāhākāra ā kāṣṭhāt pitṛbhyaḥ svadhākāra
odapātrāt svādhyāya iti || 1.13.1 ||
vaiśvadeve vakṣyamāṇena baliharaṇaprakāreṇa bhūtebhyo ‘har ahar bhūtabalir deyaḥ | eṣa bhūtayajñaḥ | manuṣyebhyaś ca yathāśakti dānaṃ kartavyam | eṣa manuṣyayajñaḥ | devebhyaś ca svāhākāreṇa pradānam ā kāṣṭhāt, aśanīyābhāve kāṣṭham api tāvad deyaṃ vaiśvadevoktena prakāreṇa | eṣa devayajñaḥ | kecid vaiśvadevāhutibhyaḥ pṛthagbhūtām imām āhutiṃ manyante, “devebhyaḥ svāhā” iti mantram icchanti, “devayajñena yakṣye” iti ca saṃkalpam icchanti | vayaṃ tu na tatheti gṛhya evācocāma [on ĀpGṛ 7.16] | kecid āhuḥ “ā kāṣṭhāt” iti vacanād aśanīyābhāvena bhojanalope ‘pi yathā kathaṃcid vaiśvadevaṃ kartavyam, puruṣasaṃskāratvād iti | apare tv aśanīyasaṃskāra iti vadanto bhojanalope vaiśvadevaṃ na kartavyam iti sthitāḥ | pitṛbhyaḥ svadhākāreṇa tāvad deyam | pātragrahaṇāt saha pātreṇa deyam | eṣa pitṛyajñaḥ | svādhyāyaḥ, “tasya vidhiḥ” [ĀpDh 1.11.22] ity ārabhyokto nityasvādhyāyaḥ | eṣa brahmayajñaḥ | itiśabdaḥ samāptau, ity ete mahāyajñā iti | na cāyam upadeśakramo ‘nuṣṭhāna upayujyate | anuṣṭhānaṃ tu brahmayajño devayajño bhūtayajñaḥ pitṛyajño manuṣyayajña iti || 1.13.1 ||
pūjāṃ prasaṅgād āha |
pūjā varṇajyāyasāṃ kāryā || 1.13.2 ||
varṇato ye jyāyāṃsaḥ praśastatarā bhavanti teṣām avareṇa varṇena pūjā kāryā, adhvany anugamanādikā, utsaveṣu ca gandhamālyādikā || 1.13.2 ||
vṛddhatarāṇāṃ ca || 1.13.3 ||
sajātīyānām api pūjā kāryā | tarapo nirdeśād vidyāvayaḥkarmabhir vṛddhānaṃ grahaṇam | hīnānām apīty eke | tathā ca manuḥ: “śūdro ‘pi daśamīṃ gataḥ” iti [MDh 2.137] || 1.13.3 ||
pūjā kāryety uktam | tadvirodhī harṣo varjya ity āha |
hṛṣṭo darpati dṛpto darmam atikrāmati dharmātikrame khalu
punar narakaḥ || 1.13.4 ||
abhimatalābhādinimittaś cittavikāro harṣaḥ, tadyukto hṛṣṭaḥ | darpati dṛpyati | darpo garvo ‘bhimānaḥ | dṛpto dharmam atikrāmati, pūjyapūjanādikaṃ prati stabdhatvāt | khalupunaḥśabdau vākyālaṃkāre | dharmātikrame khalu punar narako bhavati nirayaṃ pratipadyate | tasmād dharmātikramamūlabhūto harṣo na kartavyaḥ | yady api bhūtadāhīyeṣu doṣeṣu varjanīyeṣu harṣo ‘pi vakṣyate [cf. ĀpDh 1.23.6], tathāpīha harṣasya varjanārtho ‘yam ārambhaḥ, yogāṅgatvād vakṣyamāṇasya || 1.13.4 ||
na samāvṛtte samādeśo vidyate || 1.13.5 ||
samāvṛttaṃ śiṣyaṃ praty ācāryeṇa samādeśo na deyaḥ, idaṃ tvayā kartavyam iti | yathā asamāvṛttadaśāyām ājñā dīyate udakumbham āharetyādi, naivam idānīm | svecchayā karaṇe na pratiṣedhyam || 1.13.5 ||
oṃkāraḥ svargadvāraṃ tasmād brahmādhyeṣyamāṇa etadādi
pratipadyeta || 1.13.6 ||
oṃkāraḥ praṇavaḥ | sa ca svargasya dvāram iva, yathā dvāreṇa gṛhābhyantaraṃ prāpyate tathānena svargaḥ | tasmād brahma vedaṃ svargasādhanam adhyeṣyamāṇa etadādi anāmnātam apy oṃkāram ādau kṛtvā pratipadyeta upakrametādhyetum || 1.13.6 ||
vikathāṃ cānyāṃ kṛtvaivaṃ laukikyā vācā vyāvartate
brahma || 1.13.7 ||
adhyayane ‘nupayuktā kathā vikathā, tāṃ cānyāṃ kṛtvaitadādi pratipadyeta | evaṃ sati brahma vedaḥ laukikyā vācā vyāvartate tayā miśritaṃ na bhavati || 1.13.7 ||
punar apy oṃkāram eva stauti |
yajñeṣu caitadādayaḥ prasavāḥ || 1.13.8 ||
yajñeṣu darśapūrṇamāsādiṣu etadādayaḥ oṃkārādayaḥ prasavā anujñāvākyāni bhavanti | brahmādīnām “oṃ praṇaya,” “oṃ nirvapa,” “oṃ studhvam” iti some || 1.13.8 ||
loke ca bhūtikarmasv etadādīny eva vākyāni syur yathā
puṇyāhaṃ svasty ṛddham iti || 1.13.9 ||
yathā yajñeṣu oṃkārādayaḥ prasavāḥ tathā loke ca bhūtikarmasu pāṇigrahaṇādiṣu etadādīny eva vākyāni syuḥ, tāny udāharati – yatheti | puṇyāhavācane “oṃ karmaṇaḥ puṇyāhaṃ bhavanto bruvantu” iti vācayitvā vadati | “oṃ puṇyāhaṃ karmaṇo ‘stu” iti prativaktāraḥ | tathā “oṃ karmaṇe svasti bhavanto bruvantu” iti vācayitvā | “oṃ karmaṇe svasti” iti pare | “oṃ karmaṇa ṛddhiṃ bhavanto bruvantu” iti vācayitvā | “oṃ karma ṛddhyatām” iti pare | tasmād evaṃ praśasta oṃkāraḥ || 1.13.9 ||
nāsamaye na kṛcchraṃ kurvīta triḥśrāvaṇaṃ triḥsahavacanam
iti parihāpya || 1.13.10 ||
samayaḥ śuśrūṣā, tena vinā kṛcchraṃ duḥkhaṃ duravadhāraṇam apūrvaṃ granthaṃ na kurvīta | kriyāsāmānyavacanaḥ karotiḥ adhyayane ‘dhyāpane ca vartate | samayena vinā śiṣyo ‘pi kṛcchraṃ granthaṃ nādhīyīta, ācāryo ‘pi nādhyāpayet | tathā ca manuḥ |
dharmārthau yatra na syātāṃ śuśrūṣā vāpi tadvidhā |
na tatra vidyā vaptavyā śubhaṃ bījam ivoṣare || iti | [MDh 2.112]
kim aviśeṣeṇa | nety āha – triḥśrāvaṇaṃ triḥsahavacanaṃ parihāpya varjayitvā | triḥśrāvaṇamātre triḥsahavacanamātre cānyatarāpekṣayā kriyamāṇe śuśrūṣā nāpekṣyā | tato ‘dhike sarvatrāpekṣyeti
|| 1.13.10 ||
āvicikitsāyā brahma nigantavyam iti hārītaḥ || 1.13.11 ||
vicikitsā saṃśayaḥ tadabhāvo ‘vicikitsā yāvad utpadyate tāvad brahma nigantavyaṃ niyamapūrvakaṃ gantavyam iti hārīta ācāryo manyate | atra pakṣe triḥśrāvaṇatriḥsahavacanayor api śuśrūṣitavyam | brahmagrahaṇād aṅgeṣu nāyaṃ vidhiḥ || 1.13.11 ||
na bahirvede gatir vidyate || 1.13.12 ||
vedād bahirbhūte kāvyanāṭakādiśravaṇe gatiḥ śuśrūṣā na vidyate, yady api tadupayuktaṃ vedārthajñāne || 1.13.12 ||
samādiṣṭam adhyāpayantaṃ yāvad adhyayanam
upasaṃgṛhṇīyāt || 1.13.13 ||
ya ācāryeṇa samādiṣṭo ‘dhyāpayati taṃ yāvadadhyayanaṃ yāvad asāv adhyāpayati tāvad upasaṃgṛhṇīyāt | “tathā samādiṣṭe ‘dhyāpayati” [ĀpDh 1.7.28] ity atra sūtre ācāryadāravad vṛttir uktā, tatra ca “anyatropasaṃgrahaṇāt” [ĀpDh 1.7.27] iti vartate | ata upasaṃgrahaṇārtho ‘yam ārambhaḥ || 1.13.13 ||
nityam arhantam ity eke || 1.13.14 ||
sa cet samādiṣṭo ‘rhan bhavati vidyāsadācārādinā, tato nityam upasaṃgṛhṇīyāt ity eke manyante | svamataṃ tu yāvadadhyayanam iti || 1.13.14 ||
na gatir vidyate || 1.13.15 ||
yady apy asāv arhan bhavati, tathāpy ācārye yā gatiḥ śuśrūṣā, sā tasmin na kartavyā || 1.13.15 ||
vṛddhānāṃ tu || 1.13.16 ||
tuś cārthe | vṛddhānāṃ cāntevāsināṃ na gatir vidyate pūrvavayasāntevāsinā avaravayā ācāryo na śuśrūṣitavyaḥ | adhyayanād ūrdhvam ity eke | adhyayanakāle ‘pīty anye | kecit – avaravayasāpy antevāsinā na vārdhike gatiḥ kartavyety āhuḥ || 1.13.16 ||
brahmaṇi mitho viniyoge na gatir vidyate || 1.13.17 ||
brahmaṇi vedaviṣaye yadā mitho viniyogaḥ kriyate bahvṛco yājuṣasakāśād yajurvedam adhīte, so ‘pi tasmād ṛgvedam, tadāpi parasparaṃ śuśrūṣā na kartavyā || 1.13.17 ||
atra hetuṃ svayam evāha |
brahma vardhata ity upadiśanti || 1.13.18 ||
dvayor api brahma vardhate, saiva brahmavṛddhiḥ śuśrūṣety upadiśanty ācāryāḥ || 1.13.18 ||
niveśe vṛtte saṃvatsare saṃvatsare dvau dvau māsau
samāhita ācāryakule vased bhūyaḥ śrutam icchann iti
śvetaketuḥ || 1.13.19 ||
bhūyaḥśravaṇam icchan puruṣaḥ niveśe vṛtte dārakarmaṇi vṛtte ‘pi pratisaṃvatsaraṃ dvau dvau māsau samāhito bhūtvā ācāryakule vased iti śvetaketur ācāryo manyate || 1.13.19 ||
atra hetutvena śvetaketor eva śiṣyān prati vacanam |
etena hy ahaṃ yogena bhūyaḥ pūrvasmāt kālāc
chrutam akurvīta || 1.13.20 ||
etenānantaroktena yogena upāyenāhaṃ pūrvasmād brahmacaryakālād bhūyo ‘dhikataraṃ śrutam akurvīta kṛtavān asmi, ato yūyam api tathā kurudhvam iti || 1.13.20 ||
tac chāstrair vipratiṣiddham || 1.13.21 ||
tad idaṃ śvetaketor vacanaṃ śrutyādibhiḥ śāstrair viruddham || 1.13.21 ||
katham ity āha |
niveśe hi vṛtte naiyamikāni śrūyante || 1.13.22 ||
hiśabdo hetau | yasmān niveśe vṛtte naiyamikāni niyamena kartavyāni nityāni karmāṇi śrūyante
|| 1.13.22 ||
iti trayodaśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
kāni punas tāni |
agnihotram atithayaḥ || 1.14.1 ||
agnihotram, atithayo ‘tithipūjā |
yathā mātaram āśritya sarve jīvanti jantavaḥ |
evaṃ gṛhastham āśritya sarve jīvanti bhikṣavaḥ || iti | [VaDh 8.16]
|| 1.14.1 ||
yac cānyad evaṃyuktam || 1.14.2 ||
evaṃvidhaṃ śrāddasaṃdhyopāsanādi | evam etaiḥ karmabhir ahar ahar ākrāntasya na śiraḥkaṇḍūyane ‘py avasaro bhavati | sa kathaṃ dvau dvau māsau gurukule vased iti || 1.14.2 ||
adhyayanārthena yaṃ codayen na cainaṃ
pratyācakṣīta || 1.14.3 ||
yam ācāryaṃ māṇavako ‘dhyayanaprayojanam uddiśya codayet “śiṣyas te ‘haṃ śādhi māṃ tvāṃ prapannām” iti, sa enaṃ māṇavakaṃ naiva pratyācakṣīta | caśabdo ‘vadhāraṇe || 1.14.3 ||
kim aviśeṣeṇa | nety āha |
na cāsmin doṣaṃ paśyet || 1.14.4 ||
caṇ iti nipāto ‘sti “nipātair yadyadihantakuvinnecceccaṇkaccidyatrayuktam” [Pāṇ 8.1.30] iti | sa cedarthe vartate | “indraś ca mṛḍayāti naḥ” [RV 2.41.11] ityādau darśanāt tasyāyaṃ prayogaḥ | na ced asmin māṇavake doṣam anadhyāpyatāhetuṃ paśyed iti || 1.14.4 ||
yadṛcchāyām asaṃvṛttau gatir eva tasmin || 1.14.5 ||
samānam adhīyāneṣu māṇavakeṣu yadi kasyacid yadṛcchayā dṛṣṭahetum antareṇaiva buddhimāndyādinā adhyayanasyāsaṃvṛttiḥ syāt adhīto bhāgo māṇavakāntaravan nāgacchet tadā tasyāṃ yadṛcchāyām asaṃvṛttau tasminn ācārye gatir eva śuśrūṣaiva māṇavakasya śaraṇam | tathā ca manuḥ |
yathā khanan khanitreṇa naro vāry adhigacchati |
tathā gurugatāṃ vidyāṃ śuśrūṣur adhigacchati || iti | [MDh 2.218]
adhikaṃ śuśrūṣito guruḥ sarvātmanā taṃ śikṣayed iti || 1.14.5 ||
mātari pitary ācāryavac chuśrūṣā || 1.14.6 ||
mātṛgrahaṇena pitāmahīprapitāmahyor api grahaṇam, pitṛgrahaṇena pitamahaprapitāmahayoḥ | sarva ete ācāryavac chuśrūṣitavyāḥ || 1.14.6 ||
samāvṛttena sarve gurava upasaṃgrāhyāḥ || 1.14.7 ||
uktāś cānuktāś ca jyeṣṭhabhrātṛmātulādayaḥ sarve guravaḥ samāvṛttena ahar ahar upasaṃgrāhyāḥ
|| 1.14.7 ||
proṣya ca samāgame || 1.14.8 ||
yadi svayaṃ proṣya ca samāgato bhavati, guravo vā proṣya samāgatā bhavanti, tadāpi te upasaṃgrāhyāḥ|| 1.14.8 ||
bhrātṛṣu bhaginīṣu ca yathāpūrvam upasaṃgrahaṇam || 1.14.9 ||
pūrveṇaiva siddhe kramārtham idaṃ vacanam | yathāpūrvaṃ jyeṣṭhakrameṇeti || 1.14.9 ||
nityā ca pūjā yathopadeśam || 1.14.10 ||
“pūjā varṇajyāyasāṃ kāryā | vṛddhatarāṇāṃ ca” [ĀpDh 1.13.2–3] ity upadeśānurodhena yā nityā pūjā sā ca yathāpūrvaṃ vṛddhakrameṇa || 1.14.10 ||
ṛtvikśvaśurapitṛvyamātulān avaravayasaḥ
pratyuttāyābhivadet || 1.14.11 ||
“trivarṣapūrvaḥ śrotriyo ‘bhivādanam arhati” [ĀpDh 1.14.13] iti vakṣyati | tena avaravayasa ṛtvigādayo ‘py abhivādayante, tān abhivādayamānān pratyutthāyābhivadet, nānyeṣv iva yathāsukham āsīnavat | ata utkṛṣṭānāṃ teṣām iyam eva pūjā || 1.14.11 ||
tūṣṇīṃ vopasaṃgṛhṇīyāt || 1.14.12 ||
atha vā pratyutthāya svayam api tān tūṣṇīm upasaṃghṛhṇīyāt | vidyācāritrādyapekṣo vikalpaḥ
|| 1.14.12 ||
athābhivādyā ucyante |
daśavarṣaṃ paurasakhyaṃ pañcavarṣaṃ tu cāraṇam |
trivarṣapūrvaḥ śrotriyaḥ abhivādanam arhati || 1.14.13 ||
pure bhavaṃ pauraṃ pauraṃ ca tatsakhyaṃ ca paurasakhyaṃ sevādinibandhanaṃ bāndhavam, tad abhivādanasya nimittam | kīdṛśam | daśavarṣāntarālam | daśavarṣādhikaḥ pauraḥ sakhāśrotriyo ‘py abhivādya iti vivakṣitam | pañcavarṣaṃ tu cāraṇaṃ sakhyam ity upasamastam apekṣate | cāraṇaśabdaḥ śākhādhyāyiṣu rūḍhaḥ | teṣāṃ sakhyaṃ pañcavarṣam abhivādanasya nimittam | śrotriyaṃ vakṣyati [ĀpDh 2.6.4] | sa trivarṣapūrvatāmātreṇābhivādanam arhati, na pūrvasaṃstavam apekṣate || 1.14.13 ||
jñāyamāne vayoviśeṣe vṛddhatarāyābhivādyam || 1.14.14 ||
kramārtham idaṃ vacanam | vayoviśeṣe jñāyamāne pūrvaṃ vṛddhatarāyābhivādyaṃ abhivādanaṃ kartavyam, paścād vṛddhāyeti || 1.14.14 ||
viṣamagatāyāgurave nābhivādyam || 1.14.15 ||
uccaiḥsthāne nīcaiḥsthāne vā sthito viṣamagataḥ | tasmai guruvyatiriktāya nādbhivādyam | gurave tv abhivādyam eva, darśane sati tūṣṇīm avasthānasyāyuktatvāt || 1.14.15 ||
anvāruhya vābhivādayīta || 1.14.16 ||
idam aguruviṣayam | yatrāsāv abhivādanīyaḥ sthitas tatrānvāruhya vābhivādayīta | anvavaruhety etad api draṣṭavyam, nyāyasya tulyatvāt | gurau tu dṛṣṭamātra evābhivādanam ity uktam
|| 1.14.16 ||
sarvatra tu pratyutthāyābhivādanam || 1.14.17 ||
sarvatra gurāv agurau ca pratyutthāyaivābhivādanaṃ kartavyam || 1.14.17 ||
uttare dve sūtre nigadasiddhe –
aprayatena nābhivādyam || 1.14.18 ||
tathāprayatāya || 1.14.19 |||
aprayataś ca na pratyabhivadet || 1.14.20 ||
yady ajñānād aprayatāya kaścid abhivādayet, tathāpi so ‘prayato na pratyabhivādet
|| 1.14.20 ||
pativayasaḥ striyaḥ || 1.14.21 ||
patyur yad vayas tad eva strīṇāṃ vayaḥ | tena tadanurodhena jyeṣṭhabhāryādiṣv abhivādanam
|| 1.14.21 ||
na sopānadveṣṭitaśirā avahitapāṇir vābhivādayīta || 1.14.22 ||
avahitapāṇiḥ samitkuśādihastaḥ, dātrādihasto vā | anyat prasiddham || 1.14.22 ||
sarvanāmnā striyo rājanyavaiśyau ca na nāmnā || 1.14.23 ||
striyaḥ sarvanāmnaivābhivādayīta abhivādye ‘ham iti, na nāmnā asādhāraṇena devadatto ‘ham abhivādaya iti | evaṃ rājanyavaiśyau ca || 1.14.23 ||
mātaram ācāryadāraṃ cety eke || 1.14.24 ||
mātaram ācāryadāraṃ caite api dve sarvanāmnaivābhivādayīta, na nāmnety eke manyante | svamataṃ tu nāmnaiveti || 1.14.24 |
vayoviśeṣeṇābhivādanaṃ hīnavarṇe nāstīty āha |
daśavarṣaś ca brāhmaṇaḥ śatavarṣaś ca kṣatriyaḥ |
pitāputrau sma tau viddhi tayos tu brāḥmaṇaḥ pitā || 1.14.25 ||
śiṣyaṃ praty ācāryasyāyam upadeśaḥ | smaśabdaḥ ślokapūraṇe nipātaḥ | brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriya ity upalakṣaṇam uttamādhamavarṇānām | viddhi jānīhi | spaṣṭam anyat || 1.14.25 ||
kuśalam avaravayasaṃ vayasyaṃ vā pṛcchet || 1.14.26 ||
brāhmaṇaviṣayam idam, kṣatriyādiṣu viśeṣasya vakṣyamāṇatvāt | vayasā tulyo vayasyaḥ | avaravayasaṃ vayasyaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ pathādiṣu saṃgataṃ kuśalaṃ pṛcchet: api kuśalam iti
|| 1.14.26 ||
anāmayaṃ kṣatriyam || 1.14.27 ||
anāmayaḥ arogaḥ, tām pṛcchet: apy anāmayo bhavataḥ iti || 1.14.27 ||
anaṣṭaṃ vaiśyam || 1.14.28 ||
apy anaṣṭapaśudhano ‘si iti || 1.14.28 ||
ārogyaṃ śūdram || 1.14.29 ||
śūdram ārogyam: apy arogo bhavān iti || 1.14.29 ||
nāsaṃbhāṣya śrotriyaṃ vyativrajet || 1.14.30 ||
śrotriyaṃ pathi saṃgatam asaṃbhāṣya na vyativrajet || 1.14.30 ||
araṇye ca striyam || 1.14.31 ||
sabhayasya deśasyopalakṣaṇam araṇyagrahaṇam | tatra striyam ekākinīṃ dṛṣṭvāsaṃbhāṣya na vyativrajet | saṃbhāṣaṇaṃ ca mātṛvad bhaginīvac ca: bhagini kiṃ te karavāṇi, na bhetavyam
iti || 1.14.31 ||
iti caturdaśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
iti śrīharadattamiśraviracitāyām āpastambadharmasūtravyākhyāyām
ujjvalāyāṃ caturthaḥ paṭalaḥ
sarveṣām eva karmaṇāṃ śeṣabhūtam ācamanaṃ vidhāsyaṃs tadupayogino vidhīn āha |
upāsane gurūṇāṃ vṛddhānām atithīnāṃ home japyakarmaṇi bhojana ācamane svādhyāye ca yajñopavīti syāt || 1.15.1 ||
gurūṇām ācāryādīnām anyeṣāṃ ca vṛddhānāṃ pūjyānām atithīnāṃ copāsane yadā tān upāste tadā, home sāṅge pitryād anyatra, japyakarmaṇi japakriyāyām, bhojanācamanayoś ca, svādhyāye ‘dhyayane ca, yajñopavīti syād bhavet | vāsovinyāsaviśeṣo yajñopavītam: “dakṣiṇaṃ bāhum uddharateva dhatte savyam iti yajñopavītam” iti brāhmaṇam [TaitĀr 2.1] | vāsaso ‘saṃbhave ‘nukalpaṃ vakṣyati: “api vā sūtram evopavītārthe” iti [ĀpDh 2.4.22] | manur apy āha |
kārpāsam upavītaṃ syād viprasyordhvavṛtaṃ trivṛt | iti | [MDh 2.44]
uddhṛte dakṣiṇe pāṇāv upavīty ucyate dvijaḥ | [MDh 2.63]
iti ca | eteṣu karmasu yajñopavītavidhānāt kālāntare nāvaśyaṃbhāvaḥ || 1.15.1 ||
bhūmigatāsv apsv ācamya prayato bhavati || 1.15.2 ||
āpaḥ śuddhā bhūmigatā vaitṛṣṇyaṃ yāsu gor bhavet |
avyāptāś ced amedhyena gandhavarṇarasānvitāḥ || [MDh 5.128]
śuci gotṛptikṛt toyaṃ prakṛtisthaṃ mahīgatam | [YDh 1.192]
iti yājñavalkyaḥ |
ajā gāvo mahiṣyaś ca brāhmaṇī ca prasūtikā |
daśarātreṇa śudhyanti bhūmiṣṭhaṃ ca navodakam ||
iti manuḥ |
śrāvaṇe māsi saṃprāpte sarvā nadyo rajasvalāḥ |
iti smṛtyantare | evaṃbhūtadoṣarahitāsv apsu ācamya prayato bhavati | prāyatyārtham ācamanaṃ bhūmigatāsv apsu kartavyam iti || 1.15.2 ||
yaṃ vā prayata ācamayet || 1.15.3 ||
yaṃ vā prayato ‘nya ācamayet so ‘pi prayato bhavati | sarvathā svayaṃ vāmahastāvarjitābhir adbhir ācamanaṃ na bhavati | etena śāstrāntaroktaṃ kamaṇḍuludhāraṇam apy ācāryasya nābhimatam iti lakṣyate | alābupātreṇa nālikerapātreṇa vā svayam ācamanam ācaranti śiṣṭāḥ
|| 1.15.3 ||
na varṣadhārāsv ācāmet || 1.15.4 ||
pūrvoktena prakāreṇa prāyatyārthasyācamanasya varṣadhārāsu prasaṅgābhāvāt pipāsitasya pānapratiṣedhāro ‘yam iti kecit | apara āha – asmād eva pratiṣedhāc chikyādisthasya karakāder yā dhārā tatra prāyatyārtham ācamanaṃ na bhavaty eva || 1.15.4 ||
tathā pradarodake || 1.15.5 ||
bhūmeḥ svayaṃ dīrṇaḥ pradeśaḥ pradaraḥ, tatra yad udakaṃ tasmin bhūmigate ‘pi nācāmet
|| 1.15.5 ||
taptābhiś cākāraṇāt || 1.15.6 ||
taptābhiś cādbhiś ca nācāmed akāraṇāt | jvarādau kāraṇe sati na doṣaḥ | taptābhir iti vacanāt śṛtaśītādhibhir adoṣaḥ | tathā coṣṇānām eva pratiṣedhaḥ smṛtiṣu prāyaśo bhavati || 1.15.6 ||
riktapāṇir vayasa udyamyāpa upaspṛśet || 1.15.7 ||
vaya iti pakṣināma | yo riktapāṇiḥ san vayase pakṣiṇa udyamya tasya protsāraṇāya pāṇim udyacchet, sa tat kṛtvāpa upaspṛśet tenaiva pāṇinā | riktapāṇir iti vacanāt kāṣṭhaloṣṭhasahitasya pāṇer udyamane na doṣaḥ | kecid upasparśanam ācamanam āhuḥ || 1.15.7 ||
śaktiviṣaye na muhūrtam apy aprayataḥ syāt || 1.15.8 ||
śaktau satyāṃ muhūrtamātram apy aprayato na syāt | ācamanayogyaṃ jalaṃ dṛṣṭvaiva mūtrapurīṣādikaṃ kuryāt, yadi tāvantaṃ kālaṃ vegaṃ dhārayituṃ śaknuyāt || 1.15.8 ||
nagno vā || 1.15.9 ||
muhūrtam ap na syād iti saṃbadhyate, śaktiviṣaya iti ca | vraṇādinā kaupīnācchādanāśaktau na doṣaḥ || 1.15.9 ||
nāpsu sataḥ prayamaṇaṃ vidyate || 1.15.10 ||
yena prayato bhavati tat prayamaṇam ācamanam | karaṇe lyuṭ | tad apsu sato vartamānasya na bhavati | jalamadhye āsīno ‘pi nācāmet || 1.15.10 ||
uttīrya tv ācāmet || 1.15.11 ||
tīre uttīrya tv ācāmen na jale ity ayam artho na vidheyaḥ, pūrveṇa gatatvāt | tasmād ayam arthaḥ – yadā nadīm uttarati nāvā prakārāntareṇa vā, tadā tām uttīrya tīrāntaraṃ gataḥ prayato ‘py ācāmet, nadyāder uttaraṇam apy ācamanasya nimittam iti | tuśabdo ‘pyarthe || 1.15.11 ||
nāprokṣitam indhanam agnāv ādadhyāt || 1.15.12 ||
śraute smārte laukike vāgnau aprokṣitam indhanaṃ nādadhyāt na prakṣipet | kecil laukike necchanti || 1.15.12 ||
mūḍhasvastare cāsaṃspṛśann anyān aprayatān prayato
manyeta || 1.15.13 ||
“patitacaṇḍālasūtikodakyāśavaspṛṣṭitatspṛṣṭyupasparśane sacelodakopasparśanāc chudyet” [GDh 14.30] iti gautamaḥ | tasmin viṣaya idam ucyate, śayanatayāsanatayā vā suṣṭv āstīrṇaḥ palālādisaṅghātaḥ svastaraḥ | pṛṣodarādiṣu darśanād rūpasiddhiḥ | yatnātiślakṣṇatayā palālāder mūlāgravibhāgo na jñāyate sa mūḍhaḥ | mūḍhaś cāsau svastaraś ca mūḍhasvastaraḥ | tasmin patitādiṣv aprayateṣv āsīneṣu yadi kaścit prayata upaviśen na ca tān saṃspṛśet tadā sa prayato manyeta, yathā prayata ātmānaṃ manyate prayato ‘smīti, tathaiva manyeta | naivaṃvidhe viṣaye tatspṛṣṭinyāyaḥ pravartata iti || 1.15.13 ||
tathā tṛṇakāṣṭheṣu nikhāteṣu || 1.15.14 ||
tṛṇakāṣṭheṣu bhūmau nikhāteṣu tatspṛṣṭinyāyo na bhavati || 1.15.14 ||
prokṣya vāsa upayojayet || 1.15.15 ||
śuddham api vāsaḥ prokṣyaivopayojayed vasīta | apara āha – aśuddhasyāpi vāsasaḥ prokṣaṇam eva śuddhihetur iti || 1.15.15 ||
śunopahataḥ sacelo ‘vagāheta || 1.15.16 ||
śunā upahataḥ spṛṣṭaḥ | yady api celaṃ na śunā spṛṣṭam tathāpi sacelo ‘vagāheta bhūmigatāsv apsu snāyān noddhṛtābhiḥ | daṣṭasya tu smṛtyantare prāyaścittam | tatra vasiṣṭhaḥ:
brāhmaṇas tu śunā daṣṭo nadīṃ gatvā samudragām |
prāṇāyāmaśataṃ kṛtvā ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati || [VaDh 23.31]
aṅgirāḥ |
brahmacārī śunā daṣṭas trirātreṇaiva śudhyati |
gṛhasthas tu dvirātreṇa ekāhenāgnihotravān ||
nābher ūrdhvaṃ tu daṣṭasya tad eva dviguṇaṃ bhavet |
tad eva triguṇaṃ vaktre mūrdhni cet syāc caturguṇam ||
kṣatraviṭchūdrayonis tu snānenaiva śucir bhavet |
dviguṇaṃ tu vanasthasya tathā pravrājitasya ca ||
brāhmaṇī tu śunā daṣṭā some dṛṣṭiṃ nipātayet |
yadā na dṛśyate somaḥ prāyaścittaṃ tadā katham ||
yāṃ diśaṃ tu gataḥ somas tāṃ diśaṃ tv avalokayet |
somamārgeṇa sā pūtā pañcagavyena śudhyati || 1.15.16 ||
prakṣālya vā taṃ deśam agninā saṃspṛśya punaḥ prakṣālya
pādau cācamya prayato bhavati || 1.15.17 ||
śunā vā spṛṣṭaṃ deśaṃ prakṣālya, agninā ca saṃspṛśya, punaḥ prakṣālya, pādau ca prakṣālya, paścād ācamya prayato bhavati | vyavasthitavikalpo ‘yam,
ūrdhvaṃ nābheḥ karau muktvā yad aṅgam upahanyate |
tatra snānavidhiḥ prokto hy adhaḥ prakṣālanaṃ smṛtam ||
iti mānave darśanāt || 1.15.17 ||
agniṃ nāprayata āsīdet || 1.15.18 ||
aprayataḥ sann agniṃ nāsīdet, agner āsanno na bhavet, yāvati deśa ūṣmopalambhaḥ | atrāpy aśaktau na doṣaḥ || 1.15.18 ||
iṣumātrād ity eke || 1.15.19 ||
iṣumātrād arvāṅ nāsīdet agner āsanno na bhavet ūṣmopalambho bhavatu, mā vā bhūd ity eke manyante || 1.15.19 ||
na cainam upadhamet || 1.15.20 ||
aprayata ity eva | enam agnim aprayato nopadhamet | prayatasya na doṣaḥ,
mukhenopadhamed agniṃ mukhād agnir ajāyata |
iti smṛtyantare darśanāt | “nāgniṃ mukhenopadhamet” [MDh 4.53ab] iti mānave darśanād ubhayor vikalpaḥ | apara āha – vājasaneyike śrautaprakaraṇe “mukhād agnir ajāyata tasmān mukhenopasamindhyāt” iti darśanāc chrauteṣu mukhenaivopadhamanam, anyatra smārte pratiṣedha iti | anye tu – vaiṇavenāyasena vā suṣireṇopasamindhanam icchanti | evaṃ hi mukhavyāpārasyānvayāc chrutir apy anugṛhītā bhavati, āsyabindūnāṃ patanaśaṅkābhāvāt pratiṣedhaḥ smṛtir apīti || 1.15.20 ||
khaṭvāyāṃ nopadadhyāt || 1.15.21 ||
khaṭvāyāṃ khaṭvāyā adhaḥ agniṃ nopadadhyāt | atrāpy aśaktau na doṣaḥ || 1.15.21 ||
prabhūtaidhodake grāme yatrātmādhīnaṃ prayamaṇaṃ tatra
vāso dhārmyo brāhmaṇasya || 1.15.22 ||
prabhūtam edhodakaṃ yasmin grāme tatra brāhmaṇasya vāso dhārmyaḥ dharmyaḥ | tatrāpi na sarvatra | kiṃ tarhi | yatrātmādhīnaṃ prayamaṇaṃ prāyatyam, mūtrapurīṣaprakṣālanādīni yatrātmādhīnāni tatraiva | yatra tu kūpeṣv evodakaṃ tatra bahukūpe ‘pi na vastavyam | brāhmaṇagrahaṇād varṇāntarasyāniyamaḥ | grāmagrahaṇād evaṃbhūteṣu ghoṣādiṣv api na vastavyam || 1.15.22 ||
mūtraṃ kṛtvā purīṣaṃ vā mūtrapurīṣalepān annalepān ucchiṣṭalepān retasaś ca ye lepās tān prakṣālya pādau cācamya prayato bhavati || 1.15.23 ||
mūtraṃ purīṣaṃ vā kṛtvā utsṛjya, tayor mūtrapurīṣayor ye lepās tasmin deśe sthitāḥ pradeśāntare vā patitās tāṃt sarvān annalepān ucchiṣṭān apy ucchiṣṭalepān annalepān api, tathā retasaś ca ye lepāḥ svapnādau maithune ca, tāṃt sarvān adbhir mṛdā ca prakṣālya, pādau ca lepavarjitāv api prakṣālya, paścād ācamya prayato bhavati | atra mṛtpramāṇasya saṃkhyāyāś cānuktatvāt yāvatā gandhalepakṣayo bhavati tāvad eva vivakṣitam | tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
gandhalepakṣayakaraṃ śaucaṃ kuryād atandritaḥ | iti | [YDh 1.17]
devalas tu vyaktam evāha |
yāvat sa śuddhiṃ manyeta tāvac chaucaṃ samācaret |
pramāṇaṃ śaucasaṃkhyāyā na śiṣṭair upadiśyate || iti |
atra paiṭhīnasiḥ |
mūtroccāre kṛte śaucaṃ na syād antarjalāśaye |
anyatroddhṛtya kuryāt tu sarvadaiva samāhitaḥ || iti || 1.15.23 ||
iti pañcadaśaḥ khaṇdaḥ
tiṣṭhan nācāmet prahvo vā || 1.16.1 ||
tiṣṭhan prahvo vā nācāmet | nāyaṃ pratiṣedhaḥ śakyo vaktum | katham | “āsīnas trir ācāmet” [ĀpDh 1.16.2] iti vakṣyati | tataś ca yathā śayānasyācamanaṃ na bhavet tathā tiṣṭhataḥ prahvasya vā na bhavati | evaṃ tarhi nāyaṃ śaucārthasyācamanasya pratiṣedhaḥ | kiṃ tarhi | pānīyapānasya pratiṣedhaḥ | tathā ca gautamaḥ: “nāñjalinā pibet | na tiṣṭhan” iti [GDh 9.9–10] |
apara āha – asmād eva pratiṣedhāt kvacit tiṣṭhataḥ prahvasya cācamanam abhyanujñātaṃ bhavati; bhūmigatāsv apsu tīrasyāyogyatve ūrudadhne nābhidadhne vā jale sthitasyācamanaṃ bhavati | gautamīye ‘pi: “na tiṣṭhann uddhṛtodakenācāmet” [GDh 9.11] iti sūtracchedād uddhṛttodakenaiva tiṣṭhataḥ pratiṣedha iti || 1.16.1 ||
athācamanavidhiḥ |
āsīnas trir ācāmed dhṛdayaṃgamābhir adbhiḥ || 1.16.2 ||
adbhiḥ, dvitīyārthe tṛtīyā | atrānuktaṃ smṛtyantaravacanād upaskriyate | āsīnaḥ śucau deśe nāsane, bhojanānte tv āsane, dakṣiṇaṃ bāhum ūrvantare kṛtvā, prāṅmukha upaviṣṭa udaṅmukho vā hṛdayaṃgamā āpaḥ karatalasthitāsu yāvatīṣu māṣo nimajjati tāvatīḥ phenabudbudarahitā vīkṣitās trir ācāmet pibed brāhmaṇaḥ, kṣatriyaḥ kaṇṭhagāḥ, vaiśyas tālugāḥ, śūdro jihvāspṛṣṭaḥ sakṛt || 1.16.2 ||
trir oṣṭhau parimṛjet || 1.16.3 ||
parimṛjyāt || 1.16.3 ||
dvir ity eke || 1.16.4 ||
tulyavikalpaḥ || 1.16.4 ||
sakṛd upaspṛśet || 1.16.5 ||
madhyamābhis tisṛbhir aṅgulībhir evoṣṭhau || 1.16.5 ||
dvir ity eke || 1.16.6 ||
tulyavikalpaḥ || 1.16.6 ||
dakṣiṇena pāṇinā savyaṃ prokṣya pādau śiraś cendriyāṇy
upaspṛśec cakṣuṣī nāsike śrotre ca || 1.16.7 ||
dakṣiṇena pāṇinā savyaṃ pāṇiṃ prokṣya, tathā pādau śiraś ca, indriyāṇy upaspṛśed aṅguḷibhiḥ | sarveṣām indriyāṇāṃ prasaṅge parisaṃcaṣṭe – cakṣuṣī nāsike śrotre ceti | indriyāṇīti vacanaṃ svarūpakathanamātram | tatrāṅguṣṭhānāmikābhyāṃ cakṣuṣī | kecid yugapat, kecit pṛthak pṛthak | aṅguṣṭhapradeśinībhyāṃ nāsike | aṅguṣṭhakaniṣṭhikābhyāṃ śrotre | atra sahabhāvasyāśakyatvāt pṛthagbhāvasya niścitatvāt pūrvatrāpi pṛthag eveti yuktam || 1.16.7 ||
athāpa upaspṛśet || 1.16.8 ||
indriyasparśanānantaraṃ hastau prakṣālayet || 1.16.8 ||
bhokṣyamāṇas tu prayato ‘pi dvir ācāmed dviḥ parimṛjet
sakṛd upaspṛśet || 1.16.9 ||
bhojanaṃ kariṣyan prayato ‘pi dvir ācamanaṃ kuryāt | atra viśeṣaḥ – dviḥ parimṛjet, na vikalpena triḥ | sakṛd upaspṛśet, na vikalpena dviḥ | prayato ‘pīti vacanād aprāyatye sarvatra dvirācamanam ācāryasyābhipretam | atra smṛtyantaram |
kṣutvā suptvā ca bhuktvā ca ṣṭhīvitvoktvānṛtaṃ vacaḥ | [MDh 5.145ab]
ācāntaḥ punar ācāmed vāso ‘pi paridhāya ca || [YDh 1.196] iti || 1.16.9 ||
śyāvāntaparyantāv oṣṭhāv upaspṛśyācāmet || 1.16.10 ||
dantamūlāt prabhṛty oṣṭhau | tatrālomakaḥ pradeśaḥ śyāvaḥ | tasyāntaḥ salomakaḥ | tatparyantāv oṣṭhāv upaspṛśyācāmet | oṣṭhayor alomakapradeśam aṅguḷyā kāṣṭhādinā vopaspṛśyācāmed iti
|| 1.16.10 ||
na śmaśrubhir ucchiṣṭo bhavaty antarāsye sadbhir yāvan na
hastenopaspṛśati || 1.16.11 ||
śmaśrūṇi yadāsyāntar bhavanti tadā tair antarāsye sadbhir ucchiṣṭo na bhavati, yāvan na hastenopaspṛśati | upasparśane tūcchiṣṭo bhavati, tataś cācāmed iti | asmād eva pratiṣedhād jñāyate – yat kiṃcid api dravyam antarāsye sad ucchiṣṭatāyāṃ nimittam iti || 1.16.11 ||
ya āsyād bindavaḥ patanta upalabhyante teṣv ācamanaṃ
vihitam || 1.16.12 ||
bhāṣamāṇasyāsyāt patanto ye lālābindavaḥ upalabhyante cakṣuṣā sparśanena vā ye upalabdhuṃ yogyās teṣv ācamanaṃ vihitam | vedoccāraṇe tu gautamaḥ: “mantrabrāhmaṇam uccārayato ye bindavaḥ śarīra upalabhyante na teṣv ācamanam” iti [GDh ?] || 1.16.12 ||
ye bhūmau na teṣv ācāmed ity eke || 1.16.13 ||
ye bhūmau bindavaḥ patanti na śarīre teṣu nācāmed ity eke manyante [cf. MDh 5.142; YDh 1.195]; ViDh 23.54; BDh 1.10.34 | svamataṃ tu teṣv apy ācāmed iti || 1.16.13 ||
svapne kṣavathau śiṅghāṇikāśrvālambhe lohitasya keśānām agner gavāṃ brāhmaṇasya striyāś cālambhe mahāpathaṃ ca gatvāmedhyaṃ copaspṛśyāprayataṃ ca manuṣyaṃ nīvīṃ ca paridhāyāpa upaspṛśet || 1.16.14 ||
svapnaḥ svāpaḥ, kṣavathuḥ kṣutam, tayoḥ kṛtayoḥ | śiṅghāṇikā nāsikāmalam, aśru netrajalam, tayor ālambhe sparśe | lohitasya rudhirasya, keśānāṃ śirogatānāṃ bhūmigatānāṃ ca, agnyādīnāṃ caturṇām ālambhe | mahāpathaṃ ca gatvā | amedhyaṃ ca govyatiriktānāṃ mūtrapurīṣe tāmbūlaniṣekādi copaspṛśya, aprayataṃ ca manuṣyam upaspṛśya, nīvī prasiddhā, tadyogād adhovāso lakṣyate, tac ca paridhāyāpa upaspṛśet | keṣucit snānam, keṣicit ācamanam, keṣucit sparśanamātram, yāvatā prayato manyate || 1.16.14 ||
ārdraṃ vā śakṛd oṣadhīr bhūmiṃ vā || 1.16.15 ||
upaspṛśed ity eva | triṣv ārdaśabdaḥ saṃbadhyate liṅgavacanādipariṇāmena, ārdraṃ vā śakṛt, oṣadhīr vā ārdrāḥ, ārdrāṃ bhūmiṃ vā | pūrvokteṣv eva sarveṣu vaikalpikam idam || 1.16.15 ||
evam ācamanaṃ sanimittam uktam |
athābhakṣyādhikāraḥ |
hiṃsārthenāsinā māṃsaṃ chinnam abhojyam || 1.16.16 ||
asigrahaṇaṃ kṣurāder apy upalakṣaṇam | yan māṃsaṃ pākakāle hiṃsārthenāsinā chinnaṃ tad abhojyam || 1.16.16 ||
dadbhir apūpasya nāpacchindyāt || 1.16.17 ||
apūpagrahaṇaṃ mūlaphalāder apy upalakṣaṇam | dvitīyārthe ṣaṣṭhī | dantair apūpaṃ nāpachindyāt, kiṃ tu hastādibhir apacchidya bhakṣayet || 1.16.17 ||
yasya kule mriyeta na tatrānirdaśe bhoktavyam || 1.16.18 ||
yasya kule kaścin mriyetāsapiṇḍatāyāṃ satyāṃ tatrānirgate daśāhe na bhoktavyam | anirdaśa ity āśaucakālopalakṣaṇam | tena kṣatriyādiṣv adhikam, pakṣiṇyādiṣu nyūnam || 1.16.18 ||
tathānutthitāyāṃ sūtikāyām || 1.16.19 ||
sūtakā sūtikā, tasyām anutthitāyām | utthānaṃ nāma sūtikāgāre niveśitānām udakumbhādīnām apanayanam | tac ca daśame ‘hani bhavati “daśamyām utthitāyām” [ĀpGṛ 15.8] ity gṛhye uktatvāt | atrāpy āśaucakālopalakṣaṇatvād yāvad āśaucaṃ tāvad abhojanam | atra aṅgirāḥ |
brahmakṣatraviśāṃ bhuktvā na doṣas tv agnihotriṇām |
sūtake śāva āśauce tv asthisaṃcayanāt param || iti || 1.16.19 ||
antaḥśave ca || 1.16.20 ||
yāvad grāmān na hriyate śavas tāvat tatra na bhoktavyam | ācāras tu dhanuḥśatād arvāk | tatrāpi pradīpam āropya, udakumbhaṃ copanidhāya bhuñjate, yadi samānavaṃśaṃ gṛhaṃ na bhavati
|| 1.16.20 ||
aprayatopahatam annam aprayataṃ na tv abhojyam || 1.16.21 ||
aprayatenāśucinopahataṃ spṛṣṭam aprayataṃ bhavati | kiṃ tv aśuddham apy abhojyaṃ na bhavati | kaḥ punar aprayatasyābhojyasya ca viśeṣaḥ | ucyate – aprayatam annam agnāv adhiśritam adbhiḥ prokṣitaṃ bhasmanā mṛdā vā saṃspṛṣṭaṃ vācā ca praśastaṃ prayataṃ bhavati bhojyaṃ ca | abhojyaṃ tu laśunādi na kathaṃcid apīti || 1.16.21 ||
aprayatena tu śūdreṇopahṛtam abhojyam || 1.16.22 ||
aprayatena tu śūdreṇopahatam ānītam annam abhojyam, spṛṣṭam aspṛṣṭaṃ ca | spṛṣṭam evety anye || 1.16.22 ||
yasmiṃś cānne keśaḥ syāt || 1.16.23 ||
tad apy abhojyam | etac ca pākadaśāyām eva patitena keśena saha yat pakvam annaṃ tadviṣayam | paścāt keśasaṃsarge tu ghṛtaprakṣepādinā saṃskṛtasya bhojyatvaṃ smṛtyantaroktam || 1.16.23 ||
anyad vāmedhyam || 1.16.24 ||
anyad vāmedhyaṃ nakhādi yasminn anne syāt tad apy abhojyam | idam api pūrvavat | atra bodhāyanaḥ: “keśakīṭanakharomākhupurīṣāṇi dṛṣṭvā tāvan mātram annam uddhṛtya śeṣaṃ bhojyam” iti [BDh 2.12.6] | vasiṣṭhas tu: “kāmaṃ tu keśakīṭādīn utsṛjyādbhiḥ prokṣya bhasmāvakīrya vācā praśatam upayuñjīta” iti [VaDh 14.23] || 1.16.24 ||
amedhyair avamṛṣṭam || 1.16.25 ||
amedhyaiḥ kalañjapalāṇḍvādibhir avamṛṣṭaṃ spṛṣṭam abhojyam || 1.16.25 ||
kīṭo vāmedhyasevī || 1.16.26 ||
“yasmiṃś cānne keśaḥ syāt” [ĀpDh 1.16.23] iti vyavahitam api saṃbadhyate | amedhyasevī kīṭaḥ pūtyaṇḍākhyaḥ || 1.16.26 ||
mūṣikalāṅgaṃ vā || 1.16.27 ||
pūrvavat saṃbandhaḥ | mūṣikalā mūṣikapurīṣam | aṅgaṃ vā | samastam api mūṣikagrahaṇaṃ saṃbadhyate | yasminn anne mūṣikasyāṅgaṃ pucchapādādi bhavati tad apy abhojyam || 1.16.27 ||
padā vopahatam || 1.16.28 ||
prayatenāpi yat padā spṛṣṭaṃ tad apy abhojyam || 1.16.28 ||
sicā vā || 1.16.29 ||
sig vastradaśā | parihitasya vāsasaḥ sicā yat spṛṣṭaṃ tad apy abhojyam || 1.16.29 ||
śunā vāpapātreṇa vā dṛṣṭam || 1.16.30 ||
dṛṣṭam iti pratyekam abhisaṃbadhyate | śunā vā yad dṛṣṭam apapātreṇa vā yad dṛṣṭaṃ tad apy abhojyam | patitacaṇḍālasūtikodakyādayo ‘papātrāḥ, apagatāḥ pātrebhyaḥ | na hi te pātre bhoktuṃ labhante || 1.16.30 ||
sicā vopahṛtam || 1.16.31 ||
aparihitasya śuddhasyāpi vāsasaḥ sicā yad upahṛtaṃ tad apy abhojyam || 1.16.31 ||
dāsyā vā naktam āhṛtam || 1.16.32 ||
dāsyā rātrāv āhṛtam abhojyam | strīliṅganirdeśād dāsenāhṛte na doṣaḥ | anye liṅgam avivakṣitaṃ manyante | naktam iti vacanād divā na doṣaḥ || 1.16.32 ||
iti ṣoḍaśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
yatra śūdra upaspṛśet || 1.17.1 ||
yadā bhojanadaśāyāṃ śūdra upaspṛśet tadāpi na bhuñjīta | bhuñjānagrahaṇād anyadā śūdropasparśane nāprāyatyam iti kecit | anye tu – sadā bhavaty evāprāyatyam, bhojanadaśāyām ādhikyapratipādanāya niṣedha iti || 1.17.1 ||
anarhadbhir vā samānapaṅktau || 1.17.2 ||
sarvatra vāśabdaḥ samuccaye | abhijanavidyāvṛttarahitā anarhantaḥ | taiḥ saha samānāyāṃ paṅktau na bhuñjīta || 1.17.2 ||
bhuñjāneṣu vā yatrānūtthāyocchiṣṭaṃ prayacched
ācāmed vā || 1.17.3 ||
samānapaṅktāv ity anuvartate | samānapaṅktau bahuṣu bhuñjāneṣu yady eko ‘nūtthāya bhojanād viramya ucchiṣṭaṃ śiṣyādibhyaḥ prayacchet, ācāmed vā, tasyāṃ paṅktāv itareṣāṃ ca na bhoktavyam | ato bahuṣu bhuñjāneṣu eko madhye na viramet | bhojanakaṇṭaka iti tam ācakṣate
|| 1.17.3 ||
kutsayitvā vā yatrānnaṃ dadyuḥ || 1.17.4 ||
mūrkha, vaidhaveya, viṣaṃ bhuṅkṣva, ity evaṃ kutsayitvā yatrānnaṃ dadyuḥ tad apy abhojyam
|| 1.17.4 ||
manuṣyair avaghrātam anyair vāmedhyaiḥ || 1.17.5 ||
manuṣyair anyair vā mārjārādibhir amedhyair avaghrātam annam abhojyam | avety upasargaprayogād dūrasthair gandhasyāghrāṇe na doṣaḥ || 1.17.5 ||
na nāvi bhuñjīta || 1.17.6 ||
nāvy āsīno na bhuñjīta śuddhe ‘pi pātre || 1.17.6 ||
tathā prāsāde || 1.17.7 ||
prāsādo dārumayo mañcaḥ, tatrāpi na bhuñjīta || 1.17.7 ||
kṛtabhūmau tu bhuñjīta || 1.17.8 ||
bhūmāv api bhuñjānaḥ kṛtāyāṃ gomayādinā saṃskṛtāyāṃ bhuñjīta | apara āha – prāsādo ‘pi yadā kṛtabhūmir bhavati, na kevalaṃ dārumayaḥ, tadā tatra bhuñjītaiveti || 1.17.8 ||
anāprīte mṛnmaye bhoktavyam || 1.17.9 ||
yadi mṛnmaye bhuñjīta, tadā anāprīte bhoktavyam | āprītaṃ kvacit kārye pākādāv upayuktam
|| 1.17.9 ||
āprītaṃ ced abhidagdhe || 1.17.10 ||
āprītam eva cel labhyate, tadāgninā tadabhito dagdhvā tatra bhoktavyam || 1.17.10 ||
parimṛṣṭaṃ lauhaṃ prayatam || 1.17.11 ||
lauhaṃ lohavikārabhūtaṃ kāṃsyādi bhojanapātraṃ bhasmādibhiḥ parimṛṣṭaṃ sat prayataṃ bhavati | tatra bhasmanā kāṃsyam, āmlena tāmram, rājataṃ śakṛtā, sauvarṇam adbhir evetyādi smṛtyantaravaśād draṣṭavyam || 1.17.11 ||
nirlikhitaṃ dārumayam || 1.17.12 ||
dārumayaṃ bhājanaṃ nirlikhitaṃ taṣṭaṃ sat prayataṃ bhavati || 1.17.12 ||
yathāgamaṃ yajñe || 1.17.13 ||
yajñapātraṃ tu yathāgamaṃ śodhitaṃ prayataṃ bhavati | tad yathā – agnihotrahavaṇī darbhair adbhiḥ prakṣālitā, somapātrāṇi mārjālīye prakṣālitāni, ājyapātrāṇy uṣṇena vāriṇā || 1.17.13 ||
nāpaṇīyam annam aśnīyāt || 1.17.14 ||
āpaṇaḥ paṇyavīthikā | tatra krītaṃ labdhaṃ vā āpaṇīyam, tac ca kṛtānnaṃ nāśṇīyāt | vrīhyādiṣu na doṣaḥ || 1.17.14 ||
tathā rasānām āmamāṃsamadhulavaṇānīti parihāpya || 1.17.15 ||
rasānāṃ rasadravyāṇi | tāny apy āpaṇīyāni nāśnīyāt, āmamāṃsādīn varjayitvā || 1.17.15 ||
tailasarpiṣī tūpayojayed udake ‘vadhāya || 1.17.16 ||
tailasarpiṣī tv āpaṇīye apy upayojayet, udake ‘vadhāya niṣicya pākena tailasarpiṣī śodhayitvā | kāryavirodho yathā na bhavati tathodakena saṃsṛjyety anye vyācakṣate || 1.17.16 ||
kṛtānnaṃ paryuṣitam akhādyāpeyānādyam || 1.17.17 ||
kṛtānnaṃ pakvānnam, tat paryuṣitaṃ pūrvedyuḥ pakvaṃ sad akhādyam apeyam anādyaṃ ca | yathāyogaṃ kharaviśadaṃ dravaṃ mṛduviśadaṃ siddhaṃ [snigdhaṃ] ca || 1.17.17 ||
śuktaṃ ca || 1.17.18 ||
śuktaṃ kālapākenāmlībhūtaṃ tad aparyuṣitam apy akhādyāpeyānādyam || 1.17.18 ||
phāṇitapṛthukataṇḍulakarambabharujasaktuśākamāṃsa-
piṣṭakṣīravikārauṣadhivanaspatimūlaphalavarjam || 1.17.19 ||
anantaroktam vidhidvayaṃ phāṇitādīn varjayitvā draṣṭavyam | phāṇitaṃ pānaviśeṣaḥ, ikṣurasa iti kecit | bharjitānāṃ [mraṣṭānāṃ?] vrīhīṇāṃ taṇḍulāḥ pṛthūkṛtāḥ pṛthukāḥ | karambaḥ dadhisaktusamāhāro yaḥ karambha iti prasiddhaḥ | vede ‘py ubhayaṃ bhavati – “karambair juhoti,” [TaitBr 3.8.14] “dhānāḥ karambhaḥ parivāpaḥ” [TS 6.5.11] iti | bharujāḥ bhraṣṭā yavāḥ | kṣīravikāro dadhyādi | prasiddham anyat || 1.17.19 ||
atha “śuktaṃ ca” [ĀpDh 1.17.18] ity asya vidheḥ śeṣaḥ |
śuktaṃ cāparayogam || 1.17.20 ||
pareṇa dravyāntareṇa yogo yasya tat parayogam, tato ‘nyad aparayogam | tad eva śuktaṃ varjyam | yat tu dadhyādidravyāntarasaṃspṛṣṭaṃ śuktaṃ tad bhojyam eva | evaṃ ca pūrvatraivāparayogam iti viśeṣaṇaṃ vaktavyam, idam eva vā sūtram astu | sūtradvayakaraṇaṃ tv ācāryapravṛttikṛtam, yathā “salāvṛkyekasṛkolūkaśabdāḥ” [ĀpDh 1.10.19] iti pūrvaṃ sāmānyenābhidhāya “salāvṛkyām ekasṛka iti svapnaparyāntam” [ĀpDh 1.11.33] iti paścād viśeṣa uktaḥ || 1.17.20 ||
sarvaṃ madyam apeyam || 1.17.21 ||
madyaṃ madakaraṃ tat sarvam apeyam | atra tu smṛtyantaravaśād vyavasthā | atra manuḥ |
gauḍī mādhvī ca paiṣṭī ca vijñeyā trividhā surā |
yathaivaikā na pātavyā tathā sarvā dvijottamaiḥ || iti | [MDh 11.95]
surāvyatiriktaṃ tu madyaṃ brāhmaṇasya nityam apeyam | tathā ca gautamaḥ: “madyaṃ nityaṃ brāhmaṇasya kṣatriyavaiśyayos tu brahmacāriṇoḥ” [GDh 2.20] iti || 1.17.21 ||
tathailakaṃ payaḥ || 1.17.22 ||
avir elakā, tasyāḥ kṣīram apeyam || 1.17.22 ||
uṣṭrīkṣīramṛgīkṣīrasaṃdhinīkṣīrayamasūkṣīrāṇīti || 1.17.23 ||
uṣṭrīmṛgyau prasiddhe | yā garbhiṇī satī dugdhe sā saṃdhinīti śāstrāntare prasiddhā | ekakālahomety anye | ekasmin prasave yānekaṃ garbhaṃ sūte sā yamasūḥ | uṣṭryādīnāṃ kṣīrāṇy apeyāni | iti karaṇād evaṃprakārāṇām ekaśaphānāṃ kṣīram apeyam | tathā ca manuḥ |
āraṇyānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ mṛgāṇāṃ mahiṣīṃ vinā |
strīkṣīraṃ caiva varjyāni sarvaśūktāni caiva hi ||
anirdaśāyā goḥ kṣiram auṣṭram aikaśaphaṃ tathā |
āvikaṃ saṃdhinīkṣīraṃ vivatsāyāś ca goḥ payaḥ || iti | [MDh 5.9, 8]
|| 1.17.23 ||
dhenoś cānirdaśāyāḥ || 1.17.24 ||
dhenur navaprasūtikā gauḥ | cakārād ajāmahiṣyoś ca, “ajā gāvo mahiṣyaś ca” iti mānave darśanāt
|| 1.17.24 ||
tathā kīlālauṣadhīnāṃ ca || 1.17.25 ||
kīlālauṣadhayaḥ surārthā oṣadhayaḥ, tāsāṃ vikārabhūtam annaṃ nādyam || 1.17.25 ||
karañjapalaṇḍuparārikāḥ || 1.17.26 ||
karañjaṃ raktalaśunam, palaṇḍu śvetam, parārikā kṛṣṇam, ḍuṇḍubhākhyayā mlecchānāṃ prasiddham | ete cābhakṣyāḥ || 1.17.26 ||
pratipadapāṭho na śakya iti samāsenāha |
yac cānyat paricakṣate || 1.17.27 ||
yac cānyad evaṃbhūtaṃ śiṣṭāḥ paricakṣate varjayanti tad apy abhakṣyam | tatra manuḥ |
laśunaṃ gṛñjanaṃ caiva palaṇḍukavakāni ca |
abhakṣyāṇi dvijātīnām amedhyaprabhavāṇi ca || iti | [MDh 5.5]
|| 1.17.27 ||
kyākv abhojyam iti hi brāhmaṇam || 1.17.28 ||
kyāku chatrākam, tad apy abhojyam abhakṣyam | brāhmaṇagrahaṇam uktārtham || 1.17.28 ||
ekakhuroṣṭragavayagrāmasūkaraśarabhagavām || 1.17.29 ||
māṃsam ity arthād gamyate | ekakhurā aśvādayaḥ | gavayo gosadṛśaḥ paśuḥ | śarabho ‘ṣṭapāda āraṇyo mṛgaḥ | anye prasiddhāḥ | eteṣāṃ māṃsam abhakṣyam || 1.17.29 ||
dhenvanaḍuhor bhakṣyam || 1.17.30 ||
dhenvanaḍuhor māṃsaṃ bhakṣyam | gopratiṣedhasya pratiprasavaḥ || 1.17.30 ||
medhyam ānaḍuham iti vājasaneyakam || 1.17.31 ||
ānaḍuhaṃ māṃsaṃ na kevalaṃ bhakṣyam | kiṃ tarhi | medhyam apīti vājaseneyinaḥ samāmananti || 1.17.31 ||
kukkuṭo vikirāṇām || 1.17.32 ||
vyavahitam apy abhojyam iti saṃbadhyate | pādābhyāṃ vikīrya kīṭadhānyādi ye bhakṣayanti te mayūrādayo vikirāḥ | teṣāṃ madhye kukkuṭo na bhakṣyaḥ | smṛtyantaravaśād grāmyo nāraṇyaḥ
|| 1.17.32 ||
plavaḥ pratudām || 1.17.33 ||
tuṇḍena pratudya ye bhakṣayanti te dārvāghāṭādayaḥ pratudāḥ | teṣāṃ madhye plava evābhakṣyaḥ | plavaḥ śakaṭabilākhyo bakaviśeṣaḥ || 1.17.33 ||
kravyādaḥ || 1.17.34 ||
kravyaṃ māṃsam, tad eva kevalaṃ ye ‘danti te kravyādā gṛdhrādayaḥ | te ‘py abhakṣyāḥ
|| 1.17.34 ||
haṃsabhāsacakravākasuparṇāś ca || 1.17.35 ||
haṃsaḥ prasiddhaḥ | bhāsaḥ śyenākṛtiḥ pīnatuṇḍaḥ | cakravāko mithunacaraḥ | suparṇaḥ śyenaḥ | ete cābhakṣyāḥ || 1.17.35 ||
kruñcakrauñcavārdhrāṇasalakṣmaṇavarjam || 1.17.36 ||
kruñcā vṛndacarāḥ | krauñcā mithunacarāḥ | te cābhakṣyāḥ | sūtre krauñceti vibhaktilopaḥ chāndasaḥ | kim aviśeṣeṇa kruñcakrauñcā na bhakṣyāḥ | nety āha – vārdhrāṇasalakṣaṇavarjam | śveto lohito vā mūrdhā yeṣāṃ te lakṣmaṇāḥ | ta eva viśiṣyante vārdhrāṇasā iti | vārdhraṃ carma, tadākārā nāsikā yeṣāṃ tān evaṃbhūtān lakṣmanān varjayitvā kruñcakrauñcā na bhakṣyā iti | anye tv āhuḥ – kravyāda iti prāptasya pratiṣedhasya kruñcādiṣu caturṣv pratiṣedha iti | tatra lakṣmaṇā sārasī | lakṣmaṇavarjam iti “ṅyāpoḥ saṃjñāchandasor bahulam” [Pāṇ 6.3.63] iti hrasvaḥ | evaṃ kruñcādiśabdasyāpy ajādiṭābantasya || 1.17.36 ||
pañcanakhānāṃ godhākacchapaśvāviṭcharyakakhaḍgaśaśa-
pūtikhaṣavarjam || 1.17.37 ||
pañcanakhāḥ naravānaramārjārādayaḥ | teṣāṃ madhye godhādīn sapta varjayitvānye ‘bhakṣyāḥ | godhā kṛkalāsākṛtir mahākāyā | kacchapaḥ kūrmaḥ | śvāviṭ varāhaviśeṣaḥ, yasya nārācākārāṇi romāṇi | śaryakaḥ śalyakaḥ, yasya carmaṇā tanutrāṇaṃ kriyate | “śvāviṭchalyaka” iti yuktaḥ pāṭhaḥ | eke tu chakāraṃ paṭhanti, chakārāt pūrvavikāram | khaḍgo mṛgaviśeṣaḥ, yasya śṛṅgaṃ tailabhajanam | śaśaḥ prasiddhaḥ | pūtikhaṣaḥ śaśākṛtiḥ himavati prasiddhaḥ || 1.17.37 ||
abhakṣyaś ceṭo matsyānām || 1.17.38 ||
matsyānāṃ madhye ceṭākhyo matsyo na bhakṣyaḥ | itare bhakṣyāḥ || 1.17.38 ||
sarpaśīrṣī mṛduraḥ kravyādo ye cānye vikṛtā yathā
manuṣyaśirasaḥ || 1.17.39 ||
sarpasyeva śiro yasya so ‘pi matsyo na bhakṣyaḥ | mṛduro makaraḥ | ye ca kravyam evādanti śiṃśumārādayas te ‘py abhakṣyāḥ | ye coktebhyo ‘nye vikṛtākārā matsyāḥ | tatrodāharaṇam – yathā manuṣyśirasaḥ, jalamanuṣyākhyāḥ jalahastyādayaś ca | te ‘pi sarve na bhakṣyāḥ | atra manuḥ |
anumantā viśasitā nihantā krayavikrayī |
saṃskartā copahartā ca khādakaś ceti ghātakāḥ ||
māṃ sa bhakṣayitāmutra yasya māṃsam ihādmy aham |
etan māṃsasya māṃsatvaṃ pravadanti manīṣiṇaḥ ||
na māṃsabhakṣaṇe doṣo na madye na ca maithune |
pravṛttir eṣā bhūtānāṃ nivṛttis tu mahāphalā || iti | [MDh 5.51, 55, 56]
apratiṣiddheṣv api bhakṣaṇān nivṛttir eva jyāyasīty arthaḥ || 1.17.39 ||
iti saptadaśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
iti śrīharadattamiśraviracitāyām āpastambadharmasūtravyākhyāyām
ujjvalākhyāyāṃ pañcamaḥ paṭalaḥ
evaṃ tāvan nimittaduṣṭaṃ kāladuṣṭaṃ jātiduṣṭaṃ cābhojyam uktam | tatra nimittaduṣṭaṃ “yasya kula mriyeta” [ĀpDh 1.16.18] ityādi | kāladuṣṭaṃ paryuṣitādi | jātiduṣṭaṃ kalañcādi | idānīṃ pratigrahāśucīni kānicid anujñāya kānicid pratiṣedhayati |
madhv āmaṃ mārgaṃ māṃsaṃ bhūmir mūlaphalāni rakṣā
gavyūtir niveśanaṃ yugyaghāsaś cogrataḥ pratigṛhyāṇi || 1.18.1 ||
madhu pakvam apakvaṃ vā | āmaṃ taṇḍulādi | mṛgasya vikāro mārgaṃ māṃsam | bhūmiḥ śāleyādikṣetram, viśvam asthānam ity anye | mūlaphalāni mūlakāmrādīni | rakṣā abhayadānam | gavyūtir gomārgaḥ | niveśanaṃ gṛham | yugaṃ vahatīti yugyo balīvardaḥ, tasya ghāso bhakṣyaṃ palālādi | etāny ugrato ‘pi pratigrāhyāṇy adurbhikṣe ‘pi | ugraḥ pāpakarmā dvijātiḥ, vaiśyād vā śūdrāyāṃ jātaḥ | ugragrahaṇaṃ tādṛśānām upalakṣaṇam || 1.18.1 ||
etāny api nānantevāsyāhṛtānīti hārītaḥ || 1.18.2 ||
etāni madhvādīny apy antevāsyāhṛtāny eva pratigṛhyāṇi, na svayam ugrata iti hārīta ācāryo manyate || 1.18.2 ||
āmaṃ vā gṛhṇīran || 1.18.3 ||
pūrvokeṣv āmaṃ svayam eva vā gṛhṇīran dvijā iti hārītācāryasya pakṣaḥ || 1.18.3 ||
kṛtānnasya vā virasasya || 1.18.4 ||
āmasyālābhe kṛtānnasya vā virasasya lavaṇādirasair asaṃsṛṣṭasya | ṣaṣṭhīnirdeśāt stokaṃ svayam antevāsyāhṛtaṃ vā gṛhṇīran || 1.18.4 ||
na subhikṣāḥ syuḥ || 1.18.5 ||
anantarokte vidhidvaye yad gṛhītaṃ tena subhikṣāḥ suhitā na bhaveyuḥ | yāvatā prāṇayātrā bhavati tāvad eva gṛhṇīran, na yāvatā sauhityaṃ tāvad iti || 1.18.5 ||
svayam apy avṛttau suvarṇaṃ datvā paśuṃ vā bhuñjīta || 1.18.6 ||
yadā tu durbhikṣatayātmano vṛttir na labhyate prāg eva poṣyavargasya, tadā svayam apy avṛttau yatraiva labhyate tatra kṛtānnam api bhuñjīta | tatra guṇavidhiḥ – suvarṇaṃ datvā sakṛd evopakḷptam upariṣṭāt suvarṇena spṛṣṭvā | etena “paśuṃ vā datvā” iti vyākhyātaḥ | paśur agniḥ, “agniḥ paśur āsīt” [TS 5.7.26] iti mantraliṅgāt, gosūktenāgner upasthānadarśanāc ca || 1.18.6 ||
nātyantam anvavasyet || 1.18.7 ||
na punar atyantam eva sīdet || 1.18.7 ||
vṛttiṃ prāpya viramet || 1.18.8 ||
yadā vihitā vṛttir labhyate tadā niṣiddhāyā viramet, na punaḥ sakṛt pravṛttāyāḥ kim avakuṇṭhaneneti nyāyena  tatraiva rameta | atra chāndogyopaniṣat – “maṭacīhateṣu kuruṣv āṭakyā saha jāyayoṣastir ha cākrāyaṇa ibhyagrāme pradrāṇaka uvāsa | sa hebhyaṃ kulmāṣān khādantaṃ bibhikṣe” ityādi [ChUp 1.10.1–2] | mantravarṇaś ca bhavati – “avartyā śuna āntrāṇi pece” [RV 4.18.13] iti | avartyā vṛttyabhāvena | apara āha – durbhikṣe svayam apy avṛttau vā ātannivṛtter yatra kutracin nīceti dātari bhuñjāno vaset, yāṃ ca yāvatīṃ ca suvarṇamātrāṃ yaṃ kaṃcana paśuṃ vā tasmai datvā, na punar atyantam anvavasyet, vṛttiṃ prāpya viramed iti || 1.18.8 ||
evam āpadi vṛttim uktvā subhikṣe ‘nāpadi vṛttim āha |
trayāṇāṃ varṇānāṃ kṣatriyaprabhṛtīnāṃ samāvṛttena na
bhoktavyam || 1.18.9 ||
samāvṛtto dvijātiḥ kṣatriyādīnāṃ trayāṇāṃ varṇānāṃ gṛhe na bhuñjīta || 1.18.9 ||
prakṛtyā brāhmaṇasya bhoktavyaṃ kāraṇād abhojyam
|| 1.18.10 ||
brāhmaṇasya tv annaṃ prakṛtyā svabhāvenaiva bhoktavyam | kāraṇād eva tv abhojyam
|| 1.18.10 ||
kāraṇam āha |
yatrāprāyaścittaṃ karmāsevate prāyaścittavati || 1.18.11 ||
yatra yadāgnihotravaiśvadevādi nityam ābhyudayikaṃ vā aprāyaścittaṃ karmāsevate tātparyeṇa karoti, prāyaścittavaty ātmani coditaṃ prāyaścittaṃ prāṇāyāmopavāsapathikṛdādi na karoti, tadā etasmāt kāraṇād brāhmaṇasyānnam abhojyam iti || 1.18.11 ||
caritanirveṣasya bhoktavyam || 1.18.12 ||
carito nirveṣaḥ prāyaścittaṃ yena tasyānnaṃ bhoktavyam, tadbhojane na doṣaḥ | niṣṭhayā bhūtakālasyābhidhānāc caryamāṇe ‘pi nirveṣe na bhojyam | kiṃ tarhi | carite || 1.18.12 ||
sarvavarṇānāṃ svadharme vartamānānāṃ bhoktavyaṃ
śūdravarjam ity eke || 1.18.13 ||
śūdravarjitānāṃ svadharme vartamānānāṃ trayāṇāṃ varṇānāṃ bhojyam, na brāhmaṇasyaivety eke manyante || 1.18.13 ||
tasyāpi dharmopanatasya || 1.18.14 ||
tasyāpi śūdrasyānnaṃ bhojyaṃ yady asau dharmārtham upanata āśrito bhavati | dharmagrahaṇād arthārtham upanatasyābhojyam | āpatkalpaś cāyam || 1.18.14 ||
atra guṇavidhiḥ |
suvarṇaṃ datvā paśuṃ vā bhuñjīta nātyantam anvavasyed vṛttiṃ
prāpya viramet || 1.18.15 ||
gatam || 1.18.15 ||
saṃghānnam abhojyam || 1.18.16 ||
saṃgho gaṇaḥ, tasya yat svam annaṃ na ty ekasya, tad abhojyam, yady api te sarve dadyuḥ
|| 1.18.16 ||
parikruṣṭaṃ ca || 1.18.17 ||
bhoktukāmā āgacchatety evaṃ parikruśya sarvata āhūya yad dīyate tat parikruṣṭam, tad abhojyam || 1.18.17 ||
sarveṣāṃ ca śilpājīvānām || 1.18.18 ||
citrakarma citranirmāṇādikaṃ śilpaṃ ye ājīvanti teṣāṃ sarveṣām api brāhmaṇānām annam abhojyam || 1.18.18 ||
ye ca śastram ājīvanti || 1.18.19 ||
ye ca śastreṇa jīvanti teṣām apy annam abhojyam, kṣatriyavarjam, tasya vihitatvāt || 1.18.19 ||
ye cādhim || 1.18.20 ||
ājīvantīty apekṣate | svagṛhe parān vāsayitvā tebhyo bhṛtigrahaṇam ādhiḥ, yaḥ stoma iti prasiddham |
parabhūmau kuṭiṃ kṛtvā stomaṃ datvā vaset tu yaḥ || iti |
taṃ cādhiṃ ye ājīvanti teṣām apy abhojyam | ye tu prasiddham ādhim ājīvanti teṣāṃ vārdhuṣikatvād evābhojyānnatvaṃ siddham || 1.18.20 ||
bhiṣak || 1.18.21 ||
abhojyānna iti prakaraṇād gamyate | bhiṣag bhaiṣajyavṛttiḥ | dharmārthaṃ tu ye sarpadaṣṭādīṃś cikitsanti te bhojyānnā eva || 1.18.21 ||
vārdhuṣikaḥ || 1.18.22 ||
vṛddhyājīvo ‘py abhojyānnaḥ || 1.18.22 ||
dīkṣito ‘krītarājakaḥ || 1.18.23 ||
dīkṣito dīkṣaṇīyeṣṭyā saṃskṛtaḥ | so ‘pi yāvat krītarājako na bhavati somakrayaṇaṃ na karoti tāvad abhojyānnaḥ || 1.18.23 ||
agnīṣomīyasaṃsthāyām eva || 1.18.24 ||
bhoktavyam iti vakṣyamāṇam apekṣate | agnīṣomīyapaśau saṃsthite samāpta eva bhoktavyam, na prāg iti pakṣāntaram || 1.18.24 ||
hutāyāṃ vā vapāyāṃ dīkṣitasya bhoktavyam || 1.18.25 ||
agnīṣomīyasya vapāyāṃ hutāyāṃ vā dīkṣitasyānnaṃ bhoktavyam | tathā ca bahvṛcabrāhmaṇam – “aśitavyaṃ vapāyāṃ hutāyām” iti || 1.18.25 ||
pakṣāntaram āha |
yajñārthe vā nirdiṣṭe śeṣād bhuñjīrann iti hi
brāhmaṇam || 1.18.26 ||
idaṃ yajñārtham iti vyādeśe krte śeṣād bhuñjīrann iti brāhmaṇaṃ bhavati | brāhmaṇagrahaṇaṃ prītyupalabdhitaḥ pravṛtter apasmṛtitā mā bhūd iti pratyakṣam evātra brāhmaṇam iti || 1.18.26 ||
klībaḥ || 1.18.27 ||
ṣaṇḍaḥ | so ‘py abhojyānnaḥ || 1.18.27 ||
rājñāṃ praiṣakaraḥ || 1.18.28 ||
rājñām iti bahuvacanāt grāmāder yaḥ praiṣakaraḥ tasyāpi pratiṣedhaḥ || 1.18.28 ||
ahaviryājī || 1.18.29 ||
yaś cāhaviṣā nararudhirādinā yajate ābhicārādau, yathā “yam abhicaret tasya lohitam avadānaṃ kṛtvā” iti, so ‘py abhojyānnaḥ || 1.18.29 ||
cārī || 1.18.30 ||
cāro gūḍhacaraḥ spaśaḥ | so ‘py abhojyānnaḥ || 1.18.30 ||
avidhinā ca pravrajitaḥ || 1.18.31 ||
ye avidhinā pravrajitāḥ śākyādayas te ‘py abhojyānnāḥ || 1.18.31 ||
yaś cāgnīn apāsyati || 1.18.32 ||
avidhinety eva | yaś cāvidhinā utsargeṣṭyā vināgnīn apāsyati so ‘py abhojyānnaḥ || 1.18.32 ||
yaś ca sarvān varjayate sarvānnī ca śrotriyo nirākṛtir
vṛṣalīpatiḥ || 1.18.33 ||
yaś ca sarvān varyayati bhojane na kvacid api bhuṅkte, na kaṃcid bhojayati, yaś ca sarvānnī sarveṣām annaṃ bhuṅkte, tāv apy abhojyānnau | śrotriya ity ubhayoḥ śeṣaḥ, śrotriyo ‘pi sann abhojyānna eveti | nirākṛtir niḥsvādhyāyaḥ | nirvrata ity anye | so ‘py abhojyānnaḥ | vṛṣalīpatiḥ, kramavivāhe yasya vṛṣalī patnī jīvati itarā mṛtāḥ sa vṛṣalīpatiḥ | śrotriyo ‘py abhojyānna iti
|| 1.18.33 ||
ity aṣṭādaśaḥ khaṇdaḥ
matta unmatto baddho ‘ṇikaḥ pratyupaviṣṭo yaś ca
pratyupaveśayate tāvantaṃ kālam || 1.19.1 ||
matto madadravyasevayā vikṛtiṃ gataḥ | unmatto bhrāntaḥ | baddho nigalitaḥ | aṇikaḥ putrāc chrutagrāhī, putrācārya iti śāstreṣu ninditaḥ | pratyupaviṣṭaḥ ṛṇādinā kāraṇenādhamarṇādikaṃ nirudhya tatpārśva upaviṣṭaḥ | pratyupaveśayitā tu tasya parihāram akurvan tena saha kāmaṃ suciram āsyatām ity āsīnaḥ | ete mattādayas tāvantaṃ kālam abhojyānnāḥ, yāvan madādyanuvṛttiḥ | apara āha – aṇikaḥ ṛṇikaḥ ṛṇasya dātā | pratyupaveṣṭur idaṃ viśeṣaṇam
|| 1.19.1 ||
ka āśyānnaḥ || 1.19.2 ||
yady ete abhojyānnāḥ kas tarhy āśyānnaḥ, kasya tarhy aśanīyam annam iti | yady apy ete ‘bhojyānnā ity ukte pariśiṣṭā bhojyānnā iti gamyate, tathāpy anekamatopanyāsārtho ‘yaṃ praśnapūrvaka ārambhaḥ || 1.19.2 ||
ya īpsed iti kaṇvaḥ || 1.19.3 ||
ya eva prārthayate sa āśyānna iti kaṇvarṣir manyate pratiṣiddhavarjam || 1.19.3 ||
puṇya iti kautsaḥ || 1.19.4 ||
“sarvavarṇānāṃ svadharme vartamānānām” [ĀpDh 1.18.13] ity uktatvād bhojyānnāḥ sarve puṇyā eva | iha punaḥ puṇyagrahaṇam atiśayārtham | tapohomajapyaiḥ svadharmeṇa ca yuktaḥ puṇya iti kautsaḥ | sa svayam aprārthayamāno ‘pi bhojyānna iti kautsasya pakṣaḥ || 1.19.4 ||
yaḥ kaścid dadyād iti vārṣyāyaṇiḥ || 1.19.5 ||
yaḥ kaścana puṇyo vāpuṇyo vā satataṃ dānaśīlaḥ, sa bhojyānna iti vārṣyāyaṇir āha | tathā ca manuḥ |
śraddhāpūtaṃ vadānyasya hatam aśraddhayetarat || iti | [MDh 4.225]
|| 1.19.5 ||
atropapattiḥ |
yadi ha rajaḥ sthāvaraṃ puruṣe bhoktavyam atha cec calaṃ
dānena nirdoṣo bhavati || 1.19.6 ||
rajaḥ pāpam | tad yadi puruṣe kartari sthāvaraṃ sthiraṃ na bhogam antareṇa kṣīyate, tadā tataḥ pratigrahe ‘pi bhoktari saṃkramābhāvād bhoktavyam | atha cec calam upabhogam antareṇāpi kṣīyate, tadā satataṃ dānaśīle muhūrtam api pāpaṃ nāvatiṣṭhata iti kuto bhoktur doṣa iti
|| 1.19.6 ||
śuddhā bhikṣā bhoktavyaikakuṇikau kāṇvakutsau tathā
puṣkarasādiḥ || 1.19.7 ||
dhārmikeṇodyatā āhṛtā bhikṣā śuddhā | sā bhojyety ekādīnāṃ pañcānāṃ pakṣaḥ | puṣkarasādiḥ pauṣkarasādiḥ | ādivṛddhyabhāvaḥ chāndasaḥ || 1.19.7 ||
sarvatopetaṃ vārṣyāyaṇīyam || 1.19.8 ||
sarvataḥ upetaṃ sarvatopetam | chandaso guṇaḥ | upetam ayācitopapannam | tat sarvato ‘pi bhojyam iti vārṣyāyaṇīyaṃ matam || 1.19.8 ||
idānīṃ svamatam āha |
puṇyasyepsato bhoktavyam || 1.19.9 ||
kaṇvakautsayoḥ pakṣau samuccitāv ācāryasya pakṣaḥ || 1.19.9 ||
puṇyasyāpy anīpsato na bhoktavyam || 1.19.10 ||
yaḥ prārthito ‘pi nety asakṛd uktvā kathaṃcid āpāditepsaḥ so ‘nīpsann ity ucyate | tasya puṇyasyāpy abhojyam iti | apara āha – anīpsata iti kartari ṣaṣṭhī | puṇyasyāpy annaṃ na bhojyam iti, yadi bhikṣamāṇaḥ pūrvavairādinā svayam īpsan na bhavatīti || 1.19.10 ||
yataḥ kutaś cābhyudyataṃ bhoktavyam || 1.19.11 ||
sarvatopetam [ĀpDh 1.19.8] ity uktam eva punar ucyate viśeṣavivakṣayā || 1.19.11 ||
tam āha |
nānaniyogapūrvam iti hārītaḥ || 1.19.12 ||
adya tubhyam idam āhariṣyāmi tad atra bhavatā grāhyam iti nivedanaṃ niyogaḥ | tadabhāva aniyogaḥ | punar nañsamāsaḥ | dvau nañau prakṛtam arthaṃ gamayataḥ, ananiyogo niyogaḥ | tat pūrvaṃ ced abhyudyataṃ na bhojyam iti || 1.19.12 ||
atha purāṇe ślokāv udāharanti –
udyatām āhṛtāṃ bhikṣāṃ purastād apraveditām |
bhojyāṃ mene prajāpatir api duṣkṛtakāriṇaḥ ||
na tasya pitaro ‘śnanti daśa varṣāṇi pañca ca |
na ca havyaṃ vahaty agnir yas tām abhyadhimanyate ||
iti || 1.19.13 ||
atha api ca purāṇe,
sargaś ca pratisargaś ca vaṃśamanvantarāṇi ca |
vaṃśānucaritaṃ ceti purāṇaṃ pañcalakṣaṇam ||
ity evaṃlakṣaṇe bhaviṣyadādau | udyatāṃ hastābhyām udyamya dhāritām, āhṛtāṃ svayam evānītām, pūrvaniveditāṃ bhikṣāṃ duṣkṛtakāriṇo ‘pi sakāśād bhojyāṃ mene prajāpatir manuḥ | “manuḥ prajāpatir yasmin” iti dakṣe darśanāt | yas tu tām abhyadhimanyate pratyācaṣṭe, tasya pitaraḥ kavyaṃ nāśnanti | kiyantaṃ kālam | daśa varṣāṇi pañca ca | agniś ca havyaṃ na vahati tāvantam eva kalam | iti pratyākhyātur nindārthavādaḥ || 1.19.13 ||
cikitsakasya mṛgayoḥ śalyakṛntasya pāśinaḥ |
kulaṭāyāḥ ṣaṇḍakasya ca teṣām annam anādyam || 1.19.14 ||
cikitsako bhiṣagvṛttiḥ | mṛgayur mṛgaghātī lubdhakaḥ | śalyakṛntaḥ śastreṇa granthyādīnāṃ chettāmbaṣṭhaḥ | pāśī pāśavān pāśajālena mṛgādīnāṃ grāhakaḥ | kulam aṭatīti kulaṭā vyabhicāriṇī | ṣaṇḍakas tṛtīyā prakṛtiḥ | teṣāṃ cikitsakādīnām annam anādyam | cikitsakaṣaṇḍayoḥ punar vacanam udyatasyāpi pratiṣedhārtham | pūrvatra tarhi grahaṇaṃ kartum aśakyam | evaṃ tarhi – sūtrakārasya sa pratiṣedhaḥ, ayaṃ tu purāṇaśloka ity apaunarukthyam || 1.19.14 ||
athāpy udāharanti |
annāde bhrūṇahā mārṣṭi anenā abhiśaṃsati |
stenaḥ pramukto rājani yācann anṛtasaṅkaraḥ || iti || 1.19.15 ||
saṣaḍaṅgasya vedasyādhyetā bhrūṇaḥ, taṃ yo hatavān sa bhrūṇahā | so ‘nnāde mārṣṭi limpati | kim | prakaraṇād ena iti gamyate | bhrūṇaghno yo ‘nnam atti tasmiṃs tad enaḥ saṃkrāmati | tasmāt tasyodyatam apy abhojyam iti | prakaraṇasaṃgataḥ pādaḥ | itarat purāṇaśloke paṭhyamāne paṭhitam | anenasaṃ yo ‘bhiśaṃsati mithyaiva brūte idaṃ tvayā kṛtam iti, sa tasminn abhiśaṃsati tad eno mārṣṭi | manus tu |
patitaṃ patitety uktvā coraṃ coreti vā punaḥ |
vacanāt tulyadoṣaḥ syān mithyā dvirdoṣabhāg bhavet ||
iti dvaiguṇyam āha | tad abhyāse draṣṭavyam | “stenaḥ prakīrṇakeśaḥ” [ĀpDh 1.25.4] iti vakṣyati | sa eva tṛtīyapādasyārthaḥ | kartṛbhedād apaunaruktyam | saṅgaraḥ pratijñā pratiśravaḥ, satyasaṃgara iti yathā | yaḥ pratiśrutya na dadāti so ‘nṛtasaṅgaraḥ | kakāraḥ chāndasaḥ | tasmin yācan svayam eno mārṣṭi | tasmāt pratiśrutaṃ deyam iti || 1.19.15 ||
ity ekonaviṃśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
iti śrīharadattamiśraviracitāyām āpastambadharmasūtravyākhyāyām
ujjvalākhyāyāṃ ṣaṣṭhaḥ paṭalaḥ
nemaṃ laukikam arthaṃ puraskṛtya dharmāṃś caret || 1.20.1 ||
imaṃ laukikaṃ loke viditaṃ khyātilābhapūjātmakam arthaṃ prayojanaṃ puraskṛtyābhisaṃdhāya dharmān na caret || 1.21.1 ||
kiṃ kāraṇam |
niṣphalā hy abhyudaye bhavanti || 1.20.2 ||
hi yasmād evaṃ kriyamāṇā dharmā abhyudaye phalakāle niṣphalā bhavanti | lokārthaṃ hy asau dharmaṃ carati, na kartavyam iti śraddhayā, na ca śraddhayā vinā dharmaṃ phalaṃ sādhayati, “yo vai śraddhām anārambhaḥ” [TS 1.6.8] ityādiśruteḥ || 1.20.2 ||
kim idānīṃ dṛṣṭaphalaṃ tyājyam eva | nety āha |
tad yathāmre phalārthe nimite chāyā gandha ity anūtpadyete |
evaṃ dharmaṃ caryamāṇam arthā anūtpadyante || 1.20.3
tad iti vākyopanyāse | phalārthaṃ hy āmvravṛkṣo nimīyate āropyate | tasmin phalārthe nimite chāyā gandhaś cānūtpadyete | evaṃ dharmaṃ caryamāṇam arthāḥ khyātyādayo ‘nūtpadyante anuniṣpadyante, tathaiva svīkāryāḥ, na coddeśyatayā | tathā cāha |
yathekṣuhetoḥ salilaṃ prasecitaṃ tṛṇāni vallīr api ca prasiñcati |
tathā naro dharmapathena vartayan yaśaś ca kāmāṃś ca vasūni cāśnute ||
iti || 1.20.3 ||
no ced anūtpadyante na dharmahānir bhavati || 1.20.4 ||
yady api daivād arthā nānūtpadyante, tathāpi dharmas tāvad bhavati | sa ca svatantraḥ puruṣārthaḥ, kim anyair arthair iti || 1.20.4 ||
anasūyur duṣpralambhaḥ syāt kuhakaśaṭhanāstikabāla-
vādeṣu || 1.20.5 ||
kuhakaḥ prakāśe śuciḥ ekānte yatheṣṭacārī, śaṭho vakracittaḥ, nāstikaḥ pretyabhāvāpavādī, bālaḥ śrutarahitaḥ, eteṣāṃ vādeṣv asūyur na syāt | asūyayā dveṣo lakṣyate, dveṣṭā na syāt | tān viṣayīkṛtya dveṣam api na kuryāt | tathā duṣpralambhaś ca syāt | pralambhanaṃ visaṃvādanaṃ mithyāphalākhyānam, “gṛdhivañcyoḥ pralambhane” [Pāṇ 1.3.69] iti darśanāt | duṣpralambho visaṃvādayituṃ mithyāphalākhyānena pravartayitum aśakyaḥ | kuhakādivādeṣu vañcito ‘pi na syāt, tadvaśo na syād ity arthaḥ || 1.20.5 ||
vañcanasya saṃbhavam āha |
na dharmādharmau carata āvaṃ sva iti | na devagandharvā
na pitara ity ācakṣate ‘yaṃ dharmo ‘yam adharma iti || 1.20.6 ||
āvam iti chāndasaṃ rūpam | bhāṣāyāṃ tu “prathamāyāś ca dvivacane bhāṣāyām” [Pāṇ 7.2.86] ity ātvaṃ prāpnoti | yadi hi dharmādharmau vigrahavantau govyāghravac caretāṃ āvāṃ sva iti bruvāṇau, yadi vā devādayaḥ prakṛṣṭajñānā brūyur imau dharmādharmāv iti, tataḥ kuhakādivādeṣu na syād vañcanā, tadabhāvāt tu vañcanāsaṃbhava iti | idaṃ cātra draṣṭavyam – pratyakṣāder agocarau dharmādharmau, kiṃ tu nityanirdoṣavedāvagamyau, tadabhāve tanmūladharmaśāstrāvagamyāv iti || 1.20.6 ||
yatra tu prāyaścittādau viṣayavyavasthā duṣkarā tatra nirṇayam āha |
yat tv āryāḥ kriyamāṇaṃ praśaṃsanti sa dharmo yad garhante
so ‘dharmaḥ || 1.20.7 ||
āryāḥ śiṣṭās traivarṇikāḥ | bahuvacanāc catvāras trayo vā | yathāha manuḥ |
catvāro vedadharmajñāḥ parṣat traividyam eva vā |
sā brūte yaṃ sa dharmaḥ syād eko vādhyātmavittamaḥ || [=YDh 1.9]
iti || 1.20.7 ||
idānīṃ śrutismṛtyoḥ pratyakṣayor adarśane śiṣṭācārād apy avagamya dharmaḥ kārya ity āha |
sarvajanapadeṣv ekāntasamāhitam āryāṇāṃ vṛttaṃ
samyagvinītānāṃ vṛddhānām ātmavatām alolupānām
adāmbhikānāṃ vṛttasādṛśyaṃ bhajeta || 1.20.8 ||
samyagvinītāḥ “ācāryādhīnaḥ syāt” [ĀpDh 1.2.19] ityādinā vinayena saṃpannāḥ | vṛddhāḥ pariṇatavayasaḥ, yauvane viṣayavaśatāpi syād itīdam uktam | ātmavanto jitendriyāḥ | alolupāḥ akṛpaṇāḥ | adāmbhikā adharmadhvajā ekāntaprakāśayor ekavṛttāḥ | evaṃbhūtānām āryāṇāṃ sarvajanapadeṣu yad ekāntenāvyabhicāreṇa samāhitam anumataṃ vṛttam anuṣṭhānam, na mātulasutāpariṇayādivat katipayaviṣayam, tad vṛttasādṛśyaṃ bhajeta tadanurūpaṃ ceṣṭeta | na teṣām anuṣṭhānaṃ nirmūlam, bhavati ca vaidikānām utsannapāṭhabrāhmaṇānubhava iti || 1.20.8 ||
evam ubhau lokāv abhijayati || 1.20.9 ||
evaṃ śrutismṛtisadācāramūlam anuṣṭhānaṃ kurvan ubhau lokāv abhijayati imaṃ cāmuṃ
ca || 1.20.9 ||
avihitā brāhmaṇasya vaṇijyā || 1.20.10 ||
krayavikrayavyavahāro vaṇijyā | sā svayaṃ kṛtā brāhmaṇasya vṛttir na vihitā | prāptānuvādo ‘yam apavādavidhānārthaḥ || 1.20.10 ||
āpadi vyavahareta paṇyānām apaṇyāni vyudasyan || 1.20.11 ||
brāhmaṇavṛtter alābha āpat, tasyāṃ satyāṃ paṇyānām | “vyavahṛpaṇoḥ samarthayoḥ” [Pāṇ 2.3.57] karmaṇi ṣaṣṭhī | vyavahareta, krayaś ca vikrayaś ca vyavahāraḥ, paṇyāni krīṇīyād vikrīṇīta cety arthaḥ | apaṇyāni vakṣamāṇāni vyudasyan varjayan | kṛtsnāyā vaiśyavṛtter upalakṣaṇam idam | kṣatriyavṛttiś ca daṇḍāpūpikayā siddhā | tathā ca gautamaḥ: “tadalābhe kṣatriyavṛttiḥ | tadalābhe vaiśyavṛttiḥ” iti [GDh 7.6–7] || 1.20.11 ||
apaṇyāny āha |
manuṣyān rasān rāgān gandhān annaṃ carma gavāṃ
vaśāṃ śleṣmodake tokmakiṇve pippilimarīce dhānyaṃ
māṃsam āyudhaṃ sukṛtāśāṃ ca || 1.20.12 ||
manuṣyā dāsādayaḥ | rasā guḍalavaṇādayaḥ, kṣīrādayo vā | rāgāḥ kusumbhādayaḥ rajate ‘neneti | rajyanta iti vā rāgā vastrādayaḥ | gandhāś candanādayaḥ | gavāṃ madhye vaśā vandhyā gauḥ | śleṣma jatvādi, yena viśliṣṭaṃ carmādi saṃdhīyate | yathā – | “śleṣmaṇā carmaṇyaṃ vānyad vā viśliṣṭaṃ saṃśleṣayet” iti bahvṛcabrāhmaṇe darśanāt | tokmānīṣadaṅkuritāni vrīhyādīni | kiṇvaṃ surāprakṛtidravyam | sukṛtaṃ puṇyam, tasya phalaṃ sukṛtāśā | śiṣṭāni prasiddhāni | etāny apaṇyāni varjayitvā anyeṣāṃ paṇyānāṃ vyavahareta | manuṣyādīn varjayitvety eva siddhe, apaṇyānīti vacanam anyeṣām apaṇyānāṃ smṛtyantaroktānām api vyudāsārtham | tatra manuḥ |
sarvān rasān apoheta kṛtānnaṃ ca tilaiḥ saha |
aśmano lavaṇaṃ caiva paśavo ye ca mānuṣāḥ ||
sarvaṃ ca tāntavaṃ raktaṃ śāṇakṣaumāvikāni ca |
api cet syur araktāni phalamūle tathauṣadhīḥ ||
apaḥ śastraṃ viṣaṃ māṃsaṃ somaṃ gandhāṃś ca sarvaśaḥ |
kṣīraṃ kṣāraṃ dadhi ghṛtaṃ tailaṃ madhu guḍaṃ kuśān ||
āraṇyāṃś ca paśūn sarvān daṃṣṭriṇaś ca vayāṃsi ca |
madyaṃ nīlaṃ ca lākṣāṃ ca sarvāṃś caikaśaphān paśūn || [MDh 10.86–89]
iti || 1.20.12 ||
tilataṇḍulāṃs tv eva dhānyasya viśeṣeṇa na vikrīṇīyāt || 1.20.13 ||
dānyānāṃ madhye tilataṇḍulān eva viśeṣeṇātiśayena na vikrīṇīyāt na vikrīṇīta | anyeṣāṃ vikalpaḥ | svayam utpāditeṣu nāyaṃ pratiṣedhaḥ | mānave hi śrutam |
kāmam utpādya kṛṣyāṃ tu svayam eva kṛṣīvalaḥ |
vikrīṇīta tilān śuddhān dharmārtham acirasthitān || [MDh 10.90]
iti || 1.20.13 ||
avihitaś caiteṣāṃ mitho vinimayaḥ || 1.20.14 ||
vinimayaḥ parivartanam, yeṣāṃ vikrayaḥ pratiṣiddhaḥ teṣam eteṣāṃ mithaḥ paraspareṇa vinimayo ‘py avihitaḥ pratiṣiddhaḥ, na kartavya ity arthaḥ || 1.20.14 ||
teṣv eva keṣucin niyamo ‘py anujñāyate |
annena cānnasya manuṣyāṇāṃ ca manuṣyai rasānāṃ ca
rasair gandhānāṃ ca gandhair vidyayā ca vidyānām || 1.20.15 ||
annādīnāṃ vidyāntānāṃ vinimayo bhavaty evety arthaḥ | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “rasā rasaiḥ samato hīnato vā … tilataṇḍulapakvānnaṃ vidyāmanuṣyāś ca vihitāḥ parivartane” iti [VaDh 2.32, 39] | mānave tu viśeṣaḥ |
rasā rasair nimātavyā na tv eva lavaṇaṃ rasaiḥ |
kṛtānnaṃ cākṛtānnena tilā dhānyena tatsamāḥ || iti | [MDh 10.94]
gautamīye tu: “niyamas tu | rasānāṃ rasaiḥ | paśūnāṃ ca | na lavaṇakṛtānnayoḥ | samenāsamena tu pakvasya saṃpratyarthe” iti [GDh 7.16–21] | tasmān nātra pratiṣedhānuvṛttiḥ śaṅkanīyā | pūvatra coktam “brahmaṇi mitho viniyoge na gatir vidyate” iti [ĀpDh 1.16.17] | niyamābhyanujñānād eva vidyānāṃ vikrayaḥ pratiṣiddho veditavyaḥ || 1.20.15 ||
akrītapaṇyair vyavaharet || 1.20.16 ||
akrītāni svayam utpāditāni araṇyād āhṛtāni vā yāni paṇyāni tair vyavahareta || 1.20.16 ||
iti viṃśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
muñjabalvajair mūlaphalaiḥ || 1.21.1 ||
muñjabalvajās tṛṇaviśeṣāḥ || 1.21.1 ||
tṛṇakāṣṭhair avikṛtaiḥ || 1.21.2 ||
tṛṇānāṃ vikāro rajjvādibhāvaḥ, kāṣṭhānāṃ vikāraḥ sthūṇādibhāvaḥ | tṛṇatvād eva siddhe muñjabalvajagrahaṇaṃ vikārārtham || 1.21.2 ||
nātyantam anvavasyet || 1.21.3 ||
pratiṣiddhānām api vikrayavinimayābhyāṃ jīvet, na punar atyantam anvavasyet avasīdet | tathā ca gautamaḥ: “sarvathā tu vṛttir aśaktāv aśaudreṇa | tad apy eke prāṇasaṃśaye” iti [GDh 7.22–23] | manur api |
jīvitātyayam āpanno yo ‘nnam atti yatas tataḥ |
ākāśam iva paṅkena na sa doṣeṇa lipyate || [MDh 10.104]
iti || 1.21.3 ||
vṛttiṃ prāpya viramet || 1.21.4 ||
gatam || 1.21.4 ||
na patitaiḥ saṃvyavahāro vidyate || 1.21.5 ||
patitāḥ stenādayo vakṣyamāṇāḥ | taiḥ saha na kaścid api vyavahāraḥ kartavyaḥ | atra manuḥ |
saṃvatsareṇa patati patitena sahācaran |
yājanādhyāpanād yaunān na tu yānāsanāśanāt || iti | [MDh 11.181]
yānādibhiḥ saṃvatsareṇa patati, yājanādibhis tu sadya eva || 1.21.5 ||
tathāpapātraiḥ || 1.21.6 ||
apapātrāś caṇḍālādayaḥ | taiś ca saṃvyavahāro na kartavyaḥ || 1.21.6 ||
atha patanīyāni || 1.21.7 ||
dvijātikarmabhyo hāniḥ patanam, tasya nimittāni karmāṇi vakṣyante || 1.21.6 ||
steyam ābhiśastyaṃ puruṣavadho brahmojjhaṃ garbhaśātanaṃ mātuḥ pitur iti yonisaṃbandhe sahāpatye strīgamanaṃ surāpānam asaṃyogasaṃyogaḥ || 1.21.8 ||
steyaṃ suvarṇacauryam | ābhiśastyaṃ brahmahatyā, “brāhmaṇamātraṃ ca” [ĀpDh 1.24.7] hatvābhiśasta iti vakṣyamāṇatvāt | puruṣavadhaḥ manuṣyajātivadhaḥ | tena strīvadho ‘pi gṛhyate | brahmojjham, ujjha utsargaḥ, bhāve ghañ, chāndaso liṅgavyatyayaḥ | brahma vedaḥ, tasyādhītasya nāśanaṃ brahmojjham | auṣadhādiprayogeṇa garbhasya vadho garbhaśātanam | mātur yonisaṃbandhe mātṛṣvasrādau, pitur yonisaṃbandhe pitṛṣvasrādau, sahāpatye apatyena sahite, strīgamaṇaṃ mātṛṣvasṛgamanaṃ pitṛṣvasṛgamanaṃ tatsutāgamanaṃ mātulasutāgamanaṃ cety arthaḥ |
gauḍī mādhvī ca paiṣṭī ca vijñeyā trividhā surā |
yathaivaikā na pātavyā tathā sarvā dvijottamaiḥ || [MDh 11.95]
iti mānave niṣiddhāyāḥ surāyāḥ pānaṃ surāpānam | asaṃyogāḥ saṃyogānarhāḥ pratilomādayaḥ, taiḥ saṃyoga ekagṛhavāsādiḥ asaṃyogasaṃyogaḥ | etāni patanīyāni || 1.21.8 ||
gurvīsakhiṃ gurusakhiṃ ca gatvānyāṃś ca paratalpān || 1.21.9 ||
sakhīśabdasya chāndaso hrasvaḥ | gurvīsakhī mātrādīnāṃ sakhī, gurusakhī pitrādīnāṃ sakhī, tāṃ gatvā | kim | patatīty uttaratra śrutam apekṣate | anyāṃś ca paratalpān gatvā patati | talpaśabdena śayanavācinā dārā lakṣyante || 1.21.9 ||
nāgurutalpe patatīty eke || 1.21.10 ||
gurudāravyatirekeṇa parataplagamane pātityaṃ nāstīy eke manyante | yady api sāmānyena patanīyānīty uktam, prāyaścitte tu gurulaghubhāvo draṣṭavyam || 1.21.10 ||
adharmāṇāṃ tu satatam ācāraḥ || 1.21.11 ||
tuśabdaś cārthe | uktavyatiriktānām apy adharmāṇāṃ satatam ācāraḥ patanahetuḥ || 1.21.11 ||
athāśucikarāṇi || 1.21.12 ||
aśuciṃ puruṣaṃ kurvantīty aśucikarāṇi, karmāṇi tāni vakṣyante || 1.21.12 ||
śūdragamanam āryastrīṇām || 1.21.13 ||
traivarṇikastrīṇāṃ śūdragamanam aśucikaram || 1.21.13 ||
pratiṣiddhānāṃ māṃsabhakṣaṇam || 1.21.14 ||
yeṣāṃ māṃsaṃ pratiṣiddhaṃ teṣāṃ māṃsasya bhakṣaṇam aśucikaram || 1.21.14 ||
atrodāharaṇam |
śuno manuṣyasya ca kukkuṭasūkarāṇāṃ grāmyāṇāṃ
kravyādasām || 1.21.15 ||
grāmyāṇām iti vacanād āraṇyānām apratiṣedhaḥ | adanaṃ adaḥ | bhāve ‘sunpratyayaḥ | kravyaviṣayam adanaṃ yeṣāṃ te kravyādasaḥ, kevalamāṃsavṛttayo gṛdhrādayaḥ || 1.21.15 ||
manuṣyāṇāṃ mūtrapurīṣaprāśanam || 1.21.16 ||
mūtrapurīṣagrahaṇaṃ retaso ‘py upalakṣaṇam || 1.21.16 ||
śūdrocchiṣṭam apapātragamanaṃ cāryāṇām || 1.21.17 ||
śudrocchiṣṭaṃ bhuktam āryāṇāṃ traivarṇikāṇām aśucikaram | apapātrāḥ pratilomāḥ striyaḥ, tāsāṃ ca gamanam || 1.21.17 ||
etāny api patanīyānīty eke || 1.21.18 ||
yāny etāny aśucikaratvenānukrāntāni etāny api patanīyānīty eke manyante || 1.21.18 ||
ato ‘nyāni doṣavanty aśucikarāṇi bhavanti || 1.21.19 ||
uktavyatiriktāni doṣavanti karmāṇi duṣpratigrahahiṃsādīny aśucikarāṇi bhavanti || 1.21.19 ||
doṣaṃ buddhvā na pūrvaḥ parebhyaḥ patitasya samākhyāne
syād varjayet tv enaṃ dharmeṣu || 1.21.20 ||
