patitasya doṣaṃ parair aviditaṃ buddhvā parebhyaḥ samākhyāne pūrvo na syāt | parair aviditaṃ svayaṃ vidvān api na parebhyaḥ pūrvam ācakṣīta | kiṃ tu svayaṃ dharmakṛtyeṣv enaṃ varjayet, yathā pare na jānanti | anyathā doṣavān || 1.21.20 ||
ity ekaviṃśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
iti śrīharadattamiśraviracitāyām āpastambadharmasūtravyākhyāyām
ujjvalākhyāyāṃ saptamaḥ paṭalaḥ
adhyātmikān yogān anutiṣṭhen nyāyasaṃhitān
anaiścārikān || 1.22.1 ||
uktāni patanīyāny aśucikarāṇi ca karmāṇi | teṣāṃ prāyaścittāni vakṣyann ādita ātmajñānaṃ tadupayoginaś ca yogān adhikurute, tasyāpi sarvapāpaharatvena mukhyaprāyaścittatvāt | śrūyate hi:
bhidyate hṛdayagranthiś chidyante sarvasaṃśayāḥ |
kṣīyante cāsya karmāṇi tasmin dṛṣṭe parāvare || iti | [MuṇUp 2.2.8]
“tad yatheheṣīkatūlam agnau protaṃ pradūyetaivaṃ hāsya sarve pāpmānaḥ pradūyante” [ChUp 5.24.3] iti ca | yājñavalkyo ‘py āha |
ijyācāradamāhiṃsādānasvādhyāyakarmaṇām |
ayaṃ tu paramo dharmo yad yogenātmadarśanam || iti | [YDh 1.8]
adhyātmabhavān adhyātmikān | chāndaso vṛddhyabhāvaḥ | ātmano lambhayitṝn yogāṃś cittasamādhānahetūn vakṣyamāṇān akrodhādīn upāyān anutiṣṭhet seveta nyāyasaṃhitān upapattisamanvitān | upapadyante hi te nyāyataḥ krodhādīnāṃ doṣāṇāṃ nirghāte | anaiścārikān niścāraḥ cittasya bahirvikṣepaḥ, tasmai ye prabhavanti krodhādayo vakṣamāṇāḥ te naiścārikāḥ, tatpratipakṣabhūtān | akrodhādiṣu satsu cittam aniścaraṇaśīlam adhyātmalambanaṃ niścalaṃ tiṣṭhati, tasmāt tān anutiṣṭhet ātmānaṃ labdhum akrodhādilakṣaṇaṃ cittasamādhānaṃ kuryād iti || 1.22.1 ||
kiṃ punar ātmā prayatnena labdhavyaḥ | om ity āha |
ātmalābhān na paraṃ vidyate || 1.22.2 ||
ātmalābhāt param utkṛṣṭaṃ lābhāntaraṃ nāsti, tasmāt tasya lābhāya yatna āstheya iti | kaḥ punar asāv ātmā | pratyagātmā | nanv asau nityalabdhaḥ, na hi svayam eva svasyālabdho bhavati | satyam | prakṛtimelanāt taddharmatām upagato vinaṣṭasvarūpa iva bhavati | prakṛtyā hi nityasaṃbaddhaḥ puruṣaḥ | sa ca saṃbandhas tathāvidhaḥ yathā parasparaṃ viveko na jñāyate | anyo’nyadharmāś cānyo ‘nyatrādhyasyante yathā kṣīrodake saṃpṛkte na jñāyate iyat kṣīram iyad udakam, amuṣminn avakāśe kṣīram amuṣminn avakāśe udakam iti | yathā vā agnyayogolakayor abhisaṃbaddhayoḥ ye ‘gnidharmā uṣṇatvabhāsvaratvādayaḥ, te ‘yogolake ‘dhyasyante, ye cāyogolakasya dharmāḥ kāṭhinyadairghyādayaḥ te ‘gnāv adhyasyante | evaṃ hi tatra pratipattiḥ – ekaṃ vastu uṣṇaṃ bhāsvaraṃ dīrghaṃ kaṭhinam iti | tadvad ihāpi puruṣadharmāś caitanyādayaḥ prakṛtāv adhyasyante, prakṛtidharmāś ca sukhaduḥkhamohapariṇāmādayaḥ puruṣe | tataś caikaṃ vastu cetanaṃ sukhādikalilaṃ pariṇāmīti vyavahāraḥ | vastutas tu tasmin saṅghāte acetanāṃśaḥ pariṇāmī, cetanāṃśas tu tam anudhāvati | yena yena rūpeṇa pariṇamati tena tenābhedādhyāsam āpadyate | yathā kṣīrāvasthāgataṃ ghṛtaṃ kṣīre dadhyātmanā pariṇate tām apy avasthām anupraviśati, tadvad ihāpi | tad idam ucyate “tat sṛṣṭvā tad evānuprāviśat” iti [TaitUp 2.6] | sarge ‘py ātmanaḥ kartṛtvam idam eva, yad upabhoktṛtayā nimittatvam | tad evaṃ svabhāvataḥ svaccho ‘py ātmā prakṛtyā sahābhedam āpannaḥ taddharmā bhavati | evaṃ tadvikāreṇa mahatā tadvikāreṇāhaṃkāreṇety ā śarīrād draṣṭavyam, sthūlo ‘haṃ kṛśo ‘haṃ devo ‘haṃ manuṣyo ‘haṃ tiryag aham iti | tasyaivaṃgatasyāpekṣitaḥ svarūpalābhaḥ nīcair iva vardhitasya rājaputrasya | tad yathā – śabarādibhir bālyāt prabhṛti svasutaiḥ saha saṃvardhitas tajjātīyam ātmānam avagamayan mātrā svarūpe kathite labdhasvarūpa iva bhavati | tathā prakṛtyāvaśyayā svabhāvāntaraṃ nītaḥ ātmā mātṛsthānīyayā “tat tvam asi” iti śrutyā svabhāvaṃ nīyate; yad evaṃvidhaṃ pariśuddhaṃ vastu tad eva tvam asi, na tu yathā manyase manuṣyo ‘haṃ duḥkhy aham ityādi tatheti | yathā evaṃbhūto rājā sa tvam asīti rājaputraḥ | nanu “tat tvam asi” iti brahmaṇā tādātmyam ucyate | ko vā brūte neti, brahmāpi nānyad ātmanaḥ | kiṃ punar ayam ātmā ekaḥ, āho svin nānā | kim anena jñātena | tvaṃ tāvad evaṃvidhaś cidekarasaḥ nityanirmalaḥ kaluṣasaṃsargāt kaluṣatām iva gataḥ; tadviyogas te mokṣaḥ; tvayi mukte yady anye santi, te saṃsariṣyanti, kā te kṣatiḥ | atha na santi, tathāpi kas te lābhaḥ | ity alam iyatā, mahaty eṣā kathā | tad apy ete ślokā bhavanti |
nīcānāṃ vasatau tadīyatanayaiḥ sārdhaṃ ciraṃ vardhitaḥ
tajjātīyam avaiti rājatanayaḥ svātmānam apy añjasā |
saṃghāte mahadādibhiḥ saha vasan tadvat puraḥ puruṣaḥ
svātmānaṃ sukhaduḥkhamohakalilaṃ mithyaiva dhiṅ manyate ||
dātā bhogaparaḥ samagravibhavo yaḥ śāsitā duṣkṛtām
rājā sa tvam asīti mātṛmukhataḥ śrutvā yathāvat sa tu |
rājībhūya yathārtham eva yatate tadvat pumān bodhitaḥ
śrutvā tat tvam asīty apāsya duritaṃ brahmaiva saṃpadyate ||
ity evaṃ bahavo ‘pi rājatanayāḥ prāptā daśām idṛśīm
naivānyonyabhidām apāsya sahasā sarve bhajanty ekatām |
kiṃ tu sve parame pade pṛthag amī tiṣṭhanti bhinnās tathā
kṣetrajñā iti tattvamādivacasaḥ kā bhedavāde kṣatiḥ ||
teṣv eko yadi jātu mātṛvacanāt prāpto nijaṃ vaibhavam
nānye kā kṣatir asya yat kila pare santy anyathā ye sthitāḥ |
yad vānye na bhaveyur evam api ko lābho ‘sya tadvad gatiḥ
puṃsām ity abhidāṃ bhidāṃ ca na vayaṃ nirbandhya niścinmahe ||
tad ihāpekṣitam ātmajñānam upadiśyate | tac ca trividhaṃ śrutaṃ mananaṃ dhyānam iti, “śrotavyo mantavyo nididhyāsitavyaḥ” [BṛĀUp 2.4.5] iti śravaṇāt | tatra śrutam upaniṣadādiśabdajanyaṃ jñānam | mananam upapattibhir nirūpaṇam | evaṃ śrute mate cātmani sākṣātkārahetur avikṣiptena cetasā nirantarā bhāvanā dhyānam |
|| 1.22.2||
tatsiddhaye śrautaṃ jñānaṃ tāvad āha |
tatrātmalābhīyāñ chlokān udāhariṣyāmaḥ || 1.22.3 ||
tatreti vākyopanyāse | ātmalābhīyān ātmalābhaprayojanān | anupravacanādiṣu darśanāc chapratyayaḥ | ślokān pādabaddhān aupaniṣanmantrān udāhariṣyāmaḥ uddhṛtyāhariṣyāmaḥ granthe niveśayiṣyāmaḥ || 1.22.3 ||
pūḥ prāṇinaḥ sarva eva guhāśayasya
ahanyamānasya vikalmaṣasya |
acalaṃ calaniketaṃ ye ‘nutiṣṭhanti te ‘mṛtāḥ || 1.22.4 ||
guheti prakṛtināma,
yat tat smṛtaṃ kāraṇam aprameyaṃ brahma pradhānaṃ prakṛtiḥ prasūtiḥ |
ātmā guhā yonir anādyanantaṃ kṣetraṃ tathaivāmṛtam akṣaraṃ ca ||
iti purāṇe darśanāt | tasyāṃ śete tayā sahāvibhāgāpannas tiṣṭhatīti guhāśaya ātmā |
ajām ekāṃ lohitaśuklakṛṣṇāṃ bahvīṃ prajāṃ janayantīṃ sarūpām |
ajo hy eko juṣamāṇo ‘nuśete jahāty enāṃ bhuktabhogām ajo ‘nyaḥ || [ŚvUp 4.5]
iti ca mantrāntaram | ahanyamānasya, na hy asau śarīre hanyamāne ‘pi hanyate | tathā coktaṃ bhagavatā: “na hanyate hanyamāne śarīre” [BhG 2.20] | vikalmaṣasya nirlepasya, sarva eva hi dharmādharmādir antaḥkaraṇasya dharmaḥ, ātmani tv adhyastaḥ, evaṃbhūtasyātmanaḥ sarva eva prāṇinaḥ brahmādyās tiryagantāḥ prāṇādimantaḥ saṃghātāḥ pūḥ puram upabhogasthānam | yathā rājā puram adhivasan sacivair ānītān bhogān upabhuṅkte, tathāyaṃ devādiśarīram adhivasan karaṇair upasthāpitavān bhogān upabhuṅkte | tam evaṃbūtam acalaṃ sarvagatatvena niścalaṃ calaniketam, niketaṃ sthānaṃ śarīram, tad yasya calaṃ taṃ ye ‘nutiṣṭhanty upāsate evaṃbhūto ‘ham iti pratipadyante te ‘mṛtā muktā bhavantīti || 1.22.4 ||
tena viṣayasaṅgaparityāgenāyam upāsya ity āha |
yad idam idi hedi ha loke viṣayam ucyate |
vidhūya kavir etad anutiṣṭhed guhāśayam || 1.22.5 ||
yad idaṃ viṣayam etad iti sarvatra liṅgavyatyayaś chāndasaḥ, evam itiśabdasya takārasya dakāraḥ | itiśabdaḥ prasiddhau, haśabdaḥ āścarye | itiśabdenāvṛttena śabdādiṣu viṣayeṣu avāntaraprakārabhedaḥ pratipādyate | viṣayāpahṛtacetaso hi vadanti, iti ha tasyā gītam, iti ha tasyāḥ sukhasparśaḥ, iti ha tasyā rūpaṃ niṣṭaptam iva kanakam, iti ha tasyāḥ svādiṣṭo ‘dharamaṇiḥ, iti ha tasyā gandho ghrāṇatarpaṇa iti | evaṃ divyamānuṣabhedo ‘pi draṣṭavyaḥ | atrānantaram apara itiśabdo ‘dhyāhāryaḥ, iti heti heti loke yo ‘yaṃ viṣaya ucyate | sāmānyāpekṣam ekavacanam | etaṃ vidhūya guhāśayam anutiṣṭhet kavir medhāvīti || 1.22.5 ||
viṣayatyāge hetum āha |
ātmann evāham alabdhvaitad dhitaṃ sevasva nāhitam |
athānyeṣu pratīcchāmi sādhuṣṭhānam anapekṣayā |
mahāntaṃ tejasaḥ kāyaṃ sarvatra nihitaṃ prabhum || 1.22.6 ||
śiṣyaṃ praty ācāryasya vacanam etat | dvau cātra hetū viṣayāṇāṃ tyāge, parādhīnatvam ahitatvaṃ ca | mahāntaṃ guṇataḥ | tejasaḥ kāyaṃ tejasaḥ śarīraṃ tejorāśiṃ svayaṃprakāśam “ātmā [āditya] jyotiḥ samrāḍ iti hovāca” [BṛĀUp 6.3.2] iti bṛhadāraṇyakam | sarvatra nihitaṃ sarvagataṃ prabhuṃ svatantram, evaṃbhūtaṃ guhāśayam etāvantaṃ kālam aham ātman, saptamy ekavacanam asya luk, ātmany asmin madīye saṃghāte anyānapekṣayaiva labdhuṃ yogyam alabdhvā athānyeṣv indriyādiṣu taṃ taṃ viṣayaṃ pratīcchāmi, liṅarthe laṭ, pratyaiccham | idānīṃ tu taṃ labdhvā na tathāvidho ‘smi | tvam apy etad eva hitaṃ sādhuṣṭhānaṃ sādhumārgaṃ sevasva, nāhitaṃ viṣayānudhāvanam iti || 1.22.6 ||
punar apy asau kīdṛśa ity āha |
sarvabhūteṣu yo nityo vipaścid amṛto dhruvaḥ |
anaṅgo ‘śabdo ‘śarīro ‘sparśaś ca mahāñ chuciḥ |
sa sarvaṃ paramā kāṣṭhā sa vaiṣuvataṃ sa vai
vaibhājanaṃ puram || 1.22.7 ||
sarvabhūteṣu manuṣyādisaṃghāteṣu yo nityaḥ vinaśyatsu na vinaśyati, vipaścit medhāvī citsvarūpaḥ, amṛtaḥ nityatvād evāmaraṇadharmā, dhruvaḥ ekarūpaḥ vikārarahitaḥ, na pradhānavad vikāriṇaḥ sato dharmirūpeṇāsya nityatvam ity arthaḥ | anaṅgaḥ karacaraṇādyaṅgarahitaḥ | aśabdo ‘sparśa iti bhūtaguṇānām upalakṣaṇam, śabdādiguṇarahitaḥ | aśarīraḥ sūkṣmaśarīreṇāpi varjitaḥ | mahāñ chuciḥ, mahattvaṃ śaucasya viśeṣaṇam, paramārthato ‘tyantaśuddhaḥ | sa sarvaṃ prakṛtyabhedadvāreṇa | sa eva ca paramā kāṣṭhā, ataḥ paraṃ gantavyābhāvāt | sa vaiṣuvatam, viṣuvān nāma gavāmayanasya madhyebhavam ahaḥ | “ekaviṃśad etad ahar upayanti | viṣuvantaṃ madhye saṃvatsarasya” iti darśanāt | viṣuvān eva vaiṣuvatam, tad yathā saṃvatsarasya madhye bhavati, evam aṅgānām eṣa madhye bhavati | “madhyaṃ hy eṣo ‘ṅgānām ātmā” iti bahvṛcabrāhmaṇam | sa eva vaibhājanaṃ puram, vividhair mārgair bhajanīyaṃ vibhājanam, tad eva vaibhājanam | prajādir anuśatikādiś ca | yathā samṛddhaṃ puraṃ sarvair arthibhiḥ prāpyam, evam ayam apīti || 1.22.7 ||
taṃ yo ‘nutiṣṭhet sarvatra prādhvaṃ cāsya sadācaret |
durdarśaṃ nipuṇaṃ yukto yaḥ paśyet sa modeta viṣṭhape || 1.22.7 ||
tam evaṃbhūtam ātmānaṃ yo ‘nutiṣṭheta upāsīta, yaś cāsya sarvatra sarvāsv avasthāsu sadā prādhvam ānukūlyam ācaret, ānukūlyaṃ pratiṣiddhavarjanaṃ nityanaimittikakarmānuṣṭhānaṃ ca, yaś ca durdarśaṃ nipuṇaṃ sūkṣmam etaṃ yuktaḥ samāhito bhūtvā paśyet sākṣāt kuryāt, sa viṣṭape vigatatāpe sve mahimni sthito modate sarvaduḥkhavarjito bhavati, saṃsāradaśāyāṃ vā tirohitaṃ niratiśayaṃ svam ānandam anu bhavatīti || 1.22.7 ||
iti dvāviṃśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
ātman paśyan sarvabhūtāni na muhyec cintayan kaviḥ |
ātmānaṃ caiva sarvatra yaḥ paśyet sa vai brahmā
nākapṛṣṭhe virājate || 1.23.1 ||
sarvāṇi bhūtāni ātman ātmani śeṣatvena sthitāni paśyann upaniṣadādibhir jānan paścāc citayan yuktibhir nirūpayan yo na muhyet madhye mohaṃ na gacchet kavir medhāvī, paścāc ca sarvatraiva śeṣitvena sthitam ātmānaṃ paśyet sākṣāt kuryāt, sa vai brahmā brāhmaṇaḥ nākapṛṣṭhe tatsadṛśe sve mahimni sthito virājati svayaṃ prakāśate || 1.23.1 ||
nipuṇo ‘ṇīyān bisorṇāyā yaḥ sarvam
āvṛtya tiṣṭhati |
varṣīyāṃś ca pṛthivyā dhruvaḥ sarvam
ārabhya tiṣṭhati |
sa indriyair jagato ‘sya jñānād anyo
’nyasya jñeyāt parameṣṭhī vibhājaḥ ||
tasmāt kāyāḥ prabhavanti sarve
samūlaṃ śāśvatikaḥ sa nityaḥ || 1.23.2 ||
nipuṇo medhāvī citsvarūpaḥ bisorṇāyāḥ bisatantor api aṇīyān sūkṣmo yaḥ sarvam āvṛtya tiṣṭhati vyāpya sthitaḥ, yaś ca pṛthivyā api varṣīyān vṛddhataraḥ sarvagatatvād eva sarvam ārabhya viṣṭabhya śeṣitvenādhiṣṭhāya tiṣṭhati, dhruvaḥ ekarūpaḥ, asya jagato yad indriyair jñānam indriyayajaṃ jñānaṃ tasmāt, kīdrśāt, jñeyāt ananyasya, pañcamyarthe ṣaṣthī, jñeyān nīlapītādyākārād ananyabhūtaṃ nīlapītādyākāraṃ tasmād viṣayajñānād anya ity arthaḥ | śrūyate ca – “tasmād vā etasmād vijñānamayāt | anyontara ātmānandamayaḥ” [TaitUp 2.5] iti |
jñānasvarūpam atyantanirmalaṃ parmārthataḥ |
tam evārthasvarūpeṇa bhrāntidarśanataḥ sthitam || [ViPu 1.1.6]
iti purāṇam | svabhāvataḥ svacchasya cidrūpasyātmano nīlapītādyākārakāluṣyaṃ tadrūpāyā buddhivṛtter anurāgakṛtaṃ bhrāntam ity arthaḥ | vaiṣayikajñānād anya iti viśeṣaṇāt jñānātmaka ity api siddham | “satyaṃ jñānam anantaṃ brahma” [TaitUp 2.1] iti ca śrutiḥ | evaṃbhūtaḥ sa ātmā parameṣṭhī parame svarūpe tiṣṭhatīti | vibhāja ity asya pareṇa saṃbandhaḥ | vibhajaty ātmānaṃ devamanuṣyādirūpeṇa nānāśarīrānupraveśeneti vibhāk, tasmād vibhājo nimittabhūtāt sarve kāyāḥ devamanuṣyādiśarīrāṇi prabhavanti utpadyante | sa mūlaṃ sa prapañcasṛṣṭer bhoktṛtayā mūlakāraṇam, sa nityaḥ avināśī, śāśvatikaḥ ekarūpo ‘vikāraḥ || 1.23.2 ||
doṣāṇāṃ tu nirghāto yogamūla iha jīvite |
nirhṛtya bhūtadāhīyān kṣemaṃ gacchati paṇḍitaḥ || 1.23.3 |
doṣāṇāṃ vakṣyamāṇānāṃ krodhādīnāṃ nirghātaḥ nirmūlanam iha jīvite yogamūlaḥ, yogā vakṣyamāṇāḥ akrodhādayaḥ, tanmūlaḥ | tataś ca tān bhūtadāhīyān bhūtāni dahataḥ krodhādīn doṣān nirhṛtya kṣemaṃ gacchati ātmajñānadvāreṇa paṇḍito labdhajñānaḥ ātmasākṣātkārī kṣemam abhayaṃ mokṣam | “abhayaṃ vai janaka prāpnosi” [BṛĀUp 6.2.4] iti bṛhadāraṇyakam | samāptāś ślokāḥ || 1.13.3 ||
atha bhūtadāhīyān doṣān udāhariṣyāmaḥ || 1.23.4 ||
bhūtānāṃ dāho bhūtadāhaḥ, tasmai hitā bhūtadāhīyāḥ | “tasmai hitam” [Pāṇ 5.1.5] iti chaḥ || 1.13.4 ||
krodho harṣo roṣo lobho moho dambho droho mṛṣodyam
atyāśaparīvādāv asūyā kāmamanyū anātmyam ayogas teṣāṃ
yogamūlo nirghātaḥ || 1.23.5 ||
ākrośādihetuko ‘ntaḥkaraṇavikṣobhaḥ svedakampādiliṅgaḥ krodhaḥ | harṣaḥ iṣṭalabhāc cetasa udrekaḥ romāñcādiliṅgaḥ | roṣaḥ krodhasyaiva kiyān api bhedaḥ mitrādiṣu pratikūleṣu manaso vailomyamātrakāryakaram | lobho dravyasaṅgaḥ, yo dharmavyayam api ruṇaddhi | mohaḥ kāryākāryayor avivekaḥ, sa ca prāyeṇa krodhādijanyo ‘pi pṛthag upadiśyate, kadācit tu tadabhāve ‘pi saṃbhavatīti | dambho dhārmikatvaprakāśanena lokavañcanam | droho ‘pakāraḥ | mṛṣodyam anṛtavacanam | atyāśo ‘tyaśanam | parīvādaḥ paradoṣābhidhānam | asūyā paraguṇeṣv akṣamā | kāmaḥ strīsaṃgamaḥ | manyuḥ gūḍho dveṣaḥ | anātmyam ajitendriyatvaṃ jihvācāpalyādi | ayogo vikṣiptacittatā | ete bhūtadāhīyā doṣāḥ | teṣāṃ yogamūlo nighātaḥ || 1.23.5 ||
ke punas te yogāḥ | tān āha |
akrodho ‘harṣo ‘roṣo ‘lobho ‘moho ‘dambho ‘drohaḥ satyavacanam anatyāśo ‘paiśunam anasūyā saṃvibhāgas tyāga ārjavaṃ mārdavaṃ śamo damaḥ sarvabhūtair avirodho yoga āryam ānṛśaṃsaṃ tuṣṭir iti sarvāśramāṇāṃ samayapadāni tāny anutiṣṭhan vidhinā sārvagāmī bhavati || 1.23.6 ||
ete cākrodhādayo ‘pi bhāvarūpāḥ, na krodhādyabhāvamātram, krodhādinirghātahetutayopadeśāt | ke punas te | krodhādiṣu prasakteṣv api nākārṣam iti saṃkalpaḥ | aharṣaḥ iṣṭalābhālābheṣu cetasa aikarūpyam | aroṣo mitrādiṣu pratikūleṣv api manovikārābhāvaḥ | alobhaḥ santoṣo ‘lambuddhiḥ | amoho ‘vadhānam | adambho darmārtham anuṣṭhānam | adrohaḥ pareṣv apakāriṣv apy anapakāraḥ | anasūyā paraguṇeṣv anumodanam | satyavacanaṃ yathādṛṣṭārthavāditvam | saṃvibhāgaḥ ātmānam uparundhyāpi agrādidānam | tyāgo ‘parigrahaḥ | ārjavaṃ manovākkāyānām ekarūpatā | mārdavaṃ sūpagamatā | śamaḥ manyuparityāgaḥ | dama indriyajayaḥ | etābhyām eva gatatvāt pūrvatra svasmin krame akāmaḥ, amanyuḥ, ātmavattvam iti nopadiṣṭam | sarvabhūtair avirodhaḥ | sarvagrahaṇaṃ kṣudrair apy avirodhārtham | yoga aikāgryam | āryāṇāṃ bhāva āryam, śiṣṭācārānupālanam | ānṛśaṃsam ānṛśamśyaṃ vyavahāravacanādau prasaktasyāpi naiṣṭhuryasya varjanam | tuṣṭiḥ anirvedaḥ | samayo vyavasthā, sā ca prakaraṇād dharmajñānām, padaṃ viṣayaḥ | ete akrodhādayaḥ sarveṣām āśramāṇāṃ sevyāḥ, na kevalaṃ yoginām eveti dharmajñānāṃ samaya ity arthaḥ | te hi bhāvyamānāḥ krodhādīn samūlaghātaṃ ghnanti | ataś ca tāny anutiṣṭhan vidhinā sārvagāmī bhavati | tāny akordhādīni tuṣṭyantāni vidhinā yathāśāstram anutiṣṭhan sarvasmai hitaḥ sārvaḥ ātmā, taṃ gacchati prāpnoti | vidhineti vacanāt “prāṇināṃ tu vadho yatra tatra sākṣy anṛtaṃ vadet” ityādike viṣaye anṛtavacanādāv api na doṣa iti || 1.23.6 ||
iti trayoviṃśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
ity āpastambadharmasūtravyākhyāyām ujjvalāyāṃ aṣṭamaḥ paṭalaḥ
kṣatriyaṃ hatvā gavāṃ sahasraṃ vairayātanārthaṃ
dadyāt || 1.24.1 ||
kṣatriyaṃ hatvā gavāṃ sahasraṃ brāhmaṇebhyo dadyāt | kimartham | vairayātanārtham, vairaṃ pāpam, tasya yātanaṃ nirharaṇam, tadartham | “ṛṣabhaś cātrādhikaḥ sarvatra prāyaścittārthaḥ” [ĀpDh 1.24.4] iti vakṣyati | tena prāyaścittarūpam idaṃ dānam | prāyaścittaṃ ca pāpakṣayartham | tat kimarthaṃ vairayātanārtham ucyate | kecin manyante – “nābhuktaṃ kṣīyate karma” iti puṇyam apuṇyaṃ ca | prāyaścittaṃ tu naimittikaṃ karmāntaram, yathā gṛhadāhādau kṣāmavatyādaya iti | tān nirākartum idam uktam | śraute ‘py uktam: “doṣanirghātārthāni bhavanti” iti [ĀpŚr 9.1.4] | apara āha – yo yena hanyate sa tena miryamāṇas tasmin vairaṃ karoti api nāmāham enaṃ janmāntare vadhyāsam iti, tasya vairasya yātanārtham idam iti | prāyaścittārthatvam api vakṣyamāṇena siddham iti || 1.24.1 ||
śataṃ vaiśye || 1.24.2 ||
vaiśye hate gavāṃ śataṃ dadyāt || 1.24.2 ||
daśa śūdre || 1.24.3 ||
śūdre hate gavāṃ daśa dadyāt | gā iti prakaraṇāḍ gamyate || 1.24.3 ||
ṛṣabhaś cātrādhikaḥ sarvatra prāyaścittārthaḥ || 1.24.4 ||
sarveṣv eteṣu nimitteṣu ṛṣabho ‘py adhiko deyaḥ | na kevalaṃ gā eva | idaṃ prāyaścittatrayaṃ mānave samānaviṣayam | yathāha |
akāmatas tu rājanyaṃ vinipātya dvijottamaḥ |
ṛṣabhaikasahasraṃ gā dadyāc chuddhyartham ātmanaḥ ||
tryabdaṃ cared vā niyato jaṭī brahmahaṇo vratam |
vasan dūratare grāmād vṛkṣamūlaniketanaḥ ||
etad eva cared abdaṃ prāyaścittaṃ dvijottamaḥ |
pramāpya vaiśyo vṛttasthaṃ dadyād vaikaśataṃ gavām ||
etad eva vratam kṛtsnaṃ ṣaṇmāsān śūdrahā caret |
ṛṣabhaikādaśā vāpi dadyād viprāya gāḥ sthitāḥ || [MDh 11.127–30]
|| 1.24.4 ||
strīṣu caiteṣām evam || 1.24.5 ||
eteṣāṃ kṣatriyādīnāṃ strīṣu ca hatāsv evam eva prāyaścittam, yathā puruṣeṣu || 1.24.5 ||
pūrvayor varṇayor vedādhyāyaṃ hatvā savanagataṃ
vābhiśastaḥ || 1.24.6 ||
ukteṣu yau pūrvau varṇau kṣatriyavaiśyau tayor yo vedādhyāyaḥ adhītavedaḥ, taṃ hatvābhiśasto bhavati | abhiśasta iti brahmaghno ‘bhidhānam | savanagataṃ vā, tayor eva varṇayor yaḥ savanagataḥ | savanaśabdena na prātaḥsavanādīny ucyante | nāpi yāgamātram | kiṃ tarhi | somayāgaḥ | tatra yo dīkṣitaḥ savanagataḥ “brāḥmaṇo vā eṣa jāyate yo dīkṣitaḥ” iti darśanāt | taṃ ca hatvābhiśasto bhavati | pūrvayor varṇayor iti kim | brāhmaṇe mā bhūd iti | iṣyate brāhmaṇe, vakṣyati ca “brāhmaṇamātraṃ ca” iti [ĀpDh 1.24.7 ] | evaṃ tarhi śūdra mā bhūt | na śūdro vedādhyāyaḥ savanagato vā bhavati | idaṃ tarhi prayojanaṃ pūrvayor varṇayor eva yathā syāt, tayor eva yāv anulomau karaṇāmbaṣṭhau tayor mā bhūd iti | tenānye varṇadharmā anulomānām api bhavanti || 1.24.6 ||
brāhmaṇamātraṃ ca || 1.24.7 ||
hatvābhiśasto bhavati | mātragrahaṇān nābhijanavidyāsaṃskārāpekṣā || 1.24.7 ||
garbhaṃ ca tasyāvijñātam || 1.24.8 ||
tasya brāhmaṇamātrasya garbhaṃ ca strīpunnapuṃsakabhedenāvijñātaṃ hatvābhiśasto bhavati
|| 1.24.8 ||
ātreyīṃ ca striyam || 1.24.9 ||
“ṛtusnātām ātreyīm āhuḥ” [VaDh 10.94] iti vasiṣṭhaḥ | tasyeti vartate | ātreyīṃ ca brāhmaṇastriyaṃ hatvā abhiśasto bhavati brahmahā bhavati, saṃbhavaty asyāṃ brāhmaṇagarbha iti | atrigotrajā ātreyīty anye || 1.24.9 ||
tasya nirveṣaḥ || 1.24.10 ||
tasya sarvaprakārābhiśastasya nirveṣaḥ prāyaścittaṃ vakṣyate || 1.24.10 ||
araṇye kuṭiṃ kṛtvā vāgyataḥ śavaśiradhvajo ‘rdhaśāṇīpakṣam
adhonābhyuparijānv ācchādya || 1.24.11 ||
kṛveti vacanān na parakṛtā kuṭī grāhyā | vāg yatā yena sa vāgyataḥ vācaṃyamaḥ | āhitāgnyādiṣu darśanān niṣṭhāntasya paranipātaḥ | śavaśiro dhvajo yasya sa śavaśiradhvajaḥ | salopaś chāndasaḥ | svavyāpāditasya brāhmaṇasya śiro dhvajadaṇḍasyāgre protaṃ kṛtvety arthaḥ | yasya kasyacic chavasyety anye | śaṇasya vikāraḥ śāṇī paṭī, tasyā ardham ardhaśāṇī, tasyāḥ pakṣam ardhaśāṇīpakṣam āyāmavistārayor ubhayor apy ardham | adhonābhyuparijānu ca yathā bhavati tathācchadya tāvantaṃ pradeśam ācchadya | sāpekṣatvāt “grāme pratitiṣṭheta” [ĀpDh 1.24.14] iti vakṣyamāṇena saṃbandhaḥ | madhye kriyāntaravidhiḥ || 1.24.11 ||
tasya panthā antarā vartmanī || 1.24.12 ||
tasya grāmaṃ praviśataḥ vartmanī antarā śakaṭāder vartmanor madhye panthā veditavyaḥ | apara āha – yatra rathyādāv ubhayoḥ pārśvayoḥ vartmanī bhavataḥ, tatra tayor madhyena sūkarādipathena saṃcared ity arthaḥ || 1.24.12 ||
dṛṣṭvā cānyam utkrāmet || 1.24.13 ||
anyam āryaṃ dṛṣṭvā patha utkrāmet | tatra kauṭilyaḥ: “pañcāratnī rathapathaś catvāro hastipathaḥ dvau kṣudramanuṣyāṇām” iti [KĀŚ 2.4.5] | tena manuṣyeṣu dvau hastāv utkrāmed iti || 1.24.13 ||
khaṇḍena lohitakena śarāveṇa grāme pratitiṣṭheta || 1.24.14 ||
dharmamātraṃ khaṇḍanam | lohitakam anāprītam | evaṃbhūtaṃ śarāvaṃ bhikṣāpātraṃ gṛhītvā grāme pratitiṣṭheta gramaṃ gacchet || 1.24.14 ||
ko ‘bhiśastāya bhikṣām iti saptāgāraṃ caret || 1.24.15 ||
abhiśasto brahmahā, tasmai mahyaṃ ko dhārmiko bhikṣāṃ dadātīty uccair bruvānaḥ saptāgārāṇi caret | saptagrahaṇam adhikanivṛttyartham | dvitriṣv evāgāreṣu yadi paryāptaṃ labhyate tadā tāvaty eva || 1.24.15 ||
sā vṛttiḥ || 1.24.16 ||
saptasv agāreṣu yā ca yāvatī labhate saiva vṛttir aparyāptāpi || 1.24.16 ||
alabdhopavāsaḥ || 1.24.17 ||
yadi saptasv agāreṣu na kiṃcil labhyate tadopavāsa eva tasminn ahani || 1.24.17 ||
gāś ca rakṣet || 1.24.18 ||
evaṃ prāyaścittaṃ kurvann ahar ahar gāś ca rakṣet || 1.24.18 ||
tāsāṃ niṣkramaṇapraveśane dvitīyo grāme ‘rthaḥ || 1.24.19 ||
tāsāṃ gavāṃ niṣkramaṇasamaye praveśasamaye ca dvitīyo grāme ‘rthaḥ prayojanam | bhikṣārtham prathamam uktam | nānyadā grāmaṃ praveśed ity uktaṃ bhavati || 1.24.19 ||
dvādaśavarṣāṇi caritvā siddhaḥ sadbhiḥ saṃprayogaḥ || 1.24.20 ||
evaṃ dvādaśavarṣāṇi vratam etac caritvā sadbhiḥ saṃprayogaḥ kartavyaḥ | sadbhiḥ saha saṃprayujyate yena vidhinā, sa kartavyaḥ | sa ca śiṣṭācāre śāstrāntare ca siddhaḥ | sa ucyate – kṛtaprāyaścittaḥ svahaste yavasaṃ gṛhītvā gām āhvayet | sā yady āgatya śraddadhānā bhakṣayati, tadā samyag anena vrataṃ caritam iti jānīyāt, anyathā neti || 1.24.20 ||
ājipathe vā kuṭiṃ kṛtvā brāhmaṇagavyo ‘pajigīṣamāṇo vaset triḥ
pratirāddho ‘pajitya vā muktaḥ || 1.24.21 ||
saṃgrāmeṇa jetavyā dasyavo yena pathā grāmaṃ praviśya gavādikam apahṛtyāpasaranti sa ājipathaḥ | tasmin vā kuṭiṃ kṛtvā vaset | kiṃ cikīrṣan | brāhmaṇgavyaḥ, “vā chandasi” (Pāṇ 6.1.106) iti śasi pūrvasavarṇābhāve yaṇādeśaḥ, brāhmaṇagavīr apajigīṣamāṇaḥ dasyūn apajitya pratyāhartum icchan | evaṃ vasan dasyubhiḥ hriyamāṇaṃ gavādikam uddiśya tair yuddhaṃ kurvan triḥ pratirāddhaḥ tair apajitya, apajitya vā tān, gavādikaṃ pratyāhṛtya brāhmaṇebhyo datvā mukto bhavati tasmād enasaḥ | dvādaśavārṣike pravṛttasyedam | evam uttaram api
|| 1.24.21 ||
āśvamedhikaṃ vāvabhṛtam avetya mucyate || 1.24.22 ||
atha vā aśvamedhāvabhṛte snātvā mucyate || 1.24.22 ||
dharmārthasaṃnipāte ‘rthagrāhiṇa etad eva || 1.24.23 ||
dharmasyāgnihotrāder arthasya kuḍyakaraṇāder yatra yugapat saṃnipātas tatrobhayānugrahāsaṃbhave dharmalopena yo ‘rthaṃ gṛhṇāti tasyāpy etad eva prāyaścittam | atha vā yo dharmaṃ hitvārthahetoḥ kauṭasākṣyādikaṃ karoti tadviṣayam etat | atra gautamaḥ: “kauṭasākṣyaṃ rājagāmi paiśunaṃ guror anṛtābhiśaṃsanaṃ pātakasamāni” iti [GDh 20.9] | manur api |
anṛtaṃ ca samutkarṣe rājagāmini paiśunam |
guroś cālīkanirbandhaḥ samāni brahmahatyayā || iti | [MDh 11.56]
|| 1.24.23 ||
guruṃ hatvā śrotriyaṃ vā karmasamāptam etenaiva
vidhinottamād ucchvāsāc caret || 1.24.24 ||
guruḥ pitrācāryādiḥ | śrotriyo ‘dhītavedaḥ | sa yadi karmasamāpto bhavati | somāntāni karmāṇi samāptāni yasya sa evam ucyate | tau hatvā etenaivānantaroktena vidhinā ottamād ucchvāsāc caret | uttama ucchvāsaḥ prāṇaviyogaḥ, ā tasmāc caret || 1.24.24 ||
nāsyāsmin loke pratyāpattir vidyate || 1.24.25 ||
aśvamedhāvabhṛtādiṣu saṃbhavatsv apy asyāsmin loke asmin jīvite pratyāpattiḥ śuddhir nāstīty arthaḥ || 1.24.25 ||
kalmaṣaṃ tu nirhaṇyate || 1.24.26 ||
mṛtasya tu kalmaṣaṃ nirhaṇyate | tena putrādibhiḥ saṃskārādyaurdhvadaihikāḥ kāryā iti bhāvaḥ | anye tu pūrvasūtraṃ tannivṛttyarthaṃ manyante, pratyāpattiḥ putrādibhiḥ pitrādibhāvena saṃbandha iti || 1.24.26 ||
iti caturviṃśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
gurutalpagāmī savṛṣaṇaṃ śiśnaṃ parivāsyāñjalāv ādhāya
dakṣiṇāṃ diśam anāvṛttiṃ vrajet || 1.25.1 ||
gurur atra pitā, nācāryādiḥ | talpaśabdena śayanavācinā bhāryā lakṣyate | sā ca sākṣāj jananī, na tatsapatnī | tāṃ gatvā savṛṣaṇaṃ sāṇḍaṃ śiśnaṃ parivāsya kṣurādinā chitvā añjalāv ādhāya dakṣiṇāṃ diśaṃ vrajet | anāvṛttim, āvṛttir na kriyate yasyāṃ tām diśam anāvartamāno gacched iti | atha ye dakṣiṇasyodadhes tīraṃ vasanti, te ‘pi yāvad deśaṃ gatvā udadhim eva pravekṣyanti, maraṇaṃ hy atra vivakṣitam | atra saṃvartaḥ |
pitṛdārāḥ samāruhya mātṛvarjaṃ narādhamaḥ |
bhaginīṃ mātur āptāṃ vā svasāraṃ vānyamātṛjām |
etā gatvā striyo mohāt taptakṛcchāṃs samācaret || iti |
nāradas tu |
mātā mātṛṣvasā śvaśrūr mātulānī pitṛṣvasā |
pitṛvyapatnī śiṣyastrī bhaginī tatsakhī snuṣā ||
duhitācāryabhāryā ca sagotrā śaraṇāgatā |
rājñī pravrajitā dhātrī sādhvī varṇottamā ca yā ||
āsām anyatamāṃ gatvā gurutalpaga ucyate |
śiśnasyotkṛntanaṃ tatra nāno daṇḍo vidhīyate || iti || 1.25.1 ||
jvalitāṃ vā sūrmiṃ pariṣvajya samāpnuyāt || 1.25.2 ||
āyasī tāmramayī vā antaḥsuṣirā strīpratikṛtir atra sūrmiḥ | tāṃ jvalitām agnau taptāṃ pariṣvajya samāpnuyāt samāptiṃ gacchet mriyeta || 1.25.2 ||
surāpo ‘gnisparśāṃ surāṃ pibet || 1.25.3 ||
gauḍī sādhvī ca paiṣṭī ca vijñeyā trividhā surā |
yathaivaikā na patavyā tathā sarvā dvijottamaiḥ || iti | [MDh 11.95]
tasyāḥ pātā surāpaḥ | so ‘gnisparśāṃ atiśrapitāṃ surāṃ pibet | tayā dagdhakāyaḥ śuddhyati
|| 1.25.3 ||
stenaḥ prakīrṇakeśo ‘ṃse musalam ādhāya rājānaṃ gatvā
karmācakṣīta tenainaṃ hanyāt | vadhe mokṣaḥ || 1.25.4 ||
steno brāhmaṇasuvarṇahārī | aṃse sve skandhe musalam ādhāya āyasaṃ khādiraṃ vā dhārayan rājānaṃ gatvā karmācakṣīta evaṃ karmāsmi śādhi mām iti | sa tena musalena enaṃ stenaṃ hanyāt yathā mṛto bhavati | vadhena steyān mokṣo bhavati || 1.25.4 ||
anujñāte ‘nujñātāram enaḥ spṛśati || 1.25.5 ||
yadi tu rājā dayādinā tam anujānīyāt gaccheti, tadā tam anujñātāraṃ rājānam eva tad enaḥ spṛśati || 1.25.5 ||
uttaram ṛju |
agniṃ vā praviśet || 1.25.6 ||
tīkṣṇaṃ vā tapa āyacchet || 1.25.7 ||
tīkṣṇaṃ tapo mahāparākādi | tad vā āyacchet āvartayet || 1.25.7 ||
bhaktāpacayena vātmānaṃ samāpnuyāt || 1.25.8 ||
bhaktam annam | tasyāpacayo hrāsaḥ | prathame dine yāvanto grāsāḥ, te ekena nyūnā dvitīye | evaṃ tṛtīyādiṣv api ā ekasmād grāsāt | tatrāpi yadi na samāptiḥ, tatas tatraiva grāsaparimāṇāpacayaḥ kartavyaḥ | evaṃ bhaktāpacayena vātmānaṃ samāpnuyāt samāpayet
|| 1.25.8 ||
kṛcchrasaṃvatsaraṃ vā caret || 1.25.9 ||
atha vā saṃvatsaram ekaṃ nairantaryeṇa kṛcchraṃ caret | eṣām enaḥsu guruṣu gurūṇi laghuṣu laghūnīti vyavasthā || 1.25.9 ||
athāpy udāharanti || 1.25.10 ||
asminn eva viṣaye purāṇaślokam apy udāharantīty arthaḥ || 1.25.10 ||
steyaṃ kṛtvā surāṃ pītvā gurudāraṃ ca gatvā
brahmahatyām akṛtvā |
caturthakālā mitabhojinaḥ syur
apo ‘bhyavaseyuḥ savanānukalpam |
sthānāsanābhyāṃ viharanta ete
tribhir varṣair apa pāpaṃ nudante || 1.25.11 ||
brahmahatyāvyatiriktāni steyādīni kṛtvā caturthakālāḥ caturtho bhojanakālo yeṣāṃ te, yathā adya divā bhuṅkte śvo rātrāv iti, te tathoktāḥ | tathāpi mitabhojinaḥ, na mṛṣṭāśinaḥ | apo ‘bhyaveyuḥ bhūmigatāsv apsu snānaṃ kuryuḥ | savanānukalpam, yathā savanāni prātaḥsavanādīni anukḷptāni anuvṛttāni bhavanti, tathā, triṣavaṇam ity arthaḥ | tiṣṭheyur ahani, rātrāv āsīran, evaṃ sthānāsanābhyāṃ viharantaḥ kālaṃ kṣipantaḥ ete tribhir varṣais tat pāpam apanudante || 1.15.11 ||
prathamaṃ varṇaṃ parihāpya prathamaṃ varṇaṃ hatvā
saṃgrāmaṃ gatvāvatiṣṭheta | tatrainaṃ hanyuḥ || 1.25.12 ||
prathamo varṇo brāhmaṇas taṃ hatvā saṃgrāmaṃ gatvā senayor madhye ‘vatiṣṭheta | kiṃ sarve | nety āha – prathamaṃ varṇaṃ parihāpya brāhmaṇavarjam itaro varṇaḥ kṣatriyādir ity arthaḥ | tatra sthitam enaṃ te sainikā hanyuḥ, aghnantas te enasvinaḥ syuḥ, yathā rājā stenam | sa mṛtaḥ śudhyati || 1.15.12 ||
api vā lomāni tvacaṃ māṃsam iti hāvayitvāgniṃ
praviśet || 1.25.13 ||
anantaroktaviṣaya eva prāyaścittāntaram | itiśabdo lohitādīnām apy upalakṣaṇārthaḥ | ātmano lomādīny utkṛtya purohitena hāvayitvā homaṃ kārayitvā paścāt svayaṃ tasminn agnau praviśet | mṛtaḥ śudhyati | tatrāgnim upasamādhāya juhuyāt – “lomāni mṛtyor juhomi lomabhir mṛtyuṃ vāsaye svāhā | tvacaṃ mṛtyor juhomi tvacā mṛtyuṃ vāsaye svāhā | lohitaṃ mṛtyor juhomi lohitena mṛtyuṃ vāsaye svāhā | snāvāni mṛtyor juhomi snāvabhir mṛtyuṃ vāsaye svāhā | māṃsāni mṛtyor juhomi māṃsair mṛtyuṃ vāsaye svāhā | asthīni mṛtyor juhomi asthibhir mṛtyuṃ vāsaye svāhā | majjānaṃ mṛtyor juhomi majjabhir mṛtyuṃ vāsaye svāhā | medo mṛtyor juhomi medasā mṛtyuṃ vāsaye svāhā” ity ete mantrā vasiṣṭhena paṭhitāḥ [VaDh 20.26] || 1.25.13 ||
vāyasapracalākabarhiṇacakravākahaṃsabhāsamaṇḍūka-
nakulaḍerikāśvahiṃsāyāṃ śūdravat prāyaścittam || 1.25.14 ||
vāyasaḥ kākaḥ, pracalākaḥ kāmarūpī kṛkalāsaḥ, barhiṇo mayūraḥ, cakravāko divā mithunacaraḥ rātrau virahī, haṃso mānasavāsī, bhāso gṛdhraviśeṣaḥ, aśvanakulamaṇḍūkāḥ prasiddhāḥ | ḍerikā gandhamūṣikā | eṣāṃ samuditānāṃ vadhe śūdravat prāyaścittam | pratyekavadhe tu tat kalpyam | kecit pratyekavadhe etat prāyaścittam ity āhuḥ || 1.25.14 ||
iti pañcaviṃśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
dhenvanaḍuhoś cākāraṇāt || 1.26.1 ||
dhenuḥ payasvinī gauḥ, anaḍvān anovahanayogyo balīvardaḥ, tayoḥ kāraṇam antareṇa hiṃsāyāṃ śūdravat prāyaścittaṃ kartavyam | kāraṇaṃ kopo māṃsecchā vā, tena vinā, abuddhipūrvam ity arthaḥ | buddhipūrvaṃ cet vaiśyavad ityādi smṛtyantare draṣṭavyam || 1.26.1 ||
dhuryavāhapravṛttau cetareṣāṃ prāṇinām || 1.26.2 ||
dhuraṃ vahatīti dhuryo balīvardaḥ, tena voḍhuṃ śakyā dhuryavāhāḥ | tāvatsu hiṃsāyāṃ pravṛttau satyāṃ itareṣāṃ prāṇinām, kevalaṃ prāṇā eva yeṣāṃ nāsthīni santi, teṣāṃ hiṃsāyāṃ śūdravat prāyaścittam iti | atra gautamaḥ: “asthanvatāṃ sahasraṃ hatvā | anasthimatām anaḍudbhāre ca” iti [GDh 22.20–21] || 1.26.2 ||
anākrośyam ākruśyānṛtaṃ voktvā trirātram
akṣīrākṣāralavaṇabhojanam || 1.26.3 ||
yo na kathaṃcanākrośam arhati sa pitrācāryādiḥ anākrośyaḥ, tam ākruśya nindāṃ kṛtvā, anṛtaṃ voktvā pātakopapātakavarjam, trirātraṃ bhojane kṣīrādi varjayet | kṣīragrahaṇena vikārāṇāṃ dadhyādīnām api grahaṇam ity āhuḥ || 1.26.3 ||
śūdrasya saptarātram abhojanam || 1.26.4 ||
śūdras tv anantarokte viṣaye saptarātram upavaset || 1.26.4 ||
strīṇāṃ caivam || 1.26.5 ||
“kṣatriyaṃ hatvā” [ĀpDh 1.24.1] ityādiṣv anṛtavacanānteṣu nimitteṣu yāni prāyaścittāny uktāni strīṇām apy evam eva kartavyāni | etat “catvāro varṇāḥ” [ĀpDh 1.1.4] iti jātyabhidānād eva prāptaṃ san niyamārtham ucyate, ata ūrdhvaṃ puruṣasyaiva na strīṇām iti |
apara āha – jātyabhidhānād eva siddhe atideśārthṃ vacanam | atideśeṣu cārdhaṃ prāpyate iti smārto nyāyaḥ | tena strīṇām apy ardhaprāptyarthaṃ vacanam iti | tathā ca bhārgavaḥ |
aśītir yasya varṣāṇi bālo vāpy ūnaṣoḍaśaḥ |
prāyaścittārdham arhanti striyo vyādhita eva ca || 1.26.5 ||
yeṣv ābhiśastyaṃ teṣām ekāṅgaṃ chitvāprāṇihiṃsāyām || 1.26.6 ||
yeṣu hateṣu “savanagataṃ vābhiśastaḥ” [ĀpDh 1.24.6] ityādinā abhiśastatvam uktam, teṣām ekāṅgaṃ chitvā śūdravat prāyaścittaṃ kuryāt | aprāṇihiṃsāyām, yad chedanena tasyāṅgasya śaktir nihanyate || 1.26.6 ||
anāryavapaiśunapratiṣiddhācāreṣv abhakṣyābhojyāpeyaprāśane śūdrāyāṃ ca retaḥ siktvāyonau ca doṣavac ca karmābhisaṃdhipūrvaṃ kṛtvānibhisaṃdhipūrvaṃ vābliṅgābhir apa upaspṛśed vāruṇībhir vānyair vā pavitrair yathā karmābhyāsaḥ || 1.26.7 ||
āryāṇāṃ bhāvaḥ āryam, tad yasminn ācāre ‘sti tad āryavam, matvarthī yocpratyayaḥ | tato ‘nyad anāryavam asatyabhāṣaṇādi | paiśunaṃ paradoṣakathanaṃ rājagāmi | pratiṣiddhācāraḥ “ṣṭhīvanamaithunayoḥ karmāpsu varjayet” [ĀpDh 1.30.19] ityāder anuṣṭhānam | abhakṣyaṃ vṛthākṛsarādi | abhojyaṃ keśakīṭādyupahatam | apeyam “anirdaśāyā goḥ kṣīram” ityādi [cf. GDh 17.22] | eteṣāṃ prāśane | śūdrāyāṃ ca veśyāprabhṛtau retaḥ siktvā, ayonau ca jalādau retaḥ siktvā, doṣavac ca karma śrautam ābhicārikam, abhisaṃdhipūrvaṃ buddhipūrvaṃ kṛtvā, anabhisaṃdhipūrvaṃ vā parapīḍākaraṃ karma kṛtvā abliṅgābhiḥ “ āpo hi ṣṭhā mayo bhuvaḥ” [TS 5.6.1] iti tisṛbhiḥ, “hiraṇyavārṇāḥ śucayaḥ pāvakāḥ” [TS 5.6.1] iti catasṛbhir apa upaspṛśet | tūṣṇīṃ prathamaṃ snātvā paścād etair mantrair mārjanaṃ kuryāt | vāruṇībhir vā “imaṃ me varuṇa tat tvā yāmi tvan no agne sa tvan no agne” [TS 4.2.11] ity etābhir anyair vā pavitraiḥ “pavamānaḥ suvarjanaḥ” [TaitBr 1.4.8] ity etenānuvākena, śuddhavatībhiḥ [RV 8.95.7] taratsamandīyena [RV 9.58.1] ca | yathā karmābhyāsaḥ kṛtaḥ, tāvat kṛtvāpa upaspṛśet | rahasyaprāyaścittam etad ity āhuḥ || 1.26.7 ||
gardabhenāvakīrṇī nirṛtiṃ pākayajñena yajeta || 1.26.8 ||
yo brahmacārī striyam upeyāt so ‘vakīrṇī gardabhena nirṛtiṃ pākayajñena sthālīpākavidhānena yajeta | atra manuḥ |
avakīrṇī tu kāṇena gardabhena catuṣpathe |
pākayajñavidhānena yajeta nirṛtiṃ niśi || [MDh 11.118]
hārītas tu: “strīṣv avakīrṇī nirṛtyai catuṣpathe gardabhaṃ paśum ālabheta pākayajñena dharmeṇa bhūmau paśupuroḍāśaśrapaṇam apsv avadānaiḥ pracaryājyena juhoti, kāmāvakīrṇo ‘smy avakīrṇo ‘smi kāmakāmāya svāhā, kāmābhidrugdho ‘smy abhidrugdho ‘smi kāmakāmāya svāhā” iti || 1.26.8 ||
tasya śūdraḥ prāśnīyāt || 1.26.9 ||
tasya gardabhasya sarpiṣmad dhavirucchiṣṭaṃ śūdraḥ prāśnīyāt | “tena sarpiṣamatā brāhmaṇam” [ĀpGṛ 7.15] ity asyāpavādaḥ || 1.26.9 ||
mithyādhītaprāyaścittam || 1.26.10 ||
niyamātikrameṇādhītaṃ mithyādhītam, taddoṣanirharaṇāya prāyaścittaṃ vakṣyate || 1.26.10 ||
saṃvatsaram ācāryahite vartamāno vācaṃ yacchet svādhyāya evotsṛjamāno vācam ācārya ācāryadāre bhikṣācarye
ca || 1.26.11 ||
ācāryasya hite vartamāno bhūtvā saṃvatsaraṃ vācaṃyamaḥ syāt | vāgutsargaḥ svādhyāya eva, ācārye taṃ prati kāryanivedane, evam ācāryadāre | bhikṣācaryaṃ bhikṣācaraṇam, tatra bhavati bhikṣāṃ dehīti | asmād eva jñāyate asamāvṛttaviṣayam etad iti || 1.26.11 ||
evam anyeṣv api doṣavatsv apatanīyeṣūttarāṇi yāni
vakṣyāmaḥ || 1.26.12 ||
yathā mithādhītasyedaṃ prāyaścittam, evam uttarāṇi prāyaścittāni yāni vakṣyāmaḥ, tāny eṣv api | apiśabdān mithyādhīte ‘pi | doṣavatsu patanīyavyatirikteṣu karmasu, yeṣv āhatya prāyaścittaṃ noktaṃ tadviṣayāṇi draṣṭavyāni || 1.26.12 ||
kāmamanyubhyāṃ vā juhuyāt kāmo ‘kārṣīn manyur akārṣīd
iti || 1.26.13 ||
svāhākārāntābhyāṃ homaḥ, ājyaṃ dravyam || 1.26.13 ||
japed vā || 1.26.14 ||
asmin pakṣe na svāhākāraḥ | kecit tu kāmāya svāhā, manyave svāheti homam icchanti | japapakṣe tu sūtropadiṣṭau mantrāv iti | doṣābhyāsānurūpaṃ ca japahomayor āvṛttiḥ || 1.26.14 ||
parvaṇi vā tilabhakṣa upoṣya vā śvobhūta udakam upaspṛśya sāvitrīṃ prāṇāyāmaśaḥ sahasrakṛtva āvartayed aprāṇāyāmaśo vā || 1.26.15 ||
parvaṇi paurṇasāsyām amāvāsyāyāṃ vā tilān eva bhakṣayati nānyad odanādikam iti tilabhakṣaḥ | udakam upaspṛśya snātvā prāṇāyāmaśaḥ prāṇāyāmena sāvitrīṃ sahasrakṛtva āvartayet | ekasmin prāṇāyāme yāvatkṛtva āvartayituṃ śakyate tatra tāvatkṛtva āvartayet, evam ā sahasrapūrteḥ prāṇāyāmāvṛttiḥ | aprāṇāyāmaśo vā || 1.26.15 ||
iti ṣaḍviṃṣaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
śrāvaṇyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ tilabhakṣa upoṣya vā śvobhūte mahānadam udakam upaspṛśya sāvitryā samitsahasram ādadhyāj japed vā || 1.27.1 ||
giriprabhavā samudragāminī nadī mahānadī, tatra bhavaṃ mahānadam | “samitsahasraṃ yajñiyavṛkṣasya ādadhyāt” iti vacanāt na homadharmaḥ svāhākāraḥ | “juhoticodanaḥ svāhākārapradānaḥ” [ĀpPar 3.4] ity uktatvāt | japed vā || 1.127.1 ||
iṣṭiyajñakratūn vā pavitrārthān āharet || 1.27.2 ||
pavitrārthāḥ śuddhyarthāḥ | mṛgārādyā iṣṭayaḥ, yajñakratavaḥ somayāgā agniṣṭudādayaḥ | tāny etāni ṣaṭ prāyaścittāni, enaḥsu guruṣu gurūṇi laghuṣu laghūni || 1.27.2 ||
abhojyaṃ bhuktvā naiṣpurīṣyam || 1.27.3 ||
abhojyasya mārjārādimāṃsasya bhakṣaṇe niṣpurīṣabhāvaḥ kartavyaḥ | yāvad udaraṃ niṣpurīṣaṃ bhavati tāvad upavastavyam || 1.27.3 ||
tat kiyatā kālenāvāpyate | tad āha |
tat saptarātreṇāvāpyate || 1.27.4 ||
tan naiṣpurīṣyaṃ saptarātreṇāvāpyate, saptarātram upavastavyam ity arthaḥ | saptarātram upavased ity eva siddhe naiṣpurīṣyam iti vacanāt yeṣāṃ trirātreṇaiva tad avāpyate teṣām tāvataiva śuddhiḥ | tathā ca gautamaḥ: “abhojyabhojane ‘medhyaprāśane vā niṣpurīṣībhāva | trirātrāvaram abhojanam | saptarātram vā” [GDh 23.23–25] || 1.27.4 ||
hemantaśiśirayor vobhayoḥ saṃdhyor udakam
upaspṛśet || 1.27.5 ||
ubhayoḥ saṃdhyoḥ sāyaṃ prātaś ca udakam upaspṛśet bhūmigatāsv apsu snānam | uddhṛtābhir vā śītābhiḥ || 1.27.5 ||
kṛcchradvādaśarātraṃ vā caret || 1.27.6 ||
dvādaśarātrasādhyo vrataviśeṣaḥ kṛcchradvādaśarātraḥ || 1.27.6 ||
tasya vidhim āha |
tryaham anaktāśy adivāśī tatas tryahaṃ tryaham ayācitavratas tryahaṃ nāśnāti kiṃcaneti kṛcchradvādaśarātrasya
vidhiḥ || 1.27.7 ||
āditas triṣv ahaḥsu naktaṃ nāśnīyāt, divaiva bhuñjīta | tatas tryaham adivāśī rātrāv eva bhuñjīta, na divā | tatas tryaham ayācitam eva bhuñjīta | yācñāpratiṣedho ‘yam | tena svadravyasyāpratiṣedhaḥ | tathā ca gautamaḥ: “athāparaṃ tryahaṃ na kaṃcana yāceta” [GDh 26.4] iti | tatas tryahaṃ nāśnāti kiṃcana na phalādikam api | eṣa kṛcchradvādaśarātrasya vidhiḥ | atra smṛtyantaravaśād dhaviṣyam annaṃ brahmacaryaṃ strīśūdrādibhir asaṃbhāṣaṇaṃ ca draṣṭavyam || 1.27.7 ||
evam evābhyaset saṃvatsaraṃ sa kṛcchrasaṃvatsaraḥ || 1.27.8 ||
etam eva vidhiṃ saṃvatsaraṃ nirantaram abhyaset | eṣa kṛcchrasaṃvatsaro veditavyaḥ | yaḥ pūrvam uktaḥ “kṛcchrasaṃvatsaraṃ caret” [ĀpDh 1.25.8] iti || 1.27.8 ||
athāparam | bahūny apy apatanīyāni kṛtvā tribhir anaśnat
pārāyaṇaiḥ kṛtaprāyaścitto bhavati || 1.27.9 ||
athāparaṃ prāyaścittam ucyate | anaśnataiva nirantaraṃ trīṇi pārāyaṇāni kartavyāni | ādita ārabhyā samāpteḥ vedasyādhyayanaṃ pārāyaṇam | bahūny api | apiśabdāt kiṃ punar eko dvau vā
|| 1.27.9 ||
anāryāṃ śayane bibhrad dadad vṛddhiṃ kaṣāyapaḥ |
abrāhmaṇa iva vanditvā tṛṇeṣv āsīta pṛṣṭhatap || 1.27.10 ||
anāryā śūdrā | tāṃ śayane bibhrat upagacchan | dadad vṛddhiṃ vṛddhyarthaṃ dravyaṃ dadat | vṛddhyājīva ity arthaḥ | surāvyatiriktaṃ madyaṃ kaṣāyaḥ | tasya pātā kaṣāyapaḥ | yaś cābrāhmaṇa iva sarvān vandī bhūtvā stauti sa sarvo ‘pi tṛṇeṣūdayād ārabhyāsīta, yāvad ādityaḥ pṛṣṭhaṃ tapati paścād bhāgaṃ tapati | āditye tapati tadānuguṇyācaraṇāt svayam eva pṛṣṭhatab ity ucyate | abhyāse ‘bhyāsaḥ, yāvatā śuddhiṃ manyate || 1.27.10 ||
yad ekarātreṇa karoti pāpaṃ
kṛṣṇaṃ varṇaṃ brāhmaṇaḥ sevamānaḥ |
caturthakāla udakābhyavāyī
tribhir varṣais tad apahanti pāpam || 1.27.11 ||
kṛṣṇo varṇaḥ śūdraḥ | tam ājñākaro bhūtvā vṛttyarthaṃ sevamānaḥ | śiṣṭaṃ spaṣṭaṃ, gataṃ ca | anya āha – śūdrāṃ maithunena sevamāna iti | asmin pakṣe ṛtāv upagamane apatyotpattāv idaṃ draṣṭavyam | manuḥ |
vṛṣalīphenapītasya niśvāsopahatasya ca |
tasyāṃ caiva prasūtasya niṣkṛtir na vidhīyate || iti || [MDh 3.19] || 1.27.11 ||
iti saptaviṃśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
ity āpastambadharmasūtravṛttāv ujjvalāyāṃ
navamaḥ paṭalaḥ
yathā kathā ca paraparigraham abhimanyate steno ha bhavatīti kautsa-hārītau tathā kāṇva-puṣkarasādī || 1.28.1 ||
yathā kathā ca āpady anāpadi vā bhūyāṃsam alpaṃ vā paraparigrahaṃ parasvaṃ abhimanyate mamedam astv iti buddhau kurute, sarvathā stena eva bhavatīti kautsādayo manyante || 1.28.1 ||
santy apavādāḥ parigraheṣv iti vārṣyāyaṇiḥ || 1.28.2 ||
vārṣyāyaṇis tu manyate keṣucit parigraheṣu steyasyāpavādāḥ santīti || 1.28.2 ||
tān evodāharati |
śamyoṣā yugyaghāso na svāminaḥ pratiṣedhayanti || 1.28.3 ||
śamī bījakośī, tasyām uṣyante dahyante kālavaśena pacyanta iti śamyoṣā, kośīdhānyāni mudgamāṣacaṇakādīni | yugaṃ vahatīti yugyaḥ śakaṭavāhī balīvardaḥ, tasya ghāso bhakṣas tṛṇādiḥ yugyaghāsaḥ | ete ādīyamānāḥ svāmino na pratiṣedhayanti svāminaḥ pratiṣedhaṃ na kārayanti, eteṣv ādīyamāneṣu na pratiṣeddhum arhantīty arthaḥ | svayaṃ grahaṇe ‘pi na steyadoṣa iti yāvat | atra smṛtyantare viśeṣaḥ |
caṇakavrīhigodhūmayavānāṃ mudgamāṣayoḥ |
aniṣiddhair grahītavyo muṣṭir eko ‘dhvani sthitaiḥ ||
manus tu |
dvijo ‘dhvagaḥ kṣīṇavṛttiḥ dvāv ikṣū dve ca mūlake |
ādadānaḥ parakṣetrān na daṇḍaṃ dātum arhati || iti || [MDh 8.341] 1.28.3 ||
ativyavahāro vyṛddho bhavati || 1.28.4 ||
śamyoṣādiṣv api ativyavahāraḥ apahāraḥ vyṛddho duṣṭo bhavati, atimātrāpahāre steyadoṣo bhavatīty arthaḥ || 1.28.4 ||
sarvatrānumatipūrvam iti hārītaḥ || 1.28.5 ||
sarveṣu dravyeṣu sarvāsv avasthāsu svāmyanumatipūrvakam eva grahaṇam iti hārīta ācāryo manyate || 1.28.5 ||
na patitam ācāryaṃ jñātiṃ vā darśanārtho gacchet || 1.28.6 ||
“na patitaiḥ saṃvyavahāro vidyate” [ĀpDh 1.21.5] ity ukte ‘pi punar ucyate ācāryādiṣu viśeṣaṃ vakṣyāmīti || 1.28.6 ||
na cāsmād bhogān upayuñjīta || 1.28.7 ||
asmāt patitād ācāryāt, jñāter vā pitrāder vā bhogān bhogasādhanāni dāyaprāptāny api nopayuñjīta na gṛhṇīyāt || 1.28.7 ||
yadṛcchāsaṃnipāta upasaṃgṛhya tūṣṇīṃ vyativrajet || 1.28.8 ||
yadi patitair ācāryādibhiḥ yadṛcchayā saṃgatiḥ syāt, tadāvidhinopasaṃgṛhya tūṣṇīṃ taiḥ saha kiṃcid apy asaṃbhāṣya vyativrajet, na kṣaṇam api saha tiṣṭhet || 1.28.8 ||
mātā putratvasya bhūyāṃsi karmāṇy ārabhate | tasyāṃ śuśrūṣā
nityā patitāyām api || 1.28.9 ||
putratvasya | svārthikas tvaḥ, yathā “dehatvam evānyat” [ĀpDh 2.24.2] iti | putrasya kṛte mātā bhūyāṃsi dṛṣṭārthāni garbhadhāraṇāśucinirharaṇastanyadānapradakṣiṇanamaskārādīni karmāṇi karoti, tasmāt tasyāṃ patitāyām api śuśrūṣā abhyaṅgasnāpanādikā nityam eva kartavyā || 1.28.9 ||
na tu dharmasaṃnivāpaḥ syāt || 1.28.10 ||
ekasmin dharme sahānvayaḥ dharmasaṃnivāpaḥ | sa patitayā mātrā saha na kartavyaḥ | nāmasubrahmaṇyāyāṃ mātur nāmagrahaṇam, varuṇapraghāseṣu “yāvanto yajamānasyāmātyāḥ sastrīkāḥ tāvanty ekātiriktāni” [ĀpŚr 7.5.41] ity evamādikam udāharaṇam | kiṃ punar evamādiṣu mātur anvayaḥ śuśrūṣā | om ity āha – anvitā hi sā saṃmatā manyate, nirastā tu vimatā, vaiśvadevārthe tu pāke sā na bhojayitavyā | mṛtāyās tu tasyāḥ saṃskārādikāḥ kriyāḥ kartavyāḥ, neti vipratipannāḥ || 1.28.10 ||
adharmāhṛtān bhogān anujñāya na vayaṃ cādharmaś cety abhivyāhṛtyādhonābhy uparijānv ācchādya triṣavaṇam udakam upaspṛśann akṣīrākṣāralavaṇaṃ bhuñjāno dvādaśavarṣāṇi nāgāraṃ praviśet || 1.28.11 ||
brāhmaṇasvarṇaharaṇam –
caṇḍālāntyastriyo gatvā bhuktvā ca parigṛhya ca |
pataty ajñānato vipro jñānāt sāmyaṃ tu gacchati || [MDh 11.176]
ityevamādikam udāharaṇam | ye adharmāhṛtā bhogās tān anujñāya parityajya “na vayaṃ cādharmaś ca” iti praiṣaṃ brūyāt | tasyārthaḥ – vayaṃ cādharmaś ca saha na vartāmaha iti | adhonābhītyādi gatam [cf. ĀpDh 1.24.11] | nātrārdhaśāṇīpakṣaḥ bhikṣācaryaṃ vā || 1.28.11 ||
tataḥ siddhiḥ || 1.28.12 ||
etac ca dvādaśavārṣikam, tasyānte siddhiḥ śuddhir bhavati || 1.28.12 ||
atha saṃprayogaḥ syād āryaiḥ || 1.28.13 ||
prāyaścittopadeśāt siddhyupadeśāc ca siddheḥ punarvacanaṃ “jñānāt sāmyaṃ tu gacchati” [MDh 11.176] ity asyāpavādārtham || 1.28.13 ||
etad evānyeṣām api patanīyānām || 1.28.14 ||
uktavyatiriktāni yāni patanīyāni pūrvam uktāni teṣu yatrāhatya prāyaścittam anuktaṃ teṣām apy anantaroktam eva prāyaścittaṃ veditavyam | uktaviṣaye vikalpa ity anye | tatra jñānājñānakṛto vikalpaḥ || 1.28 14 ||
gurutalpagāmī tu suṣirāṃ sūrmiṃ praviśyobhayata
ādīpyābhidahed ātmānam || 1.28.15 ||
yas tu gurutalpagāmī so ‘ntaḥpraveśayogyāṃ suṣirāṃ sūrmiṃ kṛtvā praviśet | praviśyobhatoḥ pārśvayoḥ vahniṃ dīpayet | ādīpyātmānam abhidahet | “jvalitāṃ vā sūrmiṃ praviśya samāpnuyāt” [ĀpDh 1.25.2] ity atraiva kiyān api bhedaḥ | anantarasya vaikalpikasya nivṛttyarthaṃ vacanam || 1.28.15 ||
mithyaitad iti hārītaḥ || 1.28.16 ||
hārītas tu ṛṣir manyate etad anantaroktaṃ maraṇāntikaṃ prāyaścittaṃ mithyā na kartavyam iti
|| 1.28.16 ||
kuta ity āha |
yo hy ātmānaṃ paraṃ vābhimanyate ‘bhiśasta eva sa
bhavati || 1.28.17 ||
hiśabdo hetau | yasmād ya ātmānaṃ paraṃ vā abhimanyate mārayati so ‘bhiśasta eva bhavati brahmahaiva bhavati | na ca patanīyāpanodaṃ cikīrṣur anyat patanīyaṃ kartum arhatīti hetvabhidhānād abhiśastavacanāc ca anyeṣām api maraṇāntikānāṃ brāhmaṇaviṣaye
nivṛttiḥ || 1.28.17 ||
kiṃ tarhi tasya prāyaścittam ity āha |
etenaiva vidhinottamād ucchvāsāc caret | nāsyāsmin loke
pratyāpattir vidyate | kalmaṣaṃ tu nirhaṇyate || 1.28.18 ||
“adhonābhy uparijānv ācchādya” [ĀpDh 1.28.11] ity etadādi yad anantaram uktam, etenaiva vidhinā | śiṣṭaṃ gatam || 1.28.18 ||
dāravyatikramī kharājinaṃ bahirloma paridhāya dāravyatikramiṇe bhikṣām iti saptāgārāṇi caret | sā vṛttiḥ ṣaṇmāsān || 1.28.19 ||
yas tv antareṇaiva nimittaṃ kaumārān dārān parityajati sa dāravyatikramī | kharasya gārdabhasyājinaṃ bahirloma paridhāya vasitvā “dāravyatikramiṇe bhikṣāṃ datta” iti saptāgārāṇi bhikṣāṃ caret | “kaumāradāravyatikramiṇe bhikṣāṃ datta” iti vāsiṣṭhe | sā vṛttiḥ ṣaṇmāsān, tataḥ siddhiḥ || 1.28.19 ||
striyās tu bhartuvyatikrame kṛcchradvādaśarātrābhyāsas
tāvantaṃ kālam || 1.28.20 ||
bhartuvyatikrama iti chāndaso rephalopaḥ | vyatikramaḥ parityāgaḥ | yā tu strī bhartāraṃ parityajati antareṇa nimittam, tasyāḥ kṛcchradvādāśarātrābhyāsaḥ prāyaścittaṃ tāvantaṃ kālaṃ ṣaṇmāsān iti || 1.28.20 ||
atha bhrūṇahā śvājinaṃ kharājinaṃ vā bahirloma paridhāya
puruṣaśiraḥ pratīpānārtham ādāya … || 1.28.21 ||
ity aṣṭāviṃśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
khaṭvāṅgaṃ daṇḍārthe karmanāmadheyaṃ prabruvāṇaś caṅkramyeta ko bhrūṇaghne bhikṣām iti | grāme prāṇavṛttiṃ pratilabhya śūnyāgāraṃ vṛkṣamūlaṃ vābhyupāśrayen na hi sa āryaiḥ saha saṃprayogo vidyate | etenaiva vidhinottamād ucchvāsāc caret | nāsyāsmin loke pratyāpattir vidyate | kalmaṣaṃ tu nirhaṇyate || 1.29.1 ||
ṣaḍaṅgasya vedasyādhyetā, prayogaśāstrasya savyākhyasyārthavit, karmaṇām anuṣṭhātānuṣṭhāpayitā ca brāhmaṇo bhrūṇaḥ | tathā ca bodhāyanaḥ: “vedānāṃ kiṃcid adhītya brāhmaṇaḥ, ekāṃ śākhām adhītya śrotriyaḥ, aṅgādhyāyy anūcānaḥ, kalpādhyāy ṛṣikalpaḥ, sūtrapravacanādhyāyī bhrūṇaḥ” iti [BauGṛ 1.7.2–6] | taṃ yo hatvā bhrūṇahā | sa śunaḥ kharasya vājinaṃ bahirloma paridhāya puruṣasya yasya kasyacin mṛtasya śiraḥ pratīpānārtham, pratir dhātvarthavācī | “upasargasya ghañyamanuṣye bahulam” [Pāṇ 6.3.122] iti bāhulako dīrghaḥ | pānam eva pratīpānam, pānagrahaṇam upalakṣaṇam, bhojanam api tatraiva | khaṭvāṅgaṃ daṇḍārthe | khaṭvāyā aṅgaṃ kaṭvāṅgam, īṣā, tad daṇḍārthe ādāya “bhrūṇahāsmi” ity evaṃ karmanibandhanam ātmano nāmadheyaṃ prabruvāṇaś caṅkramyeta itas tataś caret | kāpālikatantraprasiddhasya vā khaṭvāṅgasya grahaṇam | bhikṣācaraṇakāle ‘pi “ko bhrūṇaghne bhikṣāṃ dadātu” iti caret, caritvā grāme prāṇavṛttiṃ prāṇayātrāmātraṃ pratilabhya śūnyāgāraṃ vṛkṣamūlaṃ vā nivāsārtham abhyupaśrayet | na hi me āryaiḥ saha saṃprayogo vidyate ity evaṃ manyamānaḥ | kiyantaṃ kālaṃ caritavyam ity āha – “etenaiva vidhinā” ityādi | gatam | “śrotriyaṃ vā karmasamāptam” [ĀpDh 1.24.24] ity atra yo granthadhāry ‘rthajñaś ca na bhavati, anuṣṭhāpayitā ca na bhavati, tasya grahaṇam || 1.28.21–1.29.1 ||
yaḥ pramatto hanti prāptaṃ doṣaphalam || 1.29.2 ||
“kṣatriyaṃ hatvā” [ĀpDh 1.24.10] ity evamādike anukrānte viṣaye yaḥ pramatto hanti pramādenābhuddhipūrvakaṃ hanti tasyāpi doṣaphalaṃ prāptam eva, na tu pramādakṛtam iti doṣābhāvaḥ || 1.29.2 ||
saha saṃkalpena bhūyaḥ || 1.29.3 ||
saṃkalpena saha vadhe kṛte bhūyaḥ prabhūtataraṃ bhavati | tena pramādakṛte laghu prāyaścittam, buddhipūrve tu gurv iti | yat punaḥ pūrvam uktaṃ “doṣavac ca karmābhisaṃdhipūrvaṃ kṛtvānabhisaṃdhipūrvaṃ vā” [ĀpDh 1.26.7] iti tatprāyaścitte viśeṣābhāvād idam uktam ||
evam anyeṣv api doṣavatsu karmasu || 1.29.4 ||
anyeṣv api hananavyatirikteṣu doṣavatsu karmasu evam eva draṣṭavyam | abuddhipūrve ‘lpadoṣaḥ, buddhipūrve mahān iti || 1.29.4 ||
tathā puṇyakriyāsu || 1.29.5 ||
puṇyakriyāsv api eṣa eva nyāyaḥ, abuddhipūrve alpaṃ phalam, buddhipūrve mahad iti | tad yathā – brāhmaṇasvāny apahṛtya coreṣu dhāvatsu yadṛcchayā kaścic chūra āgatas tān hanyāt, svayam eva vā śūraṃ dṛṣṭvā corā apahṛtāni dravyāṇy utsṛjya palāyeran tadā śūrasyālpaṃ puṇyaphalam | yadā tu buddhipūrvaṃ svayam eva corebhyaḥ pratyāhṛtya svāni svāmibhyo dadāti, tadā mahad iti | evaṃ svabhāryābuddhyā paradāragamane alpam, anyatra mahad iti || 1.29.5 ||
parīkṣārtho ‘pi brāhmaṇa āyudhaṃ nādadīta || 1.29.6 ||
guṇadoṣajñānaṃ parīkṣā, tayā arthaḥ prayojanaṃ yasya | evaṃbhūto ‘pi brāhmaṇa āyudhaṃ na gṛhṇīyāt | kiṃ punar hiṃsārtha ity apiśabdasyārthaḥ || 1.29.6 ||
asya pratiprasavaḥ |
yo hiṃsārtham abhikrāntaṃ hanti manyur eva manyuṃ spṛśati
na tasmin doṣa iti purāṇe || 1.29.7 ||
yas tu hiṃsārthaṃ māraṇārtham abhikrāntam abhipatitaṃ hanti na tasmin doṣo vidyate iti purāṇe śrutam | doṣābhāve hetuḥ – yasmān manyur eva manyuṃ spṛśati na puruṣaḥ puruṣam | atra vasiṣṭha-bodhāyanau |
svādhyāyinaṃ kule jātaṃ yo hanyād ātatāyinam |
na tena bhrūṇahā sa syāt manyus taṃ manyum ṛcchati || iti |
[VaDh 3.18; BDh 1.10.13]
manus tu |
śastraṃ dvijātibhir grāhyaṃ dharmo yatroparudhyate |
dvijātīnāṃ ca varṇānāṃ viplave kālakārite ||
ātmanaś ca paritrāṇe dakṣiṇānāṃ ca saṅgare |
strīviprābhyavapattau ca ghnan dharmeṇa na duṣyati || [MDh 8.348–49]
gautamaḥ: “prāṇasaṃśaye brāhmaṇo ‘pi śatram ādadīta” iti [GDh 7.25] | vasiṣṭhaḥ |
agnido garadaś caiva śastrapāṇir dhanāpahaḥ |
kṣetradāraharau caiva ṣaḍ ete ātatāyinaḥ ||
ātatāyinam āyāntam api vedāntapāragam |
jighāṃsantaṃ jighāṃsīyāt na tena bhrūṇahā bhavet || iti | [VaDh 3.16–17]
|| 1.29.7 ||
patitair akṛtaprāyaścittair utpāditānāṃ putrāṇām api pātityam astīti pratipādayituṃ pūrvapakṣaṃ āha |
athābhiśastāḥ samavasāya careyur dhārmyam iti sāṃśityetaretarayājakā itaretarādhyāpakā mitho
vivāhamānāḥ || 1.29.8 ||
athaśabdo ‘rthāntaraprastāvaṃ sūcayati | abhiśastāḥ patitāḥ samavasāya, avasānaṃ gṛham, sam ity ekībhāve, grāmād bahir ekasmin pradeśe gṛhāṇi kṛtvā careyuḥ | dhārmyaṃ dharmyaṃ vakṣyamāṇaṃ vṛttam iti | sāṃśitya saṃśitāṃ tīkṣṇāṃ buddhiṃ kṛtvā niścityety arthaḥ | itaretaraṃ yājayantaḥ, itaretaram adhyāpayantaḥ parasparaṃ vivāhasaṃbandhaṃ kurvantaś careyuḥ varteran iti || 1.29.8 ||
putrān saṃniṣpādya brūyur vipravrajatāsmad evaṃ hy
asmatsv āryāḥ saṃpratyapatsyateti || 1.29.9 ||
atha te putrān saṃnipṣpādya brūyuḥ, he putrāḥ asmad asmattaḥ vipravrajata vividhaṃ prakarṣeṇa sneham utsṛjyāryasamīpaṃ gacchata, evaṃ hi asmatsu asmāsu āryāḥ śiṣṭāḥ saṃpratyapatsyata | “āśaṃsāyāṃ bhūtavac ca” [Pāṇ 3.3.132] iti bhaviṣyati luṅ, sakārāt paro yakāro ‘papāṭhaś chāndaso vā | saṃpratipattiṃ kariṣyanti āryāṇām apy etad abhipretaṃ bhaviṣyati, yasmād asmābhir eva patanīyaṃ kṛtaṃ karmānuṣṭhitam, na bhavadbhiḥ | na ca patitenotpāditasya pātityam, anyatvāt || 1.29.9 ||
etad evopapādayati |
athāpi na sendriyaḥ patati || 1.29.10 ||
na hi patito bhavan sahendriyeṇa patati, puruṣa eva, nendriyaṃ śuklam iti | athāpiśabdau apicety asyārthe || 1.29.10 ||
kathaṃ na sendriyaḥ patatīty āha |
tad etena veditavyam | aṅgahīno ‘pi sāṅgaṃ janayati || 1.29.11 ||
tadanantaroktam artharūpaṃ etena vakṣyamāṇena nidarśanena veditavyam | cakṣuradyaṅgahīno ‘pi sāṅgaṃ cakṣurādimantaṃ janayati | evam adhikāravikalaḥ sādhikāraṃ janayati, striyā api kāraṇatvāt, tasyāś ca doṣābhāvāt || 1.29.11 ||
dūṣayati |
mithyaitad iti hārītaḥ || 1.29.12 ||
etad anantaroktam artharūpaṃ mithyā na yuktam iti hārītaḥ manyate || 1.29.12 ||
kuta ity āha |
dadhidhānīsadharmā strī bhavati || 1.29.13 ||
dadhi dhīyate yasyāṃ sā dadhidhānī sthālī, tayā sadharmā sadṛśī strī bhavati || 1.29.13 ||
tataḥ kim ¦
yo hi dadhidhānyām aprayataṃ paya ātacya manthati na tena dharmakṛtyaṃ kriyate | evam aśuci śuklaṃ yan nirvartate na tena saha saṃprayogo vidyate || 1.29.14 ||
yo hi dadhidhānyāṃ sthālyām aprayataṃ śvādyupahataṃ paya ātacya takrādyātañcanena saṃskṛtya manthati, na tena tadutpannena ghṛtādinā dharmakṛtyaṃ yāgādikaṃ kriyate | evaṃ patitasaṃbandhena aśuci śuklaṃ striyāṃ niṣiktaṃ śoṇitenāktaṃ yan nirvartate yena rūpeṇa niṣpādyate, na tena saha saṃprayogo vidyate śiṣṭānām | atra “aśuci śuklam” ity etat “athāpi na sendriyaḥ patati” ity asya dūṣaṇam | na hi vācanike ‘rthe yuktayaḥ kramante | tathā ca samānāyām apy utpattau putra eva patati na duhitā | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “patitenotpannaḥ patito bhavati anyatra striyāḥ | sā hi paragāminī | tām arikthām upeyāt” iti [VaDh 13.51–53] || 1.29.14 ||
abhīcārānuvyāhārāv aśucikarāv apatanīyau || 1.29.15 ||
abhicāra evābhīcāraḥ | “upasargasya dhañy amanuṣye bahulam” [Pāṇ 6.3.122] iti dīrghaḥ | abhīcāraḥ śyenayāgādiḥ | anuvyāhāraḥ śāpaḥ | tau brāhmaṇaviṣaye ‘pi kriyamāṇau aśucikarāv eva | na tu patanīyau || 1.29.15 ||
patanīyāv iti hārītaḥ || 1.29.16 ||
hārītas tu tāv api patanīyāv iti manyate || 1.29.16 ||
patanīyavṛttis tv aśucikarāṇāṃ dvādaśa māsān dvādaśārdhamāsān dvādaśa dvādaśāhān dvādaśa saptāhān dvādaśa tryāhān dvādaśa dvyahān dvādaśāhaṃ saptāhaṃ tryahaṃ dvyaham ekāham || 1.29.17 ||
aśucikarāṇām api karmaṇāṃ yeṣām āhatya prāyaścittaṃ noktam, teṣām api patanīyeṣu karmasu yā vṛttiḥ prāyaścittaṃ saiva prāyaścittiḥ | kiyantaṃ kālam | dvādaśamāsādyekāhāntam || 1.29.17 ||
kim aviśeṣeṇa sarveṣv evāśucikareṣu ayaṃ kālavikalpaḥ | nety āha |
ity aśucikaranirveṣo yathā karmābhyāsaḥ || 1.29.18 ||
ity eṣo ‘śucikaranirveṣo yathā karmābhyāsas tathā veditavyaḥ | buddhipūrve sānubandhe ‘bhyāse ca bhūyāṃsaṃ kālam, viparīte viparyaya iti || 1.29.18 ||
iti ekonatriṃśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
ity āpastambadharmasūtravṛttau ujjvalāyāṃ
daśamaḥ paṭalaḥ
“na samāvṛttā vaperan” [ĀpDh 1.10.7] “snātakasya kāle” [ĀpDh 1.8.7] ityādiṣu prasaktasya snānasya kālam āha |
vidyayā snātīty eke || 1.30.1 ||
vedavidyā vidyā | tayā saṃpannaḥ snānaṃ kuryāt, ity eke manyante | manur apy āha |
vedān adhītya vedau vā vedaṃ vāpi yathākramam |
aviplutabrahmacaryo gṛhasthāśramam āvaset || iti | [MDh 3.2]
tathā vratenāṣṭācatvāriṃśat parīmāṇena || 1.30.2 ||
parimāṇam eva parīmāṇam, chāndaso dīrghaḥ | aṣṭācatvāriṃśadgrahaṇaṃ “pādūnam, ardhonam” [ĀpDh 1.2.13–14] ityāder api pūrvoktasyopalakṣaṇam | aṣṭācatvāriṃśadādiparimāṇena vratena vā saṃpannaḥ snāyāt, asaṃpanno ‘pi vidyayā || 1.30.2 ||
vidyā vratena cety eke || 1.30.3 ||
vidyeti tṛtīyaikavacanasya lopaḥ “supāṃ suluk” [Pāṇ 7.1.39] ityādinā | vidyayā vratena cobhābhyāṃ saṃpannaḥ snāyāḍ ity eke manyante | evaṃ ca “vedam adhītya snāsyan” [ĀpGṛ 12.1] ity atra vedam adhītyety etad upalakṣaṇam | atra yājñavalkyaḥ |
vedaṃ vratāni vā pāraṃ nītvā hy ubhayam eva vā |
aviplutabrahmacaryo lakṣaṇyāṃ striyam udvahet || [YDh 1.52]
iti || 1.30.3 ||
teṣu sarveṣu snātakavad vṛttiḥ || 1.30.4 ||
vidyāsnātako vratasnātaka ubhayasnātaka iti trayaḥ snātakā uktāḥ | teṣu sarveṣu snātakavat, “tad arhati” [Pāṇ 5.1.63] iti vatiḥ | snātakārhā vṛttiḥ pūjā, “yatrāsmā apacitim” [ĀpGṛ 13.2] ityādikā kāryā | na tu vratasnātake nyūnā, nāpy ubhayasnātake ‘dhiketi || 1.30.4 ||
yady apy evam, tathāpi pūjayituḥ phalaviśeṣo ‘stīty āha |
samādhiviśeṣāc chrutiviśeṣāc ca pūjāyāṃ phalaviśeṣaḥ || 1.30.5 ||
kartavyeṣu karmasu avadhānaṃ samādhiḥ | śrutiḥ śrutam || 1.30.5 ||
atha snātakavratāni || 1.30.6 ||
ita uttaraṃ snātakavratāny adhikṛtānīti veditavyāni | yady api vakṣyamāṇeṣu kānicit sādhāraṇāny api bhavanti, tathāpi bhūmnā snātakavratāny adhikriyante || 1.30.6 ||
pūrveṇa grāmān niṣkramaṇapraveśanāni śīlayed
uttareṇa vā || 1.30.7 ||
yadā grāmān niṣkrāmati, grāmaṃ vā praviśati tadā pūrveṇa dvāreṇa uttareṇa vā kuryāt, na dvārāntareṇa | śīlayed iti vacanāt, yadṛcchayā dvārāntareṇa niṣkramaṇapraveśanayor api na prāyaścittam || 1.30.7 ||
saṃdhyoś ca bahir grāmād āsanaṃ vāgyataś ca || 1.30.8 ||
ahorātrayoḥ saṃdhānaṃ saṃdhiḥ, tau ca dvau sāyaṃ prātaś ca | “sajyotiṣyā jyotiṣo darśanāt | ubhe saṃdhye” iti gautamaḥ [GDh 2.11] | tayoḥ saṃdhyoḥ grāmād bahir āsīta, vāgyataś ca bhavet | manur apy āha |
pūrvāṃ saṃdhyāṃ japaṃs tiṣṭhet sāvitrīm ārkadarśanāt |
paścimāṃ tu samāsīta samyag ārkṣavibhāvanāt || iti | [MDh 2.101]
“tiṣṭhet pūrvām āsītottarām” iti gautamaḥ [GDh 2.11] | ete ca smṛtī brahmacāriviṣaye | snātake āsanasya vāṅniyamasya cātra vidhānāt | anye tu – āsanagrahaṇaṃ sthānasyopalakṣaṇam, vāgyamaś ca laukikyā vāco nivṛttiḥ, na sāvitrījapasyeti varṇayanti || 1.30.8 ||
āhitāgniviṣayasyāpavādaḥ |
vipratiṣedhe śrutilakṣaṇaṃ balīyaḥ || 1.30.9 ||
virodho vipratiṣedhaḥ | agnihotriṇo bahir āsanam agnihotrahomaś ca virudhyate | tathā ca śrūyate: “samudro vā eṣa yad ahorātraḥ tasyaite gāthe tīrthe yat saṃdhī tasmāt saṃdhau hotavyam” iti | tatra śrutilakṣaṇam agnihotram eva kartavyam, na smārtaṃ bahir āsanam | tasya kalpyamūlatvād itarasya kḷptamūlatvād iti | jaiminir apy āha: “virodhe tv anapekṣaṃ syād asati hy anumānam” iti [PMS 1.3.3] || 1.20.9 ||
sarvān rāgān vāsasi varjayet || 1.30.10 ||
kusumbhādayaḥ sarve rāgā vāsasi varjanīyāḥ | na kenacid raktaṃ vāso bibhṛyāt || 1.30.10 ||
kṛṣṇaṃ ca svābhāvikam || 1.30.11 ||
yat svabhāvataḥ kṛṣṇaṃ kambalādi, tad api na vasīta || 1.30.11 ||
anūdbhāsi vāso vasīta || 1.30.12 ||
udbhāsanaśīlam udbhāsi ulbaṇam, tato ‘nyad anūdbhāsi | chāndaso dīrghaḥ | evaṃbhūtaṃ vāso vasīta ācchādayet || 1.30.12 ||
apratikṛṣṭaṃ ca śaktiviṣaye || 1.30.13 ||
pratikṛṣṭaṃ nikṛṣṭaṃ jīrṇaṃ malavat sthūlaṃ ca, tadviparītam apratikṛṣṭam | tādṛśaṃ vāso vasīta śaktau satyām || 1.30.13 ||
divā ca śirasaḥ prāvaraṇaṃ varjayen mūtrapurīṣayoḥ
karma parihāpya || 1.30.14 ||
cakāraḥ pūrvāpekṣayā samuccayārthaḥ | divā ca śirasaḥ prāvaraṇaṃ paṭādinā na kuryāt | kim aviśeṣeṇa | na, mūtrapurīṣayoḥ karma kriyāṃ parihāpya varjayitvā || 1.30.14 ||
śiras tu prāvṛtya mūtrapurīṣe kuryād bhūmyāṃ kiṃcid
antardhāya || 1.30.15 ||
divā rātrau ca mūtrapurīṣe kurvan śiraḥ prāvṛtya kuryād bhūmyāṃ kiṃcit tṛṇādikam antardhāya, na sākṣād bhūmyām eva | iha kāmacāre prāpte divā ca śirasaḥ prāvaraṇaṃ varjayed ity uktam, tasya paryudāsaḥ kṛtaḥ “mūtrapurīṣayoḥ karma parihāpya” iti [ĀpDh 1.30.14] | tataś ca mūtrapurīṣakāle sa eva kāmacāraḥ sthitaḥ | ata ārabhyate – śiras tu prāvṛtyeti | evaṃ tarhi idam evāstu, na pūrvaḥ paryudāsaḥ | so ‘py avaśyaṃ kartavyaḥ, anyathā śiras tu prāvṛtyety asya rātrau caritārthatvāt divā pratiṣedha eva syāt | gautamas tu rātrau sadaiva prāvaraṇam āha: “na prāvṛtya śiro ‘hani paryaṭet | prāvṛtya tu rātrau | mūtroccāre ca” iti [GDh 9.35–37] || 1.30.15 ||
chāyāyāṃ mūtrapurīṣayoḥ karma varjayet || 1.30.16 ||
“na copajīvyāsu” iti smṛtyantare darśanāt, yasyāṃ pathikādayo viśrāmyanti sā gṛhyate, tena chatracchāyāder apratiṣedhaḥ, meghādichāyāyāś cāpratiṣedhaḥ, avarjanīyatvāt || 1.30.16 ||
svāṃ tu chāyām avameheta || 1.30.17 ||
chāndasas tugbhāvaḥ | dvitīyāśruteḥ pratiśabdo ‘dhyāhāryaḥ | avamehanaṃ mūtrīkarma anupajīvyatvān nāyaṃ pūrvasya pratiṣedhasya viṣayaḥ iti pratiprasavo ‘yaṃ na bhavati, tena sati saṃbhave svām eva chāyāṃ prati avameḍhavyam || 1.30.17 ||
na sopānan mūtrapurīṣe kuryāt kṛṣṭe pathy apsu ca | tathā ṣṭhīvanamaithunayoḥ karma apsu varjayet | agnim ādityam apo brāhmaṇaṃ gāṃ devatāś cābhimukho mūtrapurīṣayoḥ karma varjayet || 1.30.18 ||
catvāri spaṣṭāni | ṣṭhīvanam āsyaśleṣmādīnām utsargaḥ | devatāḥ devatāpratimāḥ || 1.30.18 ||
aśmānaṃ loṣṭham ārdrān oṣadhivanaspatīn ūrdhvān ācchidya
mūtrapurīṣayoḥ śundhane varjayet || 1.30.19 ||
phalapākāvasānā oṣadhayaḥ | puṣpair vinā ye phalanti te vanaspatayaḥ | ārdrān iti vacanāc chuṣkair na doṣaḥ | ūrdhān iti vacanāt vātādinimittena bhagneṣu na doṣaḥ | etair aśmādibhir mūtrapurīṣayoḥ śodhanaṃ na kuryāt || 1.30.19 ||
agnim apo brāhmaṇaṃ gā devatā dvāraṃ prativātaṃ ca
śaktiviṣaye nābhiprasārayīta || 1.30.20 ||
[śaktau satyāṃ] agnyādīn prati pādau na prasārayet || 1.30.20 ||
athāpy udāharanti || 1.30.21 ||
iti triṃśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
prāṅmukho ‘nnāni bhuñjīta uccared dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ |
udaṅmukho mūtraṃ kuryāt pratyakpādāvanejanam ||
iti || 1.31.1 ||
uccāraḥ purīṣakarma | pādāvanejanaṃ pādaprakṣālanam | bhojanādiṣu catasro diśo niyamyante | manur apy āha |
āyuṣyaṃ prāṅmukho bhuṅkte yaśasyaṃ dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ |
śriyaṃ pratyaṅmukho bhuṅkte ṛtaṃ bhuṅkta udaṅmukhaḥ || iti | [MDh 2.52]
yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
divāsaṃdhyāsu kaṇṭhasthabrahmasūtra udaṅmukhaḥ |
kuryān mūtrapurīṣe tu rātrau ced dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ || iti | [YDh 1.16]
|| 1.31.1 ||
ārāc cāvasathān mūtrapurīṣe kuryād dakṣiṇāṃ diśaṃ
dakṣiṇāparāṃ vā || 1.31.2 ||
āvasatho gṛham, tasya dūrato mūtrapurīṣe kuryāt dakṣiṇāṃ diśam | dvitīyānirdeśād abhiniṣkramyeti gamyate | dakṣiṇāparā nairṛtī || 1.31.2 ||
astam ite ca bahir grāmād āvasathād vā mūtrapurīṣayoḥ karma
varjayet || 1.31.3 ||
astam ite āditye bahir gāmān mūtrapurīṣe na kuryāt, athāntargrāme ‘pi gṛhasya dūrato na kuryāt | dṛṣṭārtho ‘yaṃ pratiṣedhaḥ, coravyāghrādiśaṅkayā | nirbhaye deśe nāsti doṣaḥ || 1.31.3 ||
devatābhidhānaṃ cāprayataḥ || 1.31.4 ||
aprayataḥ san devatānām agnyādīnām abhidhānaṃ varjayet | “apidhānam” ity apapāṭhaḥ, eṣa evārthaḥ || 1.31.4 ||
paruṣaṃ cobhayor devatānāṃ rājñaś ca || 1.31.5 ||
devatānāṃ rājñaś ca ubhayoḥ, rāśyapekṣaṃ dvivacanam | paruṣaṃ nindāṃ varjayet || 1.31.5 ||
brāhmaṇasya gor iti padopasparśanaṃ varjayet || 1.31.6 ||
brāhmaṇaṃ gāṃ ca pādena nopaspṛśet | itiśabdaḥ prakāre, tena vidyāvayovṛddhānām abrāhmaṇānām api varjanam || 1.31.6 ||
hastena cākāraṇāt || 1.31.7 ||
kāraṇam abhyaṅgakaṇḍūyanādi | tena vinā hastenāpy upasparśanaṃ varjayet pūrvoktānām
|| 1.31.7 ||
gor dakṣiṇānāṃ kumāryāś ca parīvādān varjayet || 1.31.8 ||
gor adakṣiṇāyā api, dakṣiṇānām agavām api hiraṇyādīnāṃ kumāryāḥ kanyakāyāś ca doṣān sato ‘pi na kathayet | adhyātmaprakaraṇe yogāṅgatayā parīvādaḥ pratiṣiddhaḥ, anantaraṃ ca vakṣyati: “krodhādīṃś ca bhūtadāhīyān doṣān varjayet” iti [ĀpDh 1.31.23] | idaṃ tu vacanaṃ gavādiṣu prāyaścittātirekārtham || 1.31.8 ||
spṛhantīṃ ca gāṃ nācakṣīta || 1.31.9 ||
spṛhantīṃ sasyadhānyādikaṃ bhakṣayantīṃ gāṃ svāmine na brūyāt || 1.31.9 ||
saṃsṛṣṭāṃ ca vatsenānimitte || 1.31.10 ||
yā gaur vatsena saṃsṛjyate, tām api na brūyād animitte – iyaṃ te gaur vatsena pīyata iti || animitte iti vacanāt “yasya haviṣe vatsā apākṛtā dhayeyuḥ” [ĀpŚr 9.1.23] ityādike nimitte sati vaktur nāsti doṣaḥ || 1.31.10 ||
nādhenum adhenur iti brūyāt | dhenubhavyety eva
brūyāt || 1.31.11 ||
yā ca gaur dhenuḥ payasvinī na bhavati, tām apy adhenur iti na brūyāt | kiṃ tarhi | dhenubhavyety eva brūyāt, bhaviṣyantī dhenuḥ dhenubhavyā | “dhenor bhavyāyā mum vaktavyam” iti mum na bhavati, cvyantatvenāvyayatvāt | vaktavyatve ca sati śabdaniyamo ‘yam, na punar adhenudarśana evaṃ vaktavyam || 1.31.11 ||
na bhadraṃ bhadram iti brūyāt | puṇyaṃ praśāstam ity eva
brūyāt || 1.31.12 ||
yad bhadraṃ tad bhadram iti na brūyāt, kiṃ tu puṇyaṃ praśāstam ity anyatareṇa śabdena brūyāt | praśāstaṃ praśastam, chāndaso dīrghaḥ || 1.31.12 ||
vatsatantīṃ ca nopari gacchet || 1.31.13 ||
vatsānāṃ bandhanarajjur vatsatantī, tasyā upari na gacchet tāṃ na laṅghayet | vatsagrahaṇaṃ gojāter upalakṣaṇam || 1.31.13 ||
pleṅkhāv antareṇa ca nātīyāt || 1.31.14 ||
pleṅkhau dolāstambhau | toraṇastambāv ity anye | tāv antareṇa nātīyāt tayor madhye na gacchet
|| 1.31.14 ||
nāsau me sapatna iti brūyāt | yady asau me sapatna iti brūyād
dviṣantaṃ bhrātṛvyaṃ janayet || 1.31.15 ||
asau devadatto me sapatna iti na brūyāt sadasi | kiṃ kāraṇam | yady asau me sapatna iti brūyāt dviṣantam, kriyāśabdo ‘yam, vidviṣāṇaṃ bhrātṛvyaṃ sapatnaṃ janayet | evaṃ hy ukte sa manyate, nākasmād ayaṃ brūte nūnam asya mayi dveṣo vartate iti | tataś ca tatpratīkārārthaṃ yatamānaḥ sapatna eva jāyata iti || 1.31.15 ||
nendradhanur iti parasmai prabrūyāt || 1.31.16 ||
indradhanur ākāśe paśyan parasmai na tena śabdena prabrūyāt | yady avaśyaṃ vaktavyam, “maṇidhanuḥ ity eva brūyāt” gautamīye darśanāt [GDh 9.22] || 1.31.16 ||
na patataḥ saṃcakṣīta || 1.31.17 ||
patataḥ pakṣiṇaḥ saṃghībhūyasthitān na saṃcakṣīta na gaṇayed iyanta iti | apara āha – puṇyakṣayeṇa svargāt patataḥ sukṛtinaḥ parasmai na saṃcakṣīta jyotīṃṣi patantīti na kathayed iti
|| 1.31.17 ||
udyantam astaṃyantaṃ cādityaṃ darśane varjayet || 1.31.18 ||
udayasamaye astamayasamaye cādityaṃ na paśyet | manus tu |
nekṣetodyantam ādityaṃ nāstaṃyantaṃ kadācana |
noparaktaṃ na vāristhaṃ na madhyaṃ nabhaso gatam || iti | [MDh 4.37]
|| 1.31.18 ||
divādityaḥ sattvāni gopāyati naktaṃ candramāḥ | tasmād amāvāsyāyāṃ niśāyāṃ svādhīya ātmano guptim icchet prāyatyabrahmacaryakāle caryayā ca || 1.31.19 ||
divā ahany ādityaḥ sattvāni gopāyati prāṇino rakṣati ālokadānena | naktaṃ rātrau candramāḥ | tasmād amāvāsyāyāṃ niśāyāṃ rātrau svādhīyaḥ | vakāraḥ chāndasaḥ, “antikabāḍhayor nedasādhau” [Pāṇ 5.3.63] | bāḍhataraṃ bhṛśataram ātmano guptiṃ rakṣaṇam icchet | kena prakāreṇa | prāyatyabrahmacaryābhyāṃ kāle caryayā ca | ayaṃ tāvad arthānurūpaḥ pāṭhaḥ | adhīyamānas tu pramādaḥ chāndaso vā | prayatasya bhāvaḥ prāyatyam, nityāt prāyatyāt adhikena prāyatyena snānādijena brahmacaryeṇa maithunatyāgena kāle kṛtayā caryayā ca devatārcanajapādikayā || 1.31.19 ||
kasmāt punar asyāṃ rātrau na candramā gopāyatīy āha |
saha hy etāṃ rātriṃ sūryācandramasau vasataḥ || 1.31.20 ||
etāṃ rātrim, atyantasaṃyoge dvitīyā | sarvām eva rātriṃ sūryācandramasau saha vasataḥ | na ca sūryeṇa saha vasataś candramasaḥ prakāśo ‘sti || 1.31.20 ||
na kusṛtyā grāmaṃ praviśet | yadi previśen namo rudrāya
vāstoṣpataya ity etām ṛcaṃ japed anyāṃ vā raudrīm || 1.31.21||
kusṛtiḥ kumārgaḥ | tayā grāmaṃ na praviśet | yadi gatyantarābhāvāt praviśet tato “namo rudrāya” [TaitBr 3.7.9] ityādikām ṛcaṃ japet, anyāṃ vā raudrīm, “imāṃ rudrāya tavase” [TS 4.5.10] ity etadādikām | atra vājasaneyināṃ gṛhye: “vanaṃ pravekṣyann anumantrayate namo rudrāya vanasade svasti mā saṃpārayeti | apaḥ pravekṣyann anumantrayate namo rudrāyāpsuṣade svasti mā saṃpārayeti | tasmād yat kiṃcana karma kurvan syāt tat sarvaṃ namo rudrāyety eva kuryāt sarvo hy eṣa rudraḥ iti śruteḥ” iti | bharadvājagṛhye ‘py atra viṣaye kiyān eva viśeṣaḥ || 1.31.21 ||
nābrāhmaṇāyocchiṣṭaṃ prayacchet | yadi prayacched dantān
skuptvā tasminn avadhāya prayacchet || 1.31.22 ||
abrāhmaṇaḥ śūdraḥ, “na śūdrāyocchiṣṭam anucchiṣṭaṃ vā dadyāt” [VaDh 18.14] iti vāsiṣṭhe darśanāt | tasmād ucchiṣṭaṃ na prayacched ity anāśritaviṣayam | yadi prayacchet idam āśritaviṣayam | dantān nakhena skuptvā avalikhya tanmalaṃ tasminn ucchiṣṭe avadhāya nidhāya prayacchet | skubhnāteḥ ktvāpratyaye chāndasaṃ bhakārasya cartvam, skunāter vā pakāra upajanaḥ || 1.31.22 ||
krodhādīṃś ca bhūtadāhīyān doṣān varjayet || 1.31.23 ||
krodhādayo bhūtadāhīyāḥ adhyātmapaṭale vyākhyātāḥ | tadvacanaṃ yogiviṣayam iti ayogino ‘pi snātakasya krodhādinivṛttyartham idaṃ vacanam | idam eva tarhy ubhayārtham astu yogyartham ayogyarthaṃ ceti | evaṃ siddhe tadvacanaṃ krodhādivarjanasya yogāṅgatvapratipādanārtham, tenākrodhādyananuṣṭhāne yogasiddhir eva na bhavati, na punaḥ snātakavratalopaprāyaścittam iti
|| 1.31.23 ||
ity ekaviṃśaḥ khaṇdaḥ
pravacanayukto varṣāśaradaṃ maithunaṃ varjayet || 1.32.1 ||
pravacanam adhyāpanam, tena yukto varṣāsu śaradi ca maithunaṃ varjayet ṛtāv api || 1.32.1 ||
mithunībhūya ca na tayā saha sarvāṃ rātriṃ śayīta || 1.32.2 ||
mithunībhūya maithunaṃ kṛtvā tayā saha bhāryayā saha tāṃ rātriṃ sarvāṃ na śayīta || 1.32.2 ||
śayānaś cādhyāpanaṃ varjayet || 1.32.3 ||
divā naktaṃ ca śayānasyādhyāpanapratiṣedhaḥ | svayaṃ tu dhāraṇārtham adhīyānasya na doṣaḥ
|| 1.32.3 ||
na ca tasyāṃ śayyāyām adhyāpayed yasyāṃ śayīta || 1.32.4 ||
yasyāṃ śayyāyāṃ bhāryayā saha rātrau śayīta tasyāṃ śayyāyām āsīno ‘pi nādhyāpayet
|| 1.32.4 ||
anāviḥ sraganulepaṇaḥ syāt || 1.32.5 ||
āvirbhūte prakāśite sraganulepane yasya evaṃbhūto na syāt, ṇatvaṃ pūrvavat || 1.32.5 ||
sadā niśāyāṃ dāraṃ praty alaṃkurvīta || 1.32.6 ||
dāraṃ pratīti vacanād upagamanārtham alaṃkaraṇam | tena bhāryāyā aśktyādinā upagamanāyogyatve nāyaṃ niyamaḥ || 1.32.6 ||
saśirā vamajjanam apsu varjayet || 1.32.7 ||
vamajjanam avamajjanam | “vaṣṭi vāgurirallopamavāpyor upasargayoḥ” ity akāralopaḥ | saśirā varjayet saha śirasā snānaṃ na kuryād iti | avagāhanavidhayaḥ sarve snātakavyatirikte caritārthāḥ, naimittikāś ca | snātakasya nityasnānam avagāhanarūpaṃ na bhavatīty ācāryasya pakṣaḥ || 1.32.7 ||
astam ite ca snānam || 1.32.8 ||
astam ite cāditye sarvaprakāraṃ snānaṃ varjayet || 1.32.8 ||
pālāśam āsanaṃ pāduke dantaprakṣālanam iti ca
varjayet || 1.32.9 ||
pālāśam āsanādi varjayet | dantaprakṣālanaṃ dantakāṣṭham | itiśabdaḥ prakāre | tenānyad api gṛhopakaraṇaṃ pālāśaṃ varjayet || 1.32.9 ||
stutiṃ ca guroḥ samakṣaṃ yathā susnātam iti || 1.32.10 ||
susnātam ityādikāṃ ca stutiṃ guroḥ saṃnidhau varjayet || 1.32.10 ||
ā niśāyā jāgaraṇam || 1.32.11 ||
niśā rātrer madhyamo bhāgaḥ, ā tasmāj jāgṛyāt na svapyāt || 1.32.11 ||
anadhyāyo niśāyām anyatra dharmopadeśāc
chiṣyebhyaḥ || 1.32.12 ||
niśāyām anadhyāyaḥ adhyayanam adhyāpanaṃ ca na kuryāt | śiṣyebhyas tu dharmopadeśo ‘nujñāyate || 1.32.12 ||
manasā vā svayam || 1.32.13 ||
niśāyām anadhyāyasya pratiprasavaḥ | manasā vā svayaṃ cintayed iti || 1.32.13 ||
ūrdhvam ardharātrād adhyāpanam || 1.32.14 ||
ayam api pratiprasavaḥ | niśāyām api ṣoḍaśyā nāḍikāyā ārabhya adhyāpanaṃ bhavatīti
|| 1.32.14 ||
nāpararātram utthāyānadhyāya iti saṃviśet || 1.32.15 ||
rātres tṛtīyo bhāgo ‘pararātraḥ | ūrdhvam ardharātrād utthāyādhyāpayann apararātraṃ na saṃviśet na śayīta | yady api tasminn aṣṭamyādir anadhyāyaḥ prāpto bhavati, kiṃ punaḥ svādhyāye | tathā ca manuḥ |
na niśānte pariśrānto brahmādhīṭya punaḥ svapet || [MDh 4.99]
iti || 1.32.15 ||
kāmam apaśśayīta || 1.32.16 ||
anena stambhādyapāśrayeṇa āsīnasya svāpo ‘nujñāyate | śriñ sevāyām, tatra rephalopaḥ chāndasaḥ, tathā śakārasya dvirvacanam || 1.32.16 ||
manasā vādhīyīta || 1.32.17 ||
ayam apy ūrdhvam ardharātrād utthāyādhyāpayato ‘nadhyāyaprāptāv evocyate manasā vā prāptaṃ pradeśam adhīyīta svayaṃ cintayet, upāśritya vā svapyād iti || 1.32.17 ||
kṣudrān kṣudracaritāṃś ca deśān na seveta || 1.32.18 ||
kṣudrān alpakān puruṣān na seveta, kṣudrair niṣādādibhir adhiṣṭhitāṃś ca deśān na seveta
|| 1.32.18 ||
sabhāḥ samājāṃś ca || 1.32.19 ||
sabhāḥ samājāś ca vyākhyātāḥ | tān na seveta || 1.32.19 ||
samājaṃ ced gacchet pradakṣiṇīkṛtyāpeyāt || 1.32.20 ||
yady arthāt samājān gacchet tataḥ pradakṣiṇīkṛtyāpeyāt apagacchet || 1.32.20 ||
nagarapraveśanāni ca varjayet || 1.32.21 ||
bahuvacananirdeśād bahukṛtvo nagaraṃ na praveṣṭavyam | yadā kadācid yādṛcchike praveśane ca prāyaścittam || 1.32.21 ||
praśnaṃ ca na vibrūyāt || 1.32.22 ||
vivicya vacanaṃ vivacanaṃ nirṇayaḥ, pṛṣṭam arthaṃ na vivicya brūyāt ittham idam iti | durnirūpārthaviṣayam idam || 1.32.22 ||
athāpy uadāharanti || 1.32.23 ||
api cāsminn arthe ślokam udāharanti || 1.32.23 ||
mūlaṃ tūlaṃ vṛhati durvivaktuḥ
prajāṃ paśūn āyatanaṃ hinasti |
dharmaprahrādana kumālanāya
rudan ha mṛtyur vyuvāca praśnam || iti || 1.32.24 ||
durnirūpam arthaṃ sahasā nirṇīya yo durvivakti anyathā varṇayati tasya durvivaktus tad eva durvacanaṃ mūlaṃ tūlaṃ ca vṛhati | mūlaṃ pitrādayaḥ, tūlam āgāminī sampat, tad ubhayam api vṛhaty utpāṭayati | dantyoṣṭhyo vakāraḥ | kim etāvad eva | na | prajāṃ putrādikām, paśūn gavādikān, āyatanaṃ gṛhādikaṃ ca hinasti, ato durvivacanasaṃbhavāt praśnamātram eva na vibrūyāt iti | atretihāsaḥ – kasyacid ṛṣeḥ dharmaprahrādaḥ kumālanaś ceti dvau śiṣyāv āstām |
tau kadācid araṇyān mahāntau samidbhārāv āhṛtya śramād adṛṣṭipūta evācāryagṛhe prākṣipatām | tayor ekenācāryasya putraḥ śiśur ākrānto mṛtaḥ | tataḥ śiṣyāv āhūyācāryaḥ papraccha kenāyaṃ mārita iti | tāv ubhāv api na mayety ūcatuḥ | tataḥ patitasya parityāgam aduṣṭasya parigrahaṃ kartum aśaknuvan ṛṣir mṛtyum āhūya papraccha kenāyaṃ vyāpādita iti | tato dharmasaṃkaṭe patite mṛyū rudann eva praśnaṃ vyuvāca vivicya kathitavān | katham | he dharmaprahrādana kumālanāya | ṣaṣṭhyarthe caturthī | kumālanasya nedaṃ patanīyam iti | dharmaprahrāda tvayedaṃ kṛtam iti vaktavye itarasya nāstīty uktam, tathāpītarasyāstīty arthād gamyata iti rudan vyuvāceti | haśabda aitihyadyotanārthaḥ prahrādaśabde hakārāt paro rephaś chāndasaḥ || 1.32.24 ||
gārdabhaṃ yānam ārohaṇe viṣamārohaṇāvarohaṇāni ca
varjayet || 1.32.25 ||
gardhabhayuktaṃ yānaṃ śakaṭādi ārohaṇe varjayet nārohet, tathā viṣameṣu nimnonnateṣu ārohaṇam avarohaṇaṃ ca varjayet || unnateṣv ārohaṇaṃ nimneṣv avarohaṇam || 1.32.25 ||
bāhubhyāṃ ca nadītaram || 1.32.26 ||
taraṇaṃ taraḥ | bāhubhyāṃ nadyās taraṇaṃ varjayet | bāhubhyām iti vacanāt plavādinā na doṣaḥ
|| 1.32.26 ||
nāvaṃ ca sāṃśayikīm || 1.32.27 ||
bhidyate na veti saṃśayam āpannā sāṃśayikī jīrṇā, tām nāvaṃ varjayet | nāvām iti ṣaṣṭyantapāṭhe nāvāṃ madhye sāṃśayikīṃ nāvaṃ varjayed iti || 1.32.27 ||
tṛṇachedanaloṣṭavimardanaṣṭhīvanāni cākāraṇāt || 1.32.28 ||
tṛṇachedanādīni nākāranāt kuryāt | ṣṭhīvanasya kāraṇaṃ pratiśyāyādiḥ, itaratra mṛgyam
|| 1.32.28 ||
yac cānyat paricakṣate yac cānyat paricakṣate || 1.32.29 ||
yac cānyad evaṃ yuktam ācāryāḥ paricakṣate varjayanti, tad apy akṣakrīḍādi varjayet | dviruktiḥ praśnasamāptyarthā || 1.32.29 ||
iti haradattamiśraviracitāyāṃ dharmavyākhyāyām
ujjvalāyāṃ dvātriṃśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
ekādaśaḥ paṭalaḥ samāptaḥ
prathamaḥ praśnaś ca
pāṇigrahaṇād adhi gṛhamedhinor vratam || 2.1.1 ||
pūrvasmin praśne ādyayoḥ paṭalayoḥ prāyeṇa brahmacāriṇo dharmā uktāḥ, uttareṣv aṣṭasu sarvāśramāṇām, ekādaśe samāvṛttasya | idānīṃ pāṇigrahaṇād ārabhya kartavyāni karmāṇy ucyante – pāṇir yasminn ahani gṛhyate tat pāṇigrahaṇam | adhiśabda ūrdhvārthe vartate | tasmād ūrdhvaṃ gṛhamedhinoḥ gṛhasthāśramavator yad vrataṃ kartavyam, jātāv ekavacanam, tad ucyate | “prāṇigrahaṇād adhi” iti vacanam “bhāryādir agnir dāyādir vā” [GDh 5.7] iti śāśtrāntarokto vikalpo mā bhūd iti | gṛhamedhinor iti dvivacanaṃ anyataramaraṇe mā bhūd iti | vaiśvadevaṃ tu vidhurā api kurvanti || 2.1.1 ||
kālayor bhojanam || 2.1.2 ||
kālayor ubhayor api bhojanaṃ kartavyam | sāyaṃ prātaś ca nāntareti parisaṃkhyeyam, bhojanasya rāgaprāptatvāt | mānave ca spaṣṭam etat ¦
sāyaṃ prātar dvijātīnām aśanaṃ śruticoditam |
nāntarā bhojanaṃ kuryād agnihotrasamo vidhiḥ || iti |
anye tu niyamaṃ manyante – śaktau satyāṃ kālayor avaśyaṃ bhoktavyaṃ prāṇāgnihotrasyālopāyeti | tathā ca bodhāyanaḥ |
brahmacārī gṛhastho vā yo ‘naśnaṃs tu tapaś caret |
prāṇāgnihotralopena avakīrṇī bhavet tu saḥ || iti | [BDh 2.7.9]
anyatra prāyaścittāt | prāyaścitte tu tad eva vidhānam iti || 2.1.2 ||
atṛptiś cānnasya || 2.1.3 ||
suhitārthayoge karaṇe ṣaṣṭhī bhavati “pūraṇaguṇasuhitārtha” [Pāṇ 2.2.11] iti jñāpanāt | annena tṛptiṃ na gaccheyātām | yāvat tṛptir na bhoktavyam || 2.1.3 ||
parvasu cobhayor upavāsaḥ || 2.1.4 ||
pakṣasaṃdhiḥ parva | iha tu tadyuktam ahar gṛhyate | teṣu parvasūbhayor dampatyor upavāsaḥ kartavyaḥ | upavāso bhojanalopaḥ || 2.1.4 ||
aviśeṣād ubhayor api kālayoḥ prāptāv āha |
aupavastam eva kālāntare bhojanam || 2.1.5 ||
yat kālāntare ekasmin kāle bhojanaṃ tad apy aupavastam eva upavāsa eva | “aupavastaṃ tūpavāsaḥ” iti nighaṇṭuḥ | tad api divā na rātrau, śraute tathā darśanāt: “na tasya sāyam aśnīyāt” iti | tad iha “evam ata ūrdhvam” [ĀpGṛ 7.17] ityādi gṛhye yad uktaṃ tatratya upavāso vyākhyātaḥ || 2.1.5 ||
tṛpitś cānnasya || 2.1.6 ||
parvasu sakṛd bhuñjānau yāvattṛpti bhuñjīyātām || 2.1.6 ||
yac cainayoḥ priyaṃ syāt tad etasminn ahani
bhuñjīyātām || 2.1.7 ||
etasminn ahnīti na vaktavyaṃ prakṛtatvāt | yathā “atṛptiś cānnasya” [ĀpDh 2.1.3] iti parvasu bhavati evam idam api bhaviṣyati | kiṃ ca – parvasv iti bahuvacanāntasya prakṛtasya ekasminn ahanīti ekavacanāntena pratyavamarśo nātīva samañjasam | tasmād vyavahitam api pāṇigrahaṇam ahaḥ pratyavamṛśyate | etadartham api gṛhye “etad ahar vijānīyād yad ahar bhāryām āvahata” [ĀpGṛ 8.7] ity uktam | etasmin pāṇigrahaṇe ‘hani yad enayor dampatyoḥ priyaṃ tad bhuñjīyātām, na tu “nātmārtham abhirūpam annaṃ pācayet” [ĀpDh 2.8.4] iti niṣedhasyāyaṃ viṣaya iti | pratisaṃvatsaraṃ caitat kartavyam, yathā caitre  māsi svātau kṛtavivāhasya parasminn api saṃvatsare tasmin māse evaṃ hi “tad evāhaḥ” iti bhavati | pratimāsaṃ tu nakṣatrāgame ‘pi caitrādibhedān na tad eveti pratipattiḥ | tasmāt pratisaṃvatsaram idaṃ vivāhanakṣatre kartavyam | yathā rājñām abhiṣekanakṣatram evaṃ gṛhamedhinor vivāhanakṣatram iti || 2.1.7 ||
adhaś ca śayīyātām || 2.1.8 ||
etasminn evāhani sthaṇḍilaśāyinau syātām || 2.1.8 ||
maithunavarjanaṃ ca || 2.1.9 ||
maithunavarjanaṃ caitasminn ahani kartavyam || 2.1.9 ||
śvo bhūte sthālīpākaḥ || 2.1.10 ||
sthālīpākaś cāparedyuḥ kartavyaḥ || 2.1.10 ||
tasyopacāraḥ pārvaṇena vyākhyātaḥ || 2.1.11 ||
tasya sthālīpākasya upacāraḥ prayogaprakāraḥ pārvaṇena vyākhyātaḥ | etad eva jñāpayati na sāmayācārikeṣu pārvaṇātideśaḥ pravartata iti | kecit tu sarvam evaitat parvaviṣayaṃ manyante | teṣām ukto doṣaḥ | “pārvaṇena vyākhyātaḥ” iti cānupapannam, na hi sa eva tena vyākhyāto bhavati | “śvo bhūte sthālīpākaḥ” [ĀpDh 2.1.10] iti vacanaṃ vyartham, “upoṣitābhyāṃ parvasu kāryaḥ” [ĀpGṛ 7.17] iti pūrvam eva vihitatvāt | “etad ahar vijānīyāt” [ĀpGṛ 8.7] iti cāsya prayojanaṃ tatpakṣe cintyam || 2.1.11 ||
nityaṃ loka upadiśanti || 2.1.12 ||
loke śiṣṭācāre siddham etat karma nityaṃ prativatsaraṃ kartavyam iti śiṣṭā upadiśanti | apara āha – vakṣyamāṇaṃ karma śiṣṭācārasiddhaṃ nityaṃ sārvatrikaṃ śiṣṭā upadiśanti || 2.1.2 ||
yatra kva cāgnim upasamādhāsyaṃt syāt tatra prācīr udīcīś ca tisras tisro rekhā likhitvādbhir avokṣyāgnim upasamindhyat
|| 2.1.13 ||
homaprasaṅgād idam ucyate | yatra kva ca gārhye sāmayācārike vā karmaṇi gṛhe ‘raṇye vāgnim upasamādhāsyaṃt syāt pratiṣṭhāpayiṣyaṃt syāt tatra pūrvaṃ prācīḥ prāgagrās tisro rekhā likhet tata udīcīḥ udagagrās tisro rekhā likhitvādbhir avokṣet | avokṣyāgniṃ śrotriyāgārād āhṛtaṃ pratiṣṭhāpyopasamindhyāt upasamandhīta kāṣṭhair agniṃ jvalayet | tatra “purastād udag vopakramaḥ | tathāpavargaḥ" [ĀpGṛ 1.5–6] iti paribhāṣitam | upadeśakramāc ca prācyaḥ pūrvaṃ lekhā lekhanīyāḥ tataś codīcyaḥ |
prācīḥ pūrvaṃ dakṣiṇāntam udagārambham ālikhet |
tathodīcīḥ puraḥ saṃsthaṃ paścimārambham ālikhet ||
anye tu prācīr dakṣiṇārambham ālikhanti || 2.1.13 ||
utsicyaitad udakam uttareṇa pūrveṇa vānyad
upadadhyāt || 2.1.14 ||
etad avokṣaṇaśeṣodakam agner uttarataḥ pūrvato vā utsiñcet | utsicyānyad udakaṃ pātrastham upadadhyāt tatraiva || 2.1.14 ||
nityam udadhānāny adbhir ariktāni syur gṛhamedhinor
vratam || 2.1.15 ||
gṛhe yāvanty udadhānāni udapātrāṇi ghaṭakarakādīni tāni sadā adbhir ariktāni syuḥ | etad api gṛhamedhinor vratam | punar gṛhamedhinor ti dvivacanam asmin karmaṇi svayaṃ kartṛtvam eva yathā syāt prayojakakartṛtvaṃ mā bhūd iti | anya āha – punaḥ “gṛhamedhinoḥ” iti vacanāt pūrvasūtraṃ brahmacāriviṣyeti “sāvitryā samitsahasram” [ĀpDh 1.27.1] ityādau bhavati | pāke tu striyā na bhavati “upasamādhāsyan” iti liṅgasya vivakṣitatvāt, “āryāḥ prayatāḥ” [ĀpDh 2.3.1] ity atra tu bhavatīti || 2.1.15 ||
ahany asaṃveśanam || 2.1.16 ||
saṃveśanaṃ maithunam, tad ahani na kartavyam || 2.1.16 ||
ṛtau ca saṃnipāte dāreṇānuvratam || 2.1.17 ||
rajodarśanād ārabhya ṣoḍaśāhorātrāḥ ṛtuḥ | tatra saṃnipātaḥ saṃprayogo dāreṇa saha kartavyaḥ | chāndasam ekavacanam, nityaṃ bahuvacanānto hi dāraśabdaḥ | anuvratam, śāstrato niyamo vratam, tadanurodhena | tatra manuḥ |
ṛtuḥ svābhāvikaḥ strīṇāṃ rātrayaḥ ṣōḍaśa smṛtāḥ ¦
caturbhir itaraiḥ sārdham ahobhiḥ sadvigarhitaiḥ || [MDh 3.46]
tāsām ādyāś catasras tu nindyā ekādaśī ca yā |
trayodaśī ca śeṣās tu praśastā daśa rātrayaḥ || [MDh 3.47]
amāvāśyām aṣṭamīṃ ca paurṇamāsīṃ caturdaśīm |
brahmacārī bhaven nityam apy ṛtau snātako dvijaḥ || iti | [MDh 4.128]
yājñavalkyas tu |
evaṃ gacchan striyaṃ kṣāmāṃ makhāṃ mūlaṃ ca varjayet | iti | [YDh 1.80]
ācāryas tu – caturthīprabhṛti gamanam āha: “caturthīprabhṛty ā ṣoḍaśīm uttarām uttarāṃ prajātiḥ śreyasam ṛtugamana ity upadiśanti” iti [ĀpGṛ 9.1] | tad iha ṣoḍaśasu rātriṣu āditas tisraḥ sarvathā varjyāḥ | caturthy ekādaśī trayodaśī cācāryeṇānujñātāḥ manunā niṣiddhāh | itarāsu daśasu rātriṣu yugmāsu putrā jāyante striyo ‘yugmāsu | tatra ca “uttarām uttarām” [ĀpGṛ 9.1] iti vacanāt ṣoḍaśyāṃ rātrau makhādiyogābhāve gacchataḥ putraḥ sarvata utkṛṣṭo bhavati | caturthyām avamaḥ, madhye kalpyam | evaṃ pañcadaśyām utkṛṣṭā duhitā, pañcamyām avamā, madhye kalpyam | ṣoḍaśasv eva gamanaṃ garbhahetuḥ, tatrāpi prathamam | evaṃ sthite niyamavidhir ayam, yogyatve sati ṛtāv avaśyaṃ saṃnipatet, asaṃnipatan putrotpattiṃ nirundhānaḥ pratyaveyād iti | tathā doṣasmṛtiḥ |
ṛtusnātas tu yo bhāryāṃ saṃnidhau nopagacchati |
tasyā rajasi tan māsaṃ pitaras tasya śerate || iti | [BDh 4.1.18]
putraguṇārthitayā pūrvāṃ pūrvāṃ varjayato na doṣaḥ | anye tu parisaṃkhyānaṃ manyante, ṛtāv eva saṃnipaten nānyatreti | teṣām ṛtāv aniyamād agamane ‘pi doṣābhāvād doṣasmaraṇam anupapannaṃ syāt | sarvathāpi vidhir na bhavati, rāgaprāptatvāt saṃnipātasya || 2.1.17 ||
antarāle ‘pi dāra eva || 2.1.18 ||
antarāle madhyam ṛtvor antarāle madhye ‘pi saṃnipātaḥ syat dāra eva sakāme sati | yady apy ātmano jitendriyatayā na tādṛśaṃ pāravaśyam, tathāpi bhāryāyām evecchantyāṃ tadrakṣaṇārtham avaśyaṃ saṃnipated iti | vakṣyati ca “apramattā rakṣatha tantum etam” iti [ĀpDh 2.13.6] | “anuvratam” [ĀpDh 2.1.17] ity anuvṛtteḥ pratiṣiddheṣu dineṣu na bhavati || 2.1.18 ||
brāhmaṇavacanāc ca saṃveśanam || 2.1.19 ||
yad idam anantaroktaṃ saṃveśanaṃ tatra brāhmaṇavacanaṃ pramāṇam “kāmam ā vijanitoḥ saṃbhavāma” iti [TS 2.5.1] || 2.1.19 ||
strīvāsasaiva saṃnipātaḥ syāt || 2.1.20 ||
evakāro bhinnakramaḥ | stryupabhogārthaṃ vāsaḥ strīvāsaḥ tena saṃnipāta eva syāt, na tena suprakṣālitenāpi brahmayajñādikṛtyam iti || 2.1.20 ||
yāvat saṃnipātaṃ caiva sahaśayyā || 2.1.21 ||
yāvat saṃnipātam eva dampatyoḥ sahaśayanam || 2.1.21 ||
tato nānā || 2.1.22 ||
tataḥ pṛthak śayīyātām || 2.1.22 ||
udakopasparśanam || 2.1.23 ||
tato dvayor api udakopasparśanaṃ snānaṃ kartavyam | idam ṛtukāle || 2.1.23 ||
iti prathamaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
api vā lepān prakṣālyācamya prokṣaṇam aṅgānām || 2.2.1 ||
yadi vā retaso rajasaś ca ye lepās tān adbhiḥ mṛdā ca prakṣālya ācamyāṅgānāṃ śiraḥprabhṛtīnāṃ prokṣaṇaṃ kartavyam, idam anṛtukāle || 2.2.1 ||
sarvavarṇānāṃ svadharmānuṣṭhāne paramaparimitaṃ
sukham || 2.2.2 ||
sarveṣām eva varṇānāṃ brāhmaṇādīnāṃ caturṇāṃ ye svadharmā varṇaprayuktā āśramaprayuktā ubhayaprayuktā vā teṣām avaiguṇyenāntād anuṣṭhāne sati param utkṛṣṭam aparimitam akṣayaṃ svargākhyaṃ sukhaṃ bhavati || 2.2.2 ||
tena na kevalam etāvad eva | kiṃ tarhi |
tataḥ parivṛttau karmaphalaśeṣeṇa jātiṃ rūpaṃ varṇaṃ balaṃ medhāṃ prajñāṃ dravyāṇi dharmānuṣṭhānam iti pratipadyate | tac cakravad ubhayor lokayoḥ sukha eva vartate || 2.2.3 ||
