tataḥ sukhānubhavānantaraṃ parivṛttir iha loke janma bhavati | tasyāṃ ca karmaṇo yaḥ phalaśeṣo bhoktavyaḥ tena jātiṃ brāhmaṇādikām, viśiṣṭe vā kule janma, rūpaṃ kāntimad varṇaṃ hemāditulyam, balaṃ pratipakṣanigrahakṣamam, medhāṃ granthagrahaṇaśaktim, prajñām arthagrahaṇaśaktim, dravyāṇi svarṇādīni, dharmānuṣṭhānam | itikaraṇād yac cānyad evaṃ yuktaṃ tat sarvaṃ pratipadyate | sarvatra dharmaśeṣo hetuḥ | karmāṇi bhujyamānāni sāvaśeṣāṇi bhujyante, aihikasya śarīragrahaṇāder api karmaphalatvāt | “dharmānuṣṭhānaṃ pratipadyate” ity uktam | yadā caivam tadā “sarvavarṇānāṃ svadharmānuṣṭhāne” [ĀpDh 2.2.2] ityādi “pratipadyate” ityantaṃ punar bhavatīty anuktasiddham | tasmāc cakravad ubhayor lokayoḥ iha cāmuṣmiṃś ca sukha eva vartate | na jātucid duḥkhe, sukhānubandhenaivāvṛttir bhavatīty arthaḥ || 2.2.3 ||
śarīrotpattisaṃskārā apy avaśyāpekṣā iti darśayituṃ dṛṣṭāntam āha |
yathauṣadhivanaspatīnāṃ bījasya kṣetrakarmaviśeṣe
phalaparivṛddhir evam || 2.2.4 ||
calopo ‘tra draṣṭavyaḥ | yathā cauṣadhīnāṃ vrīhyādīnāṃ vanaspatīnām āmrādīnāṃ bījasya kṣetraviśeṣe karmaviśeṣe saṃskāraviśeṣe ca, kṣetrasya vā kṛṣyādau karmaviśeṣe, phalaparivṛddhir bhavati | ta eva vrīhaya ūṣara uptā na prarohanti kṛṣyādiparikarmite tu kṣetre uptāḥ stambakarayo bhavanti | evaṃ puruṣe ‘pi garbhādhānādisaṃskārasaṃpanne draṣṭavyam
|| 2.2.4 ||
etena doṣaphalaparivṛddhir uktā || 2.2.5 ||
etenaiva nyāyena duṣṭakarmaphalaparivṛddhir apy uktā veditavyā | tatrohena paṭhitavyam “sarvavarṇānām” ityādiviparītam; “sarvavarṇānāṃ svadharmānanuṣṭhāne paramaparimitaṃ duḥkham” [cf. ĀpDh 2.2.2] | tataḥ karmaphalaśeṣeṇa duṣṭān jātyādikān dravyān tān adharmānuṣṭhānam iti tac cakravad ubhayor lokayor duḥkha eva vartate | yathauṣadhivanaspatīnāṃ bījasya kṣetrakarmaviśeṣābhāve phalahānir evam iti || 2.2.5 ||
doṣaphalaparivṛddhāv udāharaṇam āha \
steno ‘bhiśasto brāhmaṇo rājanyo vaiśyo vā parasmin loke ‘parimite niraye vṛtte jāyate cāṇḍālo brāhmaṇaḥ paulkaso rājanyo vaiṇo vaiśyaḥ || 2.2.6 ||
stenaḥ svarṇacoraḥ | abhiśasto brahmahā | steno ‘bhiśasto vā brāhmaṇādir amuṣmin loke aparimite niraye doṣaphalam anubhūya tasmin vṛtte parikṣīṇe brāhmaṇaś cāṇḍalo jāyate, rājanyaḥ paulkasaḥ | śūdrāt kṣatriyāyāṃ jātaḥ pulkasaḥ sa eva paulkasaḥ, prajñāditvād aṇ | vaiśyo vaiṇo jāyate, veṇunartako vaiṇaḥ || 2.2.6 ||
etenānye doṣaphalaiḥ karmabhiḥ paridhvaṃsā doṣaphalāsu yoniṣu jāyante varṇaparidhvaṃsāyāṃ yathā || 2.2.7 ||
varṇaparidhvaṃsā varṇebhyaḥ pracyavanam, tasyāṃ varṇaparidhvaṃsāyāṃ yathā brāhmaṇādayaś caṇḍālādyā jāyante, etena prakāreṇa stenābhiśastābhyāṃ anye ‘pi doṣaphalaiḥ karmabhir doṣaphalāsu sūkarādiṣu yoniṣu jāyante paridhvaṃsāḥ svajātibhraṣṭāḥ santa ity arthaḥ | te tathāvagantavyā iti || 2.2.7 ||
caṇḍālopasparśane saṃbhāṣāyāṃ darśane ca doṣas tatra
prāyaścittam || 2.2.8 ||
caṇḍālopasparśane doṣo bhavati | tathā saṃbhāṣāyāṃ darśane ca | upasamastam api caṇḍālagrahaṇam abhisaṃbaddhyate | tatra sarvatra prāyaścittaṃ vakṣyate || 2.2.8 ||
avagāhanam apām upasparśane saṃbhāṣāyāṃ
brāhmaṇasaṃbhāṣā darśane jyotiṣāṃ darśanam || 2.2.9 ||
upasparśane saty apām evāvagāhanaṃ prāyaścittam | ṛjunī uttare | svakarmapraśaṃsāprakaraṇe prāyaścittābhidhānaṃ svakarmapracyutānāṃ nindārtham | evaṃ nāma ninditaś caṇḍālaḥ, yasya darśane prāyaścittam | sa vā eṣa jāyate svakarmapracyuto brāhmaṇaḥ || 2.2.9 ||
iti dvitīyaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
iti śrīharadattamiśraviracitāyām āpastambadharmasūtravṛttau
ujjvalāyāṃ dvitīyapraśne prathaṃaḥ paṭalaḥ
āryāḥ prayatā vaiśvadeve ‘nnasaṃskartāraḥ syuḥ || 2.3.1 ||
āryās traivarṇikāḥ, “āryādhiṣṭhitā vā śūdrāḥ” [ĀpDh 2.3.4] ity uttaratradarśanāt | prayatāḥ snānādinā śuddhāḥ vaiśvadeve gṛhamedhinor bhojanārthe pāke, “gṛhamedhino yad aśanīyasya” [ĀpDh 2.3.12] iti darśanāt | annasaṃskartāraḥ syuḥ, annaṃ ca bhakṣyabhojyapeyādikam | tat saṃskuryuḥ | na svayaṃ nāpi striyaḥ || 2.3.1 ||
bhāṣāṃ kāsaṃ kṣavathum ity abhimukho ‘nnaṃ
varjayet || 2.3.2 ||
bhāṣā śabdoccāraṇam | kāsaḥ kaṇṭhe ghuraghurāśabdaḥ | kṣavathuḥ kṣutam | etat trayam annābhimukho na kuryāt | “saṃskartāraḥ syuḥ” [ĀpDh 2.3.1] iti bahuvacane prakṛte varjayed ity ekavacanaṃ pratyekam upadeśāt || 2.3.2 ||
keśān aṅgaṃ vāsaś cālabhyāpa upaspṛśet || 2.3.3 ||
keśādīn ātmīyān anyadīyān vālabhya spṛṣṭvā apa upaspṛśet | nedaṃ snānam | kiṃ tarhi | sparśanamātram | keśālambhe pūrvam apy upasparśanaṃ vihitam, idaṃ tu vacanam – tatrokaṃ vaikalpikaṃ śakṛdādyupasparśanaṃ mā bhūd iti || 2.3.3 ||
āryādhiṣṭhitā vā śūdrāḥ saṃskartāraḥ syuḥ || 2.3.4 ||
traivarṇikair adhiṣṭhitāḥ śūdrāś ca saṃskartāraḥ syuḥ | prakaraṇād annasyeti gamyate || 2.3.4 ||
teṣāṃ sa evācamanakalpaḥ || 2.3.5 ||
teṣāṃ śūdrāṇām annasaṃskāre adhikṛtānāṃ sa evācamanakalpo veditavyaḥ, yasyānnaṃ pacati | yadi brāhmaṇasya, hṛdayaṃgamābhir adbhiḥ | atha kṣatriyasya, kaṇṭhagābhiḥ | yadi vaiśyasya, tālugābhiḥ | indriyopasparśanaṃ ca bhavati || 2.3.5 ||
adhikam ahar ahaḥ keśaśmaśrulomanakhavāpanam || 2.3.6 ||
śūdrāḥ pacantaḥ pratyahaṃ keśādi vāpayeyuḥ | idam eṣām adhikam āryebhyaḥ || 2.3.6 ||
udakopasparśanaṃ ca saha vāsasā || 2.3.7 ||
sahaiva ca vāsasā snānaṃ kuryuḥ | āryāṇāṃ tu parihitaṃ vāso nidhāya kaupīnācchādanamātreṇāpi snānaṃ bhavati | śūdrāṇām api pākād anyatra | tathā ca manuḥ |
na vāsobhiḥ sahājasraṃ nāvijñāte jalāśaye || iti || [MDh 4.129]
|| 2.3.7 ||
api vāṣṭamīṣv eva parvasu vā vaperan || 2.3.8 ||
yadi vāṣṭamīṣv eva vaperan keśādīn, parvasv eva vā, na pratyaham | vaperann ity antarbhāvitaṇyarthaḥ, vāpayeyur ity arthaḥ | tathā ca “lomanakhavāpanam” [ĀpDh 2.3.6] iti pūrvaṃ ṇicprayuktaḥ|| 2.3.8 ||
parokṣam annaṃ saṃskṛtam agnāv adhiśrityādbhiḥ prokṣet |
tad devapavitram ity ācakṣate || 2.3.9 ||
yadi śūdrāḥ parokṣam annaṃ saṃskuryur āryair adhiṣṭhitāḥ, tadā tat parokṣam annaṃ saṃskṛtam āhṛtaṃ svayam agnāv adhiśrayet | adhiśrityādbhiḥ prokṣet | tad evaṃbhūtam annaṃ devapavitram ity ācakṣate, devānām api tat pavitram, kiṃ punar manuṣyāṇām iti || 2.3.9 ||
siddhe ‘nne tiṣṭhan bhūtam iti svāmine prabrūyāt || 2.3.10 ||
siddhe pakve ‘nne tiṣṭhan pācako ‘dhiṣṭhito vā bhūtam iti brūyāt | kasmai, yasya tad annaṃ tasmai svāmine | bhūtaṃ niṣpannam ity arthaḥ || 2.3.10 ||
tat subhūtaṃ virāḍ annaṃ tan mā kṣāyīti prativacanaḥ || 2.3.11 ||
“tat subhūtam” ityādiprativacano mantraḥ | tad annaṃ subhūtaṃ suṣṭu niṣpannaṃ virāṭ virājanasādhanam annam | tac ca mā kṣāyi kṣīṇaṃ mā bhūd ity arthaḥ || 2.3.11 ||
gṛhamedhino yad aśanīyasya homā balayaś ca
svargapuṣṭisaṃyuktāḥ || 2.3.12 ||
gṛhamedhino yad aśanīyaṃ pakvam apakvaṃ vā upasthitaṃ tasyaikadeśena homā balayaś ca vakṣyamāṇāḥ kartavyāḥ | svargaḥ puṣṭiś ca teṣāṃ phalam iti || 2.3.12 ||
teṣāṃ mantrāṇām upayoge dvādaśāham adhaḥśayyā
brahmacaryaṃ kṣāralavaṇavarjanaṃ ca || 2.3.13 ||
teṣāṃ homānāṃ balīnāṃ ca ye mantrāḥ teṣām upayoge | upayogo niyamapūrvakaṃ vidyāgrahaṇam | tathā ca bodhāyanaḥ: “teṣāṃ grahaṇe dvādaśarātram” [BauGṛ 2.9.24] ityādi | tatra dvādaśarātram adhaḥśayyā sthaṇḍilaśāyitvaṃ brahmacaryaṃ maithunavarjanaṃ kṣāralavaṇavarjanaṃ ca bhavati | upayoktur eva vratam, adhyayanāṅgatvāt | anye tu patnyā apīcchanti; upayogaḥ prathamaḥ prayogaḥ, tatra ca patnyā api sahādhikāra iti vadantaḥ || 2.3.13 ||
uttamasyaikarātram upavāsaḥ || 2.3.14 ||
uttamasya “uttamena vaihāyasam” [ĀpDh 2.4.8] iti vakṣyamāṇasya “ye bhūtāḥ pracaranti” [BauGṛ 2.8.39] ity asyā ekarātram upavāsaḥ kartavyaḥ || 2.3.14 ||
balīnāṃ tasya tasya deśe saṃskāro hastena parimṛjyāvokṣya
nyupya paścāt pariṣecanam || 2.3.15 ||
balīnāṃ madhye tasya tasya baler deśaḥ saṃskāraḥ kartavyaḥ | kaḥ punar asau | hastena parimārjanam avokṣaṇaṃ ca | taṃ kṛtvā baliṃ nivapati | nyupya paścāt pariṣecanaṃ kartavyam | upadeśakramād eva siddhe praścādgrahaṇaṃ madhye gandhamālyādidānārtham ity āhuḥ | “tasya tasya” iti vacanaṃ saty api saṃbhave sakṛd eva mārjanam avokṣaṇaṃ ca mā bhūt, ekasmin deśe samavetānām api pṛthak pṛthak yathā syād iti || 2.3.15 ||
aupāsane pacane vā ṣaḍbhir ādyaiḥ pratimantraṃ hastena
juhuyāt || 2.3.16 ||
yatra pacyate sa pacanāgniḥ | aupāsanavatām aupāsane, vidhurādeḥ pacana iti vyavasthito vikalpaḥ | anye tulayavikalpaṃ manyante | ṣaḍbhir ādyaiḥ – “agnaye svāhā, somāya svāhā, viśvebhyo devebhyaḥ svāhā, dhruvāya bhaumāya svāhā, dhruvakṣitaye svāhā, acyutakṣitaye svāhā” [cf. BauGṛ 2.8.9] ity etaiḥ | ete hi mantrāḥ mantrapāṭhe paṭhitāḥ prāgvivāhamantrebhyaḥ viśiṣṭaniyamasāpekṣagrahatvāt taiḥ saha gṛhyante | kecit – sauviṣṭakṛtam api saptamaṃ juhvati “agnaye sviṣṭakṛte svāhā” iti auṣadhahaviṣkeṣu sarvatra tasya pravṛttir iti vadantaḥ | anye tu – somāya svāhā iti na paṭhanti, sauviṣṭakṛtaṃ ṣaṣṭhaṃ paṭhanti | hastagrahaṇaṃ dravyādinivṛttyartham || 2.3.16 ||
ubhayataḥ pariṣecanaṃ yathā purastāt || 2.3.17 ||
ubhayataḥ purastād upariṣṭāc ca pariṣecanaṃ kartavyam | katham | yathā purastāt, uktaṃ gṛhye “adite ‘numanyasva” [ĀpGṛ 2.3.8] iti ca | sāmayācārikeṣu pārvaṇātideśo na pravartata iti jñāpitatvāt, aprāptavidhir ayam | anye tu – parisaṃkhyāṃ manyante, pariṣecanam eva vaiśvadeve nānyat tantram iti || 2.3.17 ||
evaṃ balīnāṃ deśe deśe samavetānāṃ sakṛt sakṛd ante
pariṣecanam || 2.3.18 ||
yathā ṣaṇṇām āhutīnāṃ pariṣecanaṃ tantram, vibhavāt evaṃ balayo ‘pi ye ekasmin deśe samavetāḥ “uttaraiḥ brahmasadane” [ĀpDh 2.4.4] ityādayaḥ teṣāṃ yad ante pariṣecanaṃ prāptam “paścāt pariṣecanam” [ĀpDh 2.3.15] ity anena vihitaṃ tat sarvānte sakṛt kartavyam, na pratyekaṃ pṛthag iti | asaty asmin sūtre pūrvatra “tasya tasya” [ĀpDh 2.3.15] iti vacanāt yathā parimārjanam avokṣaṇaṃ ca pratyekaṃ pṛthag bhavati tathā pariṣecanam api syāt | atra ca upadeśād eva ye ekadeśasthā balayaḥ teṣām eva sakṛt pariṣecanam, na yādṛcchike samavāye | tena yady apy agārasyottarapūrvadeśaḥ śayyādeśaḥ, tathāpi kāmaliṅgasya pṛthak pariṣecanaṃ bhavati || 2.3.18 ||
sati sūpasaṃspṛṣṭena kāryāḥ || 2.3.19 ||
sati sūpe tatsaṃsṛṣṭā balayaḥ kāryāḥ | anye tu – anyair api vyañjanaiḥ saṃsargam icchanti | tathā ca bodhāyanaḥ: “kāmam itareṣv āyataneṣu” iti [BauGṛ 2.8.5] | eṣa hi vyañjanānāṃ saṃskāraḥ | sūtrasyāpi – vyañjanaiḥ suṣṭūpasaṃsṛṣṭenānnena balayaḥ kāryāḥ sati saṃbhava ity arthaḥ iti
|| 2.3.19 ||
apareṇāgniṃ saptamāṣṭamābhyām udagapavargam || 2.3.20 ||
agneḥ paścāt saptamāṣṭamābhyāṃ “dharmāya dvāhā, adharmāya svāhā” [BauGṛ 2.8.11] ity etābhyāṃ baliharaṇaṃ kartavyam | udagapavargam, na prāgapavargam || 2.3.20 ||
udadhānasaṃnidhau navamena || 2.3.21 ||
udakaṃ yatra dhīyate tad udadhānaṃ maṇikākhyam | tasya saṃnidhau navamena “adbhyaḥ svāhā” [BauGṛ 2.8.9] ity etena || 2.3.21 ||
madhye ‘gārasya daśamaikādaśābhyāṃ prāgapavargam || 2.3.22 ||
“oṣadhivanaspatibhyaḥ svāhā, rakṣodevajanebhyaḥ svāhā” [BauGṛ 2.8.17] ity etābhyām | agārasya madhye prāgapavargaṃ kartavyam || 2.3.22 ||
uttarapūrvadeśe ‘gārasyottaraiś caturbhiḥ || 2.3.23 ||
agārasya ya uttarapūrvo deśaḥ tatra uttaraiś caturbhiḥ “gṛhyābhyaḥ svāhā, avasānebhyaḥ svāhā, avasānapatibhyaḥ svāhā, sarvabhūtebhyaḥ svāhā” [BauGṛ 2.8.25] ity etaiḥ prāgapavargam ity eva || 2.3.23 ||
iti tṛtīyaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
śayyādeśe kāmaliṅgena || 2.4.1 ||
śayyādeśe “kāmāya svāhā” [BauGṛ 2.8.18] ity anena || 2.4.1 ||
dehalyām antarikṣaliṅgena || 2.4.2 ||
dehalī dvārasyādhastād dāru, tasyādhovedikety ‘nye | antardvārasya ca grahaṇam | tatrāntarikṣaliṅgena “antarikṣāya svāhā” [BauGṛ 2.8.26] ity anena || 2.4.2 ||
uttareṇāpidhānyām || 2.4.3 ||
yenāpidhīyate dvāraṃ sāpidhānī kavāṭam | tad argalam ity anye | tatrottareṇa mantreṇa “yad ejati jagati yac ca ceṣṭhati nāmno bhāgo yannāmne svāhā” [BauGṛ 2.8.29] ity anena || 2.4.3 ||
uttarair brahmasadane || 2.4.4 ||
agārasyety anuvṛttes tasya yo brahmasadanākhyo deśaḥ vāśtuvidyāyāṃ prasiddho madhye ‘gārasya, tatrottarair daśabhiḥ “pṛthivyai svāhā, antarikṣāya svāhā, dive svāhā, sūryāya svāhā, candramase svāhā, nakṣatrebhyaḥ svāhā, indrāya svāhā, bṛhaspataye svāhā, prajāpataye svāhā, brahmaṇe svāhā” [BauGṛ 2.8.33] ity etaiḥ | prāgapavargam ity eva | apara āha – “madhye ‘gārasya” [ĀpDh 2.3.22] ity atra tasya deśasyopayuktatvāt brahmā yatra sīdati gārhyeṣu karmasu agner dakṣiṇataḥ brahmasadanaṃ tatreti || 2.4.4 ||
dakṣiṇataḥ pitṛliṅgeṇa prācīnāvīty avācīnapāṇiḥ kuryāt || 2.4.5 ||
anantarāṇāṃ balīnāṃ dakṣiṇataḥ pitṛliṅgena “svadhā pitṛbhyaḥ” ity anena baliṃ kuryāt, prācīnāvīty avācīnapāṇiś ca bhūtvā dakṣiṇaṃ pāṇim uttānaṃ kṛtvā aṅguṣṭhatarjanyor antarālena
|| 2.4.5 ||
raudra uttaro yathā devatābhyaḥ || 2.4.6 ||
pitṛbaler uttarato raudrabaliḥ kartavyaḥ, yathā devatābhyas tathā | “prācīnāvīty avācīnapāṇiḥ” [ĀpDh 2.4.5] iti nānuvartata ity arthaḥ | “namo rudrāya paśupataye svāhā” iti mantraḥ | atra yady api paśupatiliṅgam apy asti, tathāpi rudrasyaiva viśeṣaṇam iti raudra iti vyapadeśo nānupapannaḥ | devatāsmaraṇam api rudrāyety eva kurvanti | rudrāya paśupataya ity anye | kecit tu – uttaro mantro raudro na paśupatidevatyaḥ iti vyācakṣate | teṣāṃ deśaḥ prāg vā udag vā pitryāt || 2.4.6 ||
tayor nānā pariṣecanaṃ darmabhedāt || 2.4.7 ||
tayor anantaroktayor balyor ekasmin deśe samavetayor api nānā pṛthak pariṣecanaṃ kartavyam | kutaḥ | dharmabhedāt | pitryasyāpradakṣiṇaṃ pariṣecanam, itarasya daivatatvāt pradakṣiṇam iti
|| 2.4.7 ||
naktam evottamena vaihāyasam || 2.4.8 ||
uttamena, “ye bhūtāḥ pracaranti naktaṃ balim icchanto vitudasya preṣyāḥ | tebhyo baliṃ puṣṭikāmo harāmi mayi puṣṭiṃ puṣṭipatir dadhātu svāhā” [BauGṛ 2.8.39] ity anena vaihāyasaṃ baliṃ dadyāt | tac ca naktam eva | “vaihāyasam” iti vacanāt ākāśa eva baliḥ utkṣepyaḥ | na chadiḥpariṣkṛte deśe | tathā ca bodhāyanaḥ: “yathākāśe utkṣipati ye bhūtāḥ” [BauGṛ 2.8.38] iti | apara āha – evakāro bhinnakramaḥ, naktam uttamenaiva balir iti | tatra balyantarāṇāṃ rātrau nivṛttiḥ | anye tūhena divāpi baliṃ haranti “divā balim icchantaḥ” iti, āśvalāyanake tathā darśanāt: “divā cāribhya iti divā | naktaṃ cāribhya iti naktam” iti [ĀśGṛ 1.2.7–8] || 2.4.8 ||
Added:        [viśvebhyaś caiva devebhyo balim ākāśa utkṣipet |
divācarebhyo bhūtebhyo naktaṃcāribhya eva ca || iti ]
ya etān avyagro yathopadeśaṃ kurute nityaḥ svargaḥ
puṣṭiś ca || 2.4.9 ||
ya etān anantaroktān balīn homāṃś ca avyagraḥ samāhitamanā bhūtvā yathopadeśam upadeśānatikrameṇa kurute, ya iti vacanāt tasyeti gamyate, tasya nityaḥ svargaḥ, puṣṭiś ca nityā | “svargapuṣṭisaṃyuktāḥ” [ĀpDh 2.3.12] iti yat pūrvam uktaṃ tasyārthavādatāśaṅkā mā bhūd iti punarvacanam | puṣṭisvargau nityāv eva bhavataḥ, na prabalair api karmāntarair bādhanam iti
|| 2.4.9 ||
agraṃ ca deyam || 2.4.10 ||
baliharaṇānantaram agraṃ ca deyam bhikṣave || 2.4.10 ||
atithīn evāgre bhojayet || 2.4.11 ||
atithīn vakṣyati [ĀpDh 2.6.5] | tān evāgre bhojayet, na svayaṃ saha bhuñjīta pūrvaṃ vā | evam atithivyatiriktān anyān api bhojayitavyān paścād eva bhojayet || 2.4.11 ||
bālān vṛddhān rogasaṃbaddhān strīś cāntarvatnīḥ || 2.4.12 ||
ye ca gṛhavartino bālādayaḥ tām apy agre bhojayet | “antarvatnīḥ” ity eva siddhe strīgrahaṇaṃ svasrādīnām api grahaṇārtham, antarvatnīgrahaṇaṃ sarvapūrvārtham || 2.4.12 ||
kāle svāmināv annārthinaṃ na pratyācakṣīyātām || 2.4.13 ||
kāle vaiśvadevānte annārthinam upasthitaṃ svāminau gṛhapatī na pratyācakṣīyātām, avaśyaṃ kiṃcit tasmai deyam iti || 2.4.13 ||
abhāve kiṃ kartavyam | tatrāha |
abhāve bhūmir udakaṃ tṛṇāni kalyāṇī vāg iti |
etāni vai sato ‘gāre na kṣīyante kadā ca neti || 2.4.14 ||
bhūmir upaveśanayogyā | udakaṃ pādaprakṣālanādiyogyam | tṛṇāni śayanāsanayogyāni | kalyāṇī vāk “svāgatam āyuṣmatehāsyatām” ityādikā | etāni bhūmyādīni tāvat sataḥ satpuruṣasya nirdhanasyāpi gṛhe kadāpi na kṣīyante | vaiśabdaḥ prasiddhau | etair upacāraḥ kartavyaḥ | itiśabdaprayogād evaṃ dharmajñā upadiśantīti || 2.4.14 ||
evaṃvṛttāv anantalokau bhavataḥ || 2.4.15 ||
yau gṛhamedhinau vivāhād ārabhyāntād evaṃvṛttau bhavataḥ tayor anantā lokā bhavanti | jyotiṣṭomādibhyo ‘pi hi katipayadinasādhyebhyo duṣkaram etad ānātād vṛttam || 2.4.15 ||
brāhmaṇāyānadhīyānāyāsanam udakam annam iti deyam |
na pratyuttiṣṭhet || 2.4.16 ||
yady anadhīyāno brāhmaṇo ‘tithidharmeṇāgacchet tadā tasmai āsanādikaṃ deyam, pratyutthānaṃ tu na kartavyam | asmād eva jñāyate adhīyāne pratyutheyam iti || 2.4.16 ||
abhivādanāyaivottiṣṭhed abhivādyaś cet || 2.4.17 ||
yadi punar asāv anadhīyāno ‘pi “daśavarṣaṃ paurasakhyam” [ĀpDh 1.14.13] ityādinā abhivādyo bhavati tadā abhivādanāyaivotthiṣṭhet || 2.4.17 ||
rājanyavaiśyau ca || 2.4.18 ||
rājanyavaiśvāv adhīyānāv api na pratyutthiṣṭhet brāhmaṇaḥ | āsanādikaṃ tu deyam iti || 2.4.18 ||
śūdram abhyāgataṃ karmaṇi niyuñjyāt | athāsmai
dadyāt || 2.4.19 ||
yadi śūdro dvijātiṃ praty atithir āgacchati tadā tam udakāharaṇādau karmaṇi niyuñjyāt niyuñjīta | atha tasmin kṛte tasmai bhojanaṃ dadyāt || 2.4.19 ||
dāsā vā rājakulād āhṛtyātithivac chūdraṃ pūjayeyuḥ || 2.4.20 ||
atha vā ye asya gṛhamedhino dāsās te rājakulād āhṛtya taṃ śūdram atithivat pūjayeyuḥ | ata eva jñāyate “śūdrāṇām atithīnāṃ pūjārthaṃ vrīhyādikaṃ grāme grāme rājñā sthāpyam” iti
|| 2.4.20 ||
nityam uttaraṃ vāsaḥ kāryam || 2.4.21 ||
“upāsane gurūṇām” [ĀpDh 1.15.1] ityādinā keṣucit kāleṣu yajñopavītaṃ vihitam, iha tu prakaraṇād gṛhasthasya nityam uttaraṃ vāso dhāryam ity ucyate || 2.4.21 ||
api vā sūtram evopavītārthe || 2.4.22 ||
api vā sūtram eva sarveṣām upavītakṛtye bhavati, na vāsa eva | atra manuḥ |
kārpāsam upavītaṃ syād viprasyordhvavṛtaṃ trivṛt | iti | [MDh 2.44]
bodhāyanas tu: “kauśaṃ sūtraṃ vā tris trivṛd yajñopavītam” iti [BDh 1.8.5] |
yatra bhujyate tat samūhya nirhṛtyāvokṣya taṃ deśam amatrebhyo lepāṃt saṃkṛṣyādibiḥ saṃsṛjyottarataḥ śucau deśe rudrāya ninayet | evaṃ vāstu śivaṃ bhavati || 2.4.23 ||
yatra sthāne bhujyate tatratyam ucchiṣṭādikaṃ samūhya rāśīkṛtya nirharet, anyato nirhṛtya taṃ deśam avokṣet | tato ‘matrebhyo yeṣu pākaḥ kṛtas tāny amatrāṇi tebhyo ‘nnalepān vyañjanalepāṃś ca saṃkṛṣya kāṣṭhādināpakṛṣyādbhiḥ saṃsṛjet | saṃsṛjya gṛhasyottarataḥ śucau deśe “rudrāyedam astu” iti ninayet | evaṃ kṛte vāstu śivaṃ samṛddhaṃ bhavati || 2.4.23 ||
brāhmaṇa ācāryaḥ smaryate tu || 2.4.24 ||
tuśabdo ‘vadhāraṇārtho bhinnakramaś ca | brāhmaṇa eva sarveṣām ācāryaḥ smaryate sarvaśāstreṣu | ihāpi vakṣyati “svakarmabrāhmaṇasya” [ĀpDh 2.10.4] ityādi | anuvādo ‘yam āpadi kalpāntaraṃ vaktum || 2.4.24 ||
tad āha |
āpadi brāhmaṇena rājanye vaiśye vādhyayanam || 2.4.25 ||
kartavyam ity adhyāhāryam | brāhmaṇasyādhyāpayitur alābha āpat | tatra brāhmaṇena rājanye vaiśye vādhyayanaṃ kartavyam, na tv anadhīyānena sthātavyam | brāhmaṇeneti vacanād rājanyavaiśyayor nāyam anukalpaḥ || 2.4.25 ||
anugamanaṃ ca paścāt || 2.4.26 ||
anugamanaṃ ca paścāt pṛṣṭhataḥ kartavyaṃ yāvad adhyayanam | paścādgrahaṇaṃ lajjādinā kiyaty api pārśvagatir mā bhūd iti | sarvaśuśrūṣāprasaṅge niyamaḥ brāhmaṇasyānugamanam eva śuśrūṣeti | tathā ca gautamaḥ: “anugamanaṃ śuśrūṣā” iti [GDh 7.2] || 2.4.26 ||
tata ūrdhvaṃ brāhmaṇa evāgre gatau syāt || 2.4.27 ||
tato ‘dhyayanād ūrdhvaṃ samāpte ‘dhyayane brāhmaṇa evāgrato gacchet || 2.4.27 ||
iti caturthaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
sarvavidyānām apy upaniṣadām upākṛtyānadhyayanaṃ
tad ahaḥ || 2.5.1 ||
karmaṇi ṣaṣṭhī | sarvidyā aṅgavidyā apy upaniṣadaḥ upākṛtyādhyetum ārabhya tad ahar anadhyayanaṃ tasminn ahany adhyayanaṃ na kartavyam | upaniṣadgrahaṇaṃ prādhānyakhyāpanārtham, “brāhmaṇā āyātā vasiṣṭho ‘py āyātaḥ” itivat || 2.5.1 ||
adhītya cāviprakramaṇaṃ sadyaḥ || 2.5.2 ||
“vedam adhītya snāsyan” [ĀpGṛ 12.1] ity avasare ācāryasakāśāt sadyo viprakramaṇam na kartavyam, nāpagantavyam | prāyeṇa tu makārāt param ikāram adhīyate | tatrāpy eṣa evārthaḥ | ikāras tu chāndaso ‘papāṭho vā || 2.5.2 ||
yadi tvareta guroḥ samīkṣāyāṃ svādhyāyam adhītya kāmaṃ
gacchet | evam ubhayoḥ śivaṃ bhavati || 2.5.3 ||
yadi kāryavaśād gantuṃ tvareta tadā guror ācāryasya samīkṣāyāṃ saṃdarśane saṃśraye svādhyāyaṃ praśnāvaram adhītya yathākāmaṃ gacchet | evaṃ kṛte ubhayoḥ śiṣyācāryayoḥ śivaṃ bhavati || 2.5.3 ||
samāvṛttaṃ ced ācāryo ‘bhyāgacchet tam abhimukho ‘bhyāgamya tasyopasaṃgṛhya na bībhatsamāna udakam upaspṛśet puraskṛtyopasthāpya yathopadeśaṃ pūjayet || 2.5.4 ||
samāvṛttaṃ cec chiṣyaṃ kṛtadāram ācāryo ‘bhyāgacched atithidharmeṇa, tam abhimukho ‘bhyāgamya tasyopasaṃgṛhya karmaṇi ṣaṣṭhī, tam upasaṃgṛhya | yady api tasya caṇḍālādisparśaḥ saṃbhāvyeta, tathāpi na bībhatsamāna udakam upaspṛśet, na snāyāt | upasaṃgrahaṇe vā dhūlidhūmarau pādau spṛṣṭvā na bībhatsamāna udakam upaspṛśet | tatas taṃ puraskṛtya gṛhapraveśe agre kṛtvā pūjāsādhanāny upasthāpya yathopadeśaṃ gṛhyoktena mārgeṇa madhuparkeṇa pūjayet | pūjāvidhānaṃ gṛhyoktasyānuvādaḥ āsanādiṣu viśeṣaṃ vaktum || 2.5.4 ||
tam āha |
āsane śayane bhakṣye bhojye vāsasi vā saṃnihite
nihīnataravṛttiḥ syāt || 2.5.5 ||
saṃnihite ācārye tasminn eva gṛhe apavarakādikaṃ praviṣṭe āsanādiṣu nihīnataravṛttiḥ syāt | tarabnirdeśān nīce āsane guṇato ‘pi nikṛṣṭe āsīta | evaṃ śayanādiṣv api draṣṭavyam || 2.5.5 ||
tiṣṭhan savyena pāṇinānugṛhyācāryam ācamayet || 2.5.6 ||
tiṣṭhann iti prahva ucyate sthānayogāt, na hi sākṣāt tiṣṭhann ācamayituṃ prabhavati | savyena pāṇinā karakādikam anugṛhyādhastād gṛhītvā itareṇa dvāram avamṛjyety arthasiddhatvād anuktam | evaṃ kṛtvācāryam ācamayet svayam eva śiṣyaḥ | evaṃ hi saṃmato bhavati | ācārye prakṛte punar apy ācāryagrahaṇam ātithyād anyatrāpy ācāryam ācamayann evam evācamayed iti
|| 2.5.6 ||
anyaṃ vā samudetam || 2.5.7 ||
vāśabdaḥ samuccaye | anyam apy evam ācamayet, sa cet samudetaḥ kulaśīlavidyāvṛttavayobhir upeto bhavati || 2.5.7 ||
sthānāsanacaṅkramaṇasmiteṣv anucikīrṣan || 2.5.8 ||
vyavahitam api syād ity apekṣate | cikīrṣayā karaṇaṃ lakṣyate | sthānādiṣv ācāryasya paścādbhāvī syāt, na pūrvabhāvī na yugapadbhāvī || 2.5.8 ||
saṃnihite mūtrapurīṣavātakarmoccair bhāṣāhāsaṣṭhīvana-dantaskhalananiśṛṅkhaṇabhrukṣepaṇatālananiṣṭhyānīti
|| 2.5.9 ||
vātakarmāpānavāyor utsargaḥ | uccair bhāṣā mahatā svanena bhāṣaṇam kenāpi | hāso hasanam | ṣṭhīvanaṃ śleṣmādinirasanam | dantaskhalanaṃ dantamalāpakarṣaṇam | parasparasaṅghaṭṭanam ity anye | niśṛṅkhaṇaṃ nāsikāmalasya niḥsaraṇam | bhrukṣepaṇaṃ bhrūvikṣepaṇam | chāndaso hrasvaḥ | tālanaṃ hastayor āsphālanam | niṣṭhyam aṅgulisphoṭanam | itiśabdād anyad api svairāsanādikam | varjayed ity apekṣate | etāni mūtrakarmādīni ācāryasya saṃnidhau na kuryād
iti || 2.5.9 ||
dāre prajāyāṃ copasparśanabhāṣā visrambhapūrvāḥ
parivarjayet || 2.5.10 ||
upasparśanam āliṅganāvaghrāṇādi | bhāṣāḥ saṃbhāṣāś cāṭuprabhṛtayaḥ | etāny apy ācārye saṃnihite dāraprajāviṣaye ‘pi visrambhaṃ na kuryāt | jvarādiparīkṣāyāṃ na doṣaḥ || 2.5.10 ||
vākyena vākyasya pratighātam ācāryasya varjayet || 2.5.11 ||
ācāryavākyasya samīcīnasyetarasya vā ātmīyena vākyena tādṛśena pratighātam na kuryāt
|| 2.5.11 ||
śreyasāṃ ca || 2.5.12 ||
anyeṣām api praśastatarāṇāṃ vākyaṃ vākyena na pratihanyāt || 2.5.12 ||
sarvabhūtaparīvādākrośāṃś ca || 2.5.13 ||
sarveṣām eva bhūtānāṃ tiraścām api parīvādān doṣavādān ākrośān aślīlavādāṃś ca varjayet | parīvādasya punarvacanam atiśayena varjanārtham || 2.5.13 ||
vidyayā ca vidyānām || 2.5.14 ||
vidyayā ca vidyānāṃ parīvādākrośān varjayet | ṛgveda eva śrotramukhaḥ, anye śravaṇakaṭukā iti parīvādaḥ | taittrīyakam ucchiṣṭaśākhā, yājñavalkyādīni brāhmaṇānīdānīntanāni ityādaya ākrośāḥ
|| 2.5.14 ||
yayā vidyayā na viroceta punar ācāryam upetya niyamena
sādhayet || 2.5.15 ||
yayā vidyayādhītayā śrutayā vā na viroceta na yaśasvī syāt, tām ity arthād gamyate | tāṃ vidyāṃ punaḥ sādhayet yathā samyak siddhā bhavati tathā kuryāt | katham | ācāryaṃ tam evānyaṃ vopetya upasādya niyamenāpūrvādhigame vidyārthasya yo niyama uktas tena śuśrūṣādinā
|| 2.5.15 ||
asmin viṣaye ‘dhyāpayitur niyamaḥ |
upākaraṇādyotsarjanād adhyāpayitur niyamaḥ | lomasaṃharaṇaṃ māṃsaṃ śrāddhaṃ maithunam iti ca
varjayet || 2.5.16 ||
lomasaṃharaṇaṃ lomavāpanam | idam anāhitāgniviṣayam, āhitāgnes tu “atyalpaśo lomāni vāpayata iti vājasaneyikam” iti [ĀpŚr 4.1.5] || 2.5.16 ||
ṛtve vā jāyām || 2.5.17 ||
ṛtukāle vā jāyām upeyāt | ṛtva iti rūpasiddhir eṣā – strīṇāṃ ṛtudināni ṣoḍaśa, tatra bhavaḥ kāla ṛtvaḥ “bhave chandasi” [Pāṇ 4.4.110] iti yatpratyaye “ṛtvyavāstvya” [Pāṇ 6.4.175] iti sūtreṇa yaṇādeśo nipātitaḥ, tatra yalopaḥ chāndasaḥ | cāturmāsyeṣu prayuktaṃ “ṛtve vā jāyām | noparyās te” [ĀpŚr 8.4.7–8] ityādi yathā || 2.5.17 ||
yathāgamaṃ śiṣyebhyo vidyāsaṃpradāne niyameṣu ca yuktaḥ syāt | evaṃ vartamānaḥ pūrvāparān saṃbandhān ātmānaṃ ca kṣeme yunakti || 2.5.18 ||
yathā yena prakāreṇāgamaḥ arthapāṭhayos tathaiva śiṣyebhyo nirmatsareṇa vidyā saṃpradeyā | evaṃbhūte vidyāsaṃpradāne yuktaḥ avahitaḥ syāt | ye ca gṛhasthasya niyamā adhyāpane anyatra ca, teṣv api yuktaḥ syāt | evaṃ yukto vartamānaḥ pūrvāparān pūrvān pitṛpitāmahaprapitāmahān parāṃś ca putrapautranaptṝn saṃbandhān, karmaṇi ghañ | saṃbandhinaś ca puruṣān ātmānaṃ ca kṣeme yunakti abhaye sthāne nākapṛṣṭhe sthāpayati || 2.5.18 ||
manasā vācā prāṇena cakṣuṣā śrotreṇa         tvakchiśnodarārambhaṇān āsrāvān parīvṛñjāno ‘mṛtatvāya
kalpate || 2.5.19 ||
yaiḥ puruṣa āsrāvyate bahir ākṛṣyate te āsrāvāḥ śabdādayo viṣayāḥ | te viśiṣyante – tvakchiśnodarārambhaṇāḥ | ārabhyante ālabhyanta ity ārambhaṇāḥ | tatra tvagālambhanāḥ srakcandanādayaḥ | śiśnālambhanaṃ stryupabhogaḥ | udarālambhanā bhakṣabhojyādayaḥ | upalakṣaṇaṃ tvagādigrahaṇam | evaṃbhūtān āsrāvān manaādibhiḥ pañcabhir indriyaiḥ parīvṛñjānaḥ sarvato varjayann amṛtatvāya mokṣāya kalpate | tatra vāg iti rasanendriyam āha, prāṇa iti ghrāṇam || 2.5.19 ||
iti pañcamaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
ity āpastambadharmasūtravṛttāv ujjvalāyāṃ
dvitīyapraśne dvitīyaḥ paṭalaḥ
jātyācārasaṃśaye dharmārtham āgatam agnim upasamādhāya
jātim ācāraṃ ca pṛcchet || 2.6.1 ||
avijñātapūrvo yo dharmārtham adhyayanārtham āgacchet upasīdet “upasanno ‘smi bhagavan maitreṇa cakṣuṣā paśya śivena manasānugṛhāṇa, prasīda mām adhyāpaya” iti | taṃ jātyācārasaṃśaye saty agnim upasamādhāya “yatra kva cāgnim” [ĀpDh 2.1.13] ityādi “anyad upadadhyāt” [ĀpDh 2.1.14] ityantaṃ kṛtvā tatsaṃnidhau jātim ācāraṃ ca pṛcchet, kiṃgotro ‘si saumya, kimācāraś cāsi” iti || 2.6.1 ||
sādhutāṃ cet pratijānīte ‘gnir upadraṣṭā vāyur upaśrotādityo ‘nukhyātā sādhutāṃ pratijānīte sādhv asmā astu vitatha eṣa enasa ity uktvā śāstuṃ pratipadyeta || 2.6.2 ||
sa cet sādhutāṃ pratijānīte sādhujanmāsmi, amuṣya putro ‘muṣya pautro ‘muṣya naptā, sādhvācāraś cāsmi pitraivopānāyiṣi, śikṣitācāraś cāsmi, samyak cāvartiṣi, vidhibalena tu bālya eva sarve diṣṭāṃ gatiṃ gatāḥ, etasmāt kevalam anadhītaveda iti | tataḥ “agnir upadraṣṭā” ityādikaṃ mantram uktvā śāstuṃ śāsitum adhyāpayituṃ dharmāṃś copadeṣṭuṃ pratipadyeta upakrameta || 2.6.2 ||
agnir iva jvalann atithir abhyāgacchati || 2.6.3 ||
pañcayajñānte “atithīn evāgre bhojayet” [ĀpDh 2.4.11] ity uktam | tatprakāraṃ vaktuṃ tasyāvaśyakartavyatām anenāha – atithir gṛhān abhyāgacchann agnir iva jvalann abhyāgacchati | tasmād asau bhojanādibhir avaśyaṃ tarpayitavyaḥ | nirāśas tu gato gṛhān dahed iti || 2.6.3 ||
idānīm atithilakṣaṇaṃ vaktuṃ tadupayogi śrotriyalakṣaṇam āha |
dharmeṇa vedānām ekaikāṃ śākhām adhītya śrotriyo
bhavati || 2.6.4 ||
vidyārthasya yo niyamaḥ sa dharmaḥ, tena vedānāṃ yāṃ kāṃcana śākhām adhītya śrotriyo bhavati | puruṣasya hi prativedam ekaikā śākhā bhavati | yā pūrvaiḥ parigṛhītā adhyayanānuṣṭhānābhyām, sā prativedaṃ svaśākhā | tām adhītya śrotriyo bhavati | na tu prativedam ekaikāṃ śākhām adhītya śrotriyo bhavati, lokavirodhāt | loke hi yāṃ kāṃcana śākhām adhīyānaḥ śrotriyaḥ prasiddhaḥ || 2.6.4 ||
atithilakṣaṇam āha |
svadharmayuktaṃ kuṭumbinam abhyāgacchati
dharmapuraskaro nānyaprayojanaḥ so ‘tithir
bhavati || 2.6.5 ||
ādito yacchabdo draṣṭavyaḥ, ante sa iti darśanāt | madhye ca śrotriyalakṣaṇopadeśāt, tadupajīvanena sūtraṃ yojyam | yaḥ śrotriyaḥ svadharmayuktaṃ svadharmanirataṃ kuṭumbinaṃ bhāryayā saha vasantaṃ gṛhastham | āśramāntaranirāsārtham idam uktam | na hi te pacamānā bhavanti, bhikṣavo hi te | abhyāgacchati uddiśyāgacchati | dharmapuraskāraḥ ācāryārthaṃ bhikṣaṇaṃ dharmaḥ, taṃ puraskarotīti dharmapuraskāraḥ | karmaṇy aṇ | dharmaprayojano nānyaprayojanaḥ | ya evaṃbhūta evaṃbhūtam uddiśya āgacchati na yadṛcchayā, so ‘tithir iti | bodhāyanas tu: “śrānto dṛṣṭapūrvaḥ kevalam annārthī nānyaprayojano yaḥ so ‘tithir bhavati” | api vā sarvavarṇānām anyatamaḥ kāle yathopapannaḥ sarveṣām atithīnāṃ śreṣṭatamo ‘tithir bhavati
|| 2.6.5 ||
tasya pūjāyāṃ śāntiḥ svargaḥ puṣṭiś ca || 2.6.6 ||
tasyātitheḥ pūjāyāṃ kṛtāyāṃ śāntir upadravāṇām iha bhavati | pretya ca svargalābhaḥ || 2.6.6 ||
tam abhimukho ‘bhyāgamya yathāvayaḥ sametya tasyāsanam
āhārayet || 2.6.7 ||
tam atithim abhimukho ‘bhyāgacchet | abhyāgamya yathavayaḥ vayaso ‘nurūpaṃ pratyutthānābhivādanādinā sameyāt saṃgacchet | sametya ca tasyāsanam āhārayet śiṣyādibhiḥ, abhāve svayam evāharet || 2.6.7 ||
śaktiviṣaye nābahupādam āsanaṃ bhavatīty eke || 2.6.8 ||
śaktau satyām abahupādam āsanaṃ na deyam, kiṃ tu bahupādam eva pīṭhādikam ity eke manyante | svayaṃ tv abahupādam apy anumanyate || 2.6.8 ||
tasya pādau prakṣālayet | śūdramithunāv ity eke || 2.6.9 ||
dvau śūdrau tasya pādau prakṣālayetām ity eke manyante | dāsavata idam || 2.6.9 ||
atra viśeṣaḥ |
anyataro ‘bhiṣecane syāt || 2.6.10 ||
abhiṣecanaṃ karakādinā jalāvasekaḥ | tam ekaḥ kuryāt, ekaḥ prakṣālanam || 2.6.10 ||
tasyodakam āhārayen mṛnmayenety eke || 2.6.11 ||
mṛnmayena pātreṇa tasyodakam āhartavyam ity eke manyante | svayaṃ tu taijasena || 2.6.11 ||
nodakam āhārayed asamāvṛttaḥ || 2.6.12 ||
yadā asamāvṛtto brahmacārī ācāryapreṣitaḥ svayam eva vātitir abhyāgacchati tadā nāsāv udakam āhārayet, nāsāv udakāharaṇasya prayojakaḥ | nāsmā udakam āhartavyam iti || 2.6.12 ||
adhyayanāsāṃvṛttiś cātrādhikā || 2.6.13 ||
atrāsamāvṛtte ‘tithau adhyayanasaṃvṛttiś cādhikā itarasmād atitheḥ | adhyayanasya sahaniṣpādanam adhyayanasaṃvṛttiḥ | yaḥ pradeśaḥ tasyāgacchati sa tena saha kiyantaṃ kālaṃ vaktavya iti | prasiddhe tu pāṭhe pūrvapādāntasya, samo ‘kārasya ca chāndaso dīrghaḥ || 2.6.13 ||
sāntvayitvā tarpayed rasair bhakṣyair adbhir
avarārdhyeneti || 2.6.14 ||
tataḥ pādaprakṣālanasya samadhyayanasya vānantaram atithiṃ priyavacanena sāntvayet | sāntvayitvā gavyādibhiḥ rasaiḥ phalādibhiś ca bhakṣyaiḥ, adbhir antato ‘dbhir api tāvat tarpayet tṛptaṃ kuryāt | avarārdhyeneti jaghanyakalpatāṃ sūcayati | antata ity arthaḥ | itiśabdād evamādibhir anyair api || 2.6.14 ||
āvasathaṃ dadyād upariśayyām upastaraṇam upadhānaṃ
sāvastaraṇam abhyañjanaṃ ceti || 2.6.15 ||
āvasatho viśramasthānam | upariśayyā khaṭvā | upastaraṇaṃ tūlikā | upadhānam upabarhaṇam, avastaraṇam uparipaṭaḥ, tatsahitam upadhānam upastaraṇaṃ ca | abhyañjanaṃ pādayos tailaṃ ghṛtaṃ vā | etat sarvaṃ dadyād bhojanāt prāg pūrvaṃ vā apekṣite kāle | itiśabdād anyad apy apekṣitam || 2.6.15 ||
annasaṃskartāram āhūya vrīhīn yavān vā tadarthān
nirvapet || 2.6.16 ||
yaḥ pacati tam annasaṃskartāram āhūya tadarthān atithyarthān vrīhīn yavān vā nirvapet pṛthakkṛtya dadyāt amuṣmai paceti | vrīhiyavagrahaṇam upalakṣaṇam || 2.6.16 ||
idaṃ bhuktavatsu sarveṣv atithāv upasthite draṣṭavyam | bhojanakāle tv āha |
uddhṛtāny annāny avekṣeta idaṃ bhūyā3 idā3m iti || 2.6.17 ||
yāvanto bhoktāras tāvad vānnāny uddhṛtya pṛthakpātreṣu kuryāt | so ‘yaṃ saṃvibhāgaḥ | taṃ kṛtvā tāny annāny avekṣeta, kim idaṃ bhūyaḥ prabhūtam idaṃ veti | vicāre plutaḥ | “pūrvaṃ tu bhāṣāyām” [Pāṇ 8.2.98] ity etat tūpekṣitam | chāndasa evāyaṃ plutaprayoga iti || 2.6.17 ||
bhūya uddharety eva brūyāt || 2.6.18 ||
evam avekṣyātithyarthaṃ bhūya uddharety eva brūyāt || 2.6.18 ||
dviṣan dviṣato vā nānnam aśnīyād doṣeṇa vā mīmāṃsamānasya
mīmāṃsitasya vā || 2.6.19 ||
yaṃ svayam atithiḥ dviṣan bhavati, yo vā ātmānaṃ dveṣṭi, yo vātmānaṃ doṣeṇa mīmāṃsate ātmani steyādidoṣaṃ saṃbhāvayati, yo vā doṣeṇa mīmāṃsate ātmani steyādidoṣaṃ saṃbhāvayati, yo vā doṣeṇa mīmāṃsitaḥ yatra laukikā doṣaṃ saṃbhāvayanti, tasyāsya sarvasyānnaṃ nāśnīyāt || 2.6.19 ||
tatra hetuḥ |
pāpmānaṃ hi sa tasya bhakṣayatīti vijñāyate || 2.6.20 ||
ya evaṃvidhasyānnam aśnāti, sa tasya pāpmānam eva bhakṣayatīti vijñāyate || 2.6.20 ||
iti ṣaṣṭhaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
sa eṣa prājāpatyaḥ kuṭumbino yajño nityapratataḥ || 2.7.1 ||
sa eṣo ‘bhihitaḥ manuṣyayajñaḥ prājāpatyaḥ prajāpatinā dṛṣṭaḥ, taddevatyo vā | kuṭumbino nityaprayato yajñaḥ, nāgniṣṭomādivat kādācitkaḥ || 2.7.1 ||
tasyāgnīn saṃpādayati |
yo ‘tithīnām agniḥ sa āhavanīyo yaḥ kuṭumbe sa gārhapatyo
yasmin pacyate so ‘nvāhāryapacanaḥ || 2.7.2 ||
yo ‘tithīnāṃ jāṭharo ‘gniḥ sa āhavanīyaḥ, tatra hi hūyate | yaḥ kuṭumbe gṛhe ‘gnir aupāsanaḥ sa gārhapatyaḥ, nityadhāryatvāt | yasmin pacyate bhrāṣṭrāgnau so ‘nvāhāryapacanaḥ dakṣiṇāgniḥ, tatra hy anvāharyārthaṃ pacyate || 2.7.2 ||
ūrjaṃ puṣṭiṃ prajāṃ paśūn iṣṭāpūrtam iti gṛhāṇām aśnāti
yaḥ pūrvo ‘tither aśnāti || 2.7.3 ||
yo ‘titheḥ pūrvam aśnāti sa gṛhāṇāṃ kulasya saṃbandhi ūrgādikam aśnāti bhakṣayati vināśayati | ūrg annam | iṣṭam agnihotrādi | pūrtaṃ smārtaṃ karma khātādi | anye prasiddhāḥ || 2.7.3 ||
paya upasecanam annam agniṣṭomasaṃmitaṃ sarpiṣokthyasaṃmitaṃ madhunātirātrasaṃmitaṃ māṃsena dvādaśāhasaṃmitam udakena prajāvṛddhir āyuṣaś ca || 2.7.4 ||
paya upasecanaṃ yasya tadannam agniṣṭomatulyam | sarpiṣā upasiktam annam iti prakaraṇād gamyate tad ukthyatulyam | madhunā upasiktam annam atirātratulyam | māṃsena saha dattam annaṃ dvādaśāhatulyam | udakena dattena prajāvṛddhir bhavati, āyuṣaś ca | upasamastam api vṛddhir iti saṃbadhyate || 2.7.4 ||
priyā apriyāś cātithayaḥ svargaṃ lokaṃ gamayantīti
vijñāyate || 2.7.5 ||
apriyā udāsīnāḥ dviṣato niṣiddhatvāt || 2.7.5 ||
sa yat prātar madhyaṃdine sāyam iti dadāti savanāny eva
tāni bhavanti || 2.7.6 ||
triṣu kāleṣu dīyamānāny annāni asya yajñasya prātaḥsavanādīni trīṇi bhavanti | tasmāt sarveṣu kāleṣu dātavyam iti || 2.7.6 ||
yad anutiṣṭhaty udavasyaty eva tat || 2.7.7 ||
yat gantum uttiṣṭhantam atithim anūtthiṣṭhati tad udavasyaty eva udavasānīyā sāsya yajñasyeti | prāyeṇa ucchabdaṃ na paṭhanti, kevalam anuśabdam eva paṭhanti, tatrāpy arthaḥ sa eva || 2.7.7 ||
yat sāntvayati sā dakṣiṇā praśaṃsā || 2.7.8 ||
yat sāntvayati praśaṃsati sā praśaṃsā dakṣiṇā || 2.7.8 ||
yat saṃsādhayati te viṣṇukramāḥ || 2.7.9 ||
saṃsādhanam anuvrajanam || 2.7.9 ||
yad upāvartate so ‘vabhṛthaḥ || 2.7.10 ||
upāvartanam anuvrajya pratyāgamanam || 2.7.10 ||
iti hi brāhmaṇam || 2.7.11 ||
asya sarvaiḥ saṃbandhaḥ || 2.7.11 ||
rājānaṃ ced atithir abhyāgacchec chreyasīm asmai pūjām
ātmanaḥ kārayet || 2.7.12 ||
rājābhiṣiktaḥ kṣatriyaḥ | so ‘tithaye ‘bhyāgatāyātmano ‘pi sakāśāc chreyasīṃ pūjāṃ kārayet purohitena || 2.7.12 ||
āhitāgniṃ ced atithir abhyāgacchet svayam enam abhyudetya brūyāt | vrātya kvāvātsīr iti | vrātyodakam iti | vrātya tarpayaṃstv iti || 2.7.13 ||
yady āhitāgnim uddiśyātithir abhyāgacchet tata enam atithiṃ svayam evābhimukha upasarpet | atra svayam iti vacanād anāhitāgniḥ anyena śiṣyādinā kārayann api na duṣyati | tam abhyupetya brūyāt | vrātya kvāvātsīr iti kuśalapraśnaḥ | vrate sādhuḥ vratyaḥ sa eva vrātyaḥ iti pūjāvidhānam | kva purvasyāṃ rātrāv uṣitavān asīti | vrātyodakam iti udakadānam | vrātya tarpayaṃstv iti rasādibhis tarpayati | anusvārasakārau chāndasau | kriyābhedāt pratimantram itiśabdaḥ | etat sarveṣu kāleṣu kartavyam || 2.7.13 ||
purāgnihotrasya homād upāṃśu japet | vrātya yathā te manas tathāstv iti | vrātya yathā te vaśas tathāstv iti | vrātya yathā te priyaṃ tathāstv iti | vrātya yathā te nikāmas tathāstv iti || 2.7.14 ||
sa yadi homakālāt prāg evāsīta tadā purā homād apareṇāgniṃ darbheṣu sādayitvā “vrātya yathā te manaḥ” ityādikān mantrān upāṃśu japet brūyat | tatra pratimantram itiśabdaprayogād arthabhedāc caturṇāṃ vikalpaḥ | samuccaya ity anye | atra cādhvaryur yajamāno vā yo hotā sa japet tato juhuyāt || 2.7.14 ||
yasyoddhṛteṣv ahuteṣv agniṣv atithir abhyāgacchet svayam enam abhyudetya brūyāt | vrātyātisṛja hoṣyāmi | ity atisṛṣṭena hotavyam | anatisṛṣṭaś cej juhuyād doṣaṃ brāhmaṇam āha
|| 2.7.15 ||
uddhṛteṣv iti bahuvacanaṃ sabhyāvasathyāpekṣayā | yasya tu trayo ‘gnayaḥ tasyāhuteṣv ity anena sāmānādhikaraṇyād dhomo ‘pi triṣv api bhavati | tenāhavanīyahomād anantaram atithāv āgate triṣu homo na kṛta iti vakṣyamāṇo vidhir bhavaty eva | kaḥ punar asau | svayam enam abhyudetya brūyāt “vrātyātisṛja” anujānīhi hoṣyāmīti | tato juhudhīty atisṛjet | atisṛṣṭena hotavyam | yadi punar anatisṛṣṭo ‘nanujñāto juhuyāt tasya doṣaṃ brāhmaṇavākyam āha | tad idaṃ sarvam ātharvaṇe paṭhitaṃ pratyetavyam | atra pakṣe svayaṃ homo niyataḥ || 2.7.15 ||
ekarātriṃ ced atithīn vāsayet pārthivān lokān abhijayati dvitīyayāntarikṣyāṃs tṛtīyayā dviyāṃś caturthyā parāvato lokān aparimitābhir aparimitān lokān abhijayatīti vijñāyate || 2.7.16 ||
ya ekarātrim atithīn vāsayati sa pṛthivyāṃ bhavān lokān abhijayati | dvitīyayā rātryā antarikṣyān | tṛtiyayā dviyān | caturthyā parāvataḥ sukhasya parā mātrā yeṣu lokeṣu, tān abhijayati | aparimitābhī rātribhir aparimitān lokān abhijayatīti, vijñāyate brāhmaṇaṃ bhavati || 2.7.16 ||
asamudetaś ced atithir bruvāṇa āgacched āsanam udakam annaṃ śrotriyāya dadāmīty eva dadyāt | evam asya samṛddhaṃ bhavati || 2.7.17 ||
vidyādirahitaḥ asamutetaḥ | sa ced atithir iti bruvāṇa āgacchet tadā tasmai āsanādikaṃ śrotriyāya dadāmīty evaṃ manasi kṛtvā dadyāt | evaṃ dadato ‘sya tad dānaṃ samṛddhaṃ bhavati, śrotriyāyaiva dattaṃ bhavati || 2.7.17 ||
iti saptamaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
ity āpastambadharmasūtravṛttāv ujjvalāyāṃ
dvitīyapraśne tṛtīyaḥ paṭalaḥ
yena kṛtāvasathaḥ syād atithir na taṃ pratyuttiṣṭhet pratyavarohed vā purastāc ced abhivāditaḥ || 2.8.1 ||
yena gṛhasthenātithiḥ kṛtāvasathaḥ syāt, kṛtāvāsaḥ dattāvāsaḥ syāt, “dvitīyayāntarikṣyān” [ĀpDh 2.7.16] ityādivacanād dvitīyādiṣv ahaḥsu taṃ prati na pratyuttiṣṭhet, nāpy āsanāt pratyavarohet, sa cet tasminn ahani pūrvam evābhivāditaḥ | anabhivādite tu abhivādanāya pratyuttiṣṭhet pratyavarohec ca || 2.8.1 ||
śeṣabhojy atithīnāṃ syāt || 2.8.2 ||
“atithīn evāgre bhojayet” [ĀpDh 2.4.11] ity eva siddhe vacanam idaṃ pramādād yad annaṃ na dattam atithaye, tan na bhuñjītety evamartham || 2.8.2 ||
na rasān gṛhe bhuñjītānavaśeṣam atithibhyaḥ || 2.8.3 ||
āgāmibhyo ‘tithibhyo yathā na kiṃcid gṛhe ‘vaśiṣyate tathā gavyādayo rasā na bhojyā iti, sadyaḥ saṃpādayitum aśakyatvād rasānām || 2.8.3 ||
nātmārtham abhirūpam annaṃ pācayet || 2.8.4 ||
ātmānam uddiśyābhirūpam annaṃ svādv apūpādi na pācayet || 2.8.4 ||
gomadhuparkārho vedādhyāyaḥ || 2.8.5 ||
sāṅgasya vedasyādhyetā vedādhyāyaḥ | so ‘tithir madhuparkam arhati, gāṃ ca dakṣiṇām || 2.8.5 ||
ācārya ṛtvik snātako rājā vā dharmayuktaḥ || 2.8.6 ||
avedādhyāyā apy ācāryādayo gomadhuparkārhāḥ | ata eva jñāyate ekadeśādhyāyināv apy ṛtvigācāryau bhavata iti | dharmayukta iti rājño viśeṣaṇam | vāśabdaḥ samuccaye || 2.8.6 ||
ācāryāyartvije śvaśurāya rājñe iti parisaṃvatsarād
upatiṣṭhadbhyo gaur madhuparkaś ca || 2.8.7 ||
etad gṛhye vyākhyātam [ĀpGṛ 3.9] | gaur atra dikṣinādhikā vidhīyate || 2.8.7 ||
ko ‘sau madhuparka ity ata āha |
dadhi madhusaṃsṛṣṭaṃ madhuparkaḥ payo vā
madhusaṃsṛṣṭam || 2.8.8 ||
gṛhyoktasyānuvādo ‘yam uttaravivakṣayā || 2.8.8 ||
abhāva udakam || 2.8.9 ||
dadhipayasor abhāve udakam api deyam | madhusaṃsṛṣṭam ity eke | nety anye, punar madhusaṃsṛṣṭagrahaṇāt || 2.8.9 ||
“vedādhyayaḥ” [ĀpDh 2.8.5] ity atra vivakṣitaṃ vedam āha |
ṣaḍaṅgo vedaḥ || 2.8.10 ||
ṣaḍbhir aṅgair yukto vedo gṛhyate || 2.8.10 ||
kāni tāny aṅgānīty ata āha |
chandaḥ kalpo vyākaraṇaṃ jyotiṣaṃ niruktaṃ śīkṣā
chandovicitir iti || 2.8.11 ||
chando vedaḥ, tat kalpayatīti pratiśākhaṃ śākhāntarādhītena nyāyaprāptena cāṅgakalāpenopetasya karmaṇaḥ prayogakalpanayopaskuruta iti chandaḥkalpaḥ, kalpasūtrāṇi | vyākaraṇam arthaviśeṣam āśritya padam anvācakṣāṇaṃ padapadārthapratipādanena vedasyopakārakaṃ vidyāsthānam | sūryādīni jyotīṃśy adhikṛtya pravṛttaṃ śāstraṃ jyotiṣam | ādivṛddhyabhāve yatnaḥ kāryaḥ | tad apy adhyayanopayoginam anuṣṭhānopayoginaṃ ca kālaviśeṣaṃ pratipādayad upakārakam | niruktam api vyākaraṇasyaiva kārtsnyam | śīkṣā varṇānāṃ sthānaprayatnādikam adhyayanakāle karmaṇi ca mantrāṇām uccāraṇaprakāraṃ śikṣayatīti | pṛṣodarāditvād dīrghaḥ | gāyatryādīni chandāṃsi yayā vicīyante vivicya jñāyante sā chandovicitiḥ | etāny aṅgāni | aṅgasaṃstavād aṅgatvam |
mukhaṃ vyākaraṇaṃ tasya jyotiṣaṃ netram ucyate |
niruktaṃ śrotram uddiṣṭaṃ chandasāṃ vicitiḥ pade ||
śīkṣā grāṇantu vedasya hastau kalpān pracakṣate || iti |
upakārakatvāc ca | ukta upakāraḥ || 2.8.11 ||
atra codayati |
śabdārthārambhaṇānāṃ tu karmaṇāṃ samāmnāyasamāptau vedaśabdaḥ | tatra saṅkhyā vipratiṣiddhā || 2.8.12 ||
śabdārthatayā yāny ārabhyante na pratyakṣādipramāṇagocaratayā, tāni śabdārthārambhaṇāni karmāṇi vaidikāni agnihotrādīni, teṣāṃ samāmnāya upadeśaḥ, tasya samāptau sa yāvatā granthajātena samāptaḥ anuṣṭhānaparyanto bhavati tatra vedaśabdo vartate, vedayati dharmaṃ vidanty aneneti vā dhamam iti | na ca mantrabrāhmaṇamātreṇānuṣṭhānaparyanta upadeśo bhavati, kiṃ tu kalpasūtrair api sahitaḥ | tataś ca teṣām api vedasvarūpa evānupraveśāt pañcaivāṅgāni | tatra ṣaṭsaṅkhyā vipratiṣiddheti || 2.8.12 ||
pariharati |
aṅgānāṃ tu pradhānair avyapadeśa iti nyāyavitsamayaḥ
|| 2.8.13 ||
aṅgāny eva kalpasūtrāṇi, na vedasvarūpāṇi, pauruṣeyatvasmaraṇāt | katipayāṇy eva hi teṣu brāhmaṇavākyāni, bhūyiṣṭhāni svavākyāni | aṅgānāṃ ca teṣāṃ pradhānavācibhiḥ śabdaiḥ, chandaḥ vedaḥ brāhmaṇam, ityādibhiḥ vyapadeśo na nyāyya iti nyāyavidāṃ siddhāntaḥ | tāv imau pūrvapakṣasiddhāntau kalpasūtrādhikaraṇe spaṣṭataraṃ draṣṭavyau | yat tūktaṃ na mantrabrāhmaṇamātreṇa pūrṇa upadeśa iti, na hy eṣa sthāṇor aparādho yad enam andho na paśyatīti, puruṣāparādhaḥ sa bhavati | idaṃ tu bhavān ācaṣṭāṃ kalpasūtrakārāṇām iyam prayogakalpanā kutastyeti | nyāyopabṛṃhitābhyāṃ mantrabrāhmaṇābhyām iti vaktavyam | nānyā gatiḥ | evaṃ sati bhavān pi yatatāṃ tādṛśaḥ syām iti | tato mantrabrāhmaṇābhyām eva pūrṇam avabhotsyata iti || 2.8.13 ||
atithiṃ nirākṛtya yatra gate bhojane smaret tato
viramyopoṣya || 2.8.14 ||
ity aṣṭamaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
śvobhūte yathāmanasaṃ tarpayitvā saṃsādhayet || 2.9.1 ||
atithim abhyāgataṃ kenacit prakāreṇa nirākṛtya bhojane pravṛtto yatra gate yadavasthāṃ prāpte bhojane smaret dhiṅmayāsau nirākṛta iti, tatraiva bhojanād viramya tasminn ahany upoṣya aparedyus tam anviṣya yathāmanasaṃ yathecchaṃ tarpayitvā saṃsādhayet gacchantam anuvrajet
|| 2.8.14 – 2.9.1 ||
ā kuta ity ata āha |
yānavantam ā yānāt || 2.9.2 ||
sa ced atithir yānavān bhavati tam ā tadārohaṇād anuvrajet || 2.9.2 ||
yāvan nānujānīyād itaraḥ || 2.9.3 ||
itaro yānarahitaḥ | sa yāvan nānujānīyāt gaccheti tāvad anuvrajet || 2.9.3 ||
apratībhāyāṃ sīmno nivarteta || 2.9.4 ||
yadi tasyānyaparatayā anujñāyāṃ pratibhā buddhir na jāyate tadā sīmni prāptāyāṃ tato nivarteta | prater dīrghaś chāndasaḥ | sāṃsādhayet ityādi sarvātithisādhāraṇam, na nirākṛtamātraviṣayam || 2.9.4 ||
sarvān vaiśvadeve bhāginaḥ kurvītā śvacaṇḍālebhyaḥ || 2.9.5 ||
vaiśvadevānte bhojanārtham upasthitān sarvān eva vaiśvadeve bhāginaḥ kurvīta | ā śvacaṇḍālebhyaḥ | abhividhāv ākāraḥ | tebhyo ‘pi kiṃcid deyam | tathā ca manuḥ |
śunāṃ ca patitānāṃ ca śvapacāṃ pāparogiṇām |
vāyasānāṃ krimīṇāṃ ca śanakair nikṣiped bhuvi || iti | [MDh 3.92]
|| 2.9.5 ||
nānarhadbhyo dadyād ity eke || 2.9.6 ||
anarhadbhyaś caṇḍālādibhyaḥ na dadyād ity eke manyante | tatra dāne abhyudayaḥ, adāne na pratyavāyaḥ || 2.9.6 ||
upetaḥ strīṇām anupetasya cocchiṣṭaṃ varjayet || 2.9.7 ||
upetaḥ kṛtopanayano ‘samāvṛttaḥ, sa strīṇām upetasya ca ucchiṣṭaṃ na bhuñjīta | evaṃ sati samāvṛttasyocchiṣṭaṃ bhuñjānasya na doṣaḥ syāt | evaṃ tarhy upetaḥ āntāt kṛtadāro ‘kṛtadāro vā strīṇām anupetasya cocchiṣṭaṃ varjayet | evam apy upetasya yasya kasyacid api yad ucchiṣṭaṃ tadbhojane na doṣaḥ syāt | “pitur jyeṣṭhasya ca bhrātur ucchiṣṭaṃ bhoktavyam” [ĀpDh 1.4.11] ity etan niyamārthaṃ bhaviṣyati, pitur eva bhrātur eveti | yady evam, sūtram evedam anarthakam, tasmād eva niyamād anyatrāprasaṅgāt | idaṃ tarhi prayojanam – yadā pitānupetaḥ, putras tu prāyaścittaṃ kṛtvā kṛtopanayanaḥ, tadā taṃ prati pitur anupetasyocchiṣṭaṃ pratiṣidhyate | evaṃ jyeṣṭho ‘pi draṣṭavyaḥ | etad api nāsti prayojanam, uktam hi – “dharmavipratipattāv abhojyam” [ĀpDh 1.4.12] iti, “teṣām abhyāgamanaṃ bhojanaṃ vivāham iti ca varjayet” [ĀpDh 1.1.33] iti ca | tathā strīṇām ity etat kim artham | mātur ucchiṣṭapratiṣedhārtham | kathaṃ prasaṅgaḥ, “mātari pitary ācāryavac chuśrūṣā” [ĀpDh 1.14.6] iti vacanāt, “yad ucchiṣṭaṃ prāśnāti | havirucchiṣṭam eva tat” [ĀpDh 1.4.1–2] ity ācāryocchiṣṭhasya haviṣṭvena saṃstavāc ca | naitad api sāram, “pitur jyeṣṭhasya ca” [ĀpDh 1.4.11] ity atra pitur grahaṇād eva tasyā aprasakteḥ | tasmāt keṣucij janapadeṣu bhāryayā anupanītena ca saha bhojanam ācaranti, yathāha bodhāyanaḥ: “yāni dakṣiṇatas tāni vyākhyāsyāmaḥ | yathaitad anupanītena saha bhojanaṃ striyā saha bhojanam” [BDh 1.2.2–3] iti | tasya durācāratvam anena pratipādyate || 2.9.7 ||
sarvāṇy udakapūrvāṇi dānāni || 2.9.8 ||
sarvāṇīti vacanād bhikṣāpy udakapūrvam eva deyā || 2.9.8 ||
yathāśruti vihāre || 2.9.9 ||
vihāre yajñakarmaṇi yāni dakṣiṇādīni dānāni tāni yathāśruty eva, nodakapūrvāṇi || 2.9.9 ||
ye nityā bhāktikās teṣām anuparodhena saṃvibhāgo
vihitaḥ || 2.9.10 ||
ye nityā bhāktikā bhaktārhāḥ karmkarādayaḥ teṣām uparodho yathā na bhavati tathā vaiśvadevānte āgatebhyaḥ saṃvibhāgaḥ kartavyaḥ || 2.9.10 ||
kāmam ātmānaṃ bhāryāṃ putraṃ voparundhyān na tv eva
dāsakarmakaram || 2.9.11 ||[1]
dāso bhūtvā yaḥ karma karoti sa dāsakarmakaraḥ | tam ātmādyuparodhe ‘pi noparundhyāt, kiṃ punar āgatārthaṃ taṃ noparundhyād iti || 2.9.11 ||
tathā cātmano ‘nuparodhaṃ kuryād yathā karmasv
asamarthaḥ syāt || 2.9.12 ||
karmasu agnihotrādiṣu ārjaneṣu ca yathā svayaṃ samartho na bhavati tathātmānaṃ noparundhyāt kuṭumbī || 2.9.12 ||
athāpy udāharanti |
aṣṭau grāsā muner bhakṣaḥ ṣoḍaśāraṇyavāsinaḥ |
dvātriṃśataṃ gṛhasthasyāparimitaṃ brahmacāriṇaḥ ||
āhitāgnir anaḍvāṃś ca brahmacārī ca te trayaḥ |
aśnanta eva siddhyanti naiṣāṃ siddhir anaśnatām || iti || 2.9.13 ||
athaitasminn ātmānaṃ noparundhyād iti viṣaye ślokāv udāharanti | muneḥ saṃnyāsinaḥ aṣṭau grāsā bhakṣaḥ, āsyāvikāreṇa | araṇyavāsī vānaprasthas tasya ṣoḍaśa | dvātriṃśatam, prathamārthe dvitīyā | dvātriṃśad grāsā gṛhasthasya bhakṣaḥ | brahmacāriṇas tu vidyārthasya naiṣṭhikasya ca grāsaniyamo nāsti | dvitīyena ślokenāhitāgniviṣaye “kālayor bhojanam” [ĀpDh 2.1.2] ity api niyamo nāstīti pratpādyate | anaḍudgrahaṇaṃ dṛṣṭāntārtham | brahmacārigrahaṇaṃ dṛṣṭārtham | siddhyanti svakāryakṣamā bhavanti || 2.9.13 ||
iti navamaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
ity āpastambadharmasūtravṛttāv ujjvalāyāṃ
dvitīyapraśne caturthaḥ paṭalaḥ
bhikṣaṇe nimittam ācāryo vivāho yajño mātāpitror
bubhūrṣārhataś ca niyamavilopaḥ || 2.10.1 ||
bhikṣaṇaṃ yācanam | tatrācāryādayo nimittam | bubhūrṣā bhartum icchā | arhato vidyādimato ‘gnihotrādau niyame yogyārthasyābhavena lopaḥ || 2.10.1 ||
tatra guṇān samīkṣya yathāśakti deyam || 2.10.2 ||
tatraivaṃbhūte bhikṣaṇe yācataḥ śrutavṛttādikān samīkṣya śaktyanurupam avaśyaṃ deyam, adāne pratyavāyāt | gautamas tu nimittāntaram apy āha: “gurvarthaniveśauṣadhārthavṛttikṣīṇa-yakṣyamāṇādhyayanādhvasaṃyogavaiśvajiteṣu dravyasaṃvibhāgo bahirvedi | bhikṣamāṇeṣu kṛtānnam itareṣu” [GDh 5.21–22] iti | vaiśvajito viśvajidyāgasya kartā | sa ca sarvasvadakṣiṇaḥ
|| 2.10.2 ||
indriyaprītyarthasya tu bhikṣaṇam animittam | na tad
ādriyeta || 2.10.3 ||
indriyadvārā ātmanaḥ prītiḥ indriyaprītiḥ, tām arthamāno yo bhikṣate srakcandanādi tanmūlyaṃ vā, tadbhikṣaṇaṃ niyamena dānasya nimittaṃ na bhavati | tasmān na tad ādriyeta, adāne ‘pi na praytavāyaḥ | vivāho ‘pi dvitīyo na nimittam satyāṃ prathamāyāṃ dharmaprajāsaṃpannāyām | tadartham idaṃ vacanam, anyatra prāptyabhāvāt || 2.10.3 ||
svakarma brāhmaṇasyādhyayanam adhyāpanaṃ yajño yājanaṃ
dānaṃ pratigrahaṇaṃ dāyādyaṃ siloñchaḥ || 2.10.4 ||
“sarvavarṇānāṃ svadharmānuṣṭhāne” [ĀpDh 2.2.2] ity uktam | te ‘mī svadharmā ucyante | putrādibhyo dīyate iti dāyaḥ, tam ādatta iti dāyādaḥ, tasya bhāvo dāyādyaṃ dāyasvīkāraḥ | kṣetrādiṣu patitāni mañjarībhūtāni tataś cyutāni vā dhānyāni silaśabdasyārthaḥ | teṣām uñchanam aṅgulībhir nakhair vā ādānaṃ siloñchaḥ | etāny adhyayanādīny aṣṭau brāhmaṇasya svakarma | teṣu yajñadānādhyayanāni trīṇi dvijātisāmānyena kartavyatayā niyamyante, itarāṇy arthitayā dravyārjane pravṛttasyopāyāntaranivṛttyarthāny upadiśyante, adhyāpanādibhir eva dravyam ārjayet na cauryādibhir iti || 2.10.4 ||
anyac cāparigṛhītam || 2.10.5 ||
yac cānyat kenāpy aparigṛhītam āraṇyaṃ mūlaphalāni, tenāpi jīved iti prakaraṇād gamyate | etena nidhir vyākhyātaḥ || 2.10.5 ||
etāny eva kṣatriyasyādhyāpanayājanapratigrahaṇānīti
parihāpya daṇḍayuddhādhikāni || 2.10.6 ||
etāny eva kṣatriyasyāpi svakarma adhyāpanādīni trīṇi varjayitvā | daṇḍalabdhaṃ yuddhalabdaṃ cādhikam || 2.10.6 ||
kṣatriyavad vaiśyasya daṇḍayuddhavarjaṃ
kṛṣigorakṣyavaṇijyādhikam || 2.10.7 ||
gorakṣyaṃ gavāṃ rakṣaṇam | bhāve ṇyatpratyayaḥ | vaṇijāṃ karma vaṇijyam, krayavikrayavyavahāraḥ kusīdaṃ ca | “dūtavaṇigbhyāṃ ca” iti yapratyayaḥ || 2.10.7 ||
nānanūcānam ṛtvijaṃ vṛṇīte na paṇamānam || 2.10.8 ||
sāṅgasya vedasyādhyetā pravaktā cānūcānaḥ | atādṛśam ṛtvijaṃ na vṛṇīte, nāpy etāvad deyam iti paribhāṣamāṇam || 2.10.8 ||
ayājyo ‘nadhīyānaḥ || 2.10.9 ||
anadhītavedaṃ na yājayet tadānīm apekṣitaṃ mantraṃ yathāśakti vācayan || 2.10.9 ||
kṣatriyasya yuddhaṃ svakarmety uktam | tat kathaṃ kartavyam ity ata āha |
yuddhe tadyogā yathopāyam upadiśanti tathā
pratipattavyam || 2.10.10 ||
yuddhaviṣaye tathā pratipattavyam yathā tadyogā upāyam upadiśanti | tasmin yuddhakarmaṇi yuddhaśāstre vā yeṣām abhiyogas te tadyogāḥ || 2.10.10 ||
nyastāyudhaprakīrṇakeśaprāñjaliparāṅāvṛttānām āryā
vadhaṃ paricakṣate || 2.10.11 ||
nyastāyudhaḥ tyaktāyudhaḥ | prakīrṇakeśaḥ keśān viniyantum akṣamaḥ | prāñjaliḥ kṛtāñjaliḥ | parāṅāvṛttaḥ parāṅmukhaḥ | sarva ete bhītāḥ | teṣāṃ yuddhe vadhaṃ santo garhante | parigaṇanād anyeṣāṃ vadhe na doṣaḥ | tathā ca gautamaḥ: “na doṣo hiṃsāyām āhave” iti [GDh 10.17] | “nyastāyudhaḥ prakīrṇakeśaḥ” iti visarjanīyaṃ kecid vadanti, so ‘papāṭhaḥ | parāṅāvṛtta iti ṅakāraś chāndasaḥ || 2.10.11 ||
śāstrair adhigatānām indriyadaurbalyād vipratipannānāṃ śāstā nirveṣam upadiśed yathākarma yathoktam || 2.10.12 ||
yathāśāstraṃ garbhādhānādibhiḥ saṃskāraiḥ saṃskṛtāḥ śāstrair adhigatāḥ | teṣām indriyadaurbalyād ajitendriyatayā vipratipannānāṃ svakarmataḥ cyutānāṃ niṣiddheṣu ca pravṛttānāṃ śāstā śāsitā ācāryādiḥ nirveṣaṃ prāyaścittam upadiśet yathākarma karmānurūpam, yathoktaṃ dharmaśāstreṣu || 2.10.12 ||
tasya cec chāstram atipravarteran rājānaṃ gamayet || 2.10.13 ||
ajitendriyatayā vipratipannā narāḥ tasya cec chāsituḥ śāstraṃ śāsanam atipravarteran na tatra tiṣṭheyuḥ sa rājānaṃ gamayet evam asau karotīti || 2.10.13 ||
rājā purohitaṃ dharmārthakuśalam || 2.10.14 ||
sa rājā dharmaśāstreṣv arthaśāstreṣu ca kuśalaṃ purohitaṃ gamayet, vinīyatām asāv iti
|| 2.10.14 ||
sa brāhmaṇān niyuñjyāt || 2.10.15 ||
sa purohito brāhmaṇāś ced atikramakāriṇaḥ prāpitās tān niyuñjyāt niyuñjīta anurūpeṣu prāyaścitteṣu || 2.10.15 ||
atha yadi te tatrāpi na tiṣṭheyuḥ tadā kiṃ kartayam ity āha |
balaviśeṣeṇa vadhadāsyavarjaṃ niyamair upaśoṣayet || 2.10.15 ||
tatas tān niyamair bandhanopavāsādibhir upaśoṣayet | balaviśeṣeṇa balānurūpam | vadhadāsyavarjam, vadhas tāḍanādi, vadhaṃ dāsyaṃ ca varjayitvā sarvam anyad bandhanādikaṃ balānurūpaṃ kārayet, yāvat te manyeraṃś carema prāyaścittam iti || 2.10.15 ||
iti daśamaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
evaṃ brāhmaṇaviṣaya uktam, itareṣv āha |
itareṣāṃ varṇānām ā prāṇaviprayogāt samavekṣya teṣāṃ
karmāṇi rājā daṇḍaṃ praṇayet || 2.11.1 ||
itareṣāṃ brāhmaṇavyatiriktānāṃ varṇānāṃ rājā purohitenoktaṃ daṇḍaṃ svayam eva praṇayet teṣāṃ karmāṇi samavekṣya tadanurūpam ā prāṇaviprayogāt | abhividhāv ākāraḥ || 2.11.1 ||
na ca saṃdehe daṇḍaṃ kuryāt || 2.11.2 ||
aparādhasaṃdehe rājā daṇḍaṃ na kuryat || 2.11.2 ||
kiṃ tu |
suvicitaṃ vicityā devapraśnebhyo rājā daṇḍāya
pratipadyeta || 2.11.3 ||
ā devapraśnebhyaḥ sākṣipraśnādibhiḥ śapatāntaiḥ suvicitaṃ yathā bhavati tathā vicitya nirūpya rājā daṇḍāya pratipadyeta upakrameta || 2.11.3 ||
evaṃ kurvataḥ phalam āha |
evaṃvṛtto rājobhau lokāv abhijayati || 2.11.4 ||
evaṃbhūtaṃ vṛttaṃ yasya sa evaṃvṛttaḥ | atra manuḥ |
adaṇḍyān daṇḍayan rājā daṇḍyāṃś caivāpy adaṇḍayan |
ayaśo mahad āpnoti pretya svargāc ca hīyate || iti | [MDh 8.128]
|| 2.11.4 ||
gacchatāṃ pratigacchatāṃ ca pathi samavāye kena kasmai panthā deya ity ata āha |
rājñaḥ panthā brāhmaṇenāsametya || 2.11.5 ||
rājātrābhiṣiktaḥ | sa yadi brāhmaṇena sameto na bhavati tadā tasya panthā dātavyaḥ kṣatriyair apy anabhiṣiktaiḥ kiṃ punar itaraiḥ | etadartham evedaṃ vacanam | anyatra “varṇajyāyasāṃ ca” [ĀpDh 2.11.8] iti vakṣyamāṇenaiva siddham || 2.11.5 ||
sametya tu brāhmaṇasyaiva panthāḥ || 2.11.6 ||
āpadi śiṣyabhūtabrāhmaṇviṣaym idam | śiṣyabhūtenāpi brāhmaṇena sametya tasyaiva guruṇāpi rājñā panthā deya iti || 2.11.6 ||
yānasya bhārābhinihatasyāturasya striyā iti sarvair
dātavyaḥ || 2.11.7 ||
yānaṃ śakaṭādi | bhārābhinihato bhārākrāntaḥ | āturo vyādhitaḥ | strī yā kācid api | etebhyaḥ sarvair eva varṇaiḥ panthā dātavyaḥ | itiśabdāt sthavirabālakṛśādibhyaḥ || 2.11.7 ||
varṇajyāyasāṃ cetarair varṇaiḥ || 2.11.8 ||
varṇenotkṛṣṭā varṇajyāyāṃsaḥ, teṣāṃ cetarair apakṛṣṭair varṇaiḥ bāhyaiś ca panthā dātavyaḥ
|| 2.11.8 ||
aśiṣṭapatitamattonmattānām ātmasvastyayanārthena
sarvair eva dātavyaḥ || 2.11.9 ||
aśiṣṭo mūrkhaḥ | anye prasiddhāḥ | eteṣāṃ sarvair eva sajātīyair utkṛṣṭair apakṛṣṭair varṇair bāhyaiś ca dātavyaḥ | ātmasvastyayanam ātmatrāṇam, tena prayojanena tadartham, na tv adṛṣṭārtham ity arthaḥ | atra kauṭilyena deyasya pathaḥ pramāṇam uktam: “pañcāratnī rathapathaś catvāro hastipathaḥ dvau kṣudrapaśumanuṣyāṇām” iti [KĀŚ 2.4.5] || 2.11.9 ||
dharmacaryayā jaghanyo varṇaḥ pūrvaṃ pūrvaṃ varṇam
āpadyate jātiparivṛttau || 2.11.10 ||
dharmacaryayā svadharmānuṣṭhānena jaghanyo varṇaḥ śūdrādiḥ pūrvaṃ pūrvaṃ varṇaṃ vaiśyādim āpadyate prāpnoti | jātiparivṛttau janmaparivartane | śūdro vaiśyo jāyate | tatrāpi svadharmaniṣṭhaḥ kṣatriyo jāyate | tatrāpi svadharmaparo brāhmaṇa iti | evaṃ kṣatriyavaiśyayor api draṣṭavyam || 2.11.10 ||
adharmacaryayā pūrvo vārṇo jaghanyaṃ jaghanyaṃ varṇam
āpadyate jātiparivṛttau || 2.11.11 ||
pūrveṇa gatam | mahāpātakavyatirikādharmānuṣṭhānaviṣayam etat, mahāpātakeṣu “steno ‘bhiśastaḥ” [ĀpDh 2.2.6] ityādinā nīcajātiprāpter uktatvāt || 2.11.11 ||
dharmaprajāsaṃpanne dāre nānyāṃ kurvīta || 2.11.12 ||
śrauteṣu gārhyeṣu smārteṣu ca śraddhā bhaktiś ca dharmasaṃpattiḥ | prajāsaṃpattiḥ putravattvam | evaṃbhūte dāre sati nānyām | “dāre” iti prakṛte anyām iti strīliṅganirdeśāt tatrārthād bhāryām iti gamyate | nānyāṃ bhāryāṃ kurvīta nodvahet || 2.11.12 ||
anyatarābhāve kāryā prāg agnyādheyāt || 2.11.13 ||
dharmaprajayor anyatarasyābhāve kāryā udvāhyā | tatrāpi prāg agnyādheyāt nordhvam ādhānāt | etadartham evedaṃ vacanam | ubhayasaṃpattau na kāryety ukte arthād anyatarābhāve kāryety asyāṃśasya prāptatvāt | yadā cānyatarābhāve ‘pi kāryā tadā kā śaṅkā ubhayābhāve ‘pi kāryeti
|| 2.11.13 ||
“prāg agnyādheyāt” ity atra hetuḥ |
ādhāne hi satī karmabhiḥ saṃbadhyate yeṣām
etad aṅgam || 2.11.14 ||
hi yasmād ādhāne satī vidyamānā sahānvitā karmabhiḥ saṃbadhyate adhikriyate | kaiḥ | yeṣām agnihotrādīnām etad ādhānam aṅgam upakārakaṃ taiḥ | atra satīti vacanān mṛtāyāṃ tu prāg ūrdhvaṃ cādhānāt satyām api putrasaṃpattau dharmasaṃpattyarthaṃ dāragrahaṇaṃ bhavaty eva | tathā ca manuḥ |
bhāryāyai pūrvamāriṇyai datvāgnīn antyakarmaṇi |
punar dārakriyāṃ kuryat punar ādhānam eva ca || iti | [MDh 5.168]
yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
dāhayitvāgnihotreṇa striyaṃ vṛttavatīṃ patiḥ |
āhared vidhivad dārān agnīṃś caivāvilambayan || iti | [YDh 1.89]
na vācanike ‘rthe yuktayaḥ kramante | tenaitan na codanīyam – yajamānaḥ pūrvam evānvārambhaṇīyayā saṃskṛtaḥ, na tasyāyaṃ saṃskāraḥ punar āpādayituṃ śakyaḥ, bhāryā ca pūrvam asaṃskṛtā, na tasyā darśapūrṇamāsādiṣv adhikāraḥ sa kathaṃ tayā yaṣṭum arhatīti | anvārambhaṇīyājanyaś ca saṃskāro yadi saṃyogavad ubhayaniṣṭhaḥ tadā bhāryānāśe naśyatīti puruṣasya punaḥsaṃskāro ‘pi na nopapannaḥ | yāni cānvārambhaṇīyāṃ nāpekṣante smārtāni gārhyāṇi ca tair adhikāras tasyā apy aviruddhaḥ |
nanu ca prāg agnyādhānāt karmabhiḥ saṃbadhyate gārhyaiḥ smārtaiś ca, tat kim ucyate “ādhāne hi satī karmabhiḥ saṃbadhyate” iti |
satyam | asmād eva hetunirdeśād avasīyate prāg ādhānāt satyām api dharmasaṃpattau prajāsaṃpattau ca rāgāndhasya kadācid dāragrahaṇe nātīva doṣa iti | atha yasyāhitāgner bhāryā saty eva karmasu śraddadhānā śaktā vā na bhavati, putrāś ca mṛtā anutpannā vā tasya katham | yad yoṣā yuktiḥ “dharmaprajāsaṃpannaḥ” iti “karmabhiḥ saṃbadhyate” iti ca tadā kartavyo vivāhaḥ | na ca “prāg agnyādheyāt” ity asya virodhaḥ, “anyatarābhāve kāryā” ity asyaiva sa śeṣaḥ na punar ubhayābhāve kāryety asya | bhāradvājasūtre tu yady apy aviśeṣeṇa āhitāgner dārakriyābhyanujñātā pratīyate – “atha yady āhitāgniḥ punar dāraṃ kurvīta yad agnīn notsṛjel laukikāḥ saṃpadyeran tasya punar agnyādheyaṃ kurvītety āśmarathyaḥ | punarādhānam ity ālekhanaḥ | punar agnyādheyam eva kurvītety auḍulomiḥ” iti, tasyāpy etad eva viṣayaḥ
|| 2.11.14 ||
sagotrāya duhitaraṃ na prayacchet || 2.11.15 ||
kanyāgotram eva gotraṃ yasya tasmai kanyā na deyā, hārītāya hārītā, vātsyāya vātsyāyanītyādi
|| 2.11.15 ||
mātuś ca yonisaṃbandhebhyaḥ || 2.11.16 ||
mātur yonisaṃbandhāḥ kanyāyā mātulādayaḥ, cakārāt pitur api | evaṃ tebhyo ‘sagotrebhyo ‘pi kanyā na deyā | atra manuḥ |
asapiṇḍā ca yā mātur asagotrā ca yā pituḥ |
sā praśastā dvijātīnāṃ dārakarmaṇi maithune || iti | [MDh 3.5}
vyāsas tu |
snātvā samudvahet kanyāṃ savarṇāṃ lakṣaṇānvitām |
yavīyasīṃ bhrātṛmatīm asagotrāṃ prayatnataḥ ||
mātuḥ sagotrām apy eke necchanty udvāhakarmaṇi |
janmanāmnor avijñāne udvahed aviśaṅkitaḥ ||
mātuḥ sapiṇḍā yatnena varjanīyā dvijātibhiḥ || iti |
gautamaḥ: “asamānapravarair vivāhaḥ | ūrdhvaṃ saptamāt pitṛbandhubhyo bījinaś ca | mātṛbandhubhyaḥ pañcamāt” iti [GDh 4.2–5] | śaṅkhaḥ: “dārān āharet sadṛśān asamānārṣeyān asaṃbandhān ā saptamapañcamān pitṛmātṛbandhubhyaḥ” iti | vasiṣṭhaḥ: “gṛhastho vītakrodhaharṣo guruṇānujñātaḥ snātvāsamānārṣeyām aspṛṣṭamaithunām avaravayasīṃ sadṛśīṃ bhāryāṃ vindeta pañcamīṃ mātṛbandhubhyaḥ saptamīṃ pitṛbandhubhyaḥ” iti [VaDh 18.1–2] | hārītaḥ: “śvitrī kuṣṭhy udarī yakṣmāmayāvy apy alpāyur anārṣeyam abrahmasamānārṣeyam ity etāny apatitāny api kulāni varjanīyāni bhavanti | kulānurūpāḥ prajā bhavantīty āditaḥ ṣaḍ ayajñīyatvād anārṣeyam avedatvād abrahma ekakulatvāt samānārṣeyam iti tasmāt sapta pitṛtaḥ parīkṣya pañca mātṛto ‘nagnikāṃ śreṣṭhāṃ bhrātṛmatīṃ bhāryāṃ vindeta” iti | paiṭhīnasiḥ: “asamānārṣeyāṃ kanyāṃ varayet pañca mātṛtaḥ pariharet sapta pitṛtas trīn mātṛtaḥ pañca pitṛto vā” iti | yājñavalkyaḥ |
ananyapūrvikāṃ kāntām asapiṇḍāṃ yavīyasīm |
aviplutabrahmacaryo lakṣaṇyāṃ striyam udvahet ||
arogiṇīṃ bhrātṛmatīm asamānārṣagotrajām |
pañcamāt saptamāt ūrdhvaṃ mātṛtaḥ pitṛtas tathā || iti | [YDh 1.52–53]
viṣṇuḥ: “asagotrām asamānapravarāṃ bhāryāṃ vindeta mātṛtaḥ pañcamāt pitṛtaḥ saptamāt” iti | nāradaḥ |
ā saptamāt pañcamāc ca bandhubhyaḥ pitṛmātṛtaḥ |
avivāhyāḥ sagotrāḥ syuḥ samānapravarās tathā || iti |
śātātapaḥ |
pariṇīya sagotrāṃ tu samānapravarāṃ tathā |
kṛtvā tasyāḥ samutsargam atikṛcchraṃ viśodhanam ||
mātulasya sutām ūḍhvā mātṛgotrāṃ tathaiva ca |
samānapravarāṃ caiva tyaktvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || iti |
manuḥ |
paitṛṣvaseyīṃ bhaginīṃ svasrīyāṃ mātur eva ca |
mātuś ca bhrātur āptāṃ ca gatvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret ||
etāś catasro bhāryārthe nopayaccheta buddhimān |
jñatayo nānupeyās tāḥ pataty abhyupayann adhaḥ || iti | [MDh 11.172–73]
bodhāyanaḥ: “samānagotrāṃ ced upayacchen mātṛvad enāṃ bubhṛyāt sagotrāṃ gatvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ upadiśet | vrate pariniṣṭhite brāhmaṇīṃ na tyajet | tāṃ mātṛvad bhaginīvat | garbho na duṣyati, kāśyapa iti vijñāyate | atha saṃnipāte vivāhas tadadhyāyaṃ varjayet bodhāyanasya hi tatpramāṇaṃ kartavyam | mānavyo hi prajā iti vijñāyate |
gotrāṇāṃ tu sahasrāṇi prayutāny arbudāni ca |
ūnapañcāśad evaiṣāṃ pravarā ṛṣidarśanāt ||
eka eva ṛṣir yāvat pravareṣv anu vartate |
tāvat samānagotratvam anyad bhṛgvaṅgirogaṇāt” || iti |
sumantuḥ: “pitṛpatnyaḥ sarvā mātaraḥ tadbhrātaro mātulāḥ tatsutā mātulasutās tadbhaginyo mātṛbhaginyas tasmāt tā nopayantavyāḥ” iti || 2.11.16 ||
kanyādānaprasaṅgād vivāhabhedān āha ¦
brāhme vivāhe bandhuśīlaśrutārogyāṇi budhvā prajāṃ sahatvakarmabhyaḥ pratipādayec chaktiviṣayeṇālaṃkṛtya
|| 2.11.17 ||
brahmaṇā dṛṣṭo brāhmaḥ | tasmin vivāhe varasya bandhvādīn buddhvā parīkṣya prajāṃ duhitaraṃ sahatvakarmabhyaḥ sahakartavyāni yāni karmāṇi tebhyas tāni kartuṃ pratipādayet dadyāt | śaktiviṣayeṇa, vibhaktipratirūpo ‘yaṃ nipātaḥ yathāśaktīty arthe draṣṭavyaḥ | yathāśakty alaṃkṛtya dadyād iti | eṣa brāhmo vivāhaḥ | “prajāsahatvakarmabhyaḥ” iti pāṭhe prajārthaṃ sahatvakarmārthaṃ ceti || 2.11.17 ||
ārṣe duhitṛmate mithunau gāvau deyau || 2.11.18 ||
ṛṣibhir dṛṣṭe vivāhe mithunau gāvau strigavī puṃgavaś ca duhitṛmate deyau | eṣa ārṣaḥ
|| 2.11.18 ||
daive yajñatantra ṛtvije pratipādayet || 2.11.19 ||
devair dṛṣṭe vivāhe yajñatantre vitate ṛtvije karma kurvate kanyāṃ dadyād eṣa daivo vivāhaḥ
|| 2.11.19 ||
mithaḥ kāmāt sāṃvartete sa gāndharvaḥ || 2.11.20 ||
yatra kanyāvarau mitho rahasi kāmāt parasparaṃ saṃvartete mithunī bhavataḥ sa gāndharvo vivāhaḥ | samo dīrghaḥ pūrvavat | atra saṃyogād uttarakālaṃ vivāhasaṃskāraḥ kartavyaḥ
|| 2.11.20 ||
ity ekādaśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
śaktiviṣayeṇa dravyāṇi datvāvaheran sa āsuraḥ || 2.12.1 ||
yatra vivāhe kanyāvate yathāśakti dravyāṇi datvā āvaheran kanyāṃ sa āsuraḥ | “vittenānatiḥ strīmatām āsuraḥ” [GDh 4.11] iti gautamaḥ | tena kanyāyai gṛhakṣetrābharaṇādidānena vivāho nāsuraḥ || 2.12.1 ||
duhitṛmataḥ prothayitvāvaheran sa rākṣasaḥ || 2.12.2 ||
duhitṛmataḥ kanyāvataḥ pitrādīn prothayitvā pramathya yatrāvaheran sa rākṣaso vivāhaḥ | “hatvā bhittvā ca śīrṣāṇi rudantīṃ rudadbhyo haret sa rākṣasaḥ” [ĀśGṛ 1.6.1] ity āśvalāyanaḥ | atrāpi vivāhasaṃskāraḥ kartavyaḥ – dvau cāparau vivāhau śāstrāntareṣūktau | tatra āśvalāyanaḥ: “saha dharmaṃ carata iti prājāpatyaḥ | suptāṃ pramattāṃ vā haret sa paiśācaḥ” iti [ĀśGṛ 1.6.1] | tāv iha pṛthaṅ noktau, brāhmarākṣasayor antarbhāvād iti || 2.12.2 ||
teṣāṃ traya ādyāḥ praśastāḥ pūrvaḥ pūrvaḥ śreyān || 2.12.3 ||
teṣāṃ vivāhānāṃ madhye ādyās trayo brāhmārṣadaivāḥ praśastāḥ, tatrāpi pūrvaḥ pūrvo ‘tiśayena praśasta iti || 2.12.3 ||
yathāyukto vivāhas tathāyuktā prajā bhavati || 2.12.4 ||
praśaste vivāhe jātā prajā praśastā bhavati | nindite ninditā | tatra manuḥ |
brāhmādiṣu vivāheṣu caturṣv evānupūrvaśaḥ |
brahmavarcasinaḥ putrā jāyante śiṣṭasaṃmatāḥ ||
rūpasattvaguṇopetā dhanavanto yaśasvinaḥ |
paryāptabhogā dharmiṣṭhā jīvanti ca śataṃ samāḥ ||
itareṣu tu jāyante nṛśaṃsānṛtavādinaḥ |
tanayā durvivāheṣu brahmadharmajugupsitāḥ || iti | [MDh 3.39–41]
prājāpatyena saha brāhmādyāś catvāro vivāhā brāhmaṇasya, gāndharvarākṣasau kṣatriyasya, āsuras tu vaiśyaśūdrayoḥ, paiśāco na kasyacid iti || 2.12.4 ||
pāṇisamūḍhaṃ brāhmaṇasya nāprokṣitam abhitiṣṭhet || 2.12.5 ||
brāhmaṇasya pāṇinā samūḍham upaliptaṃ saṃmṛṣṭaṃ vā bhūmipradeśam aprokṣitaṃ nābhitiṣṭhet, kiṃ tu prokṣyaivādhitiṣṭhet || 2.12.5 ||
agniṃ brāhmaṇaṃ cāntareṇa nātikrāmet || 2.12.6 ||
agner brāhmaṇasya ca madhye na gacchet || 2.12.6 ||
brāhmaṇāṃś ca || 2.12.7 ||
antareṇa nātikrāmed ity eva, brāhmaṇānāṃ ca madhye na gacchet || 2.12.7 ||
anujñāpya vātikrāmet || 2.12.8 ||
spaṣṭam || 2.12.8 ||
agnim apaś ca na yugapad dhārayīta || 2.12.9 ||
agnim udakaṃ ca na yugapad dhārayet || 2.12.9 ||
nānāgnīnāṃ ca saṃnivāpaṃ varjayet || 2.12.10 ||
pṛthagavasthitānām agnīnām ekatra samāvapanaṃ na kuryat | agnāv agniṃ na prakṣiped ity anye
|| 2.12.10 ||
pratimukham agnim āhriyamāṇaṃ nāpratiṣṭhitam bhūmau
pradakṣiṇīkuryāt || 2.12.11 ||
yadāsya gacchataḥ pratimukham agnir āhriyate tadā na taṃ pradakṣiṇīkuryat, sa ced bhūmau pratiṣṭhito na bhavati | pratiṣṭhite tv agnau dṛṣṭe pradakṣiṇīkuryad iti || 2.12.11 ||
pṛṣṭhataś cātmanaḥ pāṇī na saṃśleṣayet || 2.12.12 ||
sve pṛṣṭhabhāge svapāṇidvayaṃ na saṃśleṣayet na badhnīyāt || 2.12.12 ||
svapann abhinimrukto ‘nāśvān vāgyato rātrim āsīta
śvobhūta udakam upaspṛśya vācaṃ visṛjet || 2.12.13 ||
supte yasminn astam eti supte yasmann udeti ca |
aṃśumān abhinimrukābhyuditau tau yathākramam ||
tayoḥ abhinimrukto ‘nāśvān abhuñjānas tūṣṇīṃ sarvāṃ rātrim āsīta na śayīta | athāparedyuḥ prātaḥ snātvā vācaṃ visṛjet | ayam asya nirveṣaḥ || 2.12.13 ||
svapann abhyudito ‘nāśvān vāgyato ‘has tiṣṭhet || 2.12.14 ||
pūrveṇa gatam | udakam upaspṛśya vācaṃ visṛjed ity atrāpekṣate | tatrāstam ite snānapratiṣedhāt sāyam eva snātvā vācaṃ visṛjya saṃdhyām upāsīta || 2.12.14 ||
ā tamitoḥ prāṇam āyacched ity eke || 2.12.15 ||
yāvad aṅgānāṃ glanir bhavati tāvat prāṇam āyacchet, prāṇavāyum ākṛṣya dhārayet | prāṇāyāmaṃ kuryād ity eke manyante | śaktyapekṣo vikalpaḥ | atra manuḥ |
savyāhṛtīṃ sapraṇavāṃ gāyatrīṃ śirasā saha |
triḥ paṭhed āyataprāṇaḥ prāṇāyāmaḥ sa ucyate || iti ||
etam āvartayet yāvad glānir bhavati || 2.12.15 ||
svapnaṃ vā pāpakaṃ dṛṣṭvā || 2.12.16 ||
pāpakaḥ svapno duḥsvapnaḥ markaṭāskandanādiḥ taṃ ca dṛṣṭvā || 2.12.16 ||
arthaṃ vā siṣādhayiṣan || 2.12.17 ||
arthaḥ prayojanaṃ taṃ ca dṛṣṭam adṛṣṭaṃ vā sādhayitum icchan || 2.12.17 ||
niyamātikrame cānyasmin || 2.12.18 ||
niyamo nāma “udaṅmukho mūtraṃ kuryāt” [ĀpDh 1.31.1] ityādīnām atikrame ca | “ā tamitoḥ prāṇam āyacchet” [ĀpDh 2.12.15] iti sarvatra śeṣaḥ || 2.12.18 ||
doṣaphalasaṃśaye na tat kartavyam || 2.12.19 ||
yasmin karmaṇi kṛte pakṣe doṣaḥ phalaṃ saṃbhāvyate na tat kuryāt, yathā sabhaye deśe ekākino gamanam iti || 2.12.19 ||
evam adhyāyānadhyāye || 2.12.20 ||
saṃśaya ity upasamastam apy apekṣate | adhyāyo ‘nadhyāya iti saṃśaye ‘py evaṃ na tat kartavyam iti | “saṃdhyāv anustanite” [ĀpDh 1.9.20] ity udāharaṇam | pūrvasyaivāyaṃ prapañcah || 2.12.20 ||
na saṃśaye pratyakṣavad brūyat || 2.12.21 ||
saṃśayitam artham ātmano ‘jñānaparihārāya pratyakṣavan niścitavan na brūyāt || 2.12.21 ||
abhinimruktābhyuditakunakhiśyāvadāgradidhiṣudidhiṣūpati-paryāhitaparīṣṭaparivittaparivinnaparivividāneṣu cottarottarasminn aśucikaranirveṣo garīyān garīyān || 2.12.22 ||
ādyau dvau gatau | kunakhī kṛṣṇanakhaḥ | śvyāvā dantā yasya śyāvadan kṛṣṇadantaḥ | “vibhāṣā śyāvārokābhyām” [Pāṇ 5.4.144] iti datrādeśaḥ, tasyāntalopaś chāndasaḥ | jyeṣṭāyām anūḍhāyāṃ pūrvaṃ kanīyasyā voḍhā agradidhiṣuḥ, paścād itarasyā voḍhā didhiṣūpatiḥ | jyeṣṭhe akṛtādhāne kṛtādhānaḥ kaniṣṭhaḥ paryādhātā, jyeṣṭhaḥ paryāhitaḥ | jyeṣṭhe akṛtasomayāge kṛtasomayāgaḥ kaniṣṭhaḥ parivitta iti prasiddhaḥ, jyeṣṭhaḥ parivittiḥ | jyeṣṭhasya bhāryām upayacchamānaḥ parivinnaḥ, yasminn agṛhītabhāge vā kaniṣṭho bhāgaṃ gṛhṇāti sa jyeṣṭhaḥ parivinnaḥ, kaniṣṭhaḥ parivividānaḥ | cakāraḥ paryādhātṛprabhṛtīnāṃ samuccayārthaḥ | eteṣv abhinimrukādiṣu yo ya uttaras tasmiṃs tasmin dvādaśamāsādir aśucikaranirveṣaḥ yaḥ pūrvam uktas tatra garīyān bhavati | tatra pūrvatra pūrvatra laghīyān | abhinimruktābhyuditayos tu anantaroktaṃ prāyaścittadvayam api vikalpena bhavati || 2.12.22 ||
tac ca liṅgaṃ caritvoddhāryam ity eke || 2.12.23 ||
yasmin kaunakhyādike liṅge yat prāyaścittam uktaṃ tac caritvā kaunakhyādi liṅgam uddhared ikty eke manyante | “anyatra dārāgnibhyaḥ” iti smṛtyantaram || 2|12|23 ||
iti dvādaśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
iti dvitīye pañcamaḥ paṭalaḥ
savarṇāpūrvaśāstravihitāyāṃ yathartu gacchadhaḥ putrās
teṣāṃ karmabhiḥ saṃbandhaḥ || 2.13.1 ||
savarṇā ca apūrvā ca śāstravihitā ceti karmadhārayaḥ | sarvarṇā sajātīyā brāhmaṇasya brāḥmaṇītyādi | apūrvā anyasmā adattā, na vidyate pūrvaḥ patir asyā iti | śāstravihitā śāstroktena vivāhasaṃskāreṇa saṃskṛtā, “sagotrāya duhitaraṃ na prayacchet” [ĀpDh 2.11.15] ityādiśāstrānuguṇā vā | evaṃbhūtāyāṃ bhāryāyāṃ yathartu gṛhyoktena ṛtugamanakalpena gacchato ye putrā jāyante teṣām “svakarma brāhmaṇasya” [ĀpDh 2.10.4] ityādinā pūrvam uktaiḥ karmabhiḥ saṃbandho bhavati | “gacchadhaḥ” it dhakāro ‘papāṭhaḥ || 2.13.1 ||
dāyenāvyatikramaś cobhayoḥ || 2.13.2 ||
ubhoyoḥ mātāpitror dāyena ca teṣāṃ saṃbandho bhavati | avyatikramaś ca, cakāraś cedarthe, avyatikramaś cet, yadi te pitaraṃ ca mātaraṃ ca na vyatikrameyuḥ | vyatikrame tu dāyahānir iti | apara āha – ubhayor api dāyena teṣāṃ vyatikramo na kartavyaḥ | avaśyaṃ deyo dāya iti
|| 2.13.2 ||
pūrvavatyām asaṃskṛtāyāṃ varṇāntare ca maithune
doṣaḥ || 2.13.3 ||
pūrvavatī anyena pāṇigrahaṇena tadvatī | asaṃskṛtā vivāhasaṃskārarahitā | varṇāntaraṃ brāhmaṇādeḥ kṣatriyādiḥ | pūrvavatyādiṣu maitune sati doṣo bhavati | kasya | tayor eva mithunībhavatoḥ || 2.13.3 ||
tatrāpi doṣavān putra eva || 2.13.4 ||
tatreti pañcamyās tral, “itarābhyo ‘pi dṛśyante” iti [Pāṇ 5.3.14] | tābhyām apy ubhābhyām atiśayena putra eva doṣavān | tatra pūrvavatyām utpannau kuṇḍagolakau |
patyau jīvati kuṇḍaḥ syān mṛte bhartari golakaḥ | [MDh 3.174]
asaṃskṛtāyām utpannasya nāmāntaraṃ nāsti | kiṃ tu duṣṭatvam eva | varṇāntare tu jātyantaram | tatra gautamaḥ: “anulomā anantaraikāntaradvyantarāsu jātāḥ savarṇāmbaṣṭhograniṣādadauṣyantapāraśavāḥ | pratilomās tu sūtamāgadhāyogavakṣattṛvaidehakacaṇḍālāḥ” iti [GDh 4.16–17] | evakāro duhitṛnivṛttyarthaḥ | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “patitenotpannaḥ patito bhavaty anyatra striyāḥ sā hi paragāminī tām arikthām upeyāt” iti [VaDh 13.51–53] | kiṃ ca “strīratnaṃ duṣkulād api” iti manuḥ [MDh 2.238]
|| 2.13.4 ||
idānīṃ putrebhyo dāyavibhāgaṃ vakṣyan anyasya bhāryāyām anyenotpāditaḥ putraḥ kim utpādayituḥ, āho svit kṣetriṇaḥ | iti vicāre nirṇayam āha |
utpādayituḥ putra iti hi brāhmaṇam || 2.13.5 ||
na kevalaṃ brāhmaṇam eva, vaidikīr gādhā (gathā) atrodāharantīty āha |
athāpy udāharanti |
idānīm evāhaṃ janakaḥ strīṇām īrṣyāmi no purā |
yadā yamasya sādane janayituḥ putram abruvan ||
retodhāḥ putraṃ nayati paretya yamasādane |
tasmād bharyāṃ rakṣanti bibhyantaḥ pararetasaḥ ||
apramattā rakṣatha tantum etaṃ
mā vaḥ kṣetre parabījāni vāpsuḥ |
janayituḥ putro bhavati sāṃparāye
moghaṃ vettā kurute tantum etam || iti || 2.13.6 ||
janayituḥ putraḥ, kṣetriṇaḥ putraḥ, iti vivāde parājitasya kṣetriṇo vacanam | etāvantaṃ kalam ahaṃ janako manyamānaḥ idānīm eva strīṇām īrṣyāmi parapuruṣasaṃsargaṃ na sahe | kadedānīm | yadā yamasya sādane pitṛloke janayituḥ putro bhavati, putrakṛtyaṃ paralokagatasya janayitur eva, na kṣetriṇo ity abruvan dharmajñāḥ | eṣa evārthaḥ kiṃcid viśeṣeṇocyate – retodhāḥ bījapradaḥ putraṃ nayati putradattaṃ piṇḍādikam ātmānaṃ prāpayati paretya mṛtvā yamasya sādane pitṛloke | tasmāt kāraṇād bhāryāṃ rakṣanti pararetaso bibhyantaḥ | chāndaso num, bibhyataḥ | ato yūyam api apramattā avihitā bhūtvā etaṃ tantuṃ prajāsantānaṃ rakṣatha | loḍarthe laṭ, rakṣata | kim artham | vaḥ yuṣmākaṃ kṣetre pareṣāṃ bījāni mā vāpsuḥ | vyatyayenāyaṃ karmaṇi kartṛpratyayaḥ | mā vāpsata, uptāni mā bhūvan | apara āha – paraśabdāj jaso luk | pare puruṣāḥ vaḥ kṣetre bījāni mā vāpsur iti | yasmāt sāṃparāye paraloke janayitur eva putraphalaṃ bhavati | vettā bhāryāyā labdhā kṣetrī vyartham eva tantum etaṃ kurute ātmasātkaroti | itiśabdo gādhāsamāptau (gathā-) | etac ca kṣetriṇo ‘nujñām antareṇa putrotpādanaviṣayam | yadā tu kṣetrī vandhyo rugṇo vā prārthayate mama kṣetre putram utpādayeti, yadā vā santānakṣaye vidhavāṃ niyuñjate, yathā vicitravīryasya kṣetraṃ satyavatī vyāsena, tadotpannaḥ putraḥ ubhayor api putro bhavati bījinaḥ kṣetriṇaś ca | dvyāmuṣyāyaṇaś ca sa bhavati | tathācārya evāha – “yadi dvipitā syād ekaikasmin piṇḍe dvau dvau upalakṣayet” iti [ĀpDh 1.9.7] | yājñavalkyo ‘py āha |
aputreṇa parakṣetre niyogotpāditaḥ sutaḥ |
ubhayor apy asau rikthī piṇḍadātā ca dharmataḥ || iti | [YDh 2.130]
nārado ‘pi |
dvyāmuṣyāyaṇako dadyād dvābhyāṃ piṇḍodake pṛthak |
rikthād ardhaṃ samādadyād bījikṣetrikayos tathā || [NSm 13.22] iti |
|| 2.13.6 ||
yadi pūrvavatyādiṣu maithune doṣaḥ, kathaṃ tarhi katabharadvājau (ucathyabhāradvājau) vyatyasya bhārye jagmatuḥ, vasiṣṭhaś caṇḍālīm akṣamālām, prajāpatiḥ svaduhitaram | tatrāha |
dṛṣṭo dharmavyatikramaḥ sāhasaṃ ca pūrveṣām || 2.13.7 ||
satyaṃ dṛṣṭo ‘yam ācāraḥ, sa tu dharmavyatikramo na dharmaḥ, gṛhyamāṇakāraṇatvāt | na caitāvad eva, pūrveṣāṃ sāhasaṃ ca dṛṣṭam, yathā jāmadgnyena rāmeṇa pitṛvacanād avicāreṇa mātuḥ śiraśchinnam iti || 2.13.7 ||
kim idānīṃ teṣām api doṣaḥ | nety āha |
teṣaṃ tajoviśeṣeṇa pratyavāyo na vidyate || 2.13.8 ||
tādṛśaṃ hi teṣāṃ tejaḥ, yad evaṃvidhaiḥ pāpmabhir na pratyavayanti | tathā ca chāndogye śrūyate – “tad yathaiṣīkātūlam agnau protaṃ pradūyetaivaṃ hāsya sarve pāpmānaḥ pradūyante” iti [ChUp 5.24.3] || 12.13.8 ||
na caitāvatā arvācīnānām api tathā prasaṅga ity āha |
tad anvīkṣya prayuñjānaḥ sīdaty avaraḥ || 2.13.9 ||
tad iti “napuṃsakam anapuṃsakenaikavac ca” [Pāṇ 1.2.69] ity ekaśeṣaḥ, ekavadbhāvaś ca | taṃ ca vyatikramaṃ tac ca sāhasam anvīkṣya dṛṣṭvā svayam api tathā prayuñjāno ‘varaḥ idānīṃtanaḥ sīdati pratyavaiti | na hy agniḥ sarvaṃ dahatīty asmākam api tathā śaktir iti || 2.13.9 ||
putraprasaṅgenāha |
dānaṃ krayadharmaś cāpatyasya na vidyate || 2.13.10 ||
dānagrahaṇena vikrayo gṛhyate tyāgasāmyāt | krayadharma iti ca pratigrahasyāpi grahaṇam, dharmagrahaṇāt, svīkārasāmyāc ca | apatyasya dānapratigrahakrayavikrayā na kartavyāḥ | dvādaśavidheṣu dattakrītayor api putrayoḥ manvādibhiḥ paṭhitatvān nāyaṃ sāmānyena pratiṣedhaḥ | kiṃ tarhi | jyeṣṭhaputraviṣayaḥ, ekaputraviṣayaḥ strīviṣayo vā | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “na jyeṣṭhaṃ putraṃ dadyāt pratigṛhṇīyād vā | na tv ekaṃ putraṃ dadyāt sa hi santānāya pūrveṣāṃ | na strī putraṃ dadyāt pratigṛhṇīyād vānyatrānujñānād bhartuḥ | putraṃ pratigrahīṣyan bandhūn āhūya rājani cāvedya niveśanasya madhye vyāhṛtībhir hutvā adūrabāndhavaṃ saṃnikṛṣṭam eva pratigṛhṇīyāt” iti [VaDh 15.3–6] | viśvajiti ca sarvasvadāne gavādivad apatyaṃ na deyam iti | vikrayas tu sarvatra niṣiddhaḥ | tatropapātakeṣu yājñavalkya āha |
nāstikyaṃ vratalopaś ca sutānāṃ caiva vikrayaḥ | iti | [YDh 3.236]
bahvṛcabrāhmaṇe ‘pi śunaḥśephākhyāne dṛśyate: “sa jyeṣṭhaṃ putraṃ nigṛhṇāna uvāca” ityādi [AitBr 7.3.15] | putraprakaraṇe apatyaśabdopādānam api jyeṣṭhaputraviṣayatvasya liṅgam, na patanty anenety apatyam iti |
ṛṇam asmiṃ saṃnayaty amṛtatvaṃ ca gacchati |
pitā putrasya jātasya paśyec cej jīvato mukham || iti | [AitBr 7.13]
|| 2.13.10 ||
vivāhe duhitṛmate dānaṃ kāmyaṃ dharmārthaṃ śrūyate tasmād duhitṛmate ‘tirathaṃ śataṃ deyaṃ tan mithuyākuryād iti | tasyāṃ krayaśabdaḥ saṃstutimātraṃ dharmād dhi saṃbandhaḥ
|| 2.13.11 ||
ārṣe vivāhe duhitṛmate dānaṃ kvacid vede śrūyate | tasmād duhitṛmate rathenādhikaṃ gavāṃ śataṃ deyam | tac ca duhitṛmān mithuyākuryāt | “mā devānāṃ mithuyākarbhāgadheyam” [TS 1.3.7] iti dṛśyate | mithuyākuryād iti ko ‘rthaḥ | varāyaiva punar dadyāt iti | tad idaṃ dānaṃ kāmyaṃ kāmanimittam | “yathā yukto vivāhas tathā yuktā prajā bhavati” [ĀpDh2.10.4] iti, ṛṣitulyāḥ putrā yathā syur iti | tataś ca dharmārthaṃ prajārtham, na vikrayārtham | yas tu tasyāṃ vivāhakriyāyāṃ krayaśabdaḥ kvacit smṛtau dṛśyate sa saṃstutimātram dravyapradānasāmyāt, na mukhyakrayatvapratipādanārtham | kutaḥ | hi yasmād dharmād eva hetoḥ saṃbandho dampatyor iti | “ārṣe duhitṛmate mithunau gāvau deyau” [ĀpDh 2.11.18] ity atrāpy eṣa eva nyāyaḥ | atra manuḥ |
yāsāṃ nādadate śulkaṃ jñātayo na sa vikrayaḥ |
arhaṇaṃ tat kumārīṇām ānṛśaṃsyaṃ ca kevalam || iti | [MDh 3.54]
etac ca sarvam “dānaṃ krayadharmaś cāpatyasya na vidyate” [ĀpDh 2.13.10] ity asya vyabhicāranivṛttyartham uktam || 2.13.11 ||
atha dāyavibhāgaḥ \
ekadhanena jyeṣṭhaṃ toṣayitvā || 2.13.12 ||
iti trayodaśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
jīvan putrebhyo dāyaṃ vibhajet samaṃ klībam unmattaṃ
patitaṃ ca parihāpya || 2.14.1 ||
ekena pradhānena kenacid dhanena gavādinā jyeṣṭhaṃ putraṃ toṣayitvā tṛptaṃ kṛtvā jīvann eva putrebhyo dāyaṃ vibhajet samaṃ ātmanā parasparaṃ ca teṣām | sāmānyābhidhānāt kramāgataṃ svayam ārjitaṃ ca | klībādīn varjayitvā | klībādigrahaṇaṃ jātyandhādīnām apy upalakṣaṇam | yathāha manuḥ |
anaṃśau klībapatitau jātyandhabadhirau tathā |
unmattajaḍamūkāś ca ye ca kecin nirindriyāḥ || iti | [MDh 9.201]
andhādīnāṃ putrasadbhāve te ‘py aṃśaharāḥ | evam unmattapatitau nivṛttanimittau | klībādayas tu bhartavyāḥ | putravibhāgakālaḥ smṛtyantaravaśād grāhyaḥ | tatra nāradaḥ |
mātur nivṛtte rajasi prattāsu bhaginīṣu ca |
nivṛtte cāpi ramaṇe pitary uparataspṛhe || iti | [NSm 13.3]
yadā ca putrāṇāṃ pṛthakkarmānuṣṭhāne śaktiśraddhe bhavataḥ so ‘pi kālaḥ, “tasmād dharmyā pṛthakkriyā” [MDh 9.111] iti vacanāt | jīvann iti vacanaṃ jīvann evāvaśyaṃ putrān vibhajed eṣa dharma it pratipādanāya | anyathā tad anarthakam, ajīvato ‘prasaṅgāt | smṛtyantareṣu svayam ārjite pitur icchayā viṣamavibhāgo darśitaḥ | na sa dharma ity ācāryasya pakṣaḥ | bhāryāyā apy aṃśo na darśitaḥ | ātmana evāṃśas tasyā apīti manyate | vakṣyati ca “jāyāpatyor na vibhāgo vidyate” iti [ĀpDh 2.14.16] | kecit tu pitur dvāviṃśāv ity āhuḥ,
dvāviṃśau pratipadyeta vibhajann ātmanaḥ pitā | [NSm 13.12]
iti vacanāt | ayam apy ācāryasya pakṣo na bhavati | yathā putrāṇām ekaika evāṃśaḥ sabhāryāṇāṃ tathā pitur apīti | yad vā – putrāṇām evāṃśasāmyam, ātmanas tv ādhikyeti na doṣaḥ | atra hārītaḥ: “pitā hy āgrayaṇaḥ putrā itare grahāḥ yady āgrayaṇaḥ skanded upadasyed vā itarebhyo gṛhṇīyāt” iti | vibhāgād ūrdhvaṃ pitror jīvanābhāve putrabhāgebhyo grāhyam ity uktaṃ bhavati | iti jīvadvibhāgaḥ || 2.13.12 – 2.14.1 ||
atha mṛte kuṭumbini taddhanasya gatim āha |
putrābhāve yaḥ pratyāsannaḥ sapiṇḍaḥ || 2.14.2 ||
putrābhāve iti vacanāt satsu putreṣu ta eva gṛhṇīyuḥ, aviśeṣāt samam | tatra nāradīye viśeṣaḥ:
yac chiṣṭaṃ pitṛdāyebhyo datvarṇaṃ paitṛkaṃ ca yat |
bhrātṛbhis tad vibhaktavyam ṛṇī syād anyathā pitā || iti | [NSm 13.32]
kātyāyanas tu |
bhrātrā pitṛvyamātṛbhyāṃ kuṭumbārtham ṛṇaṃ kṛtam |
vibhāgakāle deyaṃ tad rikthibhiḥ samam eva tu || iti |
atra yājñavalkye |
pitur ūrdhvaṃ vibhajatāṃ mātāpy aṃśaṃ samaṃ haret | iti | [YDh 2.126]
tad atra noktam putrair eva saha vṛttir asyā iti | tathā ca manuḥ |
pitā rakṣati kaumāre bhartā rakṣati yauvane |
putras tu sthavirībhāve na strī svātantryam arhati || iti | [MDh 9.3]
evaṃ mātur apy abhāve tad dhanaṃ bhartṛkulalabdhaṃ svayam ārjitaṃ putrā aprattāś ca duhitaraḥ samaṃ gṛhṇīyuḥ |
strīdhanaṃ tadapatyānāṃ duhitā ca tadaṃśinī |
aprattā cet samūḍhā tu labhate sā na mātṛkam ||
iti bṛhaspatiḥ | pitṛkulalabdhas tu aprattā eva duhitaraḥ |
mātus tu yautakaṃ yat syāt kumārībhāga eva saḥ | [MDh 9.131]
iti manuḥ | atha prattā duhitaraḥ, putrāś ca, tadā katham |
jananyāṃ saṃsthitāyāṃ tu samaṃ sarve sahodarāḥ |
bhajeran mātṛkaṃ rikthaṃ bhaginyaś ca sanābhayaḥ || [MDh 9.192]
iti mānavam eva | atra vyāsaḥ |
asaṃskṛtās tu ye tatra paitṛkād eva te dhanāt |
saṃskāryā bhrātṛbhir jyeṣṭhaiḥ kanyakāś ca yathāvidhi || iti |
atra kramavivāhe bṛhaspatiḥ |
brahmakṣatriyaviṭchūdrā viprotpannās tv anukramāt |
catustridvyekabhāgena bhajeyus te yathākramam ||
kṣatrajās tridvyekabhāgā viḍjau tu dvyekabhāginau || iti |
mānave ca spaṣṭam uktam |
sarvaṃ vā rikthajātaṃ tad daśadhā pravibhajya tu |
dharmyaṃ vibhāgaṃ kurvīta vidhinānena dharmavit ||
caturo ‘ṃśān hared vipras trīn aṃśān katriyāsutaḥ |
vaiśyāputro hared dvyaṃśam ekaṃ śūdrāsuto haret || iti | [MDh 9.152–53]
yasya tu brāhmaṇī vandhyā mṛtā vā, tatra kṣatriyādisutās tridvyekabhāgāḥ | yasya tv ekasyām eva putraḥ sa sarvaṃ harec chūdrāputravarjam | yathāha devalaḥ |
anulomy ekaputras tu pituḥ sarvasvabhāg bhavet |
niṣāda ekaputras tu viprasvasya tṛtīyabhāk ||
dvau sapiṇḍaḥ sakulyo vā svadhādātā tu saṃharet || iti |
niṣādaḥ paraśavaḥ | kṣatriyādiviṣaye bṛhaspatiḥ |
na pratigrahabhūr deyā kṣatriyādisutāya vai |
yady apy asya pitā dadyān mṛte viprāsuto haret ||
śūdryā dvijātibhir jāto na bhūmer bhāgam arhati |
sajātāv āpnuyāt sarvam iti dharmo vyavasthitaḥ || iti |
yājñavalkyaḥ |
jāto ‘pi dāsyāṃ śūdreṇa kāmato ‘ṃśaharo bhavet |
mṛte pitari kuryus taṃ bhrātaras tv ardhabhāginam || iti | [YDh 2.136]
bhāryāviṣaye viṣṇuḥ: “mātaraḥ putrabhāgānusāribhāgahāriṇyaḥ” iti [ViDh 18.34] | atra |
aurasaḥ putrikā bījakṣetrajau putrikāsutaḥ |
paunarbhavaś ca kānīnaḥ sahoḍho gūḍhasaṃbhavaḥ ||
dattaḥ krītaḥ svayaṃdattaḥ kṛttrimaś cāpaviddhakaḥ |
yatra kvacotpāditaś ca putrākhyā daśa pañca ca ||
anenaiva krameṇaiṣāṃ pūrvābhāve paraḥ paraḥ |
piṇḍado ‘ṃśaharaś ceti prāyeṇa smṛtiṣu sthitiḥ || iti |
auraso dharmapatnījaḥ | “savarṇāpūrvaśāstravihitāyām” [ĀpDh2.13.1] iti pūrvam uktaḥ | gautamaḥ: “pitotsṛjet putrikām anapatyo ‘gniṃ prajāpatiṃ ceṣṭvāsmadartham apatyam iti saṃpādya” iti [GDh 28.18] | bṛhaspatiḥ |
eka evaurasaḥ pitrye dhane svamī prakīrtitaḥ |
tattulyāḥ putrikāḥ proktāḥ bhartavyās tv apare smṛtāḥ || iti |
manuḥ |
putrikāyāṃ kṛtāyāṃ tu yadi putro ‘nujāyate |
samas tatra vibhāgaḥ syāj jyeṣṭhatā nāsti hi striyāḥ || iti | [MDh 9.134]
yājñavalkyaḥ |
aputreṇa parakṣetre niyogotpāditaḥ sutaḥ |
ubhayor apy asau rikthī piṇḍadātā ca dharmataḥ || [YDh 2.127]
ayam eka evotpādayitur bījajaḥ, kṣetriṇas tu kṣetrajaḥ | bṛhaspatiḥ |
pautro ‘tha putrikāputraḥ svargaprāptikarāv ubhau |
rikthe piṇḍāmbudāne ca samau saṃparikīrtitau || iti |
kāśyapaḥ |
sapta paunarbhavāḥ kanyāḥ varjanīyāḥ kulādhamāḥ |
vācā dattā manodattā kṛtakautukamaṅgalā ||
udakasparśitā yā ca yā ca pāṇigṛhītikā |
agniṃ parigatā yā ca punarbhūprabhavā ca yā ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
klībaṃ vihāya patitaṃ yā punar labhate patim |
tasyāḥ paunarbhavo jāto vyaktam utpādakasya saḥ || iti |
manuḥ |
pitṛveśmani kanyā tu yaṃ putraṃ janayed rahaḥ |
taṃ kānīnaṃ vaden nāmnā voḍhuḥ kanyāsamudbhavaḥ || [MDh 9.172]
nāradaḥ |
kānīnaś ca sahoḍhaś ca gūḍhāyāṃ yaś ca jāyate |
teṣāṃ voḍhā pitā jñeyaḥ te ca bhāgaharāḥ pituḥ || iti | [NSm 13.16]
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
aprattā duhitā yasya putraṃ vindeta tulyataḥ |
pautrī mātāmahas tena dadyāt piṇḍaṃ hared dhanam || iti | [VaDh 17.23]
anūḍhāyām eva mṛtāyāṃ mātāmahasya putraḥ | anyathā voḍhuḥ | manuḥ |
yā garbhiṇī saṃskriyate jñātājñātāpi vā satī |
voḍhuḥ sa garbho bhavati sahoḍha iti cocyate || [MDh 9.173–74]
utpādyate gṛhe yasya na ca jñāyeta kasya saḥ |
