sa gṛhe gūḍha utpannas tasya syād yasya kalpajaḥ || [MDh 9.169–70]
dattaviṣaye bhrātṛputreṣu satsu teṣv eka eva grāhyaḥ | tatra kṣetrajaḥ pūrvam evoktaḥ | paiṭhīnasiḥ: “atha dattakrītakṛttrimaputrikāputrāḥ paraparigraheṇārṣeyeṇa jātās te asaṃhatakulīnā dvyāmuṣyāyaṇā bhavanti” iti | manuḥ |
bhrātṝṇām ekajātānām ekaś cet putravān bhavet |
sarve tenaiva putreṇa putriṇo manur abravīt || [MDh 9.182]
krīṇīyād yas tv apatyārthe mātāpitror yam antikāt |
sa hi krītaḥ sutas tasya sadṛśo ‘sadṛśo ‘pi vā || [MDh 9.173–74]
mātāpitṛvihīno yaḥ tyakto vā syād akāraṇāt |
ātmānaṃ sparśayed yasya svayaṃ dattas tu sa smṛtaḥ || [MDh 9.177
sadṛśaṃ tu prakuryātāṃ guṇadoṣavivarjitam |
putraṃ putraguṇair yuktaṃ sa vijñeyas tu kṛttrimaḥ || [MDh 9.169–70]
mātāpitṛbhyām utsṛṣṭaṃ tayor anyatareṇa vā |
yaṃ putraṃ pratigṛhṇīyād apaviddhaḥ sa ucyate || iti | [MDh 9.171]
sarve te sajātīyāḥ,
sajātīyeṣv ayaṃ proktas tanayeṣu mayā vidhiḥ | [YDh 2.135]
iti yājñavalkyavacanāt | viṣṇuḥ: “yatra kva ca notpāditas tu dvādaśaḥ” iti [ViDh 15.27] | yājñavalkyaḥ |
piṇḍado ‘ṃśaharaś caiṣāṃ pūrvābhāve paraḥ paraḥ || iti | [YDh 2.135]
manuḥ |
śreyasaḥ śreyaso ‘bhāve pāpīyān riktham arhati | iti | [MDh 9.184]
nāradaḥ |
kramād ete pravartante mṛte pitari taddhane |
jyāyaso jyāyaso ‘bhāve jaghanyas tad avāpnuyāt || iti | [NSm 13.49]
devalaḥ |
sarve hy anaurasasyaite putrā dāyaharāḥ smṛtāḥ |
aurase punar utpanne teṣu jyaiṣṭhyaṃ na tiṣṭhati ||
teṣāṃ savarṇā ye putrāḥ te tṛtīyāṃśabhāginaḥ |
śeṣās tam upajīveyuḥ grāsācchādanasaṃbhṛtāḥ || iti |
manuḥ |
ṣaṣṭhaṃ tu kṣetrajasyāṃśaṃ pradadyāt paitṛkād dhanāt |
auraso vibhajed dāyaṃ pitryaṃ pañcamam eva vā || iti | [MDh 9.146]
bṛhaspatiḥ |
kṣetrajādyās sutās tv anye pañcaṣaṭsaptabhāginaḥ || iti |
hārītaḥ: “vibhajiṣyamāṇa ekaviṃśaṃ kānīnāya dadyāt, viṃśaṃ paunarbhavāya, ekonaviṃśaṃ dvyāmuṣyāyaṇāya, aṣṭādaśaṃ kṣetrajāya, saptadaśaṃ putrikāputrāya, itarān aurasāya” iti | vasiṣṭhaḥ “putraṃ pratigrahīṣyan” iti prakramya, “tasmiṃś cet pratigṛhīta aurasa utpadyeta caturthabhāgabhāk” iti [VaDh 15.6, 9] | evam eteṣu śāstreṣu vidyamāneṣu yadācāryeṇa pūrvam uktam “teṣāṃ karmabhiḥ saṃbandhaḥ, dāyenāvyatikramaś ca” [ĀpDh 2.13.1] iti tad dharmapatnīje putre sati kṣetrajādīnāṃ samāṃśabhāktvapratiṣedhaparaṃ veditavyam |
athāvibhājyam | tatra manuḥ |
anupaghnan pitṛdravyaṃ śrameṇa yad upārjayet |
svayam āhitalabdhaṃ tan nākāmo dātum arhati || iti | [MDh 9.208]
kātyāyanaḥ |
nāvidyānāṃ tu vaidyena deyaṃ vidyādhanāt kvacit |
samavidyādhikānāṃ tu deyaṃ vaidyena tad dhanam ||
parabhaktopayogena prāptā vidyā yadānyataḥ |
tayā prāptaṃ tu vidhinā vidyāprāptaṃ tad ucyate || iti |
vyāsaḥ |
pitāmahapitṛbhyāṃ ca dattaṃ mātrā ca yad bhavet |
tasya tan nāpahartavyaṃ śauryabhāryādhanaṃ tathā || iti |
yājñavalkyaḥ |
kramād abhyāgataṃ dravyaṃ hṛtam apy uddharet tu yaḥ |
dāyādebhyo na tad dadyād vidyayā labdham eva ca || [YDh 2.121]
patyau jīvati yaḥ strībhir alaṃkāro dhṛto bhavet |
na taṃ bhajeran dāyādāḥ bhajamānāḥ patanti te || [YDh 2.200]
vyāsaḥ |
sādhāraṇaṃ samāśritya yat kiṃcid vāhanāyudham |
śauryādināpnoti dhanaṃ bhrātaras tatra bhāginaḥ ||
tasya bhāgadvayaṃ deyaṃ śeṣāḥ syuḥ samabhāginaḥ || iti
iti putra vibhāgaḥ | tadabhāve mṛtasya yaḥ pratyāsannaḥ sa dāyaṃ haret, iti vakṣyamāṇena saṃbandhaḥ |
lepabhājaś caturthādyāḥ pitrādyāḥ piṇḍabhāginaḥ |
saptamaḥ piṇḍadātaiṣāṃ sāpiṇḍyaṃ sāptapūruṣam ||
iti sapiṇḍalakṣaṇam | teṣu yo yaḥ pratyāsannaḥ sa sa gṛhṇīyād iti | bhāryāṃ tu rikthagrāhiṇaḥ sapiṇḍā rakṣeyuḥ | na tu dāyagrahaṇam ity ācāryasya pakṣaḥ | śrūyate hi “tasmāt striyo ninirdriyāḥ adāyādīḥ” iti | manur api |
nirindriyā adāyādīḥ striyo nityam iti sthitiḥ | iti | [MDh 9.18]
atra sapiṇḍādyabhāve bṛhaspatiḥ |
anyatra brāhmaṇāt kiṃ tu rājā dharmaparāyaṇaḥ |
tatstrīṇāṃ jīvanaṃ dadyād eṣa dāyavidhiḥ smṛtaḥ ||
annārthaṃ taṇḍulaprastham aparāhṇe tu sendhanam |
vasanaṃ tripaṇakrītaṃ deyam ekaṃ trimāsataḥ ||
etāvad eva sādhvīnāṃ coditaṃ vidhavādhanam |
vasanasyāśansyaiva tathaiva rajakasya ca ||
dhanaṃ vyapohya tacchiṣṭaṃ dāyādānāṃ prakalpayet |
dhūmāvasānikaṃ grāhyaṃ sabhāyāṃ snānataḥ purā ||
vasanāśanavāsāṃsi vigaṇayya dhave mṛte || iti |
vyāsaḥ |
dvisāhasraḥ paro dāyaḥ striyai deyo dhanasya tu |
yac ca bhartrā dhanaṃ dattaṃ sā yathākāmam āpnuyāt || iti |
paṇānāṃ dve sahasre parimāṇam asya dvisāhasraḥ eṣa paro dāyaḥ striyāḥ, nādhikaḥ iti | etat prabhūte dhane, jñātayaś ca na rakṣeyur iti śaṅkāyām | evam “patnīduhitaraś ca” [YDh 2.138] ityādīni yāni patnyā dāyaprāptiparāṇi tāny apy evam eva draṣṭavyāni | atra pitari bhrātari sodarye ca jīvati sati sodaryo bhrātā gṛhṇīyād ity eke | tathā ca śaṅkhaḥ: “aputrasya svaryātasya bhrātṛgāmi dravyam, tadabhāve mātāpitarau hareyātāṃ patnī vā jyeṣṭhā” iti | devalaḥ |
tato dāyam aputrasya vibhajeran sahodarāḥ |
tulyā duhitaro vāpi dhriyamāṇaḥ pitāpi ca ||
savarṇā bhrātaro mātā bhāryā ceti yathākramam || iti |
yājñavalkyaḥ |
saṃsṛṣṭinas tu saṃsṛṣṭī sodaryasya tu sodaraḥ |
dadyāc cāpahared vāṃśaṃ jātasya ca mṛtasya ca ||
anyodaryas tu saṃsṛṣṭī nānyodaryo dhanaṃ haret |
asaṃsṛṣṭy api nādadyāt saṃsṛṣṭo nānyamātṛjaḥ || iti | [YDh 2.141–42]
atra sodarasyeti viśeṣavacanāt “patnī duhitaraś ca” [YDh 2.138] ity atra bhrātṛgrahaṇaṃ bhinnodaraviṣayam iti | pratyāsattyatiśayāt pitaiveti vayam | tadabhāve sodaryaḥ | tadabhāve tatputrāḥ | tadabhāve bhinnodarāḥ | tadabhāve pitṛvyaḥ iti draṣṭavyam | mātrādayo ‘pi striyo jīvanamātraṃ labheran iti || 2.14.2 ||
tadabhāva ācārya ācāryābhāve ‘ntevāsī hṛtvā tadartheṣu
dharmakṛtyeṣu vopayojayet || 2.14.3 ||
sapiṇḍābhāve ācāryo dāyaṃ hareta | tasyāpy abhāve ‘ntevāsī hareta | hṛtvā ca tadartheṣu dharmakṛtyeṣu taṭākakhananādiṣūpayojayet | vāśabdāt svayaṃ vopayuñjīta || 2.14.3 ||
duhitā vā || 2.14.4 ||
duhitā vā dāyaṃ hareta | putrābhāva ity eke | anantarokte viṣaya ity anye || 2.14.4 ||
sarvābhāve rājā dāyaṃ hareta || 2.14.5 ||
sarvagrahaṇāt bandhūnāṃ sagotrāṇāṃ cābhāve || 2.14.5 ||
jyetṣṭho dāyāda ity eke || 2.14.6 ||
jyeṣṭha eva putro dāyahara ity eke manyante | itare tu tam upajīveyuḥ | so ‘pi tān piteva paripālayed iti | tathā ca gautamaḥ: “sarvaṃ vā pūrvajasyetarān bibhṛyāt pitṛvat” iti [GDh 28.3]
|| 2.14.6 ||
deśaviśeṣe suvarṇaṃ kṛṣṇā gāvaḥ kṛṣṇaṃ bhaumaṃ
jyeṣṭhasya || 2.14.7 ||
kvacid deśe suvarṇādi jyeṣṭhasya bhāga ity āhuḥ | bhūmau jātaṃ bhaumaṃ dhānyam, kṛṣṇaṃ bhaumaṃ māṣādi | kṛṣṇāyasam ity anye || 2.14.7 ||
rathaḥ pituḥ paribhāṇḍaṃ ca gṛhe || 2.14.8 ||
rathaḥ pitur aṃśaḥ, gṛhe ca yat paribhāṇḍaṃ upakaraṇam pīṭhādikaṃ tad api || 2.14.8 ||
alaṃkāro bhāryāyā jñātidhanaṃ cety eke || 2.14.9 ||
bhāryāyās tu dhṛto ‘laṃkāro ‘ṃśaḥ | jñātibhyaḥ pitrādibhyaś ca yal labdhaṃ dhanaṃ tac cety evam eke manyante || 2.14.9 ||
tac chāstrair vipratiṣiddham || 2.14.10 ||
yad uktaṃ jyeṣṭho dāyāda iti tac chāstraviruddham || 2.14.10 ||
yena viruddhaṃ tad darśayati |
manuḥ putrebhyo dāyaṃ vyabhajad ity aviśeṣeṇa
śrūyate || 2.14.11 ||
putrebhya iti bahuvacananirdeśād aviśeṣeṇa śravaṇam || 2.14.11 ||
atra codyam |
athāpi tasmāj jyeṣṭhaṃ putraṃ dhanena niravasāyayantīty
ekavac chrūyate || 2.14.12 ||
athāpi nunu cety arthaḥ | jyeṣṭhaṃ putraṃ dhanena niravasāyayanti pṛthakkurvantīy ekavad api śrūyate | yathā eka eva jyeṣṭho dāyādaḥ tadanurūpam api śrūyate iti || 2.14.12 ||
pariharati |
athāpi nityānuvādam avidhim āhur nyāyavido yathā tasmād ajāvayaḥ paśūnāṃ sahacarantīti | tasmāt snātakasya mukhaṃ rephāyatīva | tasmād bastaś ca śrotriyaś ca strīkāmatamāv
iti || 2.14.13 ||
athāpīti parihāropakrame | paśūnāṃ madhye ajāś cāvayaś ca jātibhede ‘pi saha carantīti | rephā śobhā | iha tu tadvatyabhedopacāraḥ, tataḥ kyaṣ | snātakasya mukhaṃ kuṇḍalādinā śobhate | ivaśabdo vākyālaṃkāre | śrotriyasya strīkāmatamatvam ācāryakule cirakālaṃ brahmacārivāsāt | yathaitāni vākyāni dṛṣṭamātram anuvadanti na kiṃcid vidadhati, tathā “tasmāj jyeṣṭhaṃ putram” [TS 2.5.2] ityādikam apīti nyāyavida āhuḥ | na kevalam ayam evānuvādaḥ | kiṃ tarhi | “manuḥ putrebhyaḥ” [TS 3.1.9] ity ayam apy anuvāda eva || 2.14.13 ||
sarve hi dharmayuktā bhāginaḥ || 2.14.14 ||
hiśabdo hetau | yasmād etāv anuvādakau na kasyacid vidhāyakau, tasmād ye dharmayuktāḥ putrāḥ sarva ete bhāginaḥ || 2.14.14 ||
yas tv adharmeṇa dravyāṇi pratipādayati jyeṣṭho 'pi
tam abhāgaṃ kurvīta || 2.14.15 ||
yas tu jyeṣṭho ‘py adharmeṇa dravyāṇi pratipādayati viniyuṅkte tam abhāgaṃ kurvīta | tasmai jivadvibhāge pitā bhāgaṃ na dadyāt, ūrdhvaṃ vibhāge ‘pi bhrātaraḥ | apiśabdāt kim utānyam iti jyeṣṭhasya prādhānyaṃ khyāpyate || 2.14.15 ||
“jīvan putrebhyaḥ” [ĀpDh 2.14.1] ity atra bhāryāyā bhāgo na pradarśitaḥ | tatra kāraṇam āha |
jāyāpatyor na vibhāgo vidyate || 2.14.16 ||
kasmāt |
pāṇigrahaṇād dhi sahatvaṃ karmasu || 2.14.17 ||
karmārthaṃ dravyam, jāyāyāś ca na pṛthak karmasv adhikāraḥ | kiṃ tarhi | sahabhāvena “yas tvayā dharmaś ca kartavyaḥ so ‘nayā saha” iti vacanāt | tatra kiṃ pṛthagdravyeṇeti || 2.14.17 ||
tathā puṇyaphaleṣu || 2.14.18 ||
puṇyaphaleṣu svargādiṣv api tathā sahatvam eveti “divi jyotir ajaram ārabhetām” ityādibhyo mantraliṅgebhyaḥ || 2.14.18 ||
dravyaparigraheṣu ca || 2.14.19 ||
dravyārjaneṣv api tathā sahatvam eva, tatra patir ārjayati jāyā gṛha nirvahatīti yogakṣemāv ubhayāyattāv iti dravyaparigrahe sahatvam || 2.14.19 ||
etad evopapādayati |
na hi bhartur vipravāse naimittike dāne steyam
upadiśanti || 2.14.20 ||
hi yasmāt bhartur vipravāse sati naimittike “chindatprāṇi dadyāt” ityādike dāne kṛte bhāryāyā na steyam upadiśanti dharmajñāḥ | yadi bhartur eva dravyaṃ syāt, syād evaṃ [eva?] steyam | “naimittike dāne” iti vacanāt vyayāntare tu steyaṃ bhavaty eva | etad eva dravyasādhāraṇye ‘pi dampatyor vaiṣamyam, yat patir yatheṣṭaṃ viniyuṅkte jāyā tv etāvad eveti | na ca patyuḥ svayam ārjanasya viniyoge jāyāyā anumatyapekṣā, svatantratvāt | svatantro hy asau gṛhe, yathā rājā rāṣṭre | ata eva bhāryāyāḥ steyaśaṅkā, na bhartuḥ || 2.14.20 ||
iti caturdaśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
etena deśakuladharmā vyākhyātāḥ || 2.15.1 ||
“jyeṣṭho dāyādaḥ” [ĀpDh 2.14.16] ityādikaṃ śāstraṃ vipratiṣedhād apramāṇam ity uktam | etena deśadharmāḥ kuladharmāś ca vyākhyātāḥ | śāstravipratiṣiddhā mātulasutāpariṇayanādayo ‘pramāṇam, viparītāḥ pramāṇam iti | gautamo ‘py āha: “deśajātikuladharmāś cāmnāyair aviruddhāḥ pramāṇam” iti [GDh 11.20] || 2.15.1 ||
mātuś ca yonisaṃbandhebhyaḥ pituś cā saptamāt puruṣād yāvatā vā saṃbandho jñāyate teṣāṃ preteṣūdakopasarśanaṃ garbhān parihāpyāparisaṃvatsarān || 2.15.2 ||
mātur yonisaṃbandhā mātulādayaḥ | pituś cā saptamāt puruṣāt saṃbandhāḥ sapiṇḍāḥ | yāvatā vāntareṇa saṃbandho jñāyate smaryate janmanā nāmnā cāmuṣyāyaṇaḥ asmatkūṭasthaḥ sa vaṃśya evaṃnāmeti | tathā ca manuḥ |
sapiṇḍatā tu puruṣe saptame vinivartate |
samānodakabhāvas tu janmanāmnor avedane || iti | [MDh 5.60]
teṣāṃ preteṣu mṛteṣūdakopasparśanaṃ kartavyam | maraṇanimittaṃ snānaṃ kartavyam | garbhān bālān aparisaṃvatsarān aparipūrṇasaṃvatsarān parihāpya varjayitvā | bāleṣu mṛteṣu snānaṃ na kartavyam iti || 2.15.2 ||
mātāpitarāv eva teṣu || 2.15.3 ||
teṣu bāleṣu mṛteṣu mātāpitarāv evodakopasparśanaṃ kuryātām || 2.15.3 ||
hartāraś ca || 2.15.4 ||
ye ca tān bālān mṛtān haranti nirharanti te ‘py udakopasparśanaṃ kuryur iti || 2.15.4 ||
bhāryāyāṃ paramagurusaṃsthāyāṃ cākālam
abhojanam || 2.15.5 ||
bhāryā patnī | paramaguravaḥ ācāryamātāpitaraḥ | saṃsthā maraṇam | bhāryāyāṃ ca saṃsthitāyāṃ paramagurūṇāṃ ca saṃsthāyāṃ satyāṃ na kevalam udakopasparśanam | kiṃ tarhi | aparedyur ā tasmāt kālād abhojanam || 2.15.5 ||
kiṃ ca |
āturavyañjanāni kurvīran || 2.15.6 ||
āturatvaṃ vyajyate yais tāni ca kurvīran bhāryādimaraṇe || 2.15.6 ||
kāni punas tāni |
keśān prakīrya pāṃsūn opyaikavāsaso dakṣiṇāmukhāh
sakṛd upamajjyottīryopaviśanti || 2.15.7 ||
keśān prakīrya teṣu pāṃsūn āvapanti | opyaikavāsasaḥ anuttarīyā dakṣiṇāmukhāḥ dakṣiṇāṃ diśaṃ nirīkṣamāṇāḥ sakṛd upamajjyodakād uttīrya tīra upaviśanti dakṣiṇāmukhā eva || 2.15.7 ||
evaṃ triḥ || 2.15.8 ||
evam uktaprakāreṇa trir upamajjyopaviśeyuḥ | 2.15.8 ||
tatpratyayam udakam utsicyāpratīkṣā grāmam etya yat striya
āhus tat kurvanti || 2.15.9 ||
tatas tatpratyayaṃ teṣāṃ mṛtānāṃ bhāryādīnāṃ yathā pratyayo bhavati mahyam idam udakaṃ dattam iti tathodakam utsiñcanti | ācārāt pitryatvāc ca tilamiśram | “bhāradvājāya yajñaśarmaṇe etat tilodakaṃ dadāmi” iti prayogaḥ | utsicyāpratīkṣāḥ pṛṣṭhato ‘nirīkṣamāṇāḥ grāmam etya gṛhaṃ praviśya | anena bahir idaṃ karmeti gamyate | yat striyaḥ kartavyam āhus tat kurvanti, agnyupasparśanagavālambhanādīni || 2.15.9 ||
itareṣu caitad evaika upadiśanti || 2.15.10 ||
“ākālam abhojanam” [ĀpDh 2.15.5] ityādi yad uktam etad itareṣu bhāryādibhyo ‘nyeṣv api sapiṇḍeṣu mṛteṣv api kartavyam ity eke ācāryā upadiśanti || 2.15.10 ||
śucīn mantravataḥ sarvakṛtyeṣu bhojayet || 2.15.11 ||
ekānte ‘pi vidhipratiṣedhānusāriṇaḥ śucayaḥ | tān mantravataḥ adhītavedān sarvakṛtyeṣu śrauteṣu gārhyeṣu smārteṣu ca karmasu daiveṣu pitryeṣu mānuṣeṣu ca bhojayet “ananto brāhmaṇabhojanam” iti smṛtyantare darśanāt || 2.15.11 ||
deśataḥ kālataḥ śaucataḥ samyakpratigṛhītṛta iti dānāni
pratipādayati || 2.15.12 ||
saptamyarthe tasiḥ | deśaḥ prayāgādiḥ | kālaḥ sūryagrahaṇādiḥ | śaucaṃ kṛcchrādisamāptiḥ | samīcīnaḥ pratigrahītā, “tulyaguṇeṣu vayovṛddhaḥ śreyān” [ĀpDh 2.17.10] ityādi | eteṣu dānāni deyāni pratipādayati dadyād iti || 2.15.12 ||
yasyāgnau na kriyate yasya cāgraṃ na dīyate na tad
bhoktavyam || 2.15.13 ||
yasyānnasyaikadeśo ‘gnau na kriyate na hūyate, yasmād voddhṛtyāgraṃ na dīyate na tad bhoktavyam || 2.15.13 ||
na kṣāralavaṇahomo vidyate || 2.15.14 ||
yad bhakṣyamāṇaṃ paśyato ‘lālotpadyate tat kṣāram, guḍamarīcalikucādi | kṣāralavaṇasaṃsṛṣṭaṃ na hotavyam || 2.15.14 ||
tathāvarānnasaṃsṛṣṭasya ca || 2.15.15 ||
avarānnaṃ kulutthādi | tatsaṃsṛṣṭasyāpy annasya homo na vidyate || 2.15.15 ||
atha yasyaivaṃvidham eva bhojyam upasthitaṃ tasya kathaṃ bhojanam | tatrāha |
ahaviṣyasya homa udācīnam uṣṇaṃ bhasmāpohya tasmin
bhasmani juhuyāt tad dhutam ahutaṃ cāgnau bhavati || 2.15.16 ||
aupāsanāt pacanād vā agner udīcīnam uṣṇaṃ bhasmāpohya tasmin bhasmani juhuyād vaiśvadevamantraiḥ | eṣo ‘haviṣyasya homaḥ | tad evaṃ kriyamāṇaṃ hutaṃ ca bhavati, bhāvārthanivṛtteḥ [-nirvṛtteḥ?] | ahutaṃ cāgnau bhavati, bhasmamātratvād iti | atra bodhāyanaḥ: “atha yady etad eva syād uttarato bhasmamiśrān aṅgārān nirūhya teṣu juhuyāt” iti | apara āha – yāny ahaviṣyāṇi vyañjanāni ahar ahar bhojyāni teṣām eva saṃskāraḥ, sakṛc ca homo ‘mantraka iti || 2.15.16 ||
uttare sūtre spaṣṭārthe |
na strī juhuyāt || 2.15.17 ||
nānupetaḥ || 2.15.18 ||
ānnaprāśanād garbhā nāprayatā bhavanti || 2.15.19 ||
annaprāśanāt prāg garbhā bālā nāprayatā bhavanti rajasvalādisparśane ‘pi | gautamas tv apāmārjanādikam icchati, yathāha: “anyatrāpamārjanapradhāvanāvokṣaṇebhyaḥ” iti [GDh 2.2.] || 2.15.19 ||
ā parisaṃvatsarād ity eke || 2.15.20 ||
yāvat saṃvatsaro na pūryate tāvan nāprayatā garbhā ity eke manyante || 2.15.20 ||
yāvatā vā diśo na prajānīyuḥ || 2.15.21 ||
yāvad digvibhāgajñānaṃ nāsti tāvan nāprayatā bhavanti || 2.15.21 ||
opanayanād ity aparam || 2.15.22 ||
upanayanād arvāṅ nāprayatā ity aparaṃ darśanam || 2.15.22 ||
atropapattiḥ |
atra hy adhikāraḥ śāstrair bhavati || 2.15.23 ||
hi yasmāt atropanayane sati vidhiniṣedhaśāstrair adhikāro bhavati || 2.15.23 ||
sā niṣṭhā || 2.15.24 ||
upanayanam api parāmṛśatas tacchabdasya niṣṭhāśabdasāmānādhikaraṇyāt strīliṅgatā | sā niṣṭhā tad upanayanam avasānam adhikārasyeti || 2.15.24 ||
smṛtiś ca || 2.15.25 ||
asminn arthe smṛtir api bhavati: “prāg upanayanāt kāmacāravādabhakṣaḥ” iti gautamaḥ [GDh 2.1] || 2.15.25 ||
iti pañcadaśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
ity āpastambadharmasūtravṛttāv ujjvalāyāṃ
dvitīyapraśne ṣaṣṭhaḥ paṭalaḥ
saha devamanuṣyā asmin loke purā babhūvuḥ | atha devā karmabhir divaṃ jagmur ahīyanta manuṣyāḥ | teṣāṃ ye tathā karmāṇy ārambhante saha devair brahmaṇā cāmuṣmin loke bhavanti athaitan manuḥ śrāddhaśabdaṃ karma
provāca || 2.16.1 ||
śrāddhavidhitsayā tasya prarocanārtho ‘yam arthavādaḥ prastūyate – purā kila devāś ca manuṣyāś ca asmin loke saha babhūvuḥ | atha taiḥ saha bhāvam asahamānā devāḥ karmabhiḥ śrautaiḥ gārhyaiḥ smārtaiś ca yathāvad anuṣṭhitair  divaṃ jagmuḥ | manuṣyās tu tathā kartum asamarthāḥ ahīyanta hīnā abhavan ihaiva loke sthitāḥ | yata evaṃ karmaṇāṃ sāmarthyam, atha idānīm api teṣāṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ madhye ye tathā karmāṇy ārabhante kurvanti, yathārabhanta devāḥ, te devair brahmaṇā ca sahāmuṣmin loke bhavanti | athaivaṃ hīyamānān manuṣyān dṛṣṭvā kṛpayā manur vaivasvataḥ śrāddhaśabdaṃ śrāddham iti śabdyamānam etat karma provāca
|| 2.16.1 ||
kim artham |
prajāniḥśreyasāya || 2.16.2 ||
tādarthye caturthī | prajānāṃ niḥśreyasārtham | “niḥśreyasā ca” iti pāṭhe chāndaso yakārasya cakāraḥ | apara āha – chandaso liṅgavyatyayaḥ, prajāniḥśreyasaṃ cāsya karmaṇaḥ phalam iti
|| 2.16.2 ||
tatra pitaro devatā brāhmaṇās tv āhavanīyārthe || 2.16.3 ||
tatra śrāddhakarmaṇi pitaraḥ pitṛpitāmahaprapitāmahā devatāḥ | brāhmaṇās tu bhuñjānā āhavanīyārthe āhavanīyakṛtye veditavyāḥ | trīṇi śrāddhe karmāṇi – homo brāhmaṇabhojanaṃ piṇḍadānaṃ ceti | tatra bhojanasya pradhānatamatvakhyāpanārtho ‘yam arthavādaḥ || 2.16.3 ||
māsi māsi kāryam || 2.16.4 ||
tad idaṃ karma māse māse kartavyam | vīpsāvacanād yāvajjīviko ‘bhyāsaḥ || 2.16.4 ||
aparapakṣasyāparāhṇaḥ śreyān || 2.16.5 ||
aparapakṣasya yāny ahāni teṣv aparāhṇaḥ praśastataraḥ || 2.16.5 ||
tathāparapakṣasya jaghanyāny ahāni || 2.16.6 ||
tasyaiva pakṣasya yāny ahāni pañcadaśa teṣāṃ yathottaraṃ śreyastvam || 2.16.6 ||
sarveṣv evāparapakṣasyāhaḥsu kriyamāṇe pitṝn prīṇāti | kartus
tu kālābhiniyamāt phalaviśeṣaḥ || 2.16.7 ||
sarveṣv evāhaḥsu pitṝṇāṃ tṛptir aviśiṣṭā | yas tu kartā pratipadādike kāle niyamena śrāddhaṃ karoti, sarveṣu māseṣu pratipady eva dvitīyāyām evetyādi | tasya kartus tasmāt kālābhiniyamāt phalaviśeṣo bhavati || 2.16.7 ||
ko ‘sāv iti | ata āha |
prathame ‘hani kriyamāṇe strīprāyam apatye jāyate || 2.16.8 ||
yaḥ pratipadi niyamena śrāddhaṃ karoti tasyāpatye prajāsantāne strīprāyam apatyaṃ jāyate | prāyeṇa striyo jāyante || 2.16.8 ||
dvitīye stenāḥ || 2.16.9 ||
putrā jāyante || 2.16.9 ||
tṛtīya brahmavarcasinaḥ || 2.16.10 ||
vṛttādhyayanasaṃpattiḥ brahmavarcasam | tadvanto brahmavarcasinaḥ || 2.16.10 ||
caturthe kṣudrapaśumān || 2.16.11 ||
kṣudrāḥ paśavo ‘jāvyādayaḥ, tais tadvān kartā bhavati | uttaratrāpy ekavacaneṣu kartur vādo draṣṭavyaḥ || 2.16.11 ||
pañcame pumāṃsaḥ | bahvapatyo na cānapatyaḥ pramīyate
|| 2.16.12 ||
pumāṃsa eva bhavanti, bahavaś ca bhavanti, na cānapatyaḥ pramīyate jīvastv eva putreṣu saṃnihiteṣu ca svayaṃ mriyate | na teṣu mṛteṣu, nāpi deśāntaraṃ gateṣu, nāpi svayaṃ deśāntaraṃ gata iti || 2.16.12 ||
ṣaṣṭhe ‘dhvaśīlo ‘kṣaśīlaś ca || 2.16.13 ||
adhvaśīlaḥ pānthaḥ | akṣaśīlaḥ kitavaḥ || 2.16.13 ||
saptame karṣe rāddhiḥ || 2.16.14 ||
karṣaḥ kṛṣiḥ | rāddhiḥ siddhiḥ || 2.16.14 ||
aṣṭame puṣṭiḥ|| 2.16.15 ||
navama ekakhurāḥ || 2.16.16 ||
ekakhurā aśvādayaḥ || 2.16.16 ||
daśame vyavahāre rāddhiḥ || 2.16.17 ||
vyavahāro vāṇijyam | śāstraparijñānaṃ vā || 2.16.17 ||
ekādaśe kṛṣṇāyasaṃ trapusīsam || 2.16.18 ||
kṛṣṇamayaḥ kṛṣṇāyasam | trapusīse lohaviśeṣau || 2.16.18 ||
dvādaśe paśumān || 2.16.19 ||
dvādaśyāṃ bahavaḥ paśavo bhavanti || 2.16.19 ||
trayodaśe bahuputro bahumitro darśanīyāpatyaḥ |
yuvamāriṇas tu bhavanti || 2.16.20 ||
trayodaśyāṃ bahavaḥ putrā mitrāṇi ca bhavanti | apatyāni ca darśanīyāni bhavanti | kiṃ tu te putrā yuvamāriṇo bhavanti yuvāna eva mriyante || 2.16.20 ||
caturdaśa āyudhe rāddhiḥ || 2.16.21 ||
saṃgrāme jayaḥ || 2.16.21 ||
pañcadaśe puṣṭiḥ || 2.16.22 ||
tatra dravyāṇi tilamāṣā vrīhiyavā āpo mūlaphalāni ca || 2.16.23 ||
tatra śrāddhe tilādīni dravyāṇi yathāyatham avaśyam upayojyāni || 2.16.23 ||
snehavati tv evānne tīvratarā pitṝṇāṃ prītir drāghīyāṃsaṃ ca
kālam || 2.16.24 ||
yad vā tad vānnaṃ bhavatu, snehavati tu tasminn ājyādibhir upasikte pitṝṇāṃ tīvratarā prakṛṣṭatarā prītir bhavati | sā ca drāghīyāṃsaṃ kālam anuvartate || 2.16 24 ||
tathā dharmāhṛtena dravyeṇa tīrthe pratipannena || 2.16.25 ||
dharmārjitaṃ yad dravyaṃ pātre ca pratipāditaṃ tenāpi tathā tīvratarā pitṝṇāṃ prītir drāghīyāṃsaṃ ca kālam iti || 2.16.25 ||
saṃvatsaraṃ gavyena prītiḥ || 2.16.26 ||
uttaratra māṃsaśravaṇāt ihāpi māṃsasya grahaṇam | gavyena māṃsena saṃvatsaraṃ pitṝṇāṃ prītir bhavati || 2.16.26 ||
bhūyāṃsam ato māhiṣeṇa || 2.16.27 ||
māhiṣeṇa māṃsena ataḥ saṃvatsarād bhūyāṃsaṃ kālaṃ prītir bhavati || 2.16.27 ||
etena grāmyāraṇyānāṃ paśūnāṃ māṃsaṃ medhyaṃ
vyākhyātam || 2.16.28 ||
etena māhiṣeṇa māṃsenānyeṣām api grāmyāṇām ajādīnām āraṇyānāṃ ca śaśādīnāṃ paśūnāṃ māṃsaṃ medhyaṃ vyākhyātam pitṝṇāṃ prītikaram iti | medhyagrahaṇaṃ pratiṣiddhānāṃ grahaṇaṃ mā bhūd iti || 2.16.28 ||
iti ṣoḍaśaḥ khaṇdaḥ
khaḍgopastaraṇe khaḍgamāṃsenānantaṃ kālam || 2.17.1 ||
khaḍgacarmopastaraṇeṣv āsaneṣūpaviṣṭebhyo brāhmaṇebhyo dattena khaḍgamāṃsenānantaṃ kālaṃ pitṝṇāṃ prītir bhavati | “ānantyam” iti pāṭhe svārthe ṣyañ || 2.17.1 ||
tathā śatabaler matsyasya māṃsena || 2.17.2 ||
śatabalir bahuśakalo rohitākhyaḥ || 2.17.2 ||
vārdhāṇasasya ca || 2.17.3 ||
vyākhyāto vārdhrāṇasaḥ | tasya māṃsenānantaṃ kālaṃ prītiḥ || 2.17.3 ||
prayataḥ prasannmanāḥ sṛṣṭo bhojayed brāhmaṇān brahmavido
yonigotramantrāntevāsyasaṃbandhān || 2.17.4 ||
prayataḥ snānācamanādinā śuddhaḥ | prasannamanā avyākulacittaḥ | sṛṣṭaḥ utsāhavān, “sṛṣṭaś ced brāhmaṇavadhe ahatvāpi” [GDh 22.11] iti darśanāt | “vṛttisargatāyaneṣu kramaḥ” [Pāṇ 1.3.38] ity atra ca sarga utsāhaḥ | evaṃbhūto brāhmaṇān bhojayet | kīdṛśān | brahmavidaḥ ātmavidaḥ | yonyādibhir asaṃbandhān | yonisaṃbandhā mātulādayaḥ | gotrasaṃbandhāḥ sagotrāḥ | mantrasaṃbandhā ṛtvijaḥ yājyāś ca | antevāsisaṃbandhāḥ śiṣyā ācāryāś ca || 2.17.4 ||
guṇahānyāṃ tu pareṣāṃ samudetaḥ sodaryo ‘pi
bhojayitavyaḥ || 2.17.5 ||
yadi pare agotrasaṃbandhā vṛttādiguṇahīnā eva labhyante tadā samudeto vṛttādibhir yuktaḥ sodaryo ‘pi bhojayitavyaḥ, kim aṅga syālādaya ity apiśabdasyārthaḥ || 2.17.5 ||
etenāntevāsino vyākhyātāḥ || 2.17.6 ||
etena sodaryeṇāntevāsinaḥ, bahuvacananirdeśāt pūrvanirdiṣṭā yonyādisaṃbandhāḥ sarva eva, vyākhyātāḥ, anyeṣām alābhe samudetā bhojayitavyā iti | atra manuḥ |
eṣa vai prathamaḥ kalpaḥ pradāne havyakavyayoḥ |
anukalpas tv ayaṃ jñeyaḥ sadā sadbhir anuṣṭhitaḥ ||
mātāmahaṃ mātulaṃ ca svasrīyaṃ śvaśuraṃ gurum |
dauhitraṃ viṭpatiṃ bandhuṃ ṛtvigyājyau ca bhojayet || iti || [MDh 3.147–48]
|| 2.17.6 ||
athāpy udāharanti || 2.17.7 ||
saṃbandhino na bhojyā ity asminn arthe dharmajñā vacanam udāharanti || 2.17.7 ||
saṃbhojinī nāma piśācabhikṣā
naiṣā pitṝn gacchati nota devān |
ihaiva sā carati kṣīṇapuṇyā
śālāntare gaur iva naṣṭavatsā || 2.17.8 ||
parasparaṃ bhuñjate ‘syām iti saṃbhojinī | adhikaraṇe lyuṭ | nāmedam asyāḥ piśācabhikṣāyāḥ | naiṣā pitṝn gacchati nāpi devān | kiṃ tu kṣīṇapuṇyā paralokaprayojanarahitā satī sā ihaiva loke carati | yathā mṛtavatsā gaur gṛhābhyantara eva carati na bahir gacchati || 2.17.8 ||
tad vyācaṣṭe |
ihaiva saṃbhuñjatī dakṣiṇā kulāt kulaṃ vinaśyatīti || 2.17.9 ||
saṃbhuñjatī parasparabhojanasya nimittabhūtā dakṣiṇā śrāddhadānakriyā gṛhād gṛhaṃ gatvā ihaiva loke niśyatīty artha iti || 2.17.9
atha bahuṣu tulyaguṇeṣūpasthiteṣu kaḥ parigrāhyaḥ |
tulyaguṇeṣu vayovṛddhaḥ śreyān dravyakṛśaś cepsan || 2.17.10 ||
yo vayasā vṛddhaḥ sa tāvad grāhyaḥ, tatrāpi yo dravyeṇa kṛśaḥ īpsan lipsamānaś ca bhavati sa grāhyaḥ | yad vā – vayovṛddho grāhyaḥ adravyakṛśo ‘pi, dravyakṛśo ‘py avṛddho ‘pīti | dvayos tu samavāye yathārucīti || 2.17.10 ||
pūrvedyur nivedanam || 2.17.11 ||
śrāddhadināt pūrvedyuḥ brāhmaṇebhyo nivedayitavyam | “śvaḥ śrāddhaṃ bhavitā tatra bhavatā āhavanīyārthe prasādaḥ kartavyaḥ” iti || 2.17.11 ||
aparedyur dvitīyam || 2.17.12 ||
aparedyuḥ śrāddhadine dvitīyaṃ nivedanaṃ kartavyam | “adya śrāddham” iti || 2.17.12 ||
tṛtīyam āmantraṇam || 2.17.13 ||
āmantraṇam āhvānam bhojanakāle “siddham āgamyatām” iti tṛtīyaṃ bhavati || 2.17.13 ||
triḥprāyam eke śrāddham upadiśanti || 2.17.14 ||
na kevalaṃ nivedanam eva triḥ bhavati | kiṃ tarhi | yac ca yāvac ca śrāddhe kriyate tat sarvaṃ trir āvartyam ity eke manyante | atra pakṣe homabhojanapiṇḍānām apy āvṛttis tasminn evāparāhṇe || 2.17.14 ||
yathā prathamam evaṃ dvitīyaṃ tṛtīyaṃ ca || 2.17.15 ||
yena prakāreṇa prathamaṃ śrāddhaṃ tathaiva dvitīyaṃ tṛtīyaṃ ca kartavyam || 2.17.15 ||
sarveṣu vṛtteṣu sarvataḥ samavadāya śeṣasya grāsavarārdhyaṃ
prāśnīyād yathoktam || 2.17.16 ||
sarveṣu śrāddheṣu triṣv api vṛtteṣu samāpteṣu sarvatas trayāṇāṃ śrāddhānāṃ ya odanaśeṣaḥ tataḥ samavadāya grāsavarārdhyaṃ prāśnīyāt | tathoktaṃ gṛhye: “uttareṇa yajuṣā śeṣasya grāsavarārdhyaṃ prāśnīyāt” iti [ĀpGṛ 21.9] | tatra prayogakramaḥ – pūrvedyur navāvarebhyo nivedanam | tadvad aparedyuḥ prātaḥ | bhojanakāle āmantraṇam | tato homādipiṇḍanidhānāntam ekaikam apavṛjya tataḥ sarvataḥ samavadāya “prāṇe niviṣṭo ‘mṛtam” [TS 2.20] iti mantreṇa prāśanam iti || 2.17.16 ||
udīcyavṛttis tv āsanagatānāṃ hasteṣūdapātrānayanam || 2.17.17 ||
prāgudañcau vibhajate haṃsaḥ kṣīrodakaṃ yathā |
viduṣāṃ śabdasiddhyarthaṃ sā naḥ pātu śarāvatī || [Kāśikā on Pāṇ 1.1.75]
iti vaiyākaraṇaḥ | tasyāḥ śarāvatyāḥ udaktīravāsina udīcyāḥ, teṣāṃ vṛttir ācāraḥ, āsaneṣūpaviṣṭānāṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ hasteṣūdapātrānayanam, udapātrād arghyapātrādaya ādāyārghadānam iti | “pitar idaṃ te arghyam,” “pitāmahedaṃ te arghyam,” “prapitāmahedaṃ te arghyam” iti mantrāḥ āśvalāyanake [ĀśGṛ 4.7.10] | yady apy udīcyā vṛttir ity uktam, tathāpi prakaraṇapāṭhasāmarthyāt sarveṣām api bhavati || 2.17.17 ||
uddhriyatām agnau ca kriyatām ity āmantrayate || 2.17.18 ||
homakāle “uddhriyatām agnau ca kriyatām” ity anena mantreṇa brāhmaṇān āmantrayate | mantre adhīṣṭhe loṭpratyayaḥ || 2.17.18 ||
kāmam uddhriyatāṃ kāmam agnau ca kriyatām ity atisṛṣṭa
uddharej juhuyāc ca || 2.17.19 ||
atha brāhmaṇāḥ “kāmam uddhriyatāṃ kāmam agnau ca kriyatām” ity atisṛjeyur anujñāpayeyuḥ | taiś cātisṛṣṭa uddharej juhuyāc ca | uddharaṇaṃ nāma brahmanārthaṃ pakvād annād anyasmin pātre pṛthakkaraṇam || 2.17.19 ||
śvabhir apapātraiś ca śrāddhasya darśanaṃ
paricakṣate || 2.17.20 ||
apapātrāḥ patitādayaḥ, pratilomāś ca | taiḥ śrāddhasya darśanaṃ paricakṣate garhante śiṣṭāḥ | ato yathā te na paśyeyuḥ tathā pariśṛtena kartavyam iti || 2.17.20 ||
śvitraḥ śapiviṣṭaḥ paratalpagāmy āyudhīyaputraḥ śūdrotpanno
brāhmaṇyām ity ete śrāddhe bhuñjānāḥ paṅktidūṣaṇā
bhavanti || 2.17.21 ||
śvitraḥ svitrī śvetakuṣṭhī | śipiviṣṭaḥ khalatiḥ, vivṛtaśepha ity anye | paratalpagāmī yaḥ paratalpaṃ gatvā akṛtaprāyaścittas tasya grahaṇam | āyudhīyaputraḥ kṣatriyavṛtttim āśrito yaḥ āyudhena jīvati brāhmaṇas tasya putraḥ | śūdrād brāhmaṇyām utpannaś caṇḍālaḥ, na tasya prasaṅgaḥ, “brāhmaṇān brahmavidaḥ” [ĀpDh 2.17.4] ity uktatvāt | tasmād evaṃ vyākhyeyam | yaḥ kramavivāhe śūdrāyāṃ putram utpādya paścād brāhmaṇyām utpādayati tasya putraḥ śūdrotpanno brāhmaṇyām iti | sa hi pitā śūdraḥ saṃpannaḥ | śrūyate hi – “taj jāyā jāyā bhavati yad asyāṃ jāyate punaḥ” iti [AitBr 7.3.13] | smaryate ca |
yad ucyate dvijātīnāṃ śūdrād dāropasaṃgrahaḥ |
na tan mama mataṃ yasmāt tatrāyaṃ jāyate svayam || iti | [YDh 1.57]
ete śvitrādayaḥ śrāddhe bhuñjānāḥ paṅktidūṣaṇā bhavanti, paṅktiṃ dūṣayanti | atas te na bhojyā
iti || 2.17.21 ||
trimadhus trisuparṇas triṇāciketaś caturmedhaḥ pañcāgnir jyeṣṭhasāmago vedādhyāyy anūcānaputraḥ śrotriya ity ete śrāddhe bhuñjānāḥ paṅktipāvanā bhavanti || 2.17.22 ||
“madhu vātā ṛtāyate” [TS 4.2.9] ity eṣa tṛcas trimadhu | tatra hi pratyṛcaṃ trayo madhuśabdāḥ | iha tu tadadhyāyī puruṣas trimadhuḥ | trisuparṇaḥ “catuṣkapardā yuvatiḥ supeśāḥ” [RV 10.114.3–6] ityādikas tṛcaḥ | anye tu taittirīyake “brahmam etu mām” [TaitUp 4.38–40] ityādayas trayo ‘nuvākā ity āhuḥ | tatra hi “trisuparṇam ayācitaṃ brāhmaṇāya dadyāt” iti hi śrūyate “ā sahasrāt paṅktiṃ punanti” iti ca | pūrvavat puruṣe vṛttiḥ | trināciketaḥ, nāciketo ‘gnir bahuṣu śākhāsu adhīyate taittirīyake kaṭhavallyādiṣu śatapathe ca, taṃ yo veda mantrabrāhmaṇena saha, sa trināciketaḥ | nāciketāgnes triś cetety anye | virajānuvākādhyāyīty anye, “prāṇāpānavyānodāna” [TaitUp 4.64] ityādi | aśvamedhaḥ puruṣamedhaḥ sarvamedhaḥ pitṛmedha iti catvāro medhāḥ | tadadhyāyī caturmedhaḥ | caturṇāṃ yajñānām āhartā ity anye | pañcāgniḥ sabhyāvasathyābhyāṃ saha pañcānāṃ kāṭhakāgnīnām adhyetā | jyeṣṭhasāma talavakārāṇāṃ prasiddham “ud u tyam,” “citram” ity etayor gītam [TS 1.4.43.1–2] | tad gāyatīti jyeṣṭhasāmagaḥ | “jyeṣṭhasāmikaḥ” iti pāṭhe vrīhyāditvāṭ ṭhan | vedādhyāyī svādhyāyaparaḥ | anūcānaputraḥ traividyaputraḥ | śrotriya ity api paṭhanti | tad ādarārthaṃ draṣṭavyam | ete śrāddhe bhuñjānāḥ paṅktiṃ śodhayanti | vedādhyāyīty asyānantaram itiśabdaṃ paṭhanti, so ‘papāṭhaḥ | etena “pañcāgni” ity avibhaktikapāṭho vyākhyātaḥ || 2.17.22 ||
na ca naktaṃ śrāddhaṃ kurvīta || 2.17.23 ||
śrāddhakarmany ārabdhe kāraṇād vilambena madhye yady ādityo ‘stam iyāt tadā śrāddhaśeṣaṃ naktaṃ na kuryāt | aparedyur divaiva kurvīta || 2.17.23 ||
ārabdhe cābhojanam ā parisamāpanāt || 2.17.24 ||
pūrvedyur nivedanaprabhṛti ā piṇḍadānān madhye kartur bhojanapratiṣedhaḥ | anantaram “anyatra rāhudarśanāt” iti paṭhanti, “na ca naktam” ity asyāpavādaḥ, rāhudarśane naktam api kurvīteti | udīcyās tv etat prāyeṇa na paṭhanti | tathā ca pūrvair na vyākhyātam | praty uta “na ca naktam” ity etat somagrahaṇaviṣayaṃ vyākhyātam | paṭhyamānaṃ tu “na ca naktam” ity asyānantaraṃ paṭhituṃ yuktam || 2.17.24 ||
iti saptadaśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
ity āpastambadharmasūtravṛttāv ujjvalāyāṃ
dvitīyapraśne saptamaḥ paṭalaḥ
vilayanaṃ mathitaṃ piṇyākaṃ madhu māṃsaṃ ca
varjayet || 2.18.1 ||
vilayanaṃ navanītamalam | yasya dadhno hastādinā mathanamātraṃ na jalena miśraṇaṃ tan mathitam | tathā ca naighaṇṭukāḥ: “takraṃ hy udaśvin mathitaṃ pādāmbv ardhāmbu nijalam” iti [Amara 2.9.53] | yantre pīḍitānāṃ tilānāṃ kalkaḥ piṇyākam | madhumāṃse prasiddhe | māṃsam apratiṣiddham api | etad vilayanādikaṃ varjayet || 2.18.1 ||
kṛṣṇadhānyaṃ śūdrānnaṃ ye cānye ‘nāśyasaṃmatāḥ || 2.18.2 ||
kṛṣṇadhānyaṃ kṛṣṇakulutthādi, na kṛṣṇavrīhayaḥ | śūdrānnaṃ pakvam apakvaṃ ca | ye cānye anāśyatvena abhojyatvena saṃmatāḥ tāṃś ca varjayet || 2.18.2 ||
ahaviṣyam anṛtaṃ krodhaṃ yena ca krodhayet || 2.18.3 ||
ahaviṣyaṃ kodravādi | anṛtaṃ mithyāvādaḥ | krodhaḥ kopaḥ | yena ca kṛtena uktena vā paraṃ krodhayet tac ca varjayet || 2.18.3 ||
smṛtim icchan yaśo medhāṃ svargaṃ puṣṭiṃ dvādaśaitāni
varjayet || 2.18.4 ||
smṛtir adhigatasya smaraṇam | yaśaḥ khyātiḥ | medhā prajñā | svargapuṣṭī prasiddhe | dvādaśaitāni vilayanādīni varjayet smṛtyādikam icchan | punaḥ “varjayet” iti guṇārtho ‘nuvādaḥ smṛtyādikaṃ phalaṃ vidhātum | “dvādaśa” iti vacanaṃ vilayanāder api grahaṇārtham, ahaviṣyādikam evānantaraṃ mā saṃgrāhīti || 2.18.4 ||
adhonābhy uparijānv ācchādya triṣavaṇam udakam upaspṛśann anagnipakvavṛttir acchāyopagaḥ sthānāsanikaḥ saṃvatsaram etad vrataṃ caret || 2.18.5 ||
“adhonābhy uparijānv ācchādya” iti vyākhyātam [cf. ĀpDh 1.24.11] | triṣavaṇaṃ triṣu savaneṣu prātar madhyandine sāyam iti udakam upaspṛśan snānaṃ kurvan anagnipakvavṛttiḥ, vṛttiḥ śarīrayātrā, sā agnau pakvena na kāryā | agnigrahaṇāt kālapakvasyāmrāder adoṣaḥ | acchāyopagaḥ chāyām anupagacchan | sthānāsanikaḥ sthānāsanavān; divā sthānaṃ rātrāv āsanam, na kadācic chayanam | etat “vilayanaṃ mathitam” ity ārabhyānantaram uktaṃ vrataṃ saṃvatsaraṃ caret | etad vratam aṣṭācatvāriṃśadvarṣasādhyena brahmacārivratena saṃmitaṃ sadṛśam | yāvat tasya phalaṃ tāvad asyāpīty ācakṣate dharmajñāḥ | na kevalaṃ smṛtyādikam eva prayojanam iti |
apara āha – “vilayanaṃ mathitam” [ĀpDh 2.18.1] ityādikaṃ vratāntaraṃ smṛtyādikāmasya | “adhonābhi” ityādikaṃ tu saṃmitavratam iti | etac ca brahmacāriṇo gṛhasthasya ca bhavati | tathā ca bodhāyanaḥ: “aṣṭācatvāriṃśatsaṃmitam ity ācakṣate tasya saṃkṣepaḥ saṃvatsaras taṃ saṃvatsaram anuvyākhyāsyāmaḥ sa yadi brahmacārī syān niyamam eva pratipadyetātha yady abrahmacārī syāt keśaśmaśrulomanakhāni vāpayitvā tīrthaṃ gatvā snātvā” ityādi || 2.18.5 ||
nityaśrāddham || 2.18.6 ||
athāhar ahaḥ kartavyaṃ śrāddhaṃ vakṣyate | tasya nityaśrāddham iti nāma || 2.18.6 ||
bahir grāmāc chucayaḥ śucau deśe saṃskurvanti || 2.18.7 ||
tan nityaśrāddhaṃ bahir grāmāt kartavyam | tasyānnasaṃskāraḥ – śucayaḥ śucau deśe annaṃ saṃskurvanti | “śucayaḥ” iti vacanam ādhikyārtham, “āryāḥ prayatāḥ” [ĀpDh 2.3.1] iti pūrvam eva prāyatyasya vihitatvāt || 2.18.7 ||
tatra navāni dravyāṇi || 2.18.8 ||
tatra nityaśrāddhe dravyāṇi navāny eva grāhyāṇi || 2.18.8 ||
kāni punas tāni |
yair annaṃ saṃskriyate yeṣu ca bhujyate || 2.18.9 ||
yair bhāṇḍair annaṃ saṃskriyate yeṣu ca kāṃsyādiṣu bhujyate tāni navānīti || 2.18.9 ||
tāni ca bhuktavadbhyo dadyāt || 2.18.10 ||
tāni bhāṇḍāni kāṃsyādīni ca bhuktavadbhyo brāhmaṇebhyo dadyāt | evaṃ pratyaham || 2.18.10 ||
samudetāṃś ca bhojayet || 2.18.11 ||
samudetavacanaṃ guṇādhikyārtham || 2.18.11 ||
na cātadguṇāyocchiṣṭaṃ prayacchet || 2.18.12 ||
bhāṇḍeṣu yad bhuktaśiṣṭaṃ tad ihocchiṣṭam | tad apy atadguṇāya bhuktavatāṃ ye guṇāḥ tadrahitāya na dadyāt | tadguṇāyaiva dadyād iti || 2.18.12 ||
evaṃ saṃvatsaram || 2.18.13 ||
evam etan nityaśrāddhaṃ saṃvatsaraṃ kartavyam ahar ahaḥ || 2.18.13 ||
teṣām uttamaṃ lohenājena kāryam || 2.18.14 ||
teṣāṃ saṃvatsarasyāhnāṃ uttamam ahaḥ samāptidinaṃ lohena lohitavarṇena ajena kāryaṃ kartavyam | dṛśyate cānyatrāpy etasminn artha lohaśabdaḥ | “lohas tūparo bhavaty apy atūparaḥ,” “kṛṣṇasāraṅgo lohasāraṅgo vā” iti | camakeṣu ca bhavati – “śyāmaṃ ca me lohaṃ ca me” iti
|| 2.18.4 ||
mānaṃ ca kārayet praticchannam || 2.18.15 ||
mānaṃ dhiṣṇyaṃ vedikā | dṛśyate hi minoter asminn arthe prayogaḥ “agreṇāgnīdhraṃ caturasrāvaṃ vimitaṃ viminvanti purastād unnataṃ paścān ninatam” iti | tasyaivāyam upasargarahitasya prayogaḥ | tan mānaṃ kārayet karmakaraiḥ | praticchannaṃ ca tad bhavati tiraskariṇyādinā | idam api grāmād bahir eva || 2.18.15 ||
tasyottarārdhe brāhmaṇān bhojayet || 2.18.16 ||
tasya mānasyottarasminn arthe brāhmaṇā bhojayitavyāḥ || 2.18.16 ||
ubhayān paśyati brāhmaṇāṃś ca bhuñjānān māne ca
pitṝn ity upadiśanti || 2.18.17 ||
asyaivaṃkṛtasya karmaṇo mahimnā ubhayān paśyati | kāṃś ca kāṃś ca | brāhmaṇāṃś ca bhuñjānān tasminn eva ca māne pitṝn | yathā brāhmaṇān bhuñjānān pratyakṣeṇa paśyati tathā māne samāgatān pitṝn api pratyakṣeṇeva paśyatīty upadiśanti dharmajñāḥ || 2.18.17 ||
kṛtākṛtam ata ūrdhvam || 2.18.18 ||
ata ūrdhvaṃ śrāddhaṃ kriyatāṃ vā, mā vā kāri | akaraṇe na pratyavāya iti || 2.18.18 ||
śrāddhena tṛptiṃ hi vedayante pitaraḥ || 2.18.19 ||
hi yasmāt antye ‘hani yat darśanam upagacchanti tac chrāddhena tṛptiṃ vedayante jñāpayanti śrāddhasya kartāram | tasmāt kṛtākṛtam iti || 2.18.19 ||
atha puṣṭikāmasya prayogaḥ “tiṣyeṇa” ityādiḥ “ucchiṣṭaṃ dadyuḥ” ityanta ekaḥ |
tiṣyeṇa puṣṭikāmaḥ || 2.18.20 ||
iti dvitīye aṣṭādaśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
gaurasarṣapāṇāṃ cūrṇāni kārayitvā taiḥ pāṇipādaṃ prakṣālya mukhaṃ karṇau prāśya ca yad vāto nātivāti tadāsano ‘jinaṃ bastasya prathamaḥ kalpo vāgyato dakṣiṇāmukho bhuñjīta
|| 2.19.1 ||
puṣṭikāmaḥ puruṣo vakṣyamāṇaṃ prayogaṃ kuryāt tiṣyeṇa tiṣyanakṣatreṇa | “nakṣatre ca lupi” [Pāṇ 2.3.45] ity adhikaraṇe tṛtīyā | tiṣye nakṣatre gaurasarṣapāṇāṃ saṃbandhīti cūrṇāni karmakaraiḥ kārayet | kārayitvā taiś cūṇaiḥ pāṇī pādau mukhaṃ karṇau ca prakṣālayet | prakṣālya cūrṇaśeṣaṃ prāśnīyāt | “prāsya” iti pāṭhe, prāsyet vikiret | etāvat pratitiṣyaṃ viśeṣakṛtyam | paraṃ tu pratyahaṃ kartavyam | prāśya ca yadāsanaṃ vāto nātivāti adho nātītya vāti tadāsanaḥ tādṛśāsanaḥ bhuñjīteti vakṣyamāṇena saṃbandhaḥ | tatra bastasyājinam āsanaṃ syād iti mukhyaḥ kalpaḥ | vāgyato dakṣiṇāṃ diśam abhimukho bhuñjīta || 2.18.20–2.19.1 ||
anāyuṣyaṃ tv evaṃmukhasya bhojanaṃ mātur iti
upadiśanti || 2.19.2 ||
yat evaṃmukhasya dakṣiṇāmukhasya bhojanaṃ tadbhoktuḥ yā mātā tasyā anāyuṣyam anāyuṣkaram iti dharmajñā upadiśanti | tasmān mātṛmatā naitad vrataṃ kāryam iti || 2.19.2 ||
audumbaraś camasaḥ suvarṇanābhaḥ praśāstaḥ || 2.19.3 ||
camu bhakṣaṇe, yatra camyate sa camaso bhojanapātram | audumbaras tāmramayaḥ | suvarṇanābhaḥ suvarṇena madhye alaṃkṛtaḥ sa praśāstaḥ praśasto bhojane || 2.19.3 ||
na cānyenāpi bhoktavyam || 2.19.4 ||
na cānyena kartuḥ pitrāpi tatra pātre bhoktavyam | apir dhātvarthānuvādī | “bhoktavyaḥ” iti pulliṅgapāṭhe ‘py eṣa evārthaḥ || 2.19.4 ||
yāvad grāsaṃ saṃnayann askandayan nāpajihītāpajihīta vā kṛtsnaṃ grāsaṃ grasīta sahāṅguṣṭham || 2.19.5 ||
yāvad grāsaṃ yāvad eva grasituṃ śakyaṃ tāvad eva saṃnayan piṇḍīkurvan askandayan bhūmāv annalepān ayātayan kṛtsnaṃ grāsaṃ grasītety anvayaḥ | sahāṅguṣṭham āsye grāsapraveśe yathāṅguṣṭho ‘py anupraviśati tathā sarvān eva grāsān uktena prakāreṇa grasīta | grasato madhye kriyāntaravidhiḥ – nāpajihīta bhojanapātraṃ savyena pāṇinā na vimuñcet, apajihīta vā muñced vā | kim artham idaṃ yāvatā na prakārāntaraṃ saṃbhavati | satyam | “prakramāt tu niyamyeta” [PMS 6.2.13] iti nyāyena ya eva prakāraḥ prathamabhojane sa evāntād anuṣṭheya ity evamartham idam || 2.19.5 ||
na ca mukhaśabdaṃ kuryāt || 2.19.6 ||
bhojanadaśāyām idam | evam uttaram || 2.19.6 ||
pāṇiṃ ca nāvadhūnuyāt || 2.19.7 ||
pāṇir atra dakṣiṇaḥ || 2.19.7 ||
ācamya cordhvau pāṇī dhārayed ā prodakībhāvāt || 2.19.8 ||
bhuktvācamya pāṇī ūrdhvaṃ dhārayet yāvat pragatodakau śuṣkau bhavataḥ || 2.19.8 ||
tato ‘gnim upaspṛśet || 2.19.9 ||
bhuktvā niyamenāgnir upaspraṣṭavyaḥ || 2.19.9 ||
divā ca na bhuñjītānyan mūlaphalebhyaḥ || 2.19.10 ||
mūlāni kandāḥ, phalāny āmrādīni, tebhyo ‘nyad divā na bhuñjīta | tadbhakṣaṇe na doṣaḥ
|| 2.19.10 ||
sthālīpākānudeśyāni ca varjayet || 2.19.11 ||
“tena sarpiṣmatā brāhmaṇaṃ bhojayet” [ĀpDh 2.20.3] ityādau brāhmaṇo bhūtvā na bhuñjīta | anudeśyāni pitṛbhyo devatābhyaś ca saṃkalpitāni, tāni na bhuñjīta || 2.19.11 ||
sottarācchādanaś caiva yajñopavīti bhuñjīta || 2.19.12 ||
uttarācchādanam uparivāsaḥ tena yajñopavītaṃ kṛtvā bhuñjīta | nāsya bhojane “api vā sūtram evopavītārthe” [ĀpDh 2.4.22] ity ayaṃ kalpo bhavatīty eke | samuccaya ity anye || 2.19.12 ||
naiyamikaṃ tu śrāddhaṃ snehavad eva dadyāt || 2.19.13 ||
yan niyamena kartavyaṃ māsiśrāddhaṃ tat snehadravyayuktam eva dadyāt na śuṣkam
|| 2.19.13 ||
tatra viśeṣaḥ |
sarpir māṃsam iti prathamaḥ kalpaḥ || 2.19.14 ||
spaṣṭam || 2.19.14 ||
abhāve tailaṃ śākam iti || 2.19.15 ||
sarpiṣo ‘bhāve tailam, māṃsābhāve śākam | itiśabdāt, yac cānyad evaṃ yuktam || 2.19.15 ||
maghāsu cādhikaṃ śrāddhakalpena sarpir brāhmaṇān
bhojayet || 2.19.16 ||
maghāsu ca pūrvapakṣe ‘pi śrāddhavidhānena sarpirmiśram annaṃ brāhmaṇān bhojayet
|| 2.19.16 ||
ity ekonaviṃśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
māsiśrāddhe tilānāṃ droṇaṃ droṇaṃ yenopāyena śaknuyāt
tenopayojayet || 2.20.1 ||
yenopāyena upayojituṃ śaknuyāt abhyaṅge udvartane bhakṣye bhojye ceti tenopāyena māsiśrāddhe tilānāṃ droṇaṃ droṇaṃ upayojayet | tatraikaikasya brāhmaṇasya droṇaṃ droṇam upayojayitum aśakyatvāt samuditānām upayojayet | “droṇaṃ droṇam” iti vīpsāvacanaṃ tu pratimāsi śrāddham upayojanārtham iti kecit | anye tu evaṃbhūtāḥ prabalāḥ prayatnenānviṣya bhojayitavyā iti || 2.20.1 ||
samudetāṃś ca bhojayen na cātadguṇāyocchiṣṭaṃ
dadyuḥ || 2.20.2 ||
vyākhyātam idam | “dadyuḥ” iti bahuvacanaṃ tathāvidhakartṛbahutvāpekṣam | vacanavyatyayo vā || 2.20.2 ||
atha puṣṭikāmasyāparaḥ prayogaḥ ā paṭalaparisamāpteḥ |
udagayana āpūryamāṇapakṣasyaikarātram avarārdhyam upoṣya tiṣyeṇa puṣṭikāmaḥ sthālīpākaṃ śrapayitvā mahārājam iṣṭvā tena sarpiṣmatā brāhmaṇaṃ bhojayitvā puṣṭyarthena siddhiṃ vācayīta || 2.20.3 ||
puṣṭikāmaḥ puruṣaḥ ekarātram avaram upavāsaṃ kṛtvā udagayane āpūryamāṇapakṣasya pūrvapakṣasya saṃbandhinā tiṣyeṇa tasmin nakṣatre sthālīpākaṃ śrapayitvā mahārājaṃ vaiśravaṇaṃ yajeta | ājyabhāgānte “mahārājāya svāhā” iti pradhānahomaḥ, sviṣṭakṛdādi, jayādayaḥ | pariṣecanānte tena sarpiṣmatā sthālīpākena brāhmaṇaṃ bhojayet | uttaravivakṣayedaṃ vacanam | bhojayitvā siddhiṃ vācayīta puṣṭir astv iti || 2.20.3 ||
evam ahar ahar ā parasmāt tiṣyāt || 2.20.4 ||
evam idaṃ sthālīpākaśrapaṇādi siddhivācanāntam ahar ahaḥ kartavyam ā parasmāt tiṣyāt yāvad aparas tiṣya āgacchati || 2.20.4 ||
dvau dvitīye || 2.20.5 ||
dvitīye tiṣye prāpte dvau bhojayet | anyat samānam | evam ā tṛtīyāt || 2.20.5 ||
trīṃś tṛtīye || 2.20.6 ||
tṛtīye tu tiṣye trīn bhojayet, ā caturthāt || 2.20.6 ||
evaṃ saṃvatsaram abhyuccayena || 2.20.7 ||
evam etat karma yāvat saṃvatsaraḥ paripūryate tāvat kartavyam | brāhmaṇabhojanaṃ cābhyuccayena bhavati | caturthaprabhṛti catvāraḥ, pañcamaprabhṛti pañceti, evamādi || 2.20.7 ||
evaṃ kṛte phalam āha |
mahāntaṃ poṣaṃ puṣyati || 2.20.8 ||
mahatyā puṣṭyā yukto bhavati || 2.20.8 ||
ādita evopavāsaḥ || 2.20.9 ||
upavāsas tv ādita eva puṣye bhavati, na pratitiṣyam || 2.20.9 ||
āttatejasāṃ bhojanaṃ varjayet || 2.20.10 ||
āttatejāṃsi takravājinādīni | tāni nopabhuñjīta || 2.20.10 ||
bhasmatuṣādhiṣṭhānam || 2.20.11 ||
varjayed ity eva | bhasmatuṣāṃś ca nādhitiṣṭhet nākrāmet || 2.20.11 ||
padā pādasya prakṣālanam adhiṣṭhānaṃ ca varjayet || 2.20.12 ||
ekena pādena pādāntarasya prakṣālanam adhiṣṭhānaṃ ca varjayen na kuryāt || 2.20.12 ||
preṅkholanaṃ ca pādayoḥ || 2.20.13 ||
preṅkholanam āndolanaṃ itas tataś cālanam || 2.20.13 ||
jānuni cāntyādhānaṃ jaṅghāyāḥ || 2.20.14 ||
ekasmin jānuni itarasyā jaṅghāyā antyādhānam avasthānaṃ varjayet || 2.20.14 ||
nakhaiś ca nakhavādanam || 2.20.15 ||
spaṣṭam || 2.20.15 ||
sphoṭanāni cākāraṇāt || 2.20.16 ||
parvasaṃdhīnāṃ sphoṭanāni ca varjayet akāraṇāt | kāraṇaṃ śramavātādi | “vādanasphoṭanāni” iti samāsapāṭhe ‘py eṣa evārthaḥ || 2.20.16 ||
yac cānyat paricakṣate || 2.20.17 ||
yac cānyad uktavyatiriktaṃ tṛṇacchedanādi śiṣṭāḥ praricakṣate garhante tad api varjayet
|| 2.20.17 ||
yoktā ca dharmayukteṣu dravyaparigraheṣu ca || 2.20.18 ||
ekaś caśabdo ‘narthakaḥ | kecic ca naiva paṭhanti | dharmāviruddhā ye dravyaparigrahāḥ teṣu ca yoktā utpādayitā syāt, na nirīhaḥ syāt || 2.20.18 ||
pratipādayitā ca tīrthe || 2.20.19 ||
tīrthaṃ guṇavatpātraṃ yajño vā, tatra dravyasyārjitasya pratipādayitā syāt || 2.20.19 ||
yantā cātīrthe yato na bhayaṃ syāt || 2.20.20 ||
yantā niyantā adātā, atīrthe apradātā ca syāt, yataḥ puruṣād apradāne ‘pi bhayaṃ na syāt | bhayasaṃbhave tu piśunādibhyo ‘pi deyam || 2.20 20 ||
saṃgrahītā ca manuṣyān || 2.20.21 ||
arthapradānapriyavacanānusaraṇādibhiḥ manuṣyāṇāṃ saṃgrahaṇaśīlaḥ syāt || 2.20.21 ||
bhoktā ca dharmāvipratiṣiddhān bhogān || 2.20.22 ||
dharmāviruddhāḥ ye bhogāḥ srakcandanasvabhāryāsevanādayaḥ, teṣāṃ ca bhogaśīlaḥ syāt
|| 2.20.22 ||
evam ubhau lokāv abhijayati || 2.20.23 ||
evaṃ mahatyā puṣṭyā yuktaḥ uktaprakāram anutiṣṭhan ubhau lokāv abhijayati, bhogenemaṃ lokam, tīrthe pratipādanena cāmuṃ lokam iti || 2.20.23 ||
iti viṃśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
ity āpastambadharmasūtravṛttau ujjvalāyāṃ
dvitīyapraśne aṣṭamaḥ paṭalaḥ
“sarvāśramāṇāṃ samayapadāni” [ĀpDh 1.23.6] ity uktaṃ purastāt | ke punas ta āśramā iti |
āha |
catvāra āśramā gārhasthyam ācāryakulaṃ maunaṃ
vānaprasthyam iti || 2.21.1 ||
āśramyante yeṣu śreyo’rthinaḥ puruṣāḥ ity āśramāḥ | eṣā sāmānyasaṃjñā, uttarā viśeṣasaṃjñā | gṛhe tiṣṭhati kuṭumbarakṣaṇapara iti gṛhasthaḥ, tasya bhāvo gārhasthyam, sa eka āśramaḥ | ācāryakulam ācāryakule vāsaḥ lakṣaṇayā, so ‘py ekaḥ | manu avabodhane, manuta iti muniḥ jñānaparaḥ, tasya bhāvo maunam, so ‘paraḥ | vanaṃ pratiṣṭhata iti vanaprasthaḥ, sa eva vānaprasthaḥ, prajñāditvād aṇ, tasya bhāvo vānaprasthyam | itiśabdaḥ parisamāptyarthaḥ | etāvanta evāśramā iti | caturṇām evopadeśe ‘pi “catvāra āśramāḥ” iti vacanaṃ “aikāśramyaṃ tv ācāryāḥ pratyakṣavidhānād gārhasthyasya” [GDh 3.36] iti smṛtyantaroktaṃ mā grāhīti || 2.21.1 ||
teṣu sarveṣu yathopadeśam avyagro vartamānaḥ kṣemaṃ
gacchati || 2.21.2 ||
teṣv āśrameṣu caturṣv api yathāśāstram avyagraḥ samāhitamanā bhūtvā yo vartate sa kṣemam abhayaṃ padaṃ gacchati | anenāśramavikalpa ukto veditavyaḥ | niḥśreyasārthinā anyatamasminn āśrame yathāśāstram avahitena vartitavyam iti | tathā ca gautamaḥ; “tasyāśramavikalpam eke bruvate” iti [GDh 3.1] || 2.21.2 ||
sarveṣām upanayanaprabhṛti samāna ācāryakule vāsaḥ || 2.21.3 ||
upanayanaprabhṛti ācāryakule vāsaḥ aṣṭācatvāriṃśadvarṣādīnām anyatamaḥ sarveṣām āśramāṇāṃ samānaḥ || 2.21.3 ||
sarveṣām anūtsargo vidyāyāḥ || 2.21.4 ||
anūtsargo, chāndaso dīrghaḥ | vidyāyā anusargo ‘pi sarveṣām āśramāṇāṃ samānaḥ | tasmād ācāryakule vāsaḥ samāna iti || 2.21.4 ||
buddhvā karmāṇi yat kāmayeta tad ārabheta || 2.21.5 ||
pratyāśramaṃ yāni karmāṇi vihitāni tāni buddhvā gṛhasthasyaitāni kartavyāni, eṣām ananuṣṭhāne pratyavāyaḥ, phalaṃ cedam, eṣām etāni śakyāny anuṣṭhātum, naitāni ity ācāryād upaśrutya yat karma phalaṃ vā kāmayeta tad ārabheta, tam āśramaṃ pratipadyeteti || 2.21.5 ||
tatra gārhasthyasya pūrvam eva prapañcitatvād adhyayanānantaraṃ pratipitsitasyācāryakulasya svarūpam āha |
yathā vidyārthasya niyama etenaivāntam anūpasīdata ācāryakule
śarīranyāso brahmacāriṇaḥ || 2.21.6 ||
yathā vidyārthasyopakurvāṇasya brahmacāriṇaḥ “atha brahmacaryavidhiḥ” [ĀpDh 1.2.18] ity ārabhya agnīndhanādiniyama uktaḥ, anenaiva niyamenāntam ā śarīrapātād anūpasīdataḥ upasadanaṃ kurvataḥ ācāryakule śarīranyāsaḥ śarīraparityāgo bhavati brahmacāriṇo naiṣṭhikasya | tatraivā maraṇāt tiṣṭhet, nāśramāntaraṃ gacchet, yadi tam evāśramam ātmanaḥ kṣemaṃ manyeteti | atra manuḥ |
ācārye tu khalu prete guruputre guṇānvite |
gurudāre sapiṇḍe vā guruvad vṛttim ācaret ||
eṣu tv avidyamāneṣu sthānāsanavihāravān |
prayuñjāno ‘gniśuśrūṣāṃ sādhayed deham ātmahaḥ ||
evaṃ carati yo vipro brahmacaryam aviplutaḥ |
sa gacchaty uttamaṃ sthānaṃ na cehājāyate punaḥ || iti | [MDh 2.247–49]
|| 2.21.6 ||
atha parivrājaḥ || 2.21.7 ||
atha anantaraṃ parivrājo dharma ucyate | dṛṣṭādṛṣṭārthān sarvān evārambhān parityajya ātmalābhāya saṃnyāsāśramaṃ parivrajyatīti parivrāṭ saṃnyāsī || 2.21.7 ||
ata eva brahmacaryavān pravrajati || 2.21.8 ||
ata eva brahmacaryāśramāt brahmacaryavān aviplutabrahmacaryaḥ pravrajati pravrajyāṃ kuryāt yadi tadaiva pakvakaṣāyo bhavati | śrūyate ca – “brahmacaryād eva pravrajed gṛhād vā vanād vā” iti, “yad ahar eva virajet tad ahar eva pravrajet” iti ca [JābUp 4] | “ata eva” iti vacanāt gṛhāśramaṃ praviṣṭasya tatparityāgenāśramāntaraprāptiḥ ācāryasyānabhimataiveti lakṣyate | tatrāyam abhiprāyaḥ – dāraparigrahe sati “yāvajjīvam agnihotraṃ juhuyāt” iti śrutyā virudhyate, sa kathaṃ pravrajed iti | tasmāt saty api vairāgye kāmyakarmaṇaḥ parityāgena nityāni naimittikāni ca karmāṇi kurvan pratiṣiddhāni ca varjayan gṛhastha eva mucyata iti | yathāha yājñavalkyaḥ |
nyāyārjitadhanas tattvajñānaniṣṭho ‘tithipriyaḥ|
śrāddhakṛt satyavādī ca gṛhastho ‘pi vimucyate || iti | [YDh 3.205]
atha yo ‘nāhitāgniḥ tasya viraktasya munyāśramapraveśe ko virodhaḥ | ṛṇaśrutivirodhaḥ | “jāyamāno vai brāḥmaṇas tribhir ṛṇavā jāyate, brahmacaryeṇa ṛiṣibhyo yajñena devebhyaḥ prajayā pitṛbhyaḥ” iti [TS 6.3.10] | manur api |
ṛṇāni trīṇy apākṛtya mano mokṣe niveśayet |
anapākṛtya mokṣaṃ tu sevamāno vrajaty adhaḥ || iti || [MDh 6.35]
mokṣo mokṣāśramaḥ | nanv evaṃ brahmacaryād api pravrajyā nopapadyate | atha tatra “yad ahar eva virajet” [JābUp 4] iti śrutyā yuktaṃ pravrajitum | viraktasya gṛhasthasyāpi yuktaṃ bhaviṣyati, smaryate ca:
prājāpatyāṃ nirupyeṣṭiṃ sarvavedasadakṣiṇām |
ātmany agnīn samāropya brāhmaṇaḥ pravrajed gṛhāt || iti || [MD 6.38]
tathā yo gṛhastho vṛddho mṛtabhāryaḥ punar dārakriyāyām asamarthaḥ tasyāpi yujyate pravrajyā | tasmāt “yad ahar eva virajet” [JābUp 4] ity eṣa eva kālaḥ pravrajyāyāḥ, sarvam anyad aviraktasyeti yuktam | evakāras tu sūtre śrutyanusāreṇa prayuktaḥ, yathā – “gṛhād vā vanād vā” iti bruvāṇaiva śrutiḥ “brahmacaryād eva pravrajet” ity āha tatheti || 2.21.8 ||
tasyopadiśanti || 2.21.9 ||
tasya parivrājaḥ kartavyam upadiśanti dharmajñāḥ || 2.21.9 ||
anagnir aniketaḥ syād aśarmāśaraṇo muniḥ | svādhyāya evotsṛjamāno vācaṃ grāme prāṇavṛttiṃ pratilabyāniho ‘namutraś caret || 2.21.10 ||
brahmacāriṇaḥ samidādhānam agnikāryam, gṛhasthasyaupāsanam agnihotrahomādi, vānaprasthasya “śrāmaṇakenāgnim ādhāya” [GDh 3.27] iti vihitenāgnau homādi | tasya tu naivaṃvidhaṃ kiṃcid agnikāryam astīty anagniḥ | niketo nivāsasthānaṃ svabhūtam, tadabhāvād aniketaḥ | śarma sukhaṃ tasya nāstīy aśarmā | kiṃcid api śaraṇatvenāpratipannaḥ, na vā kasyacic charaṇabhūta ity aśaraṇaḥ | svādhyāyaḥ praṇavādipavitrāṇāṃ japaḥ | atra bodhāyanaḥ: “vṛkṣamūlo vedanyāsī veda vṛkṣas tasya mūlaṃ praṇavaḥ praṇavātmako vedaḥ praṇavo brahmabhūyāya kalpata iti hovāca prajāpatiḥ” [BDh 2.18.24–26] iti | tatraiva vācaṃ visṛjet | anyatra maunavrataḥ syāt | yāvatā prāṇā dhriyante sā prāṇavṛttiḥ tāvatīṃ bhikṣāṃ pratilabhya | etāvān asya grāme praveśaḥ, anyadā bahir vāsaḥ | ihārthāḥ kṛṣyādayaḥ paralokārthāś ca japahomādayaḥ yasya na santi so ‘niho ‘namutra ity uktaḥ | evaṃbhūtaś caret | naikasmin grāme dvyahamātram vaset | atra gautamaḥ: “na dvitīyām apartu rātriṃ grāme vaset” [GDh 3.21] iti, “varṣāsu dhruvaśīlaḥ”[GDh 3.13] iti || 2.21.10 ||
tasya muktam ācchādanaṃ vihitam || 2.21.11 ||
yat parair muktaṃ parityaktam ayogyatayā tat tasya vihitam ācchādanam, tad asāv ācchādayet | “nirṇijya” [GDh 3.19] iti gautamaḥ || 2.21.11 ||
sarvataḥ parimokṣam eke || 2.21.12 ||
sarvair eva vāsobhiḥ parimokṣam eke upadiśanti na kiṃcid api vāso bibhṛyāt, nagna eva cared iti | apara āha – sarvato vidhito niṣadhataś ca asya parimokṣam eke bruvate, na kiṃcid asya kṛtyam, na kiṃcid asya varjyam iti || 2.21.12 ||
etad evodāharaṇaiḥ prapañcayati |
satyānṛte sukhaduḥkhe vedān imaṃ lokam amuṃ ca
parityajyātmānam anvicchet || 2.21.13 ||
satyaṃ vaktavyam iti yo niyamaḥ taṃ parityajya, tathā “tatra vaktavyam anṛtaṃ tad dhi satyād viśiṣyate” [MDh 8.104] ityādike viṣaye anṛtaṃ vaktavyam iti yo niyamas taṃ ca parityajya, sukhaṃ mṛṣṭabhojanādijanyam, duḥkhaṃ śītavātādijanyam, vedān svādhyāyādhyayanam, imaṃ lokaṃ aihalaukikaṃ kāmyaṃ karma, amuṃ ca pāralaukikaṃ ca kāmyaṃ sarvam etat parityajya ātmānam adhyātmapaṭalokaṃ anvicchet upāsīteti || 2.21.13 ||
tad evaṃ jñānabalāvalambanena hatavidhiniṣedhā ye svairaṃ pravartante siddhānāṃ teṣāṃ matam upanyastam | athaiṣām evaṃ svairacāriṇāṃ kiṃ trāṇam | tatrāha |
buddhe kṣeprāpaṇam || 2.21.14 ||
ātmani buddhe avagate sati tad eva jñānaṃ sarvam aśubhaṃ prakṣālya kṣemaṃ prāpayati | śrūyate hi – “na vardhate karmaṇā no kanīyān tasyaiva syāt padavittaṃ viditvā na lipyate karmaṇā pāpakena,” [BṛĀUp 6.4.23] “yatheṣīkātūlam agnau protaṃ pradūyeta evaṃ hāsya sarve pāpmānaḥ pradūyante” [ChUp 5.24.3] iti | smaryate ca |
yathaidhāṃsi samiddho ‘gnir bhasmasāt kurute ‘rjuna |
jñānāgniḥ sarvakarmāṇi bhasmasāt kurute tathā || iti | [BhG 5.24.3]
|| 2.21.14 ||
tad idaṃ nirākaroti |
tac chāstrair vipratiṣiddham || 2.21.15 ||
yāni yater eva kartavyakarmapratipādakāni śāstrāṇi tair eva tad vipratiṣiddham | tatra manuḥ |
krudhyantaṃ na pratikrudhyed ākruṣṭaḥ kuśalaṃ vadet |
saptadvārāvakīrṇāṃ ca na vācam anṛtāṃ vadet ||
na cotpātanimittābhyāṃ na nakṣatrāṅgavidyayā |
nānuśāsanavādābhyāṃ bhikṣāṃ lipseta karhicit || iti | [MDh 6.48, 50]
ato yatim eva prakṛtya yāni karmāṇi vihitāni tāni kartavyāni || 2.21.15 ||
“buddhe kṣemaprāpaṇam” [ĀpDh 2.21.14] ity etac ca pratyakṣaviruddham ity āha |
buddhe cet kṣemaprāpaṇam ihaiva na duḥkham
upalabheta || 2.21.16 ||
ātmabodhamātreṇa cet kṣemaṃ prāpyate ihaiva śarīre duḥkhaṃ nopalabheta jñānī | na caitad asti | na ca jñānināṃ mūrdhābhiṣiktam anyo ‘pi kṣudhāduḥkham eva tāvat kṣaṇamātraṃ soḍhuṃ prabhavati || 2.21.16 ||
etena paraṃ vyākhyātam || 2.21.17 ||
paralokabhavam api duḥkham etenaiva vyākhyātam, na svairacāriṇāṃ nivartata iti | tasmāt svakarmabhiḥ paripakvakaṣāyaḥ śravaṇamanananididhyāsanaiḥ sākṣāt kṛtātmasvarūpaḥ pratiṣiddheṣu kaṭākṣam apy anikṣipan aṣṭāṅgayoganirato mucyata iti | atra bodhāyanaḥ: “ekadaṇḍī tridaṇḍī vā” iti [BDh 2.18.1] | gautamo ‘pi: “muṇḍaḥ śikhī vā” iti [GDh 3.22]
|| 2.21.17 ||
atha vānaprasthaḥ || 2.21.18 ||
anantaraṃ vānaprasthāśrama ucyate || 2.21.18 ||
ata eva brahmacaryavān pravrajati || 2.21.19 ||
pravrajati prakarṣeṇa vrajati apunaḥpraveśāya vanaṃ pratiṣṭhata iti | tathā ca gautamaḥ: “grāmaṃ ca na praviśet” iti [GDh 3.33] | gatam anyat | uttaraṃ ca || 2.21.19 ||
tasyopadiśanti || 2.21.20 ||
ekāgnir aniketaḥ syād aśarmāśaraṇo muniḥ | svādhyāya
evotsṛjamāno vācam || 2.21.21 ||
kaḥ punar asyaikāgniḥ | na tāvad aupāsanam, brahmacāritvāt | tasmāl laukikāgnau yathāpūrvaṃ sāyaṃ prātaḥ samidha ādadhyād ity artho vivakṣitaḥ | apara āha – “śrāmaṇakenāgnim ādhāya” [GDh 3.27] iti gāutamaḥ | śrāmaṇakaṃ nāma vaikhānasasūtram, taduktena prakāreṇa eko ‘gnir ādheyaḥ, tasmin sāyaṃ prātaḥ agnikāryam iti | anyad gatam || 2.21.21 ||
ity ekaviṃśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
tasyāraṇyam ācchādanaṃ vihitam || 2.22.1 ||
araṇye bhavam āraṇyam ajinavalkalādi || 2.22.1 ||
tato mūlaiḥ phalaiḥ parṇais tṛṇair iti vartayaṃś
caret || 2.22.2 ||
tato mūlādibhir vartayan vṛttiṃ prāṇayātrāṃ kurvan caret caraṇaśīlaḥ syāt || 2.22.2 ||
antataḥ pravṛttāni || 2.22.3 ||
mūlādibhiḥ svayaṃgṛhītaiḥ kaṃcit kālaṃ vartayitvā antataḥ ante pravṛttāni svayam eva patitāni | “abhiniśrayet” iti vakṣyamāṇena saṃbandhaḥ | tāny abhiniśritya taiḥ vartayed iti || 2.22.3 ||
tato ‘po vāyum ākāśam ity abhiniśrayet || 2.22.4 ||
tataḥ kaṃcit kālam abbhakṣaḥ, tato vāyubhakṣaḥ, tataḥ ākāśam abhiniśrayet na kiṃcid bhakṣayet | abhiniśrayaṇaṃ sevanam || 2.22.4 ||
teṣām uttara uttaraḥ saṃyogaḥ phalato viśiṣṭaḥ || 2.22.5 ||
saṃyujyate saṃśrīyata iti saṃyogaḥ | teṣāṃ mūlādīnāṃ madhye uttaram uttaraṃ dravyaṃ saṃśrīyamāṇaṃ phalato viśiṣṭam iti || 2.22.5 ||
atha vānaprasthasyaivānupūrvyam eka upadiśanti || 2.22.6 ||
atheti pakṣāntaropanyāse | pūrvaṃ brahmacaryād eva vanapraveśa uktaḥ | eke tv ācāryāḥ tasyaiva vānaprathasyānupūrvyaṃ karmāṇy upadiśanti || 2.22.6 ||
katham |
vidyāṃ samāpya dāraṃ kṛtvāgnīn ādhāya karmāṇy ārabhate
somāvarārdhyāni yāni śrūyante || 2.22.7 ||
brahmacarye sthito vidyāṃ samāpya gṛhasthaś ca bhūtvā agnīn ādhāya karmāṇi kuryāt | kāni | somāvarārdhyāni, avarārdhaṃ paścārdham, tatra bhavo ‘varārdhyaḥ, somo ‘varārdhyo yeṣāṃ tāni somāvarārdhyāni somāntāni haviryajñākhyāni, cāturmāsyādīn haviryajñān somaṃ cety arthaḥ | yāni śrūyante yāni śrutau vihitāni || 2.22.7 ||
gṛhān kṛtvā sadāraḥ saprajaḥ sahāgnibhir bahir grāmād
vaset || 2.22.8 ||
atha grāmād bahir araṇye gṛhān kṛtvā sakuṭumbaḥ sahaivāgnibhiḥ grāmād bahir vaset | asmin pakṣe prāg uktaṃ “ekāgniḥ” [ĀpDh 2.21.21] ity etan nāsti || 2.22.8 ||
eko vā || 2.22.9 ||
atha vā putreṣu bhāyāṃ nikṣipya svayam eka eva vaset vane | asmin pakṣe “prājāpatyāṃ nirupyeṣṭim” [MDh 6.38] iti parivrāja uktena nyāyena śrautāgnīn ātmani samāropya śrāmaṇekenāgnim ādhāyaikāgnir bhavet || 2.22.9 ||
śiloñchena vartayet || 2.22.10 ||
vyākyātaḥ śiloñchaḥ [ĀpDh 2.10.4] | tena vartayet prāṇayātrāṃ kuryāt | idaṃ sakuṭumbasyaikākinaś ca sādhāraṇam | ekākina evety anye || 2.22.10 ||
na cāta ūrdhvaṃ pratigṛhṇīyāt || 2.22.11 ||
yadā śiloñchavṛttir jātā ata ūrdhvaṃ na kutaścid api pratigṛhṇīyāt || 2.22.11 ||
abhiṣiktaś ca juhuyāt || 2.22.12 ||
yadā juhuyāt tadā abhiṣiktaḥ snāto juhuyāt | anuvādo ‘yaṃ snānaviseṣam vidhātum || 2.22.12 ||
śanair apo ‘bhyaveyād abhighnann abhimukham ādityam
udakam upaspṛśet || 2.22.13 ||
śanair avegena jalāśayaṃ praviśet | praviśya cābhighnan hastena udakaṃ tāḍayan udakam upaspṛśet snāyāt || 2.22.13 ||
iti sarvatrodakopasparśanavidhiḥ || 2.22.14 ||
sarvavarṇāśramasādhāraṇam etat | tathā cottaratra tasya grahaṇam || 2.22.14 ||
tasya dvandvaṃ dravyāṇām eka upadiśanti
pākārthabhojanārthavāsīparaśudātrakājānām || 2.22.15 ||
yāni pākārthāni tāmrabhāṇḍādīni yāni ca bhojanārthāni kāṃsyādīni teṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ vāsyādīnāṃ caturṇām ekaikasyāṃ vidhāyāṃ dve dve dravye utpādye ity eke upadiśanti | kājam api vāsyādivad upakaraṇaviśeṣo dārumayaḥ || 2.22.15 ||
dvandvānām ekaikam ādāyetarāṇi datvāraṇyam
avatiṣṭheta || 2.22.16 ||
teṣāṃ pākādisādhanānāṃ dravyāṇām ekaikaṃ dravyaṃ svayam ādāya itarāṇi bhāryāyai datvā araṇyam avatiṣtheta upatiṣṭhet samāśrayed iti || 2.22.16 ||
tasyāraṇyenaivāta ūrdhvaṃ homo vṛttiḥ pratīkṣācchādanaṃ
ca || 2.22.17 ||
tasya vānaprasthasyāto ‘raṇyapraveśād ūrdhvam āraṇyenaiva nīvārādinā homaḥ | vṛttiḥ prāṇayātrā | pratīkṣā atithipūjā | ācchādanaṃ valkalādinā || 2.22.17 ||
yeṣu karmasu puroḍāśāś caravas teṣu kāryāḥ || 2.22.18 ||
yeṣu darśapūrṇamāsādiṣu puroḍāśā vihitā gṛhasthasya, teṣu asya tatsthāne caravaḥ kāryāḥ
|| 2.22.18 ||
sarvaṃ copāṃśu saha svādhyāyena || 2.22.19 ||
sarvaṃ ca karmakāṇḍaṃ sāṅgaṃ pradhānam upāṃśu bhavati pārāyaṇabrahmayajñādhyayanena saha, tad apy upāṃśu kartavyam iti || 2.22.19 ||
nāraṇyam abhyāśrāvayet || 2.22.20 ||
upāṃśuvacanād eva siddhe vacanam ābhimukhyapratiṣedhārtham | tenāraṇyasthāḥ yathā nābhimukhyena śṛṇuyuḥ tāvad upāṃśv iti || 2.22.20 ||
agnyarthaṃ śaraṇam || 2.22.21 ||
śaraṇaṃ gṛhaṃ tad agnyartham eva kartavyam || 2.22.21 ||
ākāśe svayam || 2.22.22 ||
svayaṃ cākāśa eva vaset || 2.22.22 ||
anupastīrṇe śayyāsane || 2.22.23 ||
śayanaṃ cāsanaṃ ca anupastīrṇe deśe kuryāt, na tu kiṃcid upastīrya || 2.22.23 ||
nave sasye prāpte purāṇam anujānīyāt || 2.22.24 ||
nave dhānye śyāmākanīvārādau prāpte jāte purāṇaṃ pūrvasaṃcitaṃ sasyam anujānīyāt parityajet | atra manuḥ |
tyajed āśvayuje māsi munyannaṃ pūrvasaṃcitam |
jīrṇāni caiva vāsāṃsi puṣpamūlaphalāni ca || iti | [MDh 6.15]
|| 2.22.24 ||
iti dvāviṃśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
bhūyāṃsaṃ vā niyamam icchann anvaham eva pātreṇa sāyaṃ
prātar artham āharet || 2.23.1 ||
idam ekākino vānaprasthasya | bhūyāṃsaṃ vā niyamam icchan na sasyaṃ saṃcinuyāt | kiṃ tarhi | anvaham eva pratyaham eva pātreṇa yena kenacit sāyaṃ prātaś cārtham aśanīyam āharet vanasthebhyaḥ || 2.23.1 ||
evaṃ kiyantaṃ kālaṃ vartayitvā |
tato mūlaiḥ phalaiḥ parṇais tṛṇair iti vartayaṃś cared antataḥ pravṛttāni tato ‘po vāyum ākāśam ity abhiniśrayet | teṣām uttara uttaraḥ saṃyogaḥ phalato viśiṣṭaḥ || 2.23.2 ||
sarvaṃ gatam [ĀpDh 2.22.2–5] || 2.23.2 ||
nirūpitā āśramāḥ | athedānīṃ pakṣapratipakṣarūpeṇa teṣām eva prādhānyam aprādhānyaṃ ca nirūpyate |
atha purāṇe ślokāv udāharanti || 2.23.3 ||
aṣṭāśītisahasrāṇi ye prajām īṣira ṛṣayaḥ |
dakṣiṇenāryamṇaḥ panthānaṃ te śmaśānāni bhejire || 2.23.4 ||
aṣṭāśītisahasrāṇi gṛhasthāḥ ṛṣayaḥ prajām īṣire prajām abhyanandan | te aryamaṇo yo dakṣiṇena panthā dakṣiṇāyanamārgaḥ taṃ prāpya chāndogyoktena dhūmādimārgeṇa gatvā punar api saṃbhūya śmaśānāni bhejire maraṇaṃ pratipedire “jāyasva mriyasva” [ChUp 5.10.8] ity ājīvaṃ jīvabhavaṃ āpedire iti gṛhasthanindā || 2.23.4 ||
aṣṭāśītisahasrāṇi ye prajāṃ neṣira ṛṣayaḥ |
uttareṇāryamṇaḥ panthānaṃ te ‘mṛtatvaṃ hi kalpate || 2.23.5 ||
ye tu prajāṃ nābhyanandan te uttarāyaṇamārgaṃ prāpya arcirādimārgeṇa gatvā amṛtatvam, vibhaktivyatyayaḥ, amṛtatvāya kalpate, vacanavyatyayaḥ, kalpante samarthāḥ saṃpadyante
|| 2.23.5 ||
ity ūrdhvaretasāṃ praśaṃsā || 2.23.6 ||
gṛhasthād anye trayo ‘py ūrdhvaretasaḥ, teṣām eva praśaṃseti || 2.23.6 ||
punar api teṣām eva prakārāntareṇa praśaṃsā |
athāpi saṃkalpasiddhayo bhavanti || 2.23.7 ||
athāpi api ca saṃkalpād eva siddhayo bhavanti teṣām ūrdhvaretasām || 2.23.7 ||
tatrodāharaṇam |
yathā varṣaṃ prajādānaṃ dūre darśanaṃ manojavatāṃ
yac cānyad evaṃ yuktam || 2.23.8 ||
yadi mahatyām anāvṛṣtau varṣatu deva iti te kāmayeran kāmavarṣī parjanyo bhavati | yadi vā kiṃcid aputram anugṛhṇīyuḥ putro ‘sya jāyatām iti putravān eva bhavati | tathā coleṣv eva sthitāḥ himavantaṃ didṛkṣeran tathaiva tad bhavati | manasa iva javo yeṣāṃ te manojavāḥ, tadbhāvo manojavatā | yadi kāmayeran amuṃ deśam iyatyām eva kālakalāyāṃ prāpnuyāmeti tato yāvatā kālena deśaṃ prāpnoti tāvatā taṃ deśaṃ prāpnuyuḥ | yac cānyad evaṃ yuktam, rogiṇām ārogyādi, tad api saṃkalpād bhavati || 2.23.8 |||
yasmād evam |
tasmāc chrutitaḥ pratyakṣaphalatvāc ca viśiṣṭān āśramān
etān eke bruvate || 2.23.9 ||
śrutitaḥ “yad ahar eva virajet tad ahar eva pravrajet” [JābUp 4] ityādiśrutyanugatatvāt, uktena prakāreṇa pratyakṣaphalatvāc ca etān ūrdhvaretasām āśramān viśiṣṭān gārhasthyād utkṛṣṭān eke bruvate iti || 2.23.9 ||
tad idaṃ gārhasthyotkṛṣṭatvapratipādanena nirākaroti |
traividyavṛddhānāṃ tu vedāḥ pramāṇam iti niṣṭhā | tatra yāni
śrūyante vrīhiyavapaśvājyapayaḥkapālapatnīsaṃbandhāny
uccair nīcaiḥ kāryam iti tair viruddha ācāro ‘pramāṇam iti
manyante || 2.23.10 ||
tryavayavā vidyā trayo vedāḥ, tāṃ ye pāṭhataś ca arthataś ca vidanti te traividyāḥ, teṣu pakvajñānāḥ traividyavṛddhāḥ, teṣāṃ vedāḥ pramāṇam atīndriyārthe iti niṣṭhā nirṇayaḥ | yathāha bhagavān jaiminiḥ: “codanālakṣaṇo ‘rtho dharmaḥ,” [PMS 1.1.2], “pratyakṣam animittam” [PMS 1.1.4] iti ca | tataś ca tatra vede yāni karmāṇi śrūyante – kiṃlakṣaṇāni, vrīhyādibhiḥ saṃbaddhāni, “uccair ṛcā kriyate, upāṃśu yajuṣā” ity evaṃprakārāṇi, taiḥ virudha ācāraḥ pramāṇaṃ na bhavatīti manyante tajjñāḥ | etad uktaṃ bhavati – sarveṣu vedeṣu sarvāsu ca śākhāsu agnihotrādīni viśvasṛjām ayanaparyantāni karmāṇy eva tātparyato vidhīyante | ato gārhasthyam eva śreṣṭham | ūrdhvaretasāṃ cāśramāḥ tadviruddhā naiva samāśrayaṇīyāḥ yadi vedāḥ pramāṇam iti | tathā ca gautamaḥ: “aikāśramyaṃ tv ācāryāḥ pratyakṣavidhānād gārhasthyasya” iti [GDh 3.36] || 2.23.10 ||
gārhasthyaṃ praśaṃsya “te śmaśānāni bhejire” [ĀpDh 2.23.4] iti nindāṃ pariharati |
yat tu śmaśānam ucyate nānākarmaṇām eṣo ‘nte
puruṣasaṃskāro vidhīyate || 2.23.11 ||
yat tu gṛhasthānāṃ śmaśānaṃ śrūyate sa eṣa nānākarmaṇām agnihotrādīnām ante pitṛmedhākhyaḥ puruṣasaṃskāro vidhīyate, na tu piśācā bhūtvā śmaśānam eva sevanta
iti || 2.23.11 ||
kuta iti | āha |
tataḥ param anantyaṃ phalaṃ svargyaśabdaṃ
śrūyate || 2.23.12 ||
tataḥ paraṃ śmaśānakarmaṇo ‘nantaram antyam aparimitiaṃ svargaśabdavācyaṃ phalaṃ śrūyate: “sa eṣa yajñāyudhī yajamāno ‘ñjasā svargaṃ lokam eti” iti vijñāyate | anantyaṃ svargyam iti yakāraś chāndasa upajanaḥ, apapāṭho vā || 2.23.12 ||
iti trayoviṃśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
punar api gārhasthyam eva prakārāntareṇa stauti |
athāpy asya prajātim amṛtam āmnāya āha | prajām anu         prajāyase tad u te martyāmṛtam iti || 2.24.1 ||
athāpi apica asya gṛhasthasya prajātiṃ prajāsantānam amṛtam amaraṇam āmnāyo veda āha | he martya maraṇadharman prajāṃ jāyamānām anu tvaṃ prajāyase tvam eva prajārūpeṇa jāyase | tad eva te maraṇadharmiṇo ‘mṛtam amaraṇam iti | na tvaṃ mriyase yatas tvaṃ prajārūpeṇa tiṣṭhasīti
|| 2.24.1 ||
upapannaṃ caitad ity āha |
athāpi sa evāyaṃ nirūḍhaḥ pṛthak pratyakṣeṇopalabhyate
dṛśyate cāpi sārūpyam | dehatvam evānyat || 2.24.2 ||
api ca sa evāyaṃ pṛthag virūḍhaḥ pratyakṣeṇopalabhyate, sa eva dvidhābhūta iva lakṣyate | dṛśyate hi sārūpyaṃ dvayoḥ | dehamātraṃ tu bhinnam | dehatvam iti svārthikastvaḥ || 2.24.2 ||
yadi putrarūpeṇāvasthānam, kim etāvatā iti | atrāha |
te śiṣṭeṣu karmasu vartamānāḥ pūrveṣāṃ sāṃparāyeṇa
kīrtiṃ svargaṃ ca vardhayanti || 2.24.3 ||
te putrāḥ śiṣṭeṣu coditeṣu karmasu vartamānāḥ avasthitāḥ pūrveṣāṃ pitṛpitāmahādīnāṃ sāṃparāyeṇa paralokena saṃbaddhānāṃ kīrtiṃ vardhayanti “asyāyaṃ putra evaṃkarmā asyāyaṃ pautraḥ” iti | svargaṃ ca vardhayanti | kīrtimatāṃ hi svargavāsa eva śrūyate
|| 2.24.3 ||
evam avarovaraḥ pareṣām || 2.24.4 ||
evam anena prakāreṇāvarovaraḥ pareṣāṃ kīrtiṃ svargaṃ ca vardhayati || 2.24.4 ||
ā bhūtasaṃplavāt te svargajitaḥ || 2.24.5 ||
bhūtasaṃplavo mahābhūtapralayaḥ, ā tasmāt te putriṇaḥ svargajīvino hi bhavanti || 2.24.5 ||
punaḥsarge bījārthā bhavantīti bhaviṣyatpurāṇe || 2.24.6 ||
pralayānantaraṃ sargaḥ punaḥsargaḥ, tatra saṃsārasya bījārthāḥ prajārthāḥ prajāpatayo bhavantīti bhaviṣyatpurāṇe śrūyate || 2.24.6 ||
athāpi prajāpater vacanam || 2.24.7 ||
api ca prajāpater api vākyam asminn arthe bhavati gārhasthyam eva variṣṭham iti || 2.24.7 ||
trayīṃ vidyāṃ brahmacaryaṃ prajātiṃ śraddhāṃ tapo yajñam anupradānam | ya etāni kurvate tair it saha smo rajo bhūtvā dhvaṃsate ‘nyat praśaṃsann iti || 2.24.8 ||
trayīṃ vidyāṃ trayāṇāṃ vedānāṃ adhyayanam, brahmacaryam aṣṭācatvāriṃśadādikam, prajātiṃ prajotpādanam, śraddhām āstikyam, tapa upavāsādi, yajñam agnihotrādi somayāgāntam, anupradānam antarvedi bahirvedi ca dānam, ya etāni kurvate tair eva saha vayaṃ smaḥ, ta evāsmākaṃ sahāyāḥ | anyat tūrdhvaretasām āśramādikaṃ praśaṃsan puruṣo rajaḥ pāṃsur bhūtvā dhvaṃsate naśyati | itiśabdo vacanasamāptyarthaḥ || 2.24.8 ||
yathaiva tarhi śiṣṭeṣu vartamānāḥ putrāḥ pūrveṣāṃ kīrtiṃ ca svargaṃ ca vardhayanti, tathaiva pratiṣiddheṣu vartamānāḥ akīrtiṃ narakaṃ ca vardhayeyuḥ | tatrāha |
tatra ye pāpakṛtas ta eva dhvaṃsanti yathā parṇaṃ vanaspateḥ |
na parān hiṃsanti || 2.24.9 ||
tatra prajāsantāne ye pāpasya kartāraḥ ta eva dhvaṃsanti dhvaṃsante, na parān pitrādīn hiṃsanti, yathā yad eva parṇaṃ vanaspateḥ kīṭādibhiḥ dūṣitaṃ tad eva patati, na vanaspatiṃ śākhāṃ vā pātayati, tadvat || 2.24.9 ||
etad evopapādayati |
nāsyāsmin loke karmabhiḥ saṃbandho vidyate tathā parasmin
karmaphalaiḥ || 2.24.10 ||
asyeti sāmānyāpekṣam ekavacanam | asya pitrādeḥ pūrvapuruṣasya asmin loke putrakṛtyaiḥ karmabhiḥ saṃbandho na vidyate | dṛṣṭānto ‘yam – yathā putrakṛtyeṣu karmasu pitrādeḥ kartṛtvaṃ nāsti, tathā parasminn api loke karmaphalair api saṃbandho nāstīty arthaḥ
|| 2.24.10 ||
tad etena veditavyam || 2.24.11 ||
yad uktaṃ ye pāpakṛtasya eva dhvaṃsante na parān hiṃsantīti, tadartharūpaṃ etena vakṣyamāṇena hetunā veditavyaṃ niścetavyam || 2.24.11 ||
prajāpater ṛṣīṇām iti sargo ‘yam || 2.24.12 ||
prajāpateḥ hiraṇyagarbhasya ṛṣīṇāṃ ca marīcyādīnām ayaṃ sargo devādis tiryagantaḥ | te cādhvastā eva sve sve pade vartante | yadi ca putrāḥ pākakṛtaḥ svayaṃ dhvaṃsamānāḥ parān api dhvaṃsayeyuḥ, tadaitan nopapadyate puṇyakṛtaḥ sukhenādyāpi vartante iti || 2.24.12 ||
atrodāharaṇam āha |
tatra ye puṇyakṛtas teṣāṃ prakṛtayaḥ parā jvalantya
upalabhyante || 2.24.13 ||
tatra sarge ye puṇyakṛtaḥ vasiṣṭhādayaḥ teṣāṃ prakṛtayaḥ śarīrāṇi parāḥ utkṛṣṭāḥ jvalantyaḥ dīpyamānāḥ upalabhyante divi, yathā saptarṣimaṇḍalam | śrūyate ca “sukṛtāṃ vā etāni jyotīṃśi yan nakṣatrāṇi” iti [TS 5.4.1] | idam api pramāṇaṃ na putrāṇāṃ dhvaṃse pūrveṣāṃ dhvaṃsa iti
|| 2.24.13 ||
syāt tu karmāvayavena tapasā vā kaścit saśarīro ‘ntavantaṃ lokaṃ jayati saṃkalpasiddhiś ca syān na tu taj jyaiṣṭhyam āśramāṇām || 2.24.14 ||
karmāvayavena pūrvārjitānāṃ karmaṇām ekadeśena bhuktaśeṣeṇa tapasā vā tīvreṇa kaścid ūrdhvaretāḥ saha śarīreṇāntavantaṃ lokaṃ jayatīti yat tat syāt saṃbhaved api, yac ca saṃkalpād eva siddhiḥ syād iti tad api syāt; na tu tat āśramāṇāṃ jyaiṣṭhyakāraṇam iti | tad evam “aikāśramyaṃ tv ācāryāḥ” [GDh 3.36] iti ayam eva pakṣaḥ sthāpitaḥ | anye manyante – sarve āśramāḥ dūṣitā bhūṣitāś ca | tataḥ “teṣu sarveṣu yathopadeśam avyagro vartamānaḥ kṣemaṃ gacchati” [ĀpDh 2.21.2] ity etad eva sthitam iti || 2.24.14 ||
iti caturviṃśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
ity āpastambadharmasūtravṛttau ujjvalāyāṃ
dvitīye praśne navamaḥ paṭalaḥ
vyākhyātāḥ sarvavarṇānāṃ sādhāranavaiśeṣikā dharmāḥ |
rājñas tu viśeṣād vakṣyāmaḥ || 2.25.1 ||
uktavakṣyamāṇasaṃkīrtanaṃ śrotṛbuddhisamādhānārtham | ahiṃsāsatyāsteyādayaḥ sarvavarṇānāṃ sādhāraṇadharmāḥ | adhyayanādayaḥ trayāṇām, adhyāpanādayo brāhmaṇasya, yuddhādayaḥ kṣatriyasya, kṛṣyādayo vaiśyasya, śuśrūṣā śūdrasya | rājātrābhiṣikto vivakṣitaḥ | tasyaiva hi vakṣyamāṇaṃ dharmajātaṃ saṃbhavati | tasya viśeṣāt viśeṣato yad vaktavyaṃ tad vakṣyāmaḥ | “viśeṣān” iti dvitīyāntapāṭhas tu yuktaḥ || 2.25.1 ||
dakṣiṇādvāraṃ veśma puraṃ ca māpayet || 2.25.2 ||
veśma gṛham, puraṃ nagaram | tad ubhayam api dakṣiṇādvāraṃ māpayet sthapatyādibhiḥ kārayet | dakṣiṇapārśve dvāraṃ yasya tat tathoktam || 2.25.2 ||
antarasyāṃ puri veśma || 2.25.3 ||
sarveṣām eva prākārāṇāṃ madhye yā pūḥ tasyām antarasyāṃ puri veśma māpayed ātmanaḥ
|| 2.25.3 ||
tasya purastād āvasathas tad āmantraṇam iti ācakṣate || 2.25.4 ||
tasya veśmanaḥ purastād āvasathaḥ kārayitavyaḥ | etya vasanty asminn ity āvasathaḥ āsthānamaṇḍapam, tasyāmantraṇam iti saṃjñā | tatra hy atithaya āmantryante || 2.25.4 ||
dakṣiṇena puraṃ sabhā dakṣiṇenodagdvārā yathobhayaṃ
saṃdṛśyeta bahir antaraṃ ceti || 2.25.5 ||
dakṣiṇenety enabantam | puram iti “enapā dvitīyā” [Pāṇ 2.3.31] iti dvitīyāntaḥ | purasya dakṣiṇataḥ adūre sabhā kārayitavyā | dakṣiṇodagdvārā dakṣiṇasyām uttarasyāṃ ca diśi dvāre yasyāh sā tathoktā | kim artham ubhayatra dvāram iti cet, yad bahirvṛttaṃ yac cābhyantaraṃ tad ubhayam api yathā saṃdṛśyetety evamartham iti | saiṣā dyūtasabhā, tasyāṃ dyūtārthinaḥ praviśanti, tad āyasthānaṃ rājñaḥ || 2.25.5 ||
sarveṣv evājasrā agnayaḥ syuḥ || 2.25.6 ||
veśamany āvasathe sabhāyām ity eteṣu sarveṣv eva sthāneṣu laukikāgnayaḥ ajasrāḥ syuḥ avicchedena dhāryāḥ || 2.25.6 ||
agnipūjā ca nityā yathā gṛhamedhe || 2.25.7 ||
teṣu cāgniṣu nityam agnipūjā kāryā yathā gṛhamedhe aupāsane, sāyaṃprātarhoma ity arthaḥ | mantrāv api tāv eva, dravyam api tad eva || 2.25.7 ||
āvasathe śrotriyāvarārdhyān atithīn vāsayet || 2.25.8 ||
āvasathākhyasthāne atithīn vāsayet | te viśeṣyante – śrotriyāvarārdhyān iti | avaraparyāyo ‘varārdhyaśabdaḥ | yadi sarvān vāsayituṃ na śaknoti śrotriyān api tāvad vāsayed iti
|| 2.25.8 ||
teṣāṃ yathāguṇam āvasathāḥ śayyānnapānaṃ ca
videyam || 2.25.9 ||
teṣām atithīnāṃ yathāguṇaṃ vidyāvṛttādyanuguṇaṃ āvasathādi videyam viśeṣeṇa deyam | āvasathāḥ apavarakādayaḥ | śayyāḥ khaṭvādayaḥ | annam odanādi | pānaṃ takrasūpādi
|| 2.25.9 ||
gurūn amātyāṃś ca nātijīvet || 2.25.10 ||
guravaḥ pitrādayaḥ | amātyāḥ mantriṇaḥ | tān nātijīvet bhakṣyabhojyācchādanādiṣu tān
nātiśayīta || 2.25.10 ||
na cāsya viṣaye kṣudhā rogeṇa himātapābhyāṃ vāvasīded abhāvād buddhipūrvaṃ vā kaścit || 2.25.11 ||
asya rājño viṣaye rāṣṭre kṣudhā āhārābhāvena bubhukṣayā, rogena vyādhinā, himena nīhāreṇa, varṣādīnām apy upalakṣaṇam etat, ātapaḥ ādityaraśmitāpaḥ, etaiḥ prakārair abhāvāt buddhipūrvaṃ vā na kaścit abrāhmaṇo ‘py avasīdet avasannaḥ syāt | rājño hy ayam aparādhaḥ yadāhārādyabhāvena kaścid avasannaḥ syāt | buddhipūrvaṃ vety atrodāharaṇam – yadā kaścit karam ṛṇaṃ vā dāpyo bhavati tadā nāsau himātapayoḥ niveśayitavyaḥ, bhojanād vā niroddhavyaḥ, tathā kurvāṇaṃ rājā daṇḍayed iti || 2.25.11 ||
sabhāyā madhye ‘dhidevanam uddhatyāvokṣyākṣān nivaped
yugmān vaibhītakān yathārthān || 2.25.12 ||
pūrvoktāyāḥ sabhāyā madhye adhidevanam | yasyopari kitavāḥ akṣaiḥ dīvyanti tat sthānam adhidevanam | tat pūrvaṃ kāṣṭhādinā uddhanti | uddhatyāvokṣati | avokṣya tatra akṣān yugmasaṃkhyān vaibhītakān vibhītakavṛkṣasya vikārabhūtān yathārthān yāvadbhiḥ dyūtaṃ nirvartate tāvato nivapati | kaḥ | yas tatra rājñā niyuktaḥ sa sabhiko nāma || 2.25.12 ||
āryāḥ śucayaḥ satyaśīlā dīvitāraḥ syuḥ || 2.25.13 ||
āryāḥ dvijātayaḥ | śucayaḥ dharmaśuddhāḥ | satyaśīlāḥ satyavādinaḥ | evaṃbhūtā eva puruṣāḥ tatra dīvitāraḥ devitāraḥ syuḥ, ta eva tatra devituṃ labhante ity arthaḥ | te ca tatra devitvā yathāparibhāṣitaṃ paṇaṃ sabhikāya datvā gaccheyuḥ | sa ca rājñe tam āyam ahar ahaḥ pratimāsaṃ pratisaṃvatsaraṃ vā dadyāt | sa eva ca sthānāntare dīvyataḥ daṇḍayet, sabhāsthāne ca kalahakārān | tatra yājñavalkyaḥ |
glahe śatikavṛddhes tu sabhikaḥ pañcakaṃ śatam |
gṛhṇīyād dhūrtakitavād itarād daśakaṃ śatam ||
sa samyak pālito dadyād rājñe bhāgaṃ yathākṛtam |
jitam udgrāhayej jetre dadyāt satyaṃ vacaḥ kṣamī || iti | [YDh 2.199–200]
|| 2.25.13 ||
āyudhagrahaṇaṃ nṛttagītavāditrāṇīti rājādhīnebhyo ‘nyatra
na vidyeran || 2.25.14 ||
āyudhagrahaṇādīni rājādhīnebhyo rājāśrayā ye puruṣāḥ tebhyo ‘nyatra na vidyeran na bhaveyuḥ | utsavādiṣv anyatrāpi bhavaty ācāraḥ || 2.25.14 ||
kṣemakṛd rājā yasya viṣaye grāme ‘raṇye vā taskarabhayaṃ
na vidyate || 2.25.15 ||
yasya rājño viṣaye grāme araṇye ca corabhayaṃ nāsti sa eva rājā kṣemakṛt kṣemakaraḥ, na tv anyaḥ śataṃ tubhyam iti dadāno ‘pi || 2.25.15 ||
iti pañcaviṃśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
bhṛtyānām anuparodhena kṣetraṃ vittaṃ ca dadad
brāhmaṇebhyo yathārham anantān lokān abhijayati || 2.26.1 ||
bhṛtyānām anuparodhena bhṛtyavargasya yathoparodho na bhavati tathā brāhmaṇebhyo yathārhaṃ vidyāvṛttānurūpaṃ kṣetraṃ vittaṃ ca dadyāt | evaṃ dadat anantān lokān abhijayati
|| 2.26.1 ||
brāhmaṇasvāny apajigīṣamāṇo rājā yo hanyate tam āhur
ātmayūpo yajño ‘nantadakṣiṇa iti || 2.26.2 ||
brāhmaṇasvāni corādibhir apahṛtāni apajigīṣamāṇo brāhmaṇebhyo dānāya tān apajitya grahītum icchan yo rājā yuddhe coraiḥ hanyate tam ātmayūpo ‘nantadakṣiṇo yajña ity āhuḥ dharmajñāḥ | saṃgrāmo yajñaḥ, tasyātmā yūpasthānīyaḥ | ātmeti śarīram āha | antarātmā paśusthānīyaḥ | pratyāninīṣitaṃ tu dravyaṃ dakṣiṇā | sūtre tu taṃ yajña ity āhuḥ iti gauṇo vādaḥ || 2.26.2 ||
etenānye śūrā vyākhyātāḥ prayojane yudhyamānās
tanutyajaḥ || 2.26.3 ||
prayojanaṃ corādibhir apahṛtānāṃ dravyāṇāṃ brāhmaṇasvānāṃ pratyānayanādi, tadarthaṃ yudhyamānā ye śūrāḥ tanutyajo bhavanti śarīrāṇi tyajanti te ‘py etena rājñā vyākhyātā ātmayūpo yajño ‘nantadakṣiṇa iti || 2.26.3 ||
grāmeṣu nagareṣu cāryāṃś chucīn satyaśīlān prajāgupataye
nidadhyāt || 2.26.4 ||
āryān śucīn satyaśīlān iti vyākhyātam [ĀpDh 2.25.13] | evaṃbhūtān puruṣān grāmeṣu nagareṣu ca prajānāṃ rakṣaṇārthaṃ nidadhyāt niyuñjīta || 2.26.4 ||
teṣāṃ puruṣās tathāguṇā eva syuḥ || 2.26.5 ||
teṣāṃ niyuktānāṃ ye puruṣā niyojyāḥ te tathāguṇā āryādiguṇā eva syuḥ || 2.26.5 ||
sarvato yojanaṃ nagaraṃ taskarebhyo rakṣyam || 2.26.6 ||
sarvataḥ sarvāsu dikṣu yojanamātraṃ nagaraṃ taskarebhyo rakṣaṇīyam | “rakṣyan” ity apapāṭhaḥ
|| 2.26.6 ||
krośo grāmebhyaḥ || 2.26.7 ||
grāmebhyas tu sarvāsu dikṣu krośo rakṣyaḥ | grāmebhya iti “yataś cādhvaparimāṇaṃ tatra pañcamī vaktavyā” iti pañcamī || 2.26.7 ||
tatra yan muṣyate tais tat pratidāpyam || 2.26.8 ||
tatra yojanamātre krośamātre vā yan muṣyate coryate te niyuktāḥ svāmibhyaḥ tat pratidadyuḥ, rājñā taiḥ tat pratidāpyam, rājā taiḥ pratidāpayed iti | prāyeṇa dantyoṣṭhyaṃ vakāraṃ paṭhanti
|| 2.26.8 ||
dhārmyaṃ śulkam avahārayet || 2.26.9 ||
tatra gautamaḥ: “viṃśatibhāgaḥ śulkaḥ paṇye” [GDh 10.26] yad vaṇigbhir vikrīyate hiṅgvādi tasya viṃśatitamaṃ bhāgaṃ rājā gṛhṇīyāt | tasya śulka iti saṃjñā | eṣa dhārmyaḥ dharmyaḥ śulkaḥ, tam adhikṛtair evāvahārayet grāhayed iti | mūlādiṣu tu viśeṣas tenaivoktaḥ: “mūlaphalapuṣpauṣadhamadhumāṃsatṛṇendanānāṃ ṣāṣṭhyam” iti [GDh 10.27] || 2.26.9 ||
akaraḥ śrotriyaḥ || 2.26.10 ||
śrotriyaḥ karaṃ na dāpyaḥ, anye dāpyāḥ || 2.26.10 ||
sarvavarṇānāṃ ca striyaḥ || 2.26.11 ||
akarāḥ | varṇagrahaṇāt pratilomādistriyo dāpyāḥ || 2.26.11 ||
kumārāś ca prāg vyañjanebhyaḥ || 2.26.12 ||
vyañjanāni śmaśrvādīni | yāvat tāni notpadyante tāvad akarāḥ kumārāḥ || 2.26.12 ||
ye ca vidyārthā vasanti || 2.26.13 ||
vidyām uddiśya ye guruṣu vasanti te jātavyañjanā api asamāptavedā akarāḥ || 2.26.13 ||
tapasvinaś ca ye dharmaparāḥ || 2.26.14 ||
tapasvinaḥ kṛcchracāndrāyaṇādipravṛttāḥ dharmaparāḥ aphalākāṅkṣiṇo nityanaimittikadharmaniratāḥ | dharmaparā iti kiṃ | ye abhicārakāmāḥ mantrasiddhaye tapas tapyante te akarāḥ mā bhūnn iti || 2.26.14 ||
śūdraś ca pādāvanektā || 2.26.15 ||
traivarṇikānāṃ pādāvanektā yaḥ pādāvanejanavṛttiḥ sa śūdro ‘py akaraḥ || 2.26.15 ||
andhamūkabadhirarogāviṣṭāś ca || 2.26.16 ||
andhādayaś cākarāḥ yāvad āndhyādi || 2.26.16 ||
ye vyarthā dravyaparigrahaiḥ || 2.26.17 ||
ye ca parivrājakādayo dravyaparigrahaiḥ vyarthāḥ niṣprayojanāḥ, śāstrato yeṣāṃ dravyaparigrahaḥ pratiṣiddhaḥ, te ‘py akarāḥ | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “akaraḥ śrotriyo rājapumān anāthaḥ pravrajito bālavṛddhataruṇapraśāntāḥ” iti [VaDh 19.23] || 2.26.17 ||
abuddhipūrvam alaṃkṛto yuvā paradāram anupraviśan
kumārīṃ vā vācā bādhyaḥ || 2.26.18 ||
yatra paradārā āsate kumārīṃ vā patiṃvarā, tatra yuvā alaṃkṛto abuddhipūrvam ajñānād anupraviśan vācā bādhyaḥ atreyam āste mātra praviśa iti || 2.26.18 ||
buddhipūrvaṃ tu duṣṭhabhāvo daṇḍyaḥ || 2.26.19 ||
yas tu vijānann eva duṣṭabhāvaḥ pratilobhanārthī praviśati sa daṇḍyo dravyānurūpam aparādhānurūpaṃ ca | duṣṭagrahaṇāt ācāryādipreṣitasya praveśe daṇḍo mā bhūd iti || 2.26.19 ||
saṃnipāte vṛtte śiśnacchedanaṃ savṛṣaṇasya || 2.26.20 ||
saṃnipāto maithunam | tasmin vṛtte śiśnacchedanaṃ daṇdaḥ | savṛṣaṇasya iti upasarjanasyāpi śiśnasya viśeṣaṇam, savṛṣanasya śiśnasya chedanam iti || 2.26.20 ||
kumāryāṃ tu svāny ādāya nāśyaḥ || 2.26.21 ||
kumāryāṃ tu saṃnipāte vṛtte sarvasvaharaṇaṃ kṛtvā deśān nirvāsyaḥ, na śiśnacchedanam
|| 2.26.21 ||
atha bhṛtye rājñā || 2.26.22 ||
saṃnipātaprabhṛti paradārakumāryau rājñā bhṛtye grāsācchādanapradānena bhartavye
|| 2.26.22 ||
rakṣye cāta ūrdhvaṃ maithunāt || 2.26.23 ||
ataḥ prathamāt saṃnipātād ūrdhvaṃ maithunāc ca rakṣye, yathā punaḥ maithunaṃ na carataḥ tathā kārye || 2.26.23 ||
nirveṣābhyupāye tu svāmibhyo ‘vasṛjet || 2.26.24 ||
yadi te evaṃ niruddhe nirveṣam abhyupetaḥ abhyupagacchataḥ tadā nirveṣābhyupāye tu svāmibhyaḥ te avasṛjet dadyāt, paradāraṃ bhartre śvaśurāya vā, kumārīṃ pitre bhrātre vā | anabhyupagame tu prāyaścittasya yāvajjīvaṃ nirodhaḥ || 2.26.24 ||
iti ṣaḍviṃśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
carite yathāpuraṃ dharmād dhi saṃbandhaḥ || 2.27.1 ||
carite tu nirveṣe yathāpuraṃ yathāpūrvaṃ dharmāt, tṛtīyārthe pañcamī, dharmeṇa saṃbandho bhavatīti | hiśabdo hetau | yasmād evaṃ tasmād avaśyaṃ prāyaścittaṃ kārayitavye, tato yajñavivahādau na kaścid doṣa iti || 2.27.1 ||
paradāraprasaṅgād ucyate |
sagotrasthānīyāṃ na parebhyaḥ samācakṣīta || 2.27.2 ||
ya anapatyaḥ ātmanaḥ śaktyabhāvaṃ niścitya kṣetrajaṃ putram icchan bhāryāṃ paratra niyuṅkte, mṛte vā tasmin tatpitrādayaḥ santānakāṅkṣiṇaḥ, tadviṣayam etat | kulāntarāt praviṣṭā strī sagotrasthānīyā | sā hi pūrvaṃ pitṛgotrā satī bhartṛgotradharmaiḥ adhikriyate | tataḥ sā bhartṛpakṣyāṇāṃ sagotrasthānīyā bhavati | bhartā tu sākṣāt sagotraḥ | tāṃ sagotrasthānīyāṃ na parebhyo ‘sagotrebhyaḥ samācakṣīta, “iyam anapatyā, asyām apatyam utpādyam” iti | sagotrāyaiva tu samācakṣīta, tatrāpi devarāya, tadabhāve sapiṇḍāya || 2.27.2 ||
kaḥ punaḥ sagotrasya viśeṣaḥ | tam āha |
kulāya hi strī pradīyata ity upadiśanti || 2.27.3 ||
hi yasmāt strī kanyā pradīyamānā kulāyaiva pradīyata iti upadiśanti dharmajñāḥ | tasmāt sagotrāyaiva samācakṣīteti || 2.27.3 ||
tam imaṃ niyogaṃ dūṣayati |
tad indiryadaurbalyād vipratipannam || 2.27.4 ||
yady apy evaṃ pūrve kṛtavantaḥ, tathāpi tad adyatve vipratipannaṃ vipratiṣiddham | kutaḥ | indriyadaurbalyāt | durbalendriyā hy adyatve manuṣyāḥ | tataś ca śāstravyājenāpi bhartṛvyatikrame atiprasaṅgaḥ syāt iti || 2.27.4 ||
sagotraviṣaye ca yo viśeṣaḥ so ‘pi nāstīty āha |
aviśiṣṭaṃ hi paratvaṃ pāṇeḥ || 2.27.5 ||
yena pāṇinā pūrvam agnisākṣikaṃ pāṇir gṛhītaḥ kanyāyāḥ tasmāt pāṇer anyo bhavati sagotrasyāpi pāṇiḥ, yasmād eva pāṇeḥ paratvam aviśiṣṭaṃ samānaṃ tasmād aviśeṣa iti | “avaśiṣṭam” ity apapāṭhaḥ || 2.27.5 ||
pāṇir anyo bhavatu | ko doṣaḥ |
tadvyatikrame khalu punar ubhayor narakaḥ || 2.27.6 ||
tasya pāṇeḥ vyatikrame ubhayor dampatyoḥ narako bhavati | khalu punar iti prasiddhidyotakau nipātāu | ataḥ patyā na sa pāṇiḥ tyājyaḥ yaḥ pūrvaṃ gṛhītaḥ | bhāryayāpi na sa pāṇiḥ tyājyaḥ, yena pūrvaṃ ātmanaḥ pāṇiḥ gṛhītaḥ || 2.27.6 ||
niyamārambhaṇo hi varṣīyān abhyudaya evam ārambhaṇād
apatyāt || 2.27.7 ||
ārabhyate ‘nenety arambhaṇaḥ | yo ‘yaṃ dampatyoḥ parasparaṃ niyamaḥ sa ārambhaṇo yasya sa niyamārambhaṇaḥ | evaṃbhūto yo ‘bhyudayaḥ sa eva varṣīyān pravṛddhataraḥ | evam uktena prakāreṇa niyogalakṣaṇena yad apatyam ārabhyate tasmād evam ārambhaṇād apatyād varṣīyān iti | “āpatyād” iti prāyeṇa paṭhanti || 2.27.7 ||
nāśya āryaś śūdrāyām || 2.27.8 ||
āryaḥ traivarṇikaḥ śūdrāyāṃ parabhāryāyāṃ prasaktaḥ rājñā rāṣṭrān nāśyaḥ nirvāsyaḥ || 2.27.8 ||
vadhyaḥ śūdra āryāyām || 2.27.9 ||
śūdras tv āryāyāṃ traivarṇikāyāṃ prasakto vadhyaḥ | etac ca yo ‘ntaḥpurādiṣu adhikṛto rakṣakaḥ san svayaṃ gacchati tasya bhavati | anyasya pūrvoktaṃ śiśnacchedanam eva | tathā ca śūdrādhikāre gautamaḥ: “āryastryabhigamane liṅgoddhāraḥ sarvasvaharaṇaṃ ca gotpā ced vadho ‘dhikaḥ” iti [GDh 12.2–3] | yājñavalkyena tu prātilomyena gamanamātre vadha uktaḥ | yathā vā |
sajātāv uttamo daṇḍa ānulomye tu madhyamaḥ |
prātilomye vadhaḥ puṃsāṃ strīṇāṃ nāsādikṛntanam ||
so ‘nubandhābhyāsādyapeṣo draṣṭavyaḥ | tathā “nāśya āryaḥ śūdrāyaṃ” [ĀpDh 2.27.8] ity ācāryavacanam api anubandhābhyāsāpekṣam, brahmaṇādeḥ kramavivāhe yā śūdra tadviṣayaṃ vā draṣṭavyam || 2.27.9 ||
dāraṃ cāsya karśayet || 2.27.10 ||
asya śūdrasya yā dārabhūtā tena bhuktā traivarṇikastrī tāṃ ca karśayet vrataniyamopavāsaiḥ | yā prajātā na bhavati, tadviṣayam etat,
brāhmaṇakṣatriyaviśāṃ striyaḥ śūdreṇa saṃgatāḥ |
aprajātā viśudhyanti prāyaścittena netarāḥ ||
iti smaraṇāt || 2.27.10 ||
savarṇāyām anyapūrvāyāṃ sakṛt saṃnipāte pādaḥ
patatīty upadiśanti || 2.27.11 ||
anyaḥ pūrvaḥ patir yasyāḥ sā anyapūrvā parabhāryā, tasyāṃ savarṇāyāṃ sakṛd gamane pādaḥ patati | patitasya dvādaśavārṣikaṃ prāyaścittam, tasya turīyāṃśaḥ trīṇi varṣāṇi prākṛtaṃ brahmacaryaṃ asya prāyaścittam | etac ca śrotriyabhāryāyāṃ ṛtukāle kāmataḥ prathamadūṣakasya brāhmaṇasya | tatra gautamaḥ: “dve paradāre trīṇi śrotriyasya” [GDh 22.29–30] iti || 2.27.11 ||
evam abhyāse pādaḥ pādaḥ || 2.27.12 ||
evam abhyāse pratyabhyāsaṃ pādaḥ pādaḥ patati || 2.27.12 ||
ataḥ |
caturthe sarvam || 2.27.13 ||
caturthe saṃnipāte sarvam eva patati | tataś ca pūrṇaṃ dvādaśavārṣikaṃ kartavyam, tṛtīye navavarṣāṇi, dvitīye ṣaḍ iti | etac ca pratiyogaṃ strībhedena prathamadūṣakasya | ekasyām eva tv abhyāse kalpyam | tatra
yat puṃsaḥ paradāreṣu yac caināṃ cārayed vratam | [MDh 11.177]
iti smaraṇāt, striyā api pratiyogaṃ pādaḥ pādaḥ patati | tadanurodhena kalpyam || 2.27.13 ||
jihvācchedaṃ śūdrasyāryaṃ dharmikam ākrośataḥ || 2.27.14 ||
śūdro dvijātīnām anyatamaṃ dhārmikaṃ svadharmasthaṃ yady ākrośati nindati garhate tadā tasya jihvā chettavyeti | manus tu sāmānyenāha |
yenāṅgenāvaro varṇo brāhmaṇasyāparādhnuyāt |
tadaṅgaṃ tasya chettavyaṃ tan manor anuśāsanam || iti | [MDh 7.279]
gautamas tu: “śūdro dvijātīn abhisaṃdhāyābhihatya ca vāgdaṇḍapāruṣyābhyām aṅgaṃ mocyo yenopahanyāt” [GDh 12.1] iti || 2.27.14 ||
vāci pathi śayyāyām āsana iti samībhavato
daṇḍatāḍanam || 2.27.15 ||
