yas tu śūdro vāgādiṣv āryaiḥ samo bhavati, na tu nyagbhūtaḥ, tasya daṇḍena tāḍanaṃ kartavyam, sa daṇḍena tādayitavyaḥ | ayam asya daṇḍaḥ || 2.27.15 ||
puruṣavadhe steye bhūmyādāna iti svāny ādāya
vadhyaḥ || 2.27.16 ||
bhūmyādānaṃ parakṣetrasya balāt svīkāraḥ | puruṣavadhādiṣu nimitteṣu śūdraḥ sarvasvaharaṇaṃ kṛtvā paścād vadhyaḥ mārayitavyaḥ || 2.27.16 ||
cakṣunirodhas tv eteṣu brāhmaṇasya || 2.27.17 ||
brāhmaṇasya tv eteṣu nimitteṣu cakṣurnirodhaḥ kartavyaḥ, paṭṭabandhādinā cakṣuṣī niroddhavye yathā yāvajjīvaṃ na paśyati, na tūtpāṭayitavye, “na śārīro brāhmaṇadaṇḍaḥ,” [GDh 12.46] “akṣato brāhmaṇo vrajet” [MDh 8.124d] iti smaraṇāt | “cakṣunirodhaḥ” iti rephalopaś chāndasaḥ || 2.27.17 ||
niyamātikramaṇam anyaṃ vā rahasi bandhayet || 2.27.18 ||
yo varṇāśramayuktān niyamān atikrāmati taṃ niyamātikramaṇam, anyaṃ vā pratiṣiddhānāṃ kartāraṃ rahasi bandhayet nigalitaṃ nirundhyāt || 2.27.18 ||
ā samāpatteḥ || 2.27.19 ||
yāvad asau niyamān pratipatsyet pratiṣiddhebhyo nivartiṣya iti brūyāt || 2.27.19 ||
asamāpattau nāśyaḥ || 2.27.20 ||
yady asau dīrghakālaṃ niruddho ‘pi na samāpadyeta tato nāśyo nirvāsyaḥ || 2.27.20 ||
ācārya ṛtvik snātako rājeti trāṇaṃ syur anyatra
vadhyāt || 2.27.20 ||
yadi daṇḍapravṛttaṃ rājānam ācāryo brūyāt “aham enam ataḥ paraṃ vārayiṣyāmi mucyatām ayam” iti tato ‘ṅgadaṇḍe prāpte arthadaṇḍam, arthadaṇḍe tāḍanam, tāḍane dhigdaṇḍam iti kṛtvā tadvaśe visṛjet | evam ṛtviji | ṛtvigācāryau rājñaḥ svabhūtau na daṇḍyasya | snātako vidyāvratābhyām | rājā anantarādiḥ | sarva ete rājñaḥ saṃmānyāḥ | atas te daṇḍyasya trāṇaṃ syuḥ, uktena prakāreṇa rakṣakā bhaveyuḥ, nānyaḥ kaścit; te ‘py anyatra vadhyāt | yasya vadhānuguṇoparādhaḥ na tasyācāryādayo ‘pi trāṇam, hantavya eva sa iti || 2.27.20 ||
iti saptaviṃśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
ity āpastambadharmasūtravṛttau ujjvalāyāṃ
dvitīye praśne daśamaḥ paṭalaḥ
kṣetraṃ parigṛhyotthānābhāvāt phalābhāve yaḥ samṛddhaḥ
sa bhāvi tad apahāryaḥ || 2.28.1 ||
vaiśyo vaiśyavṛttir vā, parasya kṣetraṃ kṛṣyarthaṃ parigṛhya yady utthānaṃ yatnaṃ kṛṣiviṣayaṃ na kuryāt, tadabhāvāc ca phalaṃ na syāt, tata eva tasmin nimitte karṣakaḥ samṛddhaś cet tasmin bhāge yad bhāvi phalaṃ tad apahāryaḥ apahārayitavyaḥ, rājñā kṣetrasvāmine dāpyaḥ || 2.28.1 ||
avaśinaḥ kīnāśasya karmanyāse daṇḍatāḍanam || 2.28.2 ||
kīnāśaḥ karṣakaḥ | tasyāvaśinaḥ asvatantrasya nirdhanasya dāsasya karmanyāse sa cet kṛṣikarma nyaset vicchindyāt, tasya daṇḍena tāḍanaṃ kartavyam, sa daṇḍena tāḍayitavyaḥ | arthābhāvān nārthadaṇḍaḥ | apara āha – avaśī avaśyaḥ avidheyaḥ | yadi kṣetraṃ parigṛhyāvaśinaḥ kīnāśasya kṛṣikarma nyaset na svayaṃ kuryāt tadā sa parigrāhako daṇḍena tāḍya iti | yadi vā avaśina iti bahuvrīhiḥ – yasya kīnāśasya vaśī svatantraḥ kṣetravān nāsti sa yadi pūrvakṛṣṭasya kṣetrasya kṛṣikarma nyaset tasya tāḍanaṃ daṇḍa iti rājapuruṣasyopadeśaḥ || 2.28.2 ||
tathā paśupasya || 2.28.3 ||
paśupo gopālaḥ tasyāpi karmanyāse pālanākaraṇe daṇḍena tāḍanaṃ daṇḍaḥ || 2.28.3 ||
avarodhanaṃ cāsya paśūnām || 2.28.4 ||
ye cāsya paśavo rakṣaṇāya samarpitāḥ teṣāṃ cāvarodhanam apaharaṇaṃ kartavyam, anyasya gopasya samarpaṇīyā iti || 2.28.4 ||
hitvā vrajam ādinaḥ karśayet paśūn nātipādayet || 2.28.5 ||
ye paśavo vraje goṣṭhe niruddhāḥ taṃ vrajaṃ hitvā ādinaḥ sasyāder bhakṣayitāro bhavanti tān karśayet bandhādinā kṛśān kuryāt | kaḥ | yat bhakṣitaṃ tadvān, rājapuruṣo vā | nātipādayet nātirodhaṃ kuryāt na tāḍayed veti || 2.28.5 ||
avarudhya paśūn māraṇe nāśane vā svāmibhyo ‘vasṛjet || 2.28.6 ||
yadi puruṣaḥ paśūn avarudhya pālayituṃ gṛhītvā bhayasthāneṣu visṛjyopekṣayā mārayet nāśayed vā, nāśanaṃ corādibhir apaharaṇam, sa svāmibhyaḥ paśūn avasṛjet pratyarpayet, paśvabhāve mūlyam || 2.28.6 ||
pramādād araṇye paśūn utsṛṣṭān dṛṣṭvā grāmam ānīya
svāmibhyo ‘vasṛjet || 2.28.7 ||
yadi svāminaḥ pramādād araṇye paśūn sṛjeyuḥ vinā pālanena tatas tān dṛṣṭvā grāmam ānīya svāmibhyo ‘rpayet | kaḥ | yas tatra rājñā rakṣakatvena niyuktaḥ || 2.28.7 ||
punaḥ pramāde sakṛd avarudhya || 2.28.8 ||
punaḥ pramādād utsṛṣṭeṣu sakṛd avarudhya svāmibhyo ‘vasṛjet || 2.28.8 ||
tata ūrdhvaṃ na sūrkṣet || 2.28.9 ||
tato dvitīyāt prayogād ūrdhvam “grāmam ānīya” ityādi yad uktaṃ tan na sūrkṣet nādriyeta, tasmin viṣaye upekṣeta || 2.28.9 ||
paraparigraham avidvān ādadāna edhodake mūle puṣpe phale
gandhe grāse śāka iti vācā bādhyaḥ || 2.28.10 ||
edhāś codakaṃ ca edhodakam | grāsaḥ gavādyartho yavasādi | sarvatra viṣayasaptamī | yaḥ paraparigraha ity avidvān ādadānaḥ ajānan edodakādikam ādatte gṛhṇāti | sa tasmin viṣaye tatra niyuktena rājapuruṣeṇa niṣṭhurayā vācā bādhyo nivāryaḥ || 2.28.10 ||
viduṣo vāsasaḥ parimoṣaṇam || 2.28.11 ||
yas tu vidvān evādatte tasya vāsaso mokṣaṇam apahāraḥ kartavyaḥ || 2.28.11 ||
adaṇḍyaḥ kāmakṛte tathā prāṇasaṃśaye bhojanam
ādadānaḥ || 2.28.12 ||
tathāśabdasya bhojanam ity anena saṃbandhaḥ | prāṇasaṃśayadaśāyām edhodakāder ādāne kāmakṛte ‘py adaṇḍyaḥ | tathā bhojanam apy ādadānaḥ prāṇasaṃśaye na daṇḍya iti || 2.28.12 ||
prāptanimitte daṇḍākarmaṇi rājānam enaḥ spṛśati || 2.28.13 ||
prāptaṃ daṇḍanimittaṃ yasya tasmin puruṣe daṇḍākarmaṇi daṇḍasyākriyāyāṃ yadi dayayā arthalobhena vā prāptaṃ daṇḍaṃ na kuryāt tadā tad eno rājānaṃ spṛśati || 2.28.13 ||
ity aṣṭāviṃśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
nanu śāstraphalaṃ prayoktari; tat katham anyakṛtam enaḥ anyaṃ spṛśati | bahuvidhatvāt kartṛbhāvasyety āha |
prayojayitā mantā karteti svarganarakaphaleṣu karmasu
bhāginaḥ || 2.29.1 ||
dharmam adharmaṃ vā kurvāṇaṃ yaḥ prayuṅkte itthaṃ kurv iti sa prayojayitā | sa cānekaprakāraḥ, ājñāpako ‘bhyarthayitā anugrāhaka iti | bhṛtyāder nikṛṣṭasya pravartanā ājñā | gurvāder ārādhyasya pravartanā abhyarthanā | anugraho dvividhaḥ, upadeśaḥ tatsamācaraṇaṃ ceti | tatra ya ittham upadiśati “tvaṃ śatrum imaṃ vyāpādaya, dharmārjane ayaṃ te ‘bhyupāyaḥ” iti sa upadeṣṭā | yaḥ punaḥ kenacij jighāṃsitaṃ palāyamānaṃ nirunaddhi, niruddhaś ca hanyate, tatra niroddhā anugrāhakaḥ | mantā anumantā, yasyānumatim antareṇa artho na nirvartate sa rājādiko dharmādharmayor anumantā | kartā sākṣāt kriyāyā nirvartakaḥ | ete trayo ‘pi svargaphaleṣu narakaphaleṣu ca karmasu dharmeṣv adharmeṣu ca bhāginaḥ phalasyāṃśabhāginaḥ aṃśabhājaḥ || 2.29.1 ||
sarveṣāṃ ca yathā kathaṃcit kartṛtvaṃ yady apy evam |
yo bhūya ārabhate tasmin phalaviśeṣaḥ || 2.29.2 ||
teṣu prayojakādiṣu yo bhūya ārabhate yasya vyāpāro ‘rthanirvṛttāvadhikam upayujyate tasmin phalaviśeṣo bhavati || 2.29.2 ||
kuṭumbinau dhanasyeśāte || 2.29.3 ||
kuṭumbinau dampatī dhanasya parigrahe viniyoge ca īśāte | yady apy evam, tathāpi bhartur anujñayā vinā strī viniyoktuṃ na prabhavati, bhartā tu prabhavati | tad etena veditavyam, “na hi bhartur vipravāse naimittike dāne steyam upadiśanti” [ĀpDh 2.14.20] iti || 2.29.3 ||
tayor anumate ‘nye ‘pi taddhiteṣu varteran || 2.29.4 ||
tayoḥ dampatyoḥ anumate anumatau satyām anye ‘pi putrādayaḥ taddhiteṣu tayoḥ aihikeṣu āmuṣmikeṣu ca varteran dravyaviniyogenāpi || 2.29.4 ||
vivāde vidyābhijanasaṃpannā vṛddhā medhāvino dharmeṣv
avinipātinaḥ || 2.29.5 ||
arthiprathyarthinoḥ vipratiṣiddho vādo vivādaḥ | tatra vidyādiguṇasaṃyuktā nirṇetāraḥ syuḥ iti vākyaśeṣaḥ | vidyā adhyayanajñānasaṃpat | abhijanaḥ kulaśuddhiḥ | vṛddhāḥ pariṇatavayasaḥ | medhāvinaḥ ūhāpohakuśalāḥ | dharmeṣu varṇāśramaprayukteṣu avinipātinaḥ, vinipātaḥ pramādaḥ tadrahitāḥ || 2.29.5 ||
saṃdehe liṅgato daiveneti vicitya || 2.29.6 ||
te ca nirṇayantaḥ saṃdehasthaleṣu liṅgataḥ anumānena daivena taptamāṣādinā | itiśabdaḥ prakāre, yac cānyad evaṃ yuktaṃ vacanavyāghātādi, tena ca vicitya arthasthitim anviṣya nirṇetāraḥ syuḥ ity adhyāhṛtena vākyaparisamāptiḥ || 2.29.6 ||
atha sākṣyavidhiḥ |
puṇyāhe prātar agnāv iddhe ‘pām ante rājavaty ubhayataḥ
samākhyāpya sarvānumate mukhyaḥ satyaṃ praśnaṃ
brūyāt || 2.29.7 ||
puṇyāho devanakṣatram, prātaḥ na madhyāhnādiṣu, agnāv iddhe ‘gnim iddhvā tatsamīpe apām ante udakam upanidhāya rājavati rājñādhiṣṭhite sadasi ubhayataḥ ubhayor arthipratyarthinoḥ kim ahaṃ yuvayoḥ pramāṇabhūtaḥ sākṣī | iti ātmānaṃ khyāpayitvā | yadi vā – ubhayataḥ ubhayor api pakṣayoḥ satyavacane cāsatyavacane ca sākṣiṇo yad bhāvi phalaṃ tat,
satyaṃ brūhy anṛtaṃ tyaktvā satyena svargam eṣyasi |
uktvānṛtaṃ mahāghoraṃ narakaṃ pratipatsyase ||
ityādinā prakāreṇa samākhyāpya, prāḍvivākādibhiḥ pṛṣṭa iti śeṣaḥ | sarvānumate arthiprathyarthinoḥ sabhyānāṃ cānumatau satyāṃ mukhyaḥ sākṣiguṇair upetaḥ doṣaiś ca varjitaḥ sākṣī praśnaṃ pṛṣṭam arthaṃ satyaṃ yathā ātmanā jñātaṃ tathā brūyāt || 2.29.7 ||
anṛte rājā daṇḍaṃ praṇayet || 2.29.8 ||
sākṣiṇā anṛtam uktam iti pratipanno rājā daṇḍaṃ praṇayet | tatra manuḥ |
yasya dṛśyeta saptāhād uktavākyasya sākṣiṇaḥ |
rogo ‘gnir jñātimaraṇam ṛṇaṃ dāpyo damaṃ ca saḥ || iti | [MDh 8.108]
na ca saṃdehe daṇḍaṃ kuryāt || 2.29.8 ||
na kevalam asatyavacane rājadaṇḍaḥ, kiṃ tarhi –
narakaś cātrādikaḥ sāṃparāye || 2.29.9 ||
sāṃparāyaḥ paralokaḥ tatra narakaś ca bhavati | na tu,
rājabhir dhṛtadaṇḍās tu kṛtvā pāpāni mānavāḥ |
nirmalāḥ svargam āyānti santaḥ sukṛtino yathā || [MDh 8.318]
ity asyāyaṃ viṣaya iti || 2.29.9 ||
satye svargaḥ sarvabhūtapraśaṃsā ca || 2.29.10 ||
satye ukte svargo bhavati, sarvāṇi ca bhūtāny enaṃ praśaṃsanti, api devāḥ || 2.29.10 ||
sā niṣṭhā yā vidyā strīṣu śūdreṣu ca || 2.29.11 ||
strīṣu śūdreṣu ca yā vidyā sā niṣṭhā vidyāsamāptiḥ | tasyām adhigatāyāṃ vidyākarma paritiṣṭhatīti || 2.29.11 ||
ātharvaṇasya vedasya śeṣa ity upadiśanti || 2.29.12 ||
atharvaṇā proktam adhīyate ātharvaṇikāḥ | “vasantādibhyaṣ ṭhak” [Pāṇ 4.2.63] | teṣām āmnāyaḥ: “ātharvaṇikasyekalopaś ca” [Pāṇ 4.3.133] ātharvaṇaḥ | tasya vedasya śeṣo ‘yaṃ “vidyā strīṣu śūdreṣu ca” ity upadiśanti dharmajñā iti | 2.29.12 ||
kṛcchrā dharmasamāptiḥ samāmnānena | lakṣaṇakarmaṇāt
tu samāpyate || 2.29.13 ||
samāmnānaṃ pratipādapāṭhaḥ, tena samāmnānena dharmasamāptiḥ kṛcchrā na śakyā kartum, kiṃ tu lakṣaṇakarmaṇā tu samāpyate | yena sāmānyena bhinnānām adhigamo bhavati tal lakṣaṇam, tasya karmaṇā karaṇena samāpyate | karmanāt tv iti dvitakārapāṭho ‘nārṣaḥ | “āt” iti vā nipātasya praśleṣaḥ, sa ca “sadyaḥ” ity asyārthe draṣṭavyaḥ || 2.29.13 ||
tatra lakṣaṇaṃ vakṣyate |
tatra lakṣaṇam | sarvajanapadeṣv ekāntasamāhitam āryāṇāṃ vṛttaṃ samyag vinītānāṃ vṛddhānām ātmavatām alolupānām adāmbhikānāṃ vṛttasādṛśyaṃ bhajeta | evam ubhau lokāv abhijayati || 2.29.14 ||
sarvajana iti gatam [ĀpDh 1.20.8–9] || 2.29.14 ||
strībhyaḥ sarvavarṇebhyaś ca dharmaśeṣān pratīyād ity eka
ity eke || 2.29.15 ||
uktavyatiriktāḥ ye dharmāḥ te dharmaśeṣāḥ, tān stryādīnām api sakāśāt pratīyād ity eke manyante | te ca pratijanapadaṃ pratikulaṃ ca bhinnāḥ tathaiva pratyetavyāḥ | tatra drāmiḍāḥ kanyā meṣasthe savitari ādityapūjām ācaranti bhūmau maṇḍalam ālikhya ityādīn udāharaṇāni | dviruktir adhyāyaparisamāptyarthā || 2.29.15 ||
ity ekonatriṃśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
iti āpastambīyadharmasūtre dvitīye praśne ekādaśaḥ paṭalaḥ samāptaḥ
iti dvitīyapraśnaḥ samāptaḥ
samāptaṃ cedam āpastambīyaṃ dharmasūtram
iti śrīharadattamiśraviracitāyāṃ āpastambīyadharmasūtravṛttau
ujjvalāyāṃ dvitīye praśne ekādaśaḥ paṭalaḥ
dvitīyaḥ praśnaś ca samāptaḥ
samāptaṃ cedaṃ āpastambīyadharmasūtravyākhyānaṃ
ujjvalākhyānam
[1] I follow Buhler. The Mysore edition reads: karmasu samarthaḥ.
